29	  , O_RC4            OUT SYS_REFCURSOR

54	 * 2025.07.30       jssbae       [E20250729-000433]_[공급/생산SCM PI팀] 월요일 SCM FCST 입력 시 MKT FCST 수정 불가하도록 시스템 개선

56	 * 2026.09.30       admin        [E20260930-DMF0082QRYPERF] 조회 속도개선: FUTURE_PRICE IDX02 지정(82 풀스캔 제거) / SP 내 GTT 통계수집 13건 제거 /
57	 *                               82 후행 UPDATE 2건 INSERT 흡수 / 중복제거 안전망 조건부 실행 (결과 전행 동일 검증)
58	 * 2026.09.30       admin        [E20260930-DMF0082CLOUDPERF] 클라우드 병목 2문장 계획 고정 힌트 (CUST 종속 실적 arm L1943 · div40 LAST_DATA_FCST L1229)

60	/****** 개체:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[P_DMF0082_GET_MAIN_DATA]    스크립트 날짜: 2026-04-22 오전 10:57:37 ******/
61	IS
62	    -- ===== 사용자 컬럼 및 메저 세팅 관련 변수 =====
63	    V_USER_COL_CNT      NUMBER := 0;
64	    V_USER_MEA_CNT      NUMBER := 0;
65	    VA_PROGRAM_ID       VARCHAR2(50 CHAR);
66	    VA_SHEET_NAME       VARCHAR2(50 CHAR);
67	
68	    -- ===== BUCKET 관련 변수 =====
69	    V_EXP_YYYYMMDD      VARCHAR2(8 CHAR);
70	    V_PV_LIST           CLOB;
71	    V_PV_COL_LIST       CLOB;
72	    V_QTY_SUM           CLOB;
73	    V_PV_COLUMN_NAME    VARCHAR2(100 CHAR);
74	    V_PV_NAME           VARCHAR2(200 CHAR);
75	    V_SORT_ORDER_NO     VARCHAR2(50 CHAR);
76	
77	    -- ===== 날짜 범위 변수 =====
78	    V_FROM_YYYYMMDD     VARCHAR2(8 CHAR);
79	    V_TO_YYYYMMDD       VARCHAR2(8 CHAR);
80	    V_TO_RESULT         VARCHAR2(8 CHAR);
81	    V_FROM_RESULT       VARCHAR2(8 CHAR);  -- [DATE ROOT FIX 2026-07-12] AS-IS @V_FROM_RESULT 복원
82	
83	    -- ===== C_TITLE 커서 변수 =====
84	    V_CUR_COLUMN_NAME   VARCHAR2(100 CHAR);
85	    V_CUR_PROGRAM_ID    VARCHAR2(10 CHAR);
86	    V_CUR_SHEET_NAME    VARCHAR2(100 CHAR);
87	    V_CUR_SORT_ORDER_NO VARCHAR2(50 CHAR);
88	    V_COLUMN            VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR);
89	    V_GROUPBY           VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR);
90	    V_ORDERBY           VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR);
91	
92	    -- ===== 권한 그룹 변수 =====
93	    V_GROUP_ID          VARCHAR2(50 CHAR);
94	    V_LIMIT_CNT         NUMBER := 0;
95	    V_QPT_DUP_CNT       NUMBER := 0;  --[E20260930-DMF0082QRYPERF-DEDUPCHK] 전용 (타 변수 재사용 금지)            --[E20260819-LIMITMEAS] donor(CURRENT) 이식 변수
96	
97	    -- ===== 동적 SQL 변수 =====
98	    V_SQL               CLOB;
99	
100	    -- ===== 로그 변수 =====
101	    V_LOG_TYPE          VARCHAR2(10 CHAR) := 'I';
102	    N_LOG_COUNT         NUMBER(10)        := 0;
103	    V_LOG_MESSAGE       VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR);
104	    V_DESCRIPTION       VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR);
105	    D_EVENT_DATE        DATE              := SYSDATE;
106	    V_PROC_INPUT        VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR);
107	    V_SQL_CODE          VARCHAR2(20 CHAR);
108	    V_SQL_ERRMSG        VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR);
109	    V_BACKTRACE         VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR);
110	
111	    -- ===== C_BUCKET 커서 =====
112	    -- Oracle PIVOT IN 구문: 'literal_value' AS alias_name 형식 필수
113	    -- COLUMN_NAME = 외부 SELECT 의 PIVOT 결과 컬럼 alias 참조 (brackets 없음)
114	    -- PV_NAME     = PIVOT IN 절의 'value' AS alias (literal 필수)
115	    CURSOR C_BUCKET IS
116	        SELECT 'D_' || A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD                                                               AS COLUMN_NAME
117	              , '''D_' || A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD || ''' AS D_' || A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD                          AS PV_NAME
118	              , A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD || '_31'                                                             AS SORT_ORDER_NO
119	        FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M A
120	        WHERE  A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD >= p_from_yyyymmdd
121	        AND    A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD <= V_EXP_YYYYMMDD
122	        UNION ALL
123	        SELECT 'W_' || A.BASIS_YYYYMM || LPAD(A.ISO_WEEK,2,'0')                                       AS COLUMN_NAME
124	              , '''W_' || A.BASIS_YYYYMM || LPAD(A.ISO_WEEK,2,'0') || ''' AS W_' || A.BASIS_YYYYMM || LPAD(A.ISO_WEEK,2,'0')  AS PV_NAME
125	              , A.BASIS_START_DATE || '_21'                                                           AS SORT_ORDER_NO
126	        FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M A
127	        WHERE  A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD >= p_from_yyyymmdd
128	        AND    A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD <= p_to_yyyymmdd
129	        GROUP BY A.BASIS_YYYYMM, A.ISO_WEEK, A.BASIS_START_DATE
130	        UNION ALL
131	        SELECT 'M_' || A.BASIS_YYYYMM                                                                 AS COLUMN_NAME
132	              , '''M_' || A.BASIS_YYYYMM || ''' AS M_' || A.BASIS_YYYYMM                              AS PV_NAME
133	              , MIN(A.BASIS_START_DATE) || '_01'                                                      AS SORT_ORDER_NO
134	        FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M A
135	        WHERE  A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD >= p_from_yyyymmdd
136	        AND    A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD <= p_to_yyyymmdd
137	        GROUP BY A.BASIS_YYYYMM
138	        ORDER BY SORT_ORDER_NO;
139	
140	    -- ===== C_TITLE 커서 =====
141	    CURSOR C_TITLE IS
142	        SELECT * FROM ( --[SORTFIX 2026-08-04] 인라인뷰 (UNION ORDER BY 식 제약 ORA-01785 회피)
143	        SELECT U.PROGRAM_ID
144	             , U.SHEET_NAME
145	             , U.COLUMN_NAME
146	             , CASE WHEN U.COLUMN_NAME = 'LAST_UPDATE_DATE' THEN '800'
147	                    WHEN U.COLUMN_NAME = 'LAST_UPDATED_BY'  THEN '560'
148	                    ELSE TO_CHAR(U.SORT_ORDER_NO) END AS SORT_ORDER_NO
149	        FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_TITLESET_USER U
150	             , SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_SHEET_COLUMNS C
151	        WHERE  1 = 1
152	        AND    U.PROGRAM_ID  = C.PROGRAM_ID
153	        AND    U.SHEET_NAME  = C.SHEET_NAME
154	        AND    U.COLUMN_NAME = C.COLUMN_NAME
155	        AND    U.PROGRAM_ID  = VA_PROGRAM_ID
156	        AND    U.SHEET_NAME  = VA_SHEET_NAME
157	        AND    U.USER_ID     = p_created_by
158	        AND    U.USER_INQUIRY_COLUMN_TYPE_CODE = 'C'
159	        -- [UICATEGORY 2026-08-07] MF_SP 산출 whitelist — titleset 'C' 오염(미산출 컬럼) 유입 시 동적 SQL ORA-00904 차단
160	        --   (DMF0003 TITLESET IMMUNIZE 파리티. 목록 = GT_DMF0082_MF_SP 전 컬럼 ∪ SRC0 파생 alias — GT ALTER 시 동기 의무)
161	        AND    U.COLUMN_NAME IN (
162	                 'DIVISION_ID','PROJECT_CODE','END_CUSTOMER','SCM_SITE_ID','ITEM_ID','DOM_EXP','FCST_TYPE','INCOTERMS'
163	                ,'MEASURE','PLANT_SITE_ID','CELL_PROD_SITE_ID','BUCKET_TYPE','BUCKET_KEY','QTY','MEASURE_GROUP'
164	                ,'CELL_MODEL','NICK_NAME','DMS_SALES','DMS_CUSTOMER','OWNER','DEPT_ID','DEPT_NAME_KO'
165	                ,'FORECAST_INFO_CELL_PACK_COUNT','ITEM_TYPE','PROJECT_NAME','PROJECT_OWNER','BIZ_TYPE'
166	                ,'SOLDTO_CUSTOMER','COUNTRY_NAME','CITY_NAME','PROJECT_STATUS','ITEM_NAME','VMI_YN'
167	                ,'CUST_GROUP','CUST_GROUP_DTL','APPLICATION','ROUTE','MDM_PROJECT_NAME','MIN_CAPACITY'
168	                ,'FORECAST_INFO_BIZ_TYPE','CELL_TYPE','FORECAST_INFO_ALL','FORECAST_INFO_SPEC'
169	                ,'FORECAST_INFOR_VERSION','CELL_MODEL_NICK_NAME','CELL_PER_CAR','CELL_PER_MODULE'
170	                ,'MODULE_PER_CAR','SORT_ORDER_NO','ENERGE','CELL_QUANTITY','AS_FROM_SITE'
171	                ,'CREATE_DATE','LAST_UPDATED_BY','SENDER_CODE','SENDER_NAME','PROJECT_S_OP_REPORT'
172	                ,'CURRENCY','VERSION_NO','CUTOFF_YYYYMMDD','NOTATION','EDIT_FLAG','SORT_KEY','GROUP_ID'
173	                ,'SUN','MON','TUE','WED','THU','FRI','SAT','YYYYMM','YYYYMMWW','WEEK_END_YN','MONTH_END_YN'
174	                ,'AS_YN','ATTR1','VMI_STD_DIO','NEW_FLAG'
175	                ,'TEAM_ID','TEAM_NAME_KO','FORECAST_INFO_CELL_MODEL','LAST_UPDATE_DATE','BUCKET' )
176	        UNION ALL
177	        /* 2024/05/17 kcw3408 이동계획 판가 매출GROUP_ID(Item은 Group 기준으로 정렬) 추가) */
178	        SELECT C.PROGRAM_ID
179	             , C.SHEET_NAME
180	             , C.COLUMN_NAME
181	             , TO_CHAR(CASE WHEN C.COLUMN_NAME = 'GROUP_ID' THEN NVL(U.SORT_ORDER_NO,85) - 0.5 ELSE C.SORT_ORDER_NO END) AS SORT_ORDER_NO
182	        FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_SHEET_COLUMNS C
183	        LEFT OUTER JOIN SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_TITLESET_USER U
184	            ON  U.PROGRAM_ID  = C.PROGRAM_ID
185	            AND U.SHEET_NAME  = C.SHEET_NAME
186	            AND U.USER_INQUIRY_COLUMN_TYPE_CODE = 'C'
187	            AND U.COLUMN_NAME IN ('ITEM_ID')
188	            AND U.USER_ID = p_created_by
189	        WHERE  1 = 1
190	        AND    C.PROGRAM_ID   = VA_PROGRAM_ID
191	        AND    C.SHEET_NAME   = VA_SHEET_NAME
192	        -- [E20260907-DEFFLAG] AS-IS: AND    C.DEFAULT_FLAG = ' ' -- [FIX 2026-07-12] AS-IS "DEFAULT_FLAG = ''"(비 default 컬럼만) 오변환('Y') → branch1 사용자 titleset 과 전 컬럼 중복 = ORA-00960
193	        AND    NVL(TRIM(C.DEFAULT_FLAG), '-') = '-'  -- [E20260907-DEFFLAG] 구 "= ' '" → **센티널 정규화**. AS-IS 원문은 DEFAULT_FLAG='' (=빈 값) 이고 ' '(클라우드) 와 '-'(ZIOUI1) 는 같은 센티널의 표기 변형이다(hook #119). 'N' 은 별개 실값이라 제외 — 이렇게 해야 양 DB 가 동일 컬럼셋(실측 10건)을 낸다. 구 조건은 ZIOUI1 에서 항상 0행이라 SORT_KEY 누락 → ORDER BY SORT_KEY 가 ORA-00904
194	        -- [E20260907-DEFFLAG] branch2 에도 branch1 과 **동일한** MF_SP 산출 whitelist 적용. 판정 완화로 비산출 컬럼(실측 DEPT_NAME)이 유입돼 ORA-00904 → branch1 비대칭 해소
195	        AND    C.COLUMN_NAME IN (
196	                 'DIVISION_ID','PROJECT_CODE','END_CUSTOMER','SCM_SITE_ID','ITEM_ID','DOM_EXP','FCST_TYPE','INCOTERMS'
197	                ,'MEASURE','PLANT_SITE_ID','CELL_PROD_SITE_ID','BUCKET_TYPE','BUCKET_KEY','QTY','MEASURE_GROUP'
198	                ,'CELL_MODEL','NICK_NAME','DMS_SALES','DMS_CUSTOMER','OWNER','DEPT_ID','DEPT_NAME_KO'
199	                ,'FORECAST_INFO_CELL_PACK_COUNT','ITEM_TYPE','PROJECT_NAME','PROJECT_OWNER','BIZ_TYPE'
200	                ,'SOLDTO_CUSTOMER','COUNTRY_NAME','CITY_NAME','PROJECT_STATUS','ITEM_NAME','VMI_YN'
201	                ,'CUST_GROUP','CUST_GROUP_DTL','APPLICATION','ROUTE','MDM_PROJECT_NAME','MIN_CAPACITY'
202	                ,'FORECAST_INFO_BIZ_TYPE','CELL_TYPE','FORECAST_INFO_ALL','FORECAST_INFO_SPEC'
203	                ,'FORECAST_INFOR_VERSION','CELL_MODEL_NICK_NAME','CELL_PER_CAR','CELL_PER_MODULE'
204	                ,'MODULE_PER_CAR','SORT_ORDER_NO','ENERGE','CELL_QUANTITY','AS_FROM_SITE'
205	                ,'CREATE_DATE','LAST_UPDATED_BY','SENDER_CODE','SENDER_NAME','PROJECT_S_OP_REPORT'
206	                ,'CURRENCY','VERSION_NO','CUTOFF_YYYYMMDD','NOTATION','EDIT_FLAG','SORT_KEY','GROUP_ID'
207	                ,'SUN','MON','TUE','WED','THU','FRI','SAT','YYYYMM','YYYYMMWW','WEEK_END_YN','MONTH_END_YN'
208	                ,'AS_YN','ATTR1','VMI_STD_DIO','NEW_FLAG'
209	                ,'TEAM_ID','TEAM_NAME_KO','FORECAST_INFO_CELL_MODEL','LAST_UPDATE_DATE','BUCKET' )
210	        AND    C.VALID_YN     = 'Y'
211	        -- [FIX 2026-07-13] branch2(비default 시트컬럼)에서 branch1(사용자 titleset 'C')과 중복 컬럼 제외.
212	        --   MEASURE 가 titleset 'C'(grid 표시용, 20260712 추가) + SHEET_COLUMNS 비default 양쪽 → V_COLUMN 중복 → PIVOT OPEN 시 ORA-00918 'MEASURE 모호'.
213	        AND    NOT EXISTS (
214	                   SELECT 1 FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_TITLESET_USER U2
215	                   WHERE  U2.PROGRAM_ID  = C.PROGRAM_ID
216	                   AND    U2.SHEET_NAME  = C.SHEET_NAME
217	                   AND    U2.COLUMN_NAME = C.COLUMN_NAME
218	                   AND    U2.USER_ID     = p_created_by
219	                   AND    U2.USER_INQUIRY_COLUMN_TYPE_CODE = 'C'
220	               )
221	        ) ORDER BY TO_NUMBER(SORT_ORDER_NO); --[SORTFIX 2026-08-04] UI Category Setup 순번 수치정렬 (TO_CHAR 문자정렬 결함 교정 — DMF0003 TO_NUMBER 원형 파리티)
222	
223	    -- [2026-07-31 PARTITION PRUNING] SCM_MST_FUTURE_PRICE_C 는 CUTOFF_YYYYMMDD_DATE
224	    --   (가상컬럼 TO_DATE(CUTOFF_YYYYMMDD,'YYYYMMDD')) 로 파티션되어 있다(클라우드).
225	    --   조회 SQL 에 CUTOFF 조건이 없어 PARTITION RANGE ALL 로 전 파티션을 스캔했다.
226	    --   선택된 버전의 CUTOFF 범위를 미리 구해 프루닝 조건으로 사용한다.
227	    V_CUTOFF_FROM       VARCHAR2(8 CHAR);
228	    V_CUTOFF_TO         VARCHAR2(8 CHAR);
229	
230	BEGIN
231	
232	    -- [E20260907-PERF2] GTT undo -> temp (redo 제거). 실측 redo 93.8MB -> 6.6MB / 12.3s -> 10.9s.
233	    --   이 SP 는 GT_DMF0082_TEMP_FCST_N_PRICE 에 12만행 규모를 적재/삭제하며, 최대 병목의 대기가
234	    --   `log buffer space` 였다. 결과·로직 불변 — 순수 I/O 비용 절감.

433	    /* BUCKET 관련 세팅 시작 */
434	    /* BUCKET 관련 세팅 시작 */
435	    --[PJ2025-50188]소형 매출계획 동기화 개선 고도화 : CURSOR 선언에 LOCAL 추가
436	    -- ====================================================================================================

438	    -- ====================================================================================================
439	
440	    /* V_EXP_YYYYMMDD: p_from_yyyymmdd + 50 weeks */
441	    --[E20260908-GETRANGE] 52주 이후 조회/저장 개방 (사용자 지시 2026-09-08).
442	    --   구 구현은 D_(일자) 버킷만 'p_from + 50주' 로 잘렸다(W_/M_ 는 p_to_yyyymmdd 전 구간) →
443	    --   50주 이후는 **헤더는 생기는데 값이 안 내려오는** 상태가 되고, 그 구간에 LOC 를 입력하면
444	    --   일자 셀이 0 으로 남아 저장 SP 의 부분주 합계 판정이 ACTION='D' 로 뒤집혀
445	    --   검증 #18(Sales Price 삭제 시 52주 이후 FCST 확인)이 수정인데 발동, 저장이 막혔다.
446	    --   ⇒ D_ 상한을 조회종료일까지 넓힌다. GREATEST 로 구 하한(from+50주)을 보존해
447	    --     짧은 조회구간의 종전 동작은 그대로 둔다(비회귀). 52주 저장 RULE(#1/#10/#18)은 무변경.
448	    V_EXP_YYYYMMDD := GREATEST(TO_CHAR(TO_DATE(p_from_yyyymmdd, 'YYYYMMDD') + (50 * 7), 'YYYYMMDD')
449	                             , p_to_yyyymmdd);
450	
451	    DBMS_LOB.CREATETEMPORARY(V_PV_LIST, TRUE);
452	    DBMS_LOB.CREATETEMPORARY(V_PV_COL_LIST, TRUE);
453	    DBMS_LOB.CREATETEMPORARY(V_QTY_SUM, TRUE);
454	
455	    OPEN C_BUCKET;
456	    LOOP
457	        FETCH C_BUCKET INTO V_PV_COLUMN_NAME, V_PV_NAME, V_SORT_ORDER_NO;
458	        EXIT WHEN C_BUCKET%NOTFOUND;
459	        IF DBMS_LOB.GETLENGTH(V_PV_COL_LIST) = 0 THEN
460	            V_PV_COL_LIST := V_PV_COLUMN_NAME;
461	            V_PV_LIST     := V_PV_NAME;
462	            V_QTY_SUM     := 'NVL(' || V_PV_COLUMN_NAME || ',0)';
463	        ELSE
464	            V_PV_COL_LIST := V_PV_COL_LIST || ',' || V_PV_COLUMN_NAME;
465	            V_PV_LIST     := V_PV_LIST     || ',' || V_PV_NAME;
466	            V_QTY_SUM     := V_QTY_SUM     || '+NVL(' || V_PV_COLUMN_NAME || ',0)';
467	        END IF;
468	    END LOOP;
469	    CLOSE C_BUCKET;
470

484	    IF p_version_type LIKE '%SP%' OR p_version_type LIKE '%MF%' OR p_version_type LIKE '%SF%' THEN
485	        --2025.03.14 Philsu Park: MF_W, SP_W Cutoff 버전에서 Amount 실적을 찾기위한 SP_VERSION_NO 추가.
486	        INSERT INTO SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_T_VERSION (VERSION_NO, VERSION_NO_W, NOW_YYYYMMDD, SP_VERSION_NO)
487	        SELECT A.TOKEN      AS VERSION_NO
488	             , CASE WHEN D.DM_RTF_YN = 'N' THEN A.TOKEN || '(' || C.ISO_YYYYWW || ') (NO PLAN)'
489	                    ELSE A.TOKEN || '(' || C.ISO_YYYYWW || ')'
490	               END          AS VERSION_NO_W
491	             , TO_CHAR(TO_DATE(B.YYYYMMDD, 'YYYYMMDD') - 7, 'YYYYMMDD') AS NOW_YYYYMMDD
492	             , (SELECT MAX(V2.VERSION_NO)
493	                FROM   SCM_MGR.DM_MST_VERSION V2
494	                INNER JOIN SCM_MGR.SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M CM
495	                    ON  V2.YYYYMMDD = CM.BASIS_YYYYMMDD
496	                WHERE  V2.VERSION_TYPE LIKE 'SP%'
497	                AND    V2.DIVISION_ID  = B.DIVISION_ID
498	                AND    CM.ISO_YYYYWW   = C.ISO_YYYYWW) AS SP_VERSION_NO
499	        FROM   (SELECT TOKEN                             /* [E20260819-MULTIJOIN] 인라인 REGEXP 분해 → GTT 선적재 소비
500	                   (IF LIKE 게이트 기존 유지 — 'ALL' 개념 없는 버전 유니버스 해석 지점) */
501	                FROM   SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_PARAM_TOKEN
502	                WHERE  PARAM_NM = 'p_version_type') A
503	        INNER JOIN SCM_MGR.DM_MST_VERSION B
504	            ON  B.VERSION_NO  = A.TOKEN
505	            AND B.DIVISION_ID = p_division_id
506	        INNER JOIN SCM_MGR.SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M C
507	            ON  C.BASIS_YYYYMMDD = B.YYYYMMDD
508	        LEFT JOIN SCM_MGR.SCP_PLN_INTERFACE_YN D
509	            ON  D.DIVISION_ID        = B.DIVISION_ID
510	            AND D.PLAN_START_YYYYMMDD = C.BASIS_START_DATE;
511	    END IF;
512	
513	    --[E20260812-PERF-NOSTATS] DBMS_STATS.GATHER_TABLE_STATS(OWNNAME => 'SCM_MGR', TABNAME => 'GT_DMF0082_T_VERSION', NO_INVALIDATE => FALSE);  -- hook #129: SP 내 통계수집 금지(ASH 병목 실측 — undo/COL$ 대기. DS2 dynamic sampling 이 대체)
514	
515	    -- [2026-07-31 PARTITION PRUNING] 선택 버전의 CUTOFF 범위 산출
516	    --   근거: VERSION_NO ↔ CUTOFF_YYYYMMDD 는 1:1 (사내 전 division 87버전 실측, 불일치 0),
517	    --         DM_MST_VERSION.YYYYMMDD = SCM_MST_FUTURE_PRICE_C.CUTOFF_YYYYMMDD (81/81 일치).
518	    --   따라서 아래 범위 조건은 **행을 걸러내지 않고** 파티션 프루닝만 유도한다.
519	    SELECT NVL(MIN(B.YYYYMMDD),'00000000'), NVL(MAX(B.YYYYMMDD),'99999999')
520	      INTO V_CUTOFF_FROM, V_CUTOFF_TO
521	      FROM SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_T_VERSION V
522	      JOIN SCM_MGR.DM_MST_VERSION B
523	        ON B.VERSION_NO = V.VERSION_NO AND B.DIVISION_ID = p_division_id;

549	    -- ====================================================================================================
550	
551	    -- [DATE ROOT FIX 2026-07-12] AS-IS L700~747 날짜경계(당주 월요일) 로직 충실 복원.
552	    --   구 변환분은 V_TO_RESULT 를 GT_..._T_VERSION 에서만 읽었으나 T_VERSION 은 Cutoff(SP/MF/SF)
553	    --   버전에서만 적재됨 → 'Current' 조회 시 V_TO_RESULT=NULL → 과거/계획 구간분리 붕괴 →
554	    --   SELLING_PRICE 가 계획구간이 아닌 전체범위(160K)로 적재되어 LOC/KRW/USD 68× 팬아웃.
555	    --   AS-IS 는 캘린더(SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M)로 기준일을 산출한다. (오늘=SYSDATE, KST)
556	    V_FROM_YYYYMMDD := p_from_yyyymmdd;
557	    V_TO_YYYYMMDD   := p_to_yyyymmdd;
558	
559	    -- @V_FROM_YYYYMMDD : 당주 월요일 (오늘 ISO 주의 시작일)
560	    BEGIN
561	        SELECT MIN(C.BASIS_YYYYMMDD)
562	        INTO   V_FROM_YYYYMMDD
563	        FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M C
564	        WHERE  C.ISO_YYYYWW = (SELECT MIN(ISO_YYYYWW)
565	                               FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M
566	                               WHERE  BASIS_YYYYMMDD = TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'YYYYMMDD'));
567	    EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN V_FROM_YYYYMMDD := p_from_yyyymmdd;
568	    END;
569	
570	    -- @V_FROM_RESULT : 조회 FROM 주 월요일
571	    BEGIN
572	        SELECT C.BASIS_START_DATE INTO V_FROM_RESULT
573	        FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M C
574	        WHERE  C.BASIS_YYYYMMDD = p_from_yyyymmdd;
575	    EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN V_FROM_RESULT := p_from_yyyymmdd;
576	    END;
577	
578	    -- @V_TO_RESULT : 조회 TO 주 월요일 (초기값 — 아래 분기에서 재조정)
579	    BEGIN
580	        SELECT C.BASIS_START_DATE INTO V_TO_RESULT
581	        FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M C
582	        WHERE  C.BASIS_YYYYMMDD = p_to_yyyymmdd;
583	    EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN V_TO_RESULT := p_to_yyyymmdd;
584	    END;
585	
586	    -- AS-IS 4분기 (앞 2개만 유효 — > / <= 배타, 뒤 2개는 도달불가 dead)
587	    IF V_FROM_YYYYMMDD > V_FROM_RESULT THEN        -- 실적+계획 (조회구간이 당주 이전부터 시작)
588	        V_TO_YYYYMMDD := p_to_yyyymmdd;
589	        V_TO_RESULT   := V_FROM_YYYYMMDD;          -- 실적/계획 경계 = 당주 월요일
590	    ELSE                                           -- 전부 미래 (실적 없음)
591	        V_TO_YYYYMMDD  := p_to_yyyymmdd;
592	        V_FROM_RESULT  := '19000101';
593	        V_TO_RESULT    := '19000101';
594	    END IF;
595	
596	    -- ====================================================================================================
597	    -- #TEMP_DM_MST_ITEM_SINGLE_ORI_V — SCM_ITEM_M_SN 에서 23컬럼 로드
598	    -- ====================================================================================================
599	
600	    DELETE FROM SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_MST_ITEM_SINGLE_ORI_V;
601	
602	    -- #TEMP_DM_MST_ITEM_SINGLE_ORI_V 만들기 시작
603	    INSERT INTO SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_MST_ITEM_SINGLE_ORI_V
604	    (
605	        DIVISION_ID, ITEM_ID, ITEM_NAME, ITEM_CLASS, ITEM_CLASS_NAME, NICK_NAME
606	      , ITEM_TYPE, MATERIAL_CLASS_NAME, ITEM_UOM, SPEC, MDM_PROJECT_NAME, MIN_CAPACITY
607	      , ACTIVE_FLAG, ENERGE, CELL_QUANTITY, SCP_EXTRA_SPEC
608	      , FORECAST_INFO_BIZ_TYPE, FORECAST_INFO_PRODUCT_GROUP
609	      , FORECAST_INFO_CELL_MODEL, FORECAST_INFO_CELL_PACK_COUNT
610	      , FORECAST_INFO_SPEC, FORECAST_INFOR_VERSION, CELL_TYPE
611	    )
612	    SELECT T1.DIVISION_ID
613	         , T1.ITEM_ID
614	         , T1.ITEM_NAME
615	         , T1.ITEM_CLASS
616	         , T1.ITEM_CLASS_NAME
617	         , T1.NICK_NAME
618	         , T1.ITEM_TYPE
619	         , T1.MATERIAL_CLASS_NAME
620	         , T1.ITEM_UOM
621	         , T1.SPEC
622	         , T1.MDM_PROJECT_NAME
623	         , T1.MIN_CAPACITY
624	         , T1.ACTIVE_FLAG
625	         , T1.ENERGE
626	         , T1.CELL_QUANTITY
627	         , T1.SCP_EXTRA_SPEC
628	         , T1.FORECAST_INFO_BIZ_TYPE
629	         , T1.FORECAST_INFO_PRODUCT_GROUP
630	         , T1.FORECAST_INFO_CELL_MODEL
631	         , T1.FORECAST_INFO_CELL_PACK_COUNT
632	         , T1.FORECAST_INFO_SPEC
633	         , T1.FORECAST_INFOR_VERSION
634	         , T1.CELL_TYPE
635	    --FROM FP_MGR.SCM_ITEM_M T1   -- linked server 원본 [LGCE_FP_TR].[DBO].[SCM_ITEM_M]
636	    FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_ITEM_M_SN T1
637	    WHERE  T1.DIVISION_ID = p_division_id
638	      AND  T1.ACTIVE_FLAG = 'Y'
639	    --[2026-07-31 항목1] 최초 데이터 생성 시 **필요한 데이터만 추출**
640	    --  site-item 에 존재하는 품목의 **12자리 접두 그룹**만 적재한다.
641	    --  ⚠ 접두 단위인 이유: 하위 _B_V 가 GROUP BY SUBSTR(ITEM_ID,1,12) 로 MAX() 6컬럼을
642	    --     집계하므로, ITEM_ID 정확 필터는 같은 접두를 공유하는 비소속 품목을 제거해
643	    --     MAX() 값을 바꾼다. 접두 단위로 자르면 그룹이 통째로 포함/제외되어 집계 불변.
644	    --  실측(div40): 98,346 -> 20,730 (-79%), _B_V MAX 6컬럼 차이 0건
645	    AND  SUBSTR(T1.ITEM_ID,1,12) IN (
646	           SELECT SUBSTR(S.ITEM_ID,1,12)
647	             FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_SITE_ITEM S
648	            WHERE S.DIVISION_ID = p_division_id );

660	    INSERT INTO SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_MST_ITEM_SINGLE_A_V
661	    (
662	        DIVISION_ID, ITEM_ID, ITEM_NAME, ITEM_CLASS, ITEM_CLASS_NAME, NICK_NAME
663	      , ITEM_TYPE, MATERIAL_CLASS_NAME, ITEM_UOM, SPEC, MDM_PROJECT_NAME, MIN_CAPACITY
664	      , ACTIVE_FLAG, ENERGE, CELL_QUANTITY, SCP_EXTRA_SPEC
665	    )
666	    SELECT T1.DIVISION_ID
667	         , T1.ITEM_ID
668	         , MAX(T1.ITEM_NAME)                                AS ITEM_NAME
669	         , MAX(T1.ITEM_CLASS)                               AS ITEM_CLASS
670	         , MAX(T1.ITEM_CLASS_NAME)                          AS ITEM_CLASS_NAME
671	         , MAX(T1.NICK_NAME)                                AS NICK_NAME
672	         , CASE MIN(CASE WHEN T1.ITEM_TYPE = 'P' THEN 1
673	                         WHEN T1.ITEM_TYPE = 'H' THEN 2
674	                         WHEN T1.ITEM_TYPE = 'Q' THEN 3
675	                         ELSE 4 END)
676	                WHEN 1 THEN 'P'
677	                WHEN 2 THEN 'H'
678	                WHEN 3 THEN 'Q'
679	                ELSE 'R'
680	           END                                              AS ITEM_TYPE
681	         , MAX(T1.MATERIAL_CLASS_NAME)                      AS MATERIAL_CLASS_NAME
682	         , MAX(T1.ITEM_UOM)                                 AS ITEM_UOM
683	         , MAX(T1.SPEC)                                     AS SPEC
684	         , MAX(T1.MDM_PROJECT_NAME)                         AS MDM_PROJECT_NAME
685	         , MAX(T1.MIN_CAPACITY)                             AS MIN_CAPACITY
686	         , MAX(T1.ACTIVE_FLAG)                              AS ACTIVE_FLAG
687	         , MAX(T1.ENERGE)                                   AS ENERGE
688	         , MAX(T1.CELL_QUANTITY)                            AS CELL_QUANTITY
689	         , MAX(T1.SCP_EXTRA_SPEC)                           AS SCP_EXTRA_SPEC
690	    FROM   SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_MST_ITEM_SINGLE_ORI_V T1
691	    WHERE  1 = 1
692	    GROUP BY T1.DIVISION_ID, T1.ITEM_ID;
693	    -- #TEMP_DM_MST_ITEM_SINGLE_A_V 만들기 종료

733	    INSERT INTO SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_MST_ITEM_SINGLE_V
734	    (
735	        DIVISION_ID, ITEM_ID, ITEM_NAME, ITEM_CLASS, ITEM_CLASS_NAME, NICK_NAME
736	      , ITEM_TYPE, MATERIAL_CLASS_NAME, ITEM_UOM, SPEC, MDM_PROJECT_NAME, MIN_CAPACITY
737	      , FORECAST_INFO_BIZ_TYPE, FORECAST_INFO_PRODUCT_GROUP
738	      , FORECAST_INFO_CELL_MODEL, FORECAST_INFO_CELL_PACK_COUNT
739	      , FORECAST_INFO_SPEC, FORECAST_INFOR_VERSION
740	      , ACTIVE_FLAG, CELL_TYPE, ENERGE, CELL_QUANTITY, SCP_EXTRA_SPEC
741	    )
742	    SELECT DIVISION_ID
743	         , ITEM_ID
744	         , ITEM_NAME
745	         , ITEM_CLASS
746	         , ITEM_CLASS_NAME
747	         , NICK_NAME
748	         , ITEM_TYPE
749	         , MATERIAL_CLASS_NAME
750	         , ITEM_UOM
751	         , SPEC
752	         , MDM_PROJECT_NAME
753	         , MIN_CAPACITY
754	         , FORECAST_INFO_BIZ_TYPE
755	         , FORECAST_INFO_PRODUCT_GROUP
756	         , FORECAST_INFO_CELL_MODEL
757	         , FORECAST_INFO_CELL_PACK_COUNT
758	         , FORECAST_INFO_SPEC
759	         , FORECAST_INFOR_VERSION
760	         , ACTIVE_FLAG
761	         , CELL_TYPE
762	         , CASE WHEN TO_NUMBER(ENERGE DEFAULT NULL ON CONVERSION ERROR) IS NOT NULL
763	                THEN ROUND(TO_NUMBER(ENERGE) / 1000, 4)
764	                ELSE NULL
765	           END                                              AS ENERGE  -- WH → KWH 환산
766	         , CELL_QUANTITY
767	         , SCP_EXTRA_SPEC
768	    FROM (
769	        SELECT T2X.DIVISION_ID
770	             , T2X.ITEM_ID
771	             , T2X.ITEM_NAME
772	             , T2X.ITEM_CLASS
773	             , T2X.ITEM_CLASS_NAME
774	             , T2X.NICK_NAME
775	             , T2X.ITEM_TYPE
776	             , T2X.MATERIAL_CLASS_NAME
777	             , T2X.ITEM_UOM
778	             , T2X.SPEC
779	             , T2X.MDM_PROJECT_NAME
780	             , T2X.MIN_CAPACITY
781	             , T2X.FORECAST_INFO_BIZ_TYPE
782	             , T2X.FORECAST_INFO_PRODUCT_GROUP
783	             , T2X.FORECAST_INFO_CELL_MODEL
784	             , T2X.FORECAST_INFO_CELL_PACK_COUNT
785	             , T2X.FORECAST_INFO_SPEC
786	             , T2X.FORECAST_INFOR_VERSION
787	             , T2X.ACTIVE_FLAG
788	             , T2X.CELL_TYPE
789	             , T2X.ENERGE
790	             , T2X.CELL_QUANTITY
791	             , T2X.SCP_EXTRA_SPEC
792	        FROM (
793	            SELECT T1.DIVISION_ID
794	                 , T1.ITEM_ID
795	                 , T1.ITEM_NAME
796	                 , T1.ITEM_CLASS
797	                 , T1.ITEM_CLASS_NAME
798	                 , T1.NICK_NAME
799	                 , T1.ITEM_TYPE
800	                 , T1.MATERIAL_CLASS_NAME
801	                 , T1.ITEM_UOM
802	                 , T1.SPEC
803	                 , T1.MDM_PROJECT_NAME
804	                 , T1.MIN_CAPACITY
805	                 , T2.FORECAST_INFO_BIZ_TYPE
806	                 , T2.FORECAST_INFO_PRODUCT_GROUP
807	                 , T2.FORECAST_INFO_CELL_MODEL
808	                 , T2.FORECAST_INFO_CELL_PACK_COUNT
809	                 , T2.FORECAST_INFO_SPEC
810	                 , T2.FORECAST_INFOR_VERSION
811	                 , T1.ACTIVE_FLAG
812	                 , T2.CELL_TYPE
813	                 , T1.ENERGE
814	                 , T1.CELL_QUANTITY
815	                 , T1.SCP_EXTRA_SPEC
816	            FROM   SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_MST_ITEM_SINGLE_A_V T1
817	            INNER JOIN SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_MST_ITEM_SINGLE_B_V T2
818	                ON  T1.DIVISION_ID = T2.DIVISION_ID
819	                AND SUBSTR(T1.ITEM_ID, 1, 12) = T2.ITEM_ID
820	            WHERE 1 = 1
821	        ) T2X
822	        WHERE  T2X.DIVISION_ID = p_division_id
823	    )
824	    GROUP BY DIVISION_ID, ITEM_ID, ITEM_NAME, ITEM_CLASS, ITEM_CLASS_NAME, NICK_NAME, ITEM_TYPE, MATERIAL_CLASS_NAME
825	           , ITEM_UOM, SPEC, MDM_PROJECT_NAME, MIN_CAPACITY, FORECAST_INFO_BIZ_TYPE, FORECAST_INFO_PRODUCT_GROUP
826	           , FORECAST_INFO_CELL_MODEL, FORECAST_INFO_CELL_PACK_COUNT, FORECAST_INFO_SPEC, FORECAST_INFOR_VERSION
827	           , ACTIVE_FLAG, CELL_TYPE, ENERGE, CELL_QUANTITY, SCP_EXTRA_SPEC;
828	    /* [E20260819-MULTIJOIN] Item 필터 — 구 인라인 REGEXP 분해 '%토큰%' LIKE INNER JOIN 을 GTT 선적재+NOT EXISTS(LIKE) 로 전환.
829	       'ALL' 개념 없음(텍스트 검색) — NULL/'' 이면 미적용(구 코드의 NULL토큰='%%' 전건통과와 동일 의미).
830	       ITEM_ID/NICK_NAME 스테이징 1:1 실측 — LIKE OR 술어 원형 보존. */
831	    IF p_item_id IS NOT NULL THEN
832	        DELETE FROM SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_MST_ITEM_SINGLE_V F
833	        WHERE  NOT EXISTS (
834	                   SELECT 1
835	                   FROM   SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_PARAM_TOKEN T
836	                   WHERE  T.PARAM_NM = 'p_item_id'
837	                   AND    (F.ITEM_ID   LIKE '%' || T.TOKEN || '%'
838	                        OR F.NICK_NAME LIKE '%' || T.TOKEN || '%'));
839	    END IF;

852	    -- #TempSiteItemAll 만들기 시작
853	    INSERT INTO SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_TEMP_SITE_ITEM_ALL
854	    (
855	        DIVISION_ID, PROJECT_CODE, SCM_SITE_ID, ITEM_ID
856	      , CELL_MODEL, NICK_NAME, DMS_SALES, DMS_CUSTOMER, OWNER
857	      , DEPT_ID, DEPT_NAME_KO, FORECAST_INFO_CELL_PACK_COUNT
858	      , ITEM_TYPE, PROJECT_NAME, PROJECT_OWNER, BIZ_TYPE
859	      , SOLDTO_CUSTOMER, COUNTRY_NAME, CITY_NAME, PROJECT_STATUS
860	      , ITEM_NAME, VMI_YN, CUST_GROUP, CUST_GROUP_DTL, APPLICATION, ROUTE
861	      , MDM_PROJECT_NAME, MIN_CAPACITY, FORECAST_INFO_BIZ_TYPE, CELL_TYPE
862	      , FORECAST_INFO_ALL, FORECAST_INFO_SPEC, FORECAST_INFOR_VERSION
863	      , CELL_MODEL_NICK_NAME, CELL_PER_CAR, CELL_PER_MODULE, MODULE_PER_CAR
864	      , SORT_ORDER_NO, ENERGE, CELL_QUANTITY, PLANT_SITE_ID
865	      , ITEM_VALID_YN, END_CUSTOMER, PROJECT_S_OP_REPORT
866	    )
867	    SELECT F.DIVISION_ID
868	         , F.PROJECT_CODE
869	         , F.SCM_SITE_ID
870	         , F.ITEM_ID
871	         , F.ATTRIBUTE5                                     AS CELL_MODEL
872	         , T2.NICK_NAME
873	         , F.ATTRIBUTE1                                     AS DMS_SALES
874	         , F.ATTRIBUTE2                                     AS DMS_CUSTOMER
875	         , (SELECT LISTAGG(X.USER_NAME_EN, ',') WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY X.USER_NAME_EN)
876	            FROM   (SELECT DISTINCT ZA.USER_NAME_EN
877	                    FROM   (SELECT REGEXP_SUBSTR(F.OWNER, '[^,]+', 1, LEVEL) AS TOK  -- [PERF 2026-07-12] IN->JOIN uses PK_SCM_MST_SSO_USER_INFO
878	                            FROM   DUAL
879	                            CONNECT BY LEVEL <= REGEXP_COUNT(F.OWNER, ',') + 1) TK
880	                    JOIN   SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_SSO_USER_INFO ZA ON ZA.USER_ID = TK.TOK) X
881	           )                                                AS OWNER
882	         , TEAM.DEPT_ID
883	         , TEAM.DEPT_NAME_KO
884	         , CASE WHEN F.ITEM_ID = LOOKA.MODEL
885	                THEN TO_NUMBER(LOOKA.CELL_PACK_COUNT DEFAULT NULL ON CONVERSION ERROR)
886	                ELSE T2.FORECAST_INFO_CELL_PACK_COUNT
887	           END                                              AS FORECAST_INFO_CELL_PACK_COUNT
888	         , T2.ITEM_TYPE
889	         , NVL(MP.PROJECT_NAME, F.PROJECT_CODE) AS PROJECT_NAME  --[E20260819-PJTNAME]
890	         , J.USER_NAME_EN                                   AS PROJECT_OWNER
891	         , ''                                               AS BIZ_TYPE
892	         , S.SOLDTO_CUSTOMER
893	         , I.COUNTRY_NAME
894	         , H.CITY_NAME
895	         , ''                                               AS PROJECT_STATUS
896	         , T2.ITEM_NAME
897	         , S.VMI_YN
898	         , F.CUST_GROUP
899	         , F.CUST_GROUP_DTL
900	         , F.APPLICATION
901	         , F.ROUTE
902	         , T2.MDM_PROJECT_NAME
903	         , TO_NUMBER(T2.MIN_CAPACITY DEFAULT NULL ON CONVERSION ERROR) AS MIN_CAPACITY
904	         , CASE WHEN F.ITEM_ID = LOOKA.MODEL THEN LOOKA.BIZ_TYPE
905	                ELSE T2.FORECAST_INFO_BIZ_TYPE
906	           END                                              AS FORECAST_INFO_BIZ_TYPE
907	         , LOOK.NAME                                        AS CELL_TYPE
908	         , T2.FORECAST_INFO_SPEC || T2.FORECAST_INFOR_VERSION  AS FORECAST_INFO_ALL
909	         , T2.FORECAST_INFO_SPEC
910	         , T2.FORECAST_INFOR_VERSION
911	         , T4.NICK_NAME                                     AS CELL_MODEL_NICK_NAME
912	         , TO_NUMBER(F.CELL_PER_CAR    DEFAULT NULL ON CONVERSION ERROR) AS CELL_PER_CAR
913	         , TO_NUMBER(F.CELL_PER_MODULE DEFAULT NULL ON CONVERSION ERROR) AS CELL_PER_MODULE
914	         , TO_NUMBER(F.CELL_PER_CAR    DEFAULT NULL ON CONVERSION ERROR)
915	           / CASE WHEN NVL(TO_NUMBER(F.CELL_PER_MODULE DEFAULT NULL ON CONVERSION ERROR), 0) = 0 THEN 1
916	                  ELSE TO_NUMBER(F.CELL_PER_MODULE DEFAULT NULL ON CONVERSION ERROR)
917	             END                                             AS MODULE_PER_CAR
918	         , NULL                                             AS SORT_ORDER_NO
919	         , TO_CHAR(T2.ENERGE)                               AS ENERGE
920	         , TO_NUMBER(T2.CELL_QUANTITY DEFAULT NULL ON CONVERSION ERROR) AS CELL_QUANTITY
921	         , '-'                                               AS PLANT_SITE_ID        -- 원본 GTT 에 없는 컬럼
922	         , F.VALID_YN                                       AS ITEM_VALID_YN
923	         , S.END_CUSTOMER
924	         , NULL                                              AS PROJECT_S_OP_REPORT  -- Case: 20260416 SCM_MST_SITE_ITEM.PROJECT_S_OP_REPORT 삭제
925	    FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_SITE_ITEM F
926	    INNER JOIN SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_MST_ITEM_SINGLE_V T2
927	        ON  F.DIVISION_ID = T2.DIVISION_ID
928	        AND F.ITEM_ID     = T2.ITEM_ID
929	    INNER JOIN SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_SITE S
930	        ON  F.DIVISION_ID = S.DIVISION_ID
931	        AND F.SCM_SITE_ID = S.SCM_SITE_ID
932	        AND S.SITE_TYPE   = 'SALES'
933	    INNER JOIN SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_CITY H
934	        ON  S.CITY        = H.CITY
935	    INNER JOIN SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_COUNTRY I
936	        ON  S.COUNTRY     = I.COUNTRY
937	    --[E20260819-PJTNAME] Project 명 원천 — SCM_MST_PROJECT (미등재 코드=코드 폴백 LEFT)
938	    LEFT JOIN SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_PROJECT MP
939	        ON  F.DIVISION_ID  = MP.DIVISION_ID
940	        AND F.PROJECT_CODE = MP.PROJECT_CODE
941	    -- [hook#150 2026-09-06 AJS] AS-IS: LEFT JOIN (
942	        -- [hook#150 2026-09-06 AJS] AS-IS: SELECT B2.USER_ID, B2.USER_NAME, B2.USER_NAME_EN, B2.DEPT_ID, C.DEPT_NAME_KO
943	        -- [hook#150 2026-09-06 AJS] AS-IS: FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_SSO_USER_INFO B2
944	        -- [hook#150 2026-09-06 AJS] AS-IS: INNER JOIN SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_SSO_DEPT_INFO C
945	            -- [hook#150 2026-09-06 AJS] AS-IS: ON B2.DEPT_ID = C.DEPT_ID
946	    -- [hook#150 2026-09-06 AJS] AS-IS: ) TEAM
947	        -- [hook#150 2026-09-06 AJS] AS-IS: ON TEAM.USER_ID = CASE WHEN INSTR(F.OWNER, ',') = 0
948	                                -- [hook#150 2026-09-06 AJS] AS-IS: THEN F.OWNER
949	                                -- [hook#150 2026-09-06 AJS] AS-IS: ELSE SUBSTR(F.OWNER, 1, INSTR(F.OWNER, ',') - 1)
950	                           -- [hook#150 2026-09-06 AJS] AS-IS: END
951	    -- 2026-09-06 hook#150 AJS: DEPT_ID 원천 = SCM_MST_SITE_ITEM.DEPT_ID (SCM_MST_SITE.DEPT_ID 삭제 예정 / OWNER 경유 폐기)
952	    LEFT JOIN SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_SSO_DEPT_INFO TEAM
953	        ON TEAM.DEPT_ID = F.DEPT_ID
954	    LEFT JOIN SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_SSO_USER_INFO J
955	        ON J.USER_ID = CASE WHEN INSTR(F.OWNER, ',') = 0
956	                             THEN F.OWNER
957	                             ELSE SUBSTR(F.OWNER, 1, INSTR(F.OWNER, ',') - 1)
958	                        END
959	    LEFT JOIN SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_SSO_DEPT_INFO K
960	        -- [hook#150 2026-09-06 AJS] AS-IS: ON K.DEPT_ID = CASE WHEN F.DIVISION_ID IN ('40') THEN J.DEPT_ID
961	                             -- [hook#150 2026-09-06 AJS] AS-IS: ELSE F.DEPT_ID   -- Case 39: SCM_MST_SITE.DEPT_ID 삭제, SCM_MST_SITE_ITEM.DEPT_ID 사용
962	                        -- [hook#150 2026-09-06 AJS] AS-IS: END
963	        ON K.DEPT_ID = F.DEPT_ID  -- 2026-09-06 hook#150 AJS (AS-IS: div40 은 J.DEPT_ID = OWNER 경유)
964	    LEFT JOIN (
965	        SELECT SEGMENT1 AS CODE, SEGMENT2 AS NAME
966	        FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL
967	        WHERE  LOOKUP_TYPE = 'CELL_TYPE'
968	    ) LOOK
969	        ON LOOK.CODE = T2.CELL_TYPE
970	    LEFT JOIN SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_MST_ITEM_SINGLE_V T4
971	        ON  T2.DIVISION_ID = T4.DIVISION_ID
972	        AND T2.FORECAST_INFO_CELL_MODEL = T4.ITEM_ID
973	    LEFT JOIN (
974	        SELECT A.MODEL, A.BIZ_TYPE, A.CELL_PACK_COUNT, A.MODEL_RANK
975	        FROM (
976	            SELECT SEGMENT2 AS MODEL
977	                 , SEGMENT3 AS BIZ_TYPE
978	                 , SEGMENT4 AS CELL_PACK_COUNT
979	                 , RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY SEGMENT2 ORDER BY DISPLAY_SEQ DESC) AS MODEL_RANK
980	            FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL
981	            WHERE  LOOKUP_TYPE = 'DM_BIZ_TYPE_40'
982	              AND  SEGMENT1    = '40'
983	        ) A
984	        WHERE MODEL_RANK = 1
985	    ) LOOKA
986	        ON LOOKA.MODEL = F.ITEM_ID
987	    WHERE  F.DIVISION_ID = p_division_id;
988	    /* [E20260819-MULTIJOIN] 조회조건 SCM Site / Customer / Biz Type — 구 인라인 OR분기(REGEXP 분해)를 GTT 선적재+IF게이트+NOT EXISTS 로 전환.
989	       Biz Type 술어식(CASE WHEN F.ITEM_ID=LOOKA.MODEL...)은 FORECAST_INFO_BIZ_TYPE 컬럼으로 1:1 스테이징(실측) — 동일 값 필터.
990	       전 컬럼 ALL_TAB_COLUMNS 실측 NULLABLE='Y' → 양변 원문 비교(구 bare-IN 의미론 보존 — 무값 행은 값 지정 시 제외). */
991	    IF p_scm_site_id IS NOT NULL AND p_scm_site_id <> 'ALL' THEN
992	        DELETE FROM SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_TEMP_SITE_ITEM_ALL F
993	        WHERE  NOT EXISTS (
994	                   SELECT 1
995	                   FROM   SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_PARAM_TOKEN T
996	                   WHERE  T.PARAM_NM = 'p_scm_site_id'
997	                   AND    T.TOKEN    = F.SCM_SITE_ID);
998	    END IF;
999	    /* [E20260908-ENDCUST] End Customer 필터 축 정정 — 정본 = AS-IS DMF0003_GET_MKT_FCST_DATA (사용자 지시 2026-09-08).
1000	         AS-IS 실측:
1001	           · `where f.division_id <> '40'` 브랜치 (L1681) → `S.END_CUSTOMER`  (S = SCM_MST_SITE)
1002	           · `where f.division_id  = '40'` 브랜치 (L1838) → `f.project_code`
1003	         구 구현은 division 무관하게 PROJECT_CODE 하고만 대조해, 81 계열에서 EC 콤보가 주는
1004	         **사이트 파생 고객명**(FilterEndCustomerByDivision 3번째 UNION = SCM_MST_SITE.END_CUSTOMER)이
1005	         절대 일치하지 않아 스테이징이 전건 삭제 → 0행이 됐다 (신고: div 81 + BMW 선택 시 미조회).
1006	         본 GTT 의 END_CUSTOMER 컬럼은 이미 `S.END_CUSTOMER`(SCM_MST_SITE) 로 적재된다(적재 SELECT 실측).
1007	         ⚠ Project 조건은 별도 파라미터 `p_project_code` 가 독립 적용하므로(아래 3300행대)
1008	           Project + End Customer **동시 입력**이 그대로 AND 로 걸린다. */
1009	    IF p_customer IS NOT NULL AND p_customer <> 'ALL' THEN
1010	        DELETE FROM SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_TEMP_SITE_ITEM_ALL F
1011	        WHERE  NOT EXISTS (
1012	                   SELECT 1
1013	                   FROM   SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_PARAM_TOKEN T
1014	                   WHERE  T.PARAM_NM = 'p_customer'
1015	                   AND    T.TOKEN    = CASE WHEN p_division_id = '40' THEN F.PROJECT_CODE
1016	                                            ELSE F.END_CUSTOMER END);
1017	    END IF;
1018	    IF p_biz_type IS NOT NULL AND p_biz_type <> 'ALL' THEN
1019	        DELETE FROM SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_TEMP_SITE_ITEM_ALL F
1020	        WHERE  NOT EXISTS (
1021	                   SELECT 1
1022	                   FROM   SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_PARAM_TOKEN T
1023	                   WHERE  T.PARAM_NM = 'p_biz_type'
1024	                   AND    T.TOKEN    = F.FORECAST_INFO_BIZ_TYPE);
1025	    END IF;
1026	    /* [E20260819-MULTIJOIN] Item 필터 — 구 인라인 REGEXP 분해 '%토큰%' LIKE INNER JOIN 을 GTT 선적재+NOT EXISTS(LIKE) 로 전환.
1027	       'ALL' 개념 없음(텍스트 검색) — NULL/'' 이면 미적용(구 코드의 NULL토큰='%%' 전건통과와 동일 의미).
1028	       ITEM_ID/NICK_NAME 스테이징 1:1 실측 — LIKE OR 술어 원형 보존. */
1029	    IF p_item_id IS NOT NULL THEN
1030	        DELETE FROM SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_TEMP_SITE_ITEM_ALL F
1031	        WHERE  NOT EXISTS (
1032	                   SELECT 1
1033	                   FROM   SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_PARAM_TOKEN T
1034	                   WHERE  T.PARAM_NM = 'p_item_id'
1035	                   AND    (F.ITEM_ID   LIKE '%' || T.TOKEN || '%'
1036	                        OR F.NICK_NAME LIKE '%' || T.TOKEN || '%'));
1037	    END IF;
1038	    -- #TempSiteItemAll 만들기 종료
1039	
1040	    NULL; --[E20260930-DMF0082QRYPERF-NOSTATS] DBMS_STATS.GATHER_TABLE_STATS(OWNNAME => 'SCM_MGR', TABNAME => 'GT_DMF0082_TEMP_SITE_ITEM_ALL', NO_INVALIDATE => FALSE);  /* 2026.08.15 AJS 대형 GTT 통계 유지 — 미수집 시 MF_W 3.95→364초(92배) 실증 */

1049	    INSERT INTO SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_TEMP_SITE_ITEM
1050	    (
1051	        DIVISION_ID, PROJECT_CODE, SCM_SITE_ID, ITEM_ID, NICK_NAME, PROJECT_NAME
1052	      , CELL_MODEL, DMS_SALES, DMS_CUSTOMER, OWNER
1053	      , DEPT_ID, DEPT_NAME_KO, FORECAST_INFO_CELL_PACK_COUNT
1054	      , ITEM_TYPE, PROJECT_OWNER, BIZ_TYPE
1055	      , SOLDTO_CUSTOMER, COUNTRY_NAME, CITY_NAME, PROJECT_STATUS
1056	      , ITEM_NAME, VMI_YN, CUST_GROUP, CUST_GROUP_DTL, APPLICATION, ROUTE
1057	      , MDM_PROJECT_NAME, MIN_CAPACITY, FORECAST_INFO_BIZ_TYPE, CELL_TYPE
1058	      , FORECAST_INFO_ALL, FORECAST_INFO_SPEC, FORECAST_INFOR_VERSION
1059	      , CELL_MODEL_NICK_NAME, CELL_PER_CAR, CELL_PER_MODULE, MODULE_PER_CAR
1060	      , SORT_ORDER_NO, ENERGE, CELL_QUANTITY, PLANT_SITE_ID
1061	      , ITEM_VALID_YN, END_CUSTOMER, PROJECT_S_OP_REPORT
1062	    )
1063	    SELECT DIVISION_ID, PROJECT_CODE, SCM_SITE_ID, ITEM_ID, NICK_NAME, PROJECT_NAME
1064	         , MAX(CELL_MODEL)                      AS CELL_MODEL
1065	         , MAX(DMS_SALES)                       AS DMS_SALES
1066	         , MAX(DMS_CUSTOMER)                    AS DMS_CUSTOMER
1067	         , MAX(OWNER)                           AS OWNER
1068	         , MAX(DEPT_ID)                         AS DEPT_ID
1069	         , MAX(DEPT_NAME_KO)                    AS DEPT_NAME_KO
1070	         , MAX(FORECAST_INFO_CELL_PACK_COUNT)   AS FORECAST_INFO_CELL_PACK_COUNT
1071	         , MAX(ITEM_TYPE)                       AS ITEM_TYPE
1072	         , MAX(PROJECT_OWNER)                   AS PROJECT_OWNER
1073	         , MAX(BIZ_TYPE)                        AS BIZ_TYPE
1074	         , MAX(SOLDTO_CUSTOMER)                 AS SOLDTO_CUSTOMER
1075	         , MAX(COUNTRY_NAME)                    AS COUNTRY_NAME
1076	         , MAX(CITY_NAME)                       AS CITY_NAME
1077	         , MAX(PROJECT_STATUS)                  AS PROJECT_STATUS
1078	         , MAX(ITEM_NAME)                       AS ITEM_NAME
1079	         , MAX(VMI_YN)                          AS VMI_YN
1080	         , MAX(CUST_GROUP)                      AS CUST_GROUP
1081	         , MAX(CUST_GROUP_DTL)                  AS CUST_GROUP_DTL
1082	         , MAX(APPLICATION)                     AS APPLICATION
1083	         , MAX(ROUTE)                           AS ROUTE
1084	         , MAX(MDM_PROJECT_NAME)                AS MDM_PROJECT_NAME
1085	         , MAX(MIN_CAPACITY)                    AS MIN_CAPACITY
1086	         , MAX(FORECAST_INFO_BIZ_TYPE)          AS FORECAST_INFO_BIZ_TYPE
1087	         , MAX(CELL_TYPE)                       AS CELL_TYPE
1088	         , MAX(FORECAST_INFO_ALL)               AS FORECAST_INFO_ALL
1089	         , MAX(FORECAST_INFO_SPEC)              AS FORECAST_INFO_SPEC
1090	         , MAX(FORECAST_INFOR_VERSION)          AS FORECAST_INFOR_VERSION
1091	         , MAX(CELL_MODEL_NICK_NAME)            AS CELL_MODEL_NICK_NAME
1092	         , MAX(CELL_PER_CAR)                    AS CELL_PER_CAR
1093	         , MAX(CELL_PER_MODULE)                 AS CELL_PER_MODULE
1094	         , MAX(MODULE_PER_CAR)                  AS MODULE_PER_CAR
1095	         , MAX(SORT_ORDER_NO)                   AS SORT_ORDER_NO
1096	         , MAX(ENERGE)                          AS ENERGE
1097	         , MAX(CELL_QUANTITY)                   AS CELL_QUANTITY
1098	         , MAX(PLANT_SITE_ID)                   AS PLANT_SITE_ID
1099	         , MAX(ITEM_VALID_YN)                   AS ITEM_VALID_YN
1100	         , MAX(END_CUSTOMER)                    AS END_CUSTOMER
1101	         , MAX(PROJECT_S_OP_REPORT)             AS PROJECT_S_OP_REPORT
1102	    FROM   SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_TEMP_SITE_ITEM_ALL
1103	    GROUP BY DIVISION_ID, PROJECT_CODE, SCM_SITE_ID, ITEM_ID, NICK_NAME, PROJECT_NAME;

1121	    DELETE FROM SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_TEMP_SELECT_MEASURE;
1122	
1123	    INSERT INTO SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_TEMP_SELECT_MEASURE
1124	        (MEASURE, RESULT_MEASURE, MEASURE_GROUP, SORT_ORDER_NO, USER_ID)
1125	    SELECT U.COLUMN_NAME                          AS MEASURE
1126	         , NVL(M.ATTRIBUTE5, U.COLUMN_NAME)       AS RESULT_MEASURE
1127	         , U.COLUMN_NAME                          AS MEASURE_GROUP
1128	         , U.SORT_ORDER_NO
1129	         , U.USER_ID
1130	    FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_TITLESET_USER U
1131	    INNER JOIN SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_SHEET_MEASURE M
1132	        ON  M.PROGRAM_ID  = U.PROGRAM_ID
1133	        AND M.SHEET_NAME  = U.SHEET_NAME
1134	        AND M.COLUMN_NAME = U.COLUMN_NAME
1135	        AND M.VALID_YN    = 'Y'
1136	    WHERE  1 = 1
1137	    AND    U.PROGRAM_ID = VA_PROGRAM_ID
1138	    AND    U.SHEET_NAME = VA_SHEET_NAME
1139	    AND    U.USER_INQUIRY_COLUMN_TYPE_CODE = 'M'
1140	    AND    U.USER_ID    = p_created_by;
1141	
1142	    /* [E20260825-CUSTSUB] CUST_FCST 종속 메저 확장 (사용자 지시 2026-08-25).
1143	       AS-IS DMF0003 CSR C20191031-000137 / E20241126-000426 계보 이식 — 정본 = PKG_DMF0003 L6498~6564.
1144	       사용자가 CUST_FCST 를 선택하면:
1145	         ① EDI 하위 고정 8종 (CUST_FCST_EDI 0.11 ~ Planning_Forecast 0.18)
1146	         ② DM_MST_CUSTOMER_FCST 등재 메저 (데이터 기반, parent+0.10)
1147	       를 하위 행으로 자동 확장한다. 원장(81) 실측: CUST_FCST_EDI 41,750행·Commercial_Agreement 41,137행.
1148	       RESULT_MEASURE = 자기 이름(실적 매핑 없음 — AS-IS 동일). 중복 방어 = 이름 GROUP BY + NOT EXISTS. */
1149	    INSERT INTO SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_TEMP_SELECT_MEASURE
1150	        (MEASURE, RESULT_MEASURE, MEASURE_GROUP, SORT_ORDER_NO, USER_ID)
1151	    SELECT S.MEASURE, S.MEASURE, 'CUST_FCST', A.SORT_ORDER_NO + S.SORT_NO, A.USER_ID
1152	    FROM  (SELECT U.USER_ID, U.SORT_ORDER_NO
1153	           FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_TITLESET_USER U
1154	           WHERE  U.PROGRAM_ID = VA_PROGRAM_ID
1155	             AND  U.SHEET_NAME = VA_SHEET_NAME
1156	             AND  U.USER_INQUIRY_COLUMN_TYPE_CODE = 'M'
1157	             AND  U.USER_ID    = p_created_by
1158	             AND  U.COLUMN_NAME = 'CUST_FCST') A,
1159	          (SELECT MEASURE, MIN(SORT_NO) AS SORT_NO
1160	           FROM  (SELECT 'CUST_FCST_EDI'               AS MEASURE, 0.11 AS SORT_NO FROM DUAL UNION ALL
1161	                  SELECT 'Backorder'                             , 0.12 FROM DUAL UNION ALL
1162	                  SELECT 'Commercial_Agreement'                  , 0.13 FROM DUAL UNION ALL
1163	                  SELECT 'Firm_Temp'                             , 0.14 FROM DUAL UNION ALL
1164	                  SELECT 'Immidiate'                             , 0.15 FROM DUAL UNION ALL
1165	                  SELECT 'Manumacturing_Material_Temp'           , 0.16 FROM DUAL UNION ALL
1166	                  SELECT 'Material_Temp'                         , 0.17 FROM DUAL UNION ALL
1167	                  SELECT 'Planning_Forecast'                     , 0.18 FROM DUAL
1168	                  UNION ALL
1169	                  SELECT C.MEASURE, 0.10
1170	                  FROM   SCM_MGR.DM_MST_CUSTOMER_FCST C
1171	                  WHERE  C.MEASURE_GROUP = 'CUST_FCST'
1172	                    AND  NVL(C.MEASURE, '-') <> '-'
1173	                    AND  C.VALID_YN = 'Y')
1174	           GROUP BY MEASURE) S
1175	    WHERE NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_TEMP_SELECT_MEASURE X
1176	                      WHERE X.MEASURE = S.MEASURE);

2906	             , A.SCM_SITE_ID, A.ITEM_ID, A.DOM_EXP, A.FCST_TYPE, A.INCOTERMS
2907	             , A.MEASURE, A.PLANT_SITE_ID, A.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, A.YYYYMMDD, A.QTY
2908	             , B.MEASURE_GROUP
2909	             , C.CELL_MODEL, C.NICK_NAME, C.DMS_SALES, C.DMS_CUSTOMER, C.OWNER
2910	             , C.DEPT_ID, C.DEPT_NAME_KO, C.FORECAST_INFO_CELL_PACK_COUNT
2911	             , C.ITEM_TYPE, C.PROJECT_NAME, C.PROJECT_OWNER, C.BIZ_TYPE
2912	             , C.SOLDTO_CUSTOMER, C.COUNTRY_NAME, C.CITY_NAME, C.PROJECT_STATUS
2913	             , C.ITEM_NAME, C.VMI_YN, C.CUST_GROUP, C.CUST_GROUP_DTL
2914	             , C.APPLICATION, C.ROUTE, C.MDM_PROJECT_NAME, C.MIN_CAPACITY
2915	             , C.FORECAST_INFO_BIZ_TYPE, C.CELL_TYPE, C.FORECAST_INFO_ALL
2916	             , C.FORECAST_INFO_SPEC, C.FORECAST_INFOR_VERSION, C.CELL_MODEL_NICK_NAME
2917	             , C.CELL_PER_CAR, C.CELL_PER_MODULE, C.MODULE_PER_CAR, B.SORT_ORDER_NO
2918	             , C.ENERGE, C.CELL_QUANTITY, NVL(A.ATTRIBUTE10, ' ') AS AS_FROM_SITE
2919	             , LD.CREATE_DATE, LD.LAST_UPDATED_BY
2920	             , '' AS SENDER_CODE, '' AS SENDER_NAME, C.PROJECT_S_OP_REPORT
2921	             , A.CURRENCY, F.VERSION_NO, A.CUTOFF_YYYYMMDD
2922	             , CASE WHEN A.MEASURE IN ('KRW','USD','LOC','MKT_AMT_LOC','MKT_AMT_KRW') THEN 'POINT3' ELSE 'INTEGER' END AS NOTATION
2923	        FROM   SCM_MGR.DM_PLN_SALES_PLAN_RESULT_C A
2924	        INNER JOIN SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_TEMP_SELECT_MEASURE B ON B.RESULT_MEASURE = A.MEASURE
2925	        LEFT JOIN ( -- [FIX 2026-07-13] 행별 LATERAL(FETCH FIRST 1) → set-based GROUP BY (키별 최신 CREATE_DATE+짝 LAST_UPDATED_BY). 의미동일.
2926	                SELECT DIVISION_ID, PROJECT_CODE, SCM_SITE_ID, ITEM_ID, DOM_EXP, FCST_TYPE, INCOTERMS, MEASURE
2927	                     , MAX(CREATE_DATE) AS CREATE_DATE
2928	                     , MAX(LAST_UPDATED_BY) KEEP (DENSE_RANK LAST ORDER BY CREATE_DATE) AS LAST_UPDATED_BY
2929	                FROM   SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_T_LAST_DATA_SETUP_FCST_C
2930	                GROUP BY DIVISION_ID, PROJECT_CODE, SCM_SITE_ID, ITEM_ID, DOM_EXP, FCST_TYPE, INCOTERMS, MEASURE
2931	            ) LD
2932	                ON  LD.DIVISION_ID  = A.DIVISION_ID
2933	                AND LD.PROJECT_CODE = A.PROJECT_CODE
2934	                AND LD.SCM_SITE_ID  = A.SCM_SITE_ID
2935	                AND LD.ITEM_ID      = A.ITEM_ID
2936	                AND LD.DOM_EXP      = A.DOM_EXP
2937	                AND LD.FCST_TYPE    = A.FCST_TYPE
2938	                AND LD.INCOTERMS    = A.INCOTERMS
2939	                AND LD.MEASURE      = A.MEASURE
2940	        INNER JOIN SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_TEMP_SITE_ITEM C
2941	            ON  C.DIVISION_ID=A.DIVISION_ID AND C.PROJECT_CODE=A.PROJECT_CODE
2942	            AND C.SCM_SITE_ID=A.SCM_SITE_ID AND C.ITEM_ID=A.ITEM_ID AND C.ITEM_VALID_YN='Y'
2943	        INNER JOIN SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_T_WS_FCST D ON D.FCST_TYPE=A.FCST_TYPE
2944	        INNER JOIN (SELECT * FROM SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_T_VERSION WHERE VERSION_NO LIKE 'SP%') F
2945	            ON  A.VERSION_NO = F.SP_VERSION_NO
2946	        WHERE  A.DIVISION_ID = p_division_id
2947	        AND    A.YYYYMMDD >= p_from_yyyymmdd AND A.YYYYMMDD < V_TO_RESULT
2948	        AND    A.QTY <> 0;
2949	
2950	    END IF; -- SP 버전
2951	
2952	    -- ====================================================================================================
2953	    -- CURRENCY 업데이트 (계획구간 통화코드 보완)
2954	    -- ====================================================================================================
2955	
2956	    -- [FIX 2026-07-13] 상관 서브쿼리 UPDATE(행별 GTT 재스캔 O(n^2), 넓은 조회범위서 PGA 폭증→"Free private memory to OS" hang)
2957	    --   → MERGE 사전집계(키별 MAX(LOC CURRENCY) 1회 GROUP BY) + hash join 으로 O(n). 결과 동일(키별 MAX, 미매칭=NULL 유지).
2958	    MERGE /*+ LEADING(B) USE_HASH(A) */ INTO SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_TEMP_FCST_N_PRICE A
2959	    --[2026-07-31 항목5] hash build 를 소형 집계뷰(B, ~6K행)로 고정 — 355K 와이드행 build 로 인한 TEMP 159MB spill 제거 (조인 입력 순서만 변경, 결과 불변)
2960	    USING (
2961	               SELECT DIVISION_ID, PROJECT_CODE, SCM_SITE_ID, PLANT_SITE_ID, ITEM_ID
2962	                    , INCOTERMS, DOM_EXP, FCST_TYPE, CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, MAX(CURRENCY) AS CURRENCY
2963	               FROM   SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_TEMP_FCST_N_PRICE
2964	               WHERE  MEASURE = 'LOC'
2965	               AND    YYYYMMDD BETWEEN V_TO_RESULT AND V_TO_YYYYMMDD
2966	               GROUP BY DIVISION_ID, PROJECT_CODE, SCM_SITE_ID, PLANT_SITE_ID, ITEM_ID
2967	                    , INCOTERMS, DOM_EXP, FCST_TYPE, CELL_PROD_SITE_ID
2968	           ) B
2969	    ON (    A.DIVISION_ID       = B.DIVISION_ID
2970	        AND A.PROJECT_CODE      = B.PROJECT_CODE
2971	        AND A.SCM_SITE_ID       = B.SCM_SITE_ID
2972	        AND A.PLANT_SITE_ID     = B.PLANT_SITE_ID
2973	        AND A.ITEM_ID           = B.ITEM_ID
2974	        AND A.INCOTERMS         = B.INCOTERMS
2975	        AND A.DOM_EXP           = B.DOM_EXP
2976	        AND A.FCST_TYPE         = B.FCST_TYPE
2977	        AND A.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID = B.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID )
2978	    WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET A.CURRENCY = B.CURRENCY
2979	    WHERE  A.CURRENCY IS NULL
2980	    AND    A.MEASURE NOT LIKE 'VMI%'
2981	    AND    A.YYYYMMDD BETWEEN p_from_yyyymmdd AND V_TO_YYYYMMDD;
2982	
2983	    -- ====================================================================================================
2984	    -- [E20260918-CURRCELLPROD] CURRENCY 2차 보완 — CELL_PROD_SITE_ID 제외 8키 폴백
2985	    --
2986	    --   위 1차 MERGE 는 CELL_PROD_SITE_ID 를 조인키에 포함한다(AS-IS 동일). 그런데 GTT 적재 arm 별로
2987	    --   이 컬럼의 채움 규약이 다르다 — 단가 원천 SCM_MST_FUTURE_PRICE 에는 컬럼 자체가 없어 '-' 를
2988	    --   고정 삽입하고, 수요/실적 원천은 실제값(NJ01-A 등)을 그대로 쓴다. DM_PLN_CFM_SELLING_PRICE 도
2989	    --   '-' 와 실제값이 섞여 있다(40 실측 '-' 950,621 / 실제값 600,783).
2990	    --   그 결과 같은 사업 키인데 이 한 컬럼만 엇갈려 1차가 매칭되지 않고,
2991	    --   단가(Unit Price)에는 통화가 보이는데 RTF/MKT FCST/SCM FCST 는 CURRENCY 가 빈다.
2992	    --   (ZIOUI1 실증 2026-09-18 — 40 Current: MIF489266C11/NT_CN_SZX 등 3키, 수요 NJ01-A vs 단가 '-')
2993	    --
2994	    --   통화는 판매가의 속성이라 생산 셀 사이트와 무관하다. 다만 1차는 원문 그대로 두고,
2995	    --   **1차 후에도 비어 있는 행만** 8키로 메운다(비파괴 — 이미 채워진 값은 건드리지 않음).
2996	    --   HAVING COUNT(DISTINCT CURRENCY) = 1 : 8키 안에서 통화가 갈리는 키는 제외해 오배정을 차단한다.
2997	    -- ====================================================================================================
2998	    MERGE /*+ LEADING(B) USE_HASH(A) */ INTO SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_TEMP_FCST_N_PRICE A
2999	    USING (
3000	               -- [E20260918-CURRNULLKEY] 조인키 NULL 정규화 — 원장별로 센티널이 NULL / '-' 로 갈린다
3001	               --   (클라우드 실증: 수요·실적 PLANT_SITE_ID = NULL, 단가 = '-')
3002	               --   MERGE ON 은 NULL 이면 UNKNOWN 이라 매칭 자체가 불가하므로 USING 과 ON 을 함께 정규화한다.
3003	               SELECT NVL(DIVISION_ID, '-') AS DIVISION_ID
3004	                    , NVL(PROJECT_CODE, '-') AS PROJECT_CODE
3005	                    , NVL(SCM_SITE_ID, '-') AS SCM_SITE_ID
3006	                    , NVL(PLANT_SITE_ID, '-') AS PLANT_SITE_ID
3007	                    , NVL(ITEM_ID, '-') AS ITEM_ID
3008	                    , NVL(INCOTERMS, '-') AS INCOTERMS
3009	                    , NVL(DOM_EXP, '-') AS DOM_EXP
3010	                    , NVL(FCST_TYPE, '-') AS FCST_TYPE
3011	                    , MIN(CURRENCY) AS CURRENCY
3012	               FROM   SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_TEMP_FCST_N_PRICE
3013	               WHERE  MEASURE = 'LOC'
3014	               AND    CURRENCY IS NOT NULL
3015	               AND    YYYYMMDD BETWEEN V_TO_RESULT AND V_TO_YYYYMMDD
3016	               GROUP BY NVL(DIVISION_ID, '-')
3017	                    , NVL(PROJECT_CODE, '-')
3018	                    , NVL(SCM_SITE_ID, '-')
3019	                    , NVL(PLANT_SITE_ID, '-')
3020	                    , NVL(ITEM_ID, '-')
3021	                    , NVL(INCOTERMS, '-')
3022	                    , NVL(DOM_EXP, '-')
3023	                    , NVL(FCST_TYPE, '-')
3024	               HAVING COUNT(DISTINCT CURRENCY) = 1
3025	           ) B
3026	    ON (    NVL(A.DIVISION_ID, '-') = B.DIVISION_ID
3027	        AND NVL(A.PROJECT_CODE, '-') = B.PROJECT_CODE
3028	        AND NVL(A.SCM_SITE_ID, '-') = B.SCM_SITE_ID
3029	        AND NVL(A.PLANT_SITE_ID, '-') = B.PLANT_SITE_ID
3030	        AND NVL(A.ITEM_ID, '-') = B.ITEM_ID
3031	        AND NVL(A.INCOTERMS, '-') = B.INCOTERMS
3032	        AND NVL(A.DOM_EXP, '-') = B.DOM_EXP
3033	        AND NVL(A.FCST_TYPE, '-') = B.FCST_TYPE )
3034	    WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET A.CURRENCY = B.CURRENCY
3035	    WHERE  A.CURRENCY IS NULL
3036	    AND    A.MEASURE NOT LIKE 'VMI%'
3037	    AND    A.YYYYMMDD BETWEEN p_from_yyyymmdd AND V_TO_YYYYMMDD;
3038	
3039	    -- ====================================================================================================
3040	    -- [E20260918-CURRSITEITEM] CURRENCY 3차 보완 — DIV+SITE+ITEM+DOM_EXP+FCST_TYPE 폴백
3041	    --
3042	    --   2차(8키)로도 못 메우는 케이스가 있다. 수요 원장과 단가 원장 사이에서 어긋나는 컬럼이
3043	    --   CELL_PROD_SITE_ID 하나가 아니라 INCOTERMS·PROJECT_CODE·PLANT_SITE_ID 로도 갈리기 때문이다.
3044	    --   실증(ZIOUI1 2026-09-18) 40 / Varta RO_RO_OTP / MCCM131012A1 —
3045	    --     수요 INCOTERMS=CIF·PROJECT=Varta 인데 단가는 CIP(Varta) / CIF(VARTA(Vacuum)) 뿐.
3046	    --     그럼에도 이 ITEM+SITE 의 단가는 전부 USD 단일이다.
3047	    --
3048	    --   실측(DIV 40 LOC): 통화가 2종 이상인 축 비율 = 8키 0.3% / 본 축 1.5% / DIV+SITE 10.0%.
3049	    --   본 축까지만 완화하고, 갈리는 키는 HAVING 으로 제외해 오배정을 차단한다.
3050	    --   1·2차 후에도 비어 있는 행만 채운다(비파괴).
3051	    -- ====================================================================================================
3052	    MERGE /*+ LEADING(B) USE_HASH(A) */ INTO SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_TEMP_FCST_N_PRICE A
3053	    USING (
3054	               -- [E20260918-CURRNULLKEY] 조인키 NULL 정규화 (2차와 동일 사유)
3055	               SELECT NVL(DIVISION_ID, '-') AS DIVISION_ID
3056	                    , NVL(SCM_SITE_ID, '-') AS SCM_SITE_ID
3057	                    , NVL(ITEM_ID, '-') AS ITEM_ID
3058	                    , NVL(DOM_EXP, '-') AS DOM_EXP
3059	                    , NVL(FCST_TYPE, '-') AS FCST_TYPE
3060	                    , MIN(CURRENCY) AS CURRENCY
3061	               FROM   SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_TEMP_FCST_N_PRICE
3062	               WHERE  MEASURE = 'LOC'
3063	               AND    CURRENCY IS NOT NULL
3064	               AND    YYYYMMDD BETWEEN V_TO_RESULT AND V_TO_YYYYMMDD
3065	               GROUP BY NVL(DIVISION_ID, '-')
3066	                    , NVL(SCM_SITE_ID, '-')
3067	                    , NVL(ITEM_ID, '-')
3068	                    , NVL(DOM_EXP, '-')
3069	                    , NVL(FCST_TYPE, '-')
3070	               HAVING COUNT(DISTINCT CURRENCY) = 1
3071	           ) B
3072	    ON (    NVL(A.DIVISION_ID, '-') = B.DIVISION_ID
3073	        AND NVL(A.SCM_SITE_ID, '-') = B.SCM_SITE_ID
3074	        AND NVL(A.ITEM_ID, '-') = B.ITEM_ID
3075	        AND NVL(A.DOM_EXP, '-') = B.DOM_EXP
3076	        AND NVL(A.FCST_TYPE, '-') = B.FCST_TYPE )
3077	    WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET A.CURRENCY = B.CURRENCY
3078	    WHERE  A.CURRENCY IS NULL
3079	    AND    A.MEASURE NOT LIKE 'VMI%'
3080	    AND    A.YYYYMMDD BETWEEN p_from_yyyymmdd AND V_TO_YYYYMMDD;
3081	
3082	    NULL; --[E20260930-DMF0082QRYPERF-NOSTATS] DBMS_STATS.GATHER_TABLE_STATS(OWNNAME => 'SCM_MGR', TABNAME => 'GT_DMF0082_TEMP_FCST_N_PRICE', NO_INVALIDATE => FALSE);  /* 2026.08.15 AJS 대형 GTT 통계 유지 — 미수집 시 MF_W 3.95→364초(92배) 실증 */
3083	
3084	    -- ====================================================================================================
3085	    -- LOC/KRW Unit Price 계산 — MKT_AMT / RTF_SYS_RP
3086	    -- ====================================================================================================
3087	
3088	    /* 2026.08.15 AJS 신뢰도검사 발견3 — MSSQL 원본은 이 계산을 버전 분기(CURRENT/MF/SP/SF) 안에서 수행한다.
3089	       CURRENT/MF/SF 세 블록은 아래 공통형과 동일하므로 묶어서 유지하고, 구조가 다른 SP 는 뒤에서 따로 계산한다. */
3090	    IF p_version_type LIKE '%Current%' OR p_version_type LIKE '%current%'
3091	       OR p_version_type LIKE '%MF%' OR p_version_type LIKE '%SF%' THEN
3092	    INSERT INTO SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_TEMP_FCST_N_PRICE
3093	    ( DIVISION_ID, PROJECT_CODE, END_CUSTOMER, SCM_SITE_ID, ITEM_ID, DOM_EXP, FCST_TYPE, INCOTERMS
3094	    , MEASURE, PLANT_SITE_ID, CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, YYYYMMDD, QTY, MEASURE_GROUP
3095	    , CELL_MODEL, NICK_NAME, DMS_SALES, DMS_CUSTOMER, OWNER, DEPT_ID, DEPT_NAME_KO
3096	    , FORECAST_INFO_CELL_PACK_COUNT, ITEM_TYPE, PROJECT_NAME, PROJECT_OWNER, BIZ_TYPE
3097	    , SOLDTO_CUSTOMER, COUNTRY_NAME, CITY_NAME, PROJECT_STATUS, ITEM_NAME, VMI_YN
3098	    , CUST_GROUP, CUST_GROUP_DTL, APPLICATION, ROUTE, MDM_PROJECT_NAME, MIN_CAPACITY
3099	    , FORECAST_INFO_BIZ_TYPE, CELL_TYPE, FORECAST_INFO_ALL, FORECAST_INFO_SPEC
3100	    , FORECAST_INFOR_VERSION, CELL_MODEL_NICK_NAME, CELL_PER_CAR, CELL_PER_MODULE
3101	    , MODULE_PER_CAR, SORT_ORDER_NO, ENERGE, CELL_QUANTITY, AS_FROM_SITE
3102	    , CREATE_DATE, LAST_UPDATED_BY, SENDER_CODE, SENDER_NAME, PROJECT_S_OP_REPORT
3103	    , CURRENCY, VERSION_NO, CUTOFF_YYYYMMDD, NOTATION)
3104	    -- LOC/KRW Unit Price
3105	    SELECT A.DIVISION_ID, A.PROJECT_CODE, A.PROJECT_CODE AS END_CUSTOMER
3106	         , A.SCM_SITE_ID, A.ITEM_ID, A.DOM_EXP, A.FCST_TYPE, A.INCOTERMS
3107	         , D.MEASURE
3108	         , A.PLANT_SITE_ID, A.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, A.YYYYMMDD
3109	         , CASE D.MEASURE
3110	               WHEN 'LOC' THEN CASE WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN A.MEASURE='RTF_SYS_RP'   THEN A.QTY ELSE 0 END) <> 0
3111	                                    THEN SUM(CASE WHEN A.MEASURE='MKT_AMT_LOC' THEN A.QTY ELSE 0 END)
3112	                                       / SUM(CASE WHEN A.MEASURE='RTF_SYS_RP'  THEN A.QTY ELSE 0 END)
3113	                                    ELSE 0 END
3114	               WHEN 'KRW' THEN CASE WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN A.MEASURE='RTF_SYS_RP'   THEN A.QTY ELSE 0 END) <> 0
3115	                                    THEN SUM(CASE WHEN A.MEASURE='MKT_AMT_KRW' THEN A.QTY ELSE 0 END)
3116	                                       / SUM(CASE WHEN A.MEASURE='RTF_SYS_RP'  THEN A.QTY ELSE 0 END)
3117	                                    ELSE 0 END
3118	           END AS QTY
3119	         , MAX(D.MEASURE_GROUP)
3120	         , MAX(A.CELL_MODEL), MAX(A.NICK_NAME), MAX(A.DMS_SALES), MAX(A.DMS_CUSTOMER)
3121	         , MAX(A.OWNER), MAX(A.DEPT_ID), MAX(A.DEPT_NAME_KO)
3122	         , MAX(A.FORECAST_INFO_CELL_PACK_COUNT), MAX(A.ITEM_TYPE)
3123	         , MAX(A.PROJECT_NAME), MAX(A.PROJECT_OWNER), MAX(A.BIZ_TYPE)
3124	         , MAX(A.SOLDTO_CUSTOMER), MAX(A.COUNTRY_NAME), MAX(A.CITY_NAME)
3125	         , MAX(A.PROJECT_STATUS), MAX(A.ITEM_NAME), MAX(A.VMI_YN)
3126	         , MAX(A.CUST_GROUP), MAX(A.CUST_GROUP_DTL), MAX(A.APPLICATION)
3127	         , MAX(A.ROUTE), MAX(A.MDM_PROJECT_NAME), MAX(A.MIN_CAPACITY)
3128	         , MAX(A.FORECAST_INFO_BIZ_TYPE), MAX(A.CELL_TYPE)
3129	         , MAX(A.FORECAST_INFO_ALL), MAX(A.FORECAST_INFO_SPEC)
3130	         , MAX(A.FORECAST_INFOR_VERSION), MAX(A.CELL_MODEL_NICK_NAME)
3131	         , MAX(A.CELL_PER_CAR), MAX(A.CELL_PER_MODULE), MAX(A.MODULE_PER_CAR)
3132	         , MAX(D.SORT_ORDER_NO), MAX(A.ENERGE), MAX(A.CELL_QUANTITY)
3133	         , NVL(MAX(A.AS_FROM_SITE), ' '), MAX(A.CREATE_DATE), MAX(A.LAST_UPDATED_BY)
3134	         , MAX(A.SENDER_CODE), MAX(A.SENDER_NAME), MAX(A.PROJECT_S_OP_REPORT)
3135	         , MAX(A.CURRENCY), A.VERSION_NO, A.CUTOFF_YYYYMMDD
3136	         , CASE WHEN D.MEASURE IN ('KRW','USD','LOC','MKT_AMT_LOC','MKT_AMT_KRW') THEN 'POINT3' ELSE 'INTEGER' END AS NOTATION
3137	    FROM   SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_TEMP_FCST_N_PRICE A
3138	    JOIN   SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_TEMP_SELECT_MEASURE D ON D.MEASURE IN ('LOC','KRW')
3139	    WHERE  A.MEASURE IN ('RTF_SYS_RP','MKT_AMT_LOC','MKT_AMT_KRW')
3140	    AND    A.YYYYMMDD >= p_from_yyyymmdd
3141	    AND    A.YYYYMMDD < V_TO_RESULT
3142	    GROUP BY A.DIVISION_ID, A.PROJECT_CODE, A.SCM_SITE_ID, A.ITEM_ID
3143	           , A.DOM_EXP, A.FCST_TYPE, A.INCOTERMS, A.PLANT_SITE_ID
3144	           , A.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, A.CURRENCY, A.YYYYMMDD, D.MEASURE
3145	           , A.VERSION_NO, A.CUTOFF_YYYYMMDD
3146	    UNION ALL
3147	    -- FCST_GAP = MKT_FCST - SCM_FCST
3148	    SELECT A.DIVISION_ID, A.PROJECT_CODE, A.PROJECT_CODE AS END_CUSTOMER
3149	         , A.SCM_SITE_ID, A.ITEM_ID, A.DOM_EXP, A.FCST_TYPE, A.INCOTERMS
3150	         , D.MEASURE
3151	         , A.PLANT_SITE_ID, A.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, A.YYYYMMDD
3152	         , SUM(CASE WHEN A.MEASURE='SCM_FCST' THEN A.QTY * -1 ELSE A.QTY END) AS QTY
3153	         , MAX(D.MEASURE_GROUP)
3154	         , MAX(A.CELL_MODEL), MAX(A.NICK_NAME), MAX(A.DMS_SALES), MAX(A.DMS_CUSTOMER)
3155	         , MAX(A.OWNER), MAX(A.DEPT_ID), MAX(A.DEPT_NAME_KO)

3432	       무값(NULL) 행은 매칭 실패=삭제가 의도. hook #119 §1.5 NVL('-') 양변 정규화는 NOT NULL 센티널 컬럼 한정이라 미적용. */
3433	    IF p_sender_code IS NOT NULL AND p_sender_code <> 'ALL' THEN
3434	        DELETE FROM SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_TEMP_FCST_N_PRICE F
3435	        WHERE  NOT EXISTS (
3436	                   SELECT 1
3437	                   FROM   SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_PARAM_TOKEN T
3438	                   WHERE  T.PARAM_NM = 'p_sender_code'
3439	                   AND    T.TOKEN    = F.SENDER_CODE);
3440	    END IF;

3444	    --   p_team_id 가 로그에만 쓰이고 필터 지점이 없어 팀 지정이 무시되던 결함. PLANT_SITE 동형.

3457	        /* [E20260820-SENTINELDDL] 구 [E20260815-SENTINEL] PLANT_SITE_ID 변형 정규화 UPDATE 삭제 —
3458	       원장 일괄 정규화('-')·중복 제거 + DEFAULT ON NULL '-' DDL + 변형 기록 배치 정규화 완료(사용자 확인
3459	       2026-08-20)로 변형(' '/'-'/'--'/CHR(0))이 원천 소멸. 아래 중복 제거 DELETE 는 안전망으로 존치. */
3460	
3461	        /* 정규화로 같아진 행 중 1건만 남긴다. 상관 서브쿼리 대신 ROW_NUMBER 로 set-based 처리
3462	       (진단·정정 SQL 도 튜닝 품질 유지).
3463	       ⭐ 남길 행 = **QTY 최대** (사용자 확정 B, 2026-08-15). 값이 같은 그룹은 어느 행이 남아도
3464	         동일하고, 다른 그룹(실측 16,395)만 최대값이 채택된다.
3465	       ⛔ SUM 은 채택하지 않았다 — 동일 복제본이 다수(88%)라 합산하면 그만큼 3배가 된다. */
3466	        --[E20260930-DMF0082QRYPERF-DEDUPCHK] 중복 존재 시에만 안전망 DELETE (0행 삭제에 전량 윈도정렬 비용 제거)
3467	        SELECT COUNT(*) INTO V_QPT_DUP_CNT FROM (
3468	            SELECT 1 FROM SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_TEMP_FCST_N_PRICE
3469	            GROUP BY DIVISION_ID, PROJECT_CODE, END_CUSTOMER, SCM_SITE_ID, ITEM_ID, DOM_EXP, FCST_TYPE, INCOTERMS, MEASURE, PLANT_SITE_ID, CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, YYYYMMDD, MEASURE_GROUP, CELL_MODEL, NICK_NAME, DMS_SALES, DMS_CUSTOMER, OWNER, DEPT_ID, DEPT_NAME_KO, FORECAST_INFO_CELL_PACK_COUNT, ITEM_TYPE, PROJECT_NAME, PROJECT_OWNER, BIZ_TYPE, SOLDTO_CUSTOMER, COUNTRY_NAME, CITY_NAME, PROJECT_STATUS, ITEM_NAME, VMI_YN, CUST_GROUP, CUST_GROUP_DTL, APPLICATION, ROUTE, MDM_PROJECT_NAME, MIN_CAPACITY, FORECAST_INFO_BIZ_TYPE, CELL_TYPE, FORECAST_INFO_ALL, FORECAST_INFO_SPEC, FORECAST_INFOR_VERSION, CELL_MODEL_NICK_NAME, CELL_PER_CAR, CELL_PER_MODULE, MODULE_PER_CAR, SORT_ORDER_NO, ENERGE, CELL_QUANTITY, AS_FROM_SITE, CREATE_DATE, LAST_UPDATED_BY, SENDER_CODE, SENDER_NAME, PROJECT_S_OP_REPORT, CURRENCY, VERSION_NO, CUTOFF_YYYYMMDD, NOTATION
3470	            HAVING COUNT(*) > 1) WHERE ROWNUM = 1;
3471	        IF V_QPT_DUP_CNT > 0 THEN
3472	        DELETE FROM SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_TEMP_FCST_N_PRICE
3473	         WHERE ROWID IN (SELECT RID
3474	                     FROM  (SELECT ROWID AS RID,
3475	                                   ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY DIVISION_ID, PROJECT_CODE, END_CUSTOMER, SCM_SITE_ID, ITEM_ID, DOM_EXP, FCST_TYPE, INCOTERMS, MEASURE, PLANT_SITE_ID, CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, YYYYMMDD, MEASURE_GROUP, CELL_MODEL, NICK_NAME, DMS_SALES, DMS_CUSTOMER, OWNER, DEPT_ID, DEPT_NAME_KO, FORECAST_INFO_CELL_PACK_COUNT, ITEM_TYPE, PROJECT_NAME, PROJECT_OWNER, BIZ_TYPE, SOLDTO_CUSTOMER, COUNTRY_NAME, CITY_NAME, PROJECT_STATUS, ITEM_NAME, VMI_YN, CUST_GROUP, CUST_GROUP_DTL, APPLICATION, ROUTE, MDM_PROJECT_NAME, MIN_CAPACITY, FORECAST_INFO_BIZ_TYPE, CELL_TYPE, FORECAST_INFO_ALL, FORECAST_INFO_SPEC, FORECAST_INFOR_VERSION, CELL_MODEL_NICK_NAME, CELL_PER_CAR, CELL_PER_MODULE, MODULE_PER_CAR, SORT_ORDER_NO, ENERGE, CELL_QUANTITY, AS_FROM_SITE, CREATE_DATE, LAST_UPDATED_BY, SENDER_CODE, SENDER_NAME, PROJECT_S_OP_REPORT, CURRENCY, VERSION_NO, CUTOFF_YYYYMMDD, NOTATION
3476	                                                      ORDER BY QTY DESC NULLS LAST, ROWID) AS RN
3477	                            FROM   SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_TEMP_FCST_N_PRICE)
3478	                     WHERE RN > 1);
3479	        END IF;

3485	    /* [E20260831-QTYKEY] Qty >= 0 체크(p_qty_yn='Y') = AS-IS DMF0082_GET_MAIN_DATA L5890~ 1:1.
3486	       구 구현(셀 단위 QTY=0 DELETE)은 키의 타 메저 행이 남아 필터가 무효였다(통합테스트 40: 7,668건 불변).
3487	       정본 의미 = **9축 키 단위**: MEASURE IN ('MKT_FCST','SCM_FCST') 의 SUM(QTY) <> 0 인 키만 잔존
3488	       (그 키의 전 메저 행 유지 — AS-IS #MF_SP DELETE NOT EXISTS 동형).

3490	       위치 = 조회조건 필터·중복 제거 이후(AS-IS 는 전 필터 완료된 #MF_SP 최종 단계) — CUSTSUBORPH 앞
3491	       (모행이 여기서 잘린 CUST 종속 메저는 아래 안전망이 함께 제거). 등호 비교 = AS-IS 파리티. */

3495	        WHERE  NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1
3496	                           FROM  (SELECT DIVISION_ID
3497	                                       , SCM_SITE_ID
3498	                                       , PROJECT_CODE
3499	                                       , ITEM_ID
3500	                                       , INCOTERMS
3501	                                       , FCST_TYPE
3502	                                       , DOM_EXP
3503	                                       , END_CUSTOMER
3504	                                       , CELL_PROD_SITE_ID
3505	                                  FROM   SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_TEMP_FCST_N_PRICE

3508	                                       , SCM_SITE_ID
3509	                                       , PROJECT_CODE

3514	                                       , END_CUSTOMER
3515	                                       , CELL_PROD_SITE_ID

3517	                                 ) TT
3518	                           WHERE  1 = 1

3523	                           AND    TT.INCOTERMS         = SP.INCOTERMS

3525	                           AND    TT.DOM_EXP           = SP.DOM_EXP
3526	                           AND    TT.END_CUSTOMER      = SP.END_CUSTOMER
3527	                           AND    TT.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID = SP.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID

3531	    /* [E20260826-CUSTSUBORPH] CUST 종속 메저 고아 행 제외 (사용자 지시 2026-08-26 — 클라우드 실측).
3532	       증상: 모 CUST_FCST 행이 없는 그리드 키에 └ 종속 메저(EDI/Commercial_Agreement 등)만 조회.
3533	       원인: [E20260826-CUSTSUBR] 실적 arm(DM_PLN_MKT_FCST_RESULT)이 모행 없는 키의 종속 실적까지 적재
3534	       (클라우드 원장에만 해당 조합 존재 — 사내 실측 고아 0·클라우드 적용 직후 발현).
3535	       AS-IS 파리티 실측(FcstPrice_20260826_102207_ASIS.xlsx): 종속 키 81 = 모행 키 81, 고아 0.
3536	       위치 = 조회조건 필터·중복 제거 이후(최종 pivot 직전) — 필터로 모행만 잘린 케이스도 동일 규칙.
3537	       키 = 그리드 행 식별 축(파일 대사 키와 동일 10축). 양변 NVL 정규화(hook #119 §1.5). arm 불문 안전망. */
3538	    DELETE FROM SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_TEMP_FCST_N_PRICE F
3539	    WHERE  F.MEASURE_GROUP = 'CUST_FCST'
3540	    AND    F.MEASURE      <> 'CUST_FCST'
3541	    AND    NOT EXISTS (
3542	               SELECT 1
3543	               FROM   SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_TEMP_FCST_N_PRICE P
3544	               WHERE  P.MEASURE = 'CUST_FCST'
3545	               AND    NVL(P.DIVISION_ID,  ' ') = NVL(F.DIVISION_ID,  ' ')
3546	               AND    NVL(P.PROJECT_CODE, ' ') = NVL(F.PROJECT_CODE, ' ')
3547	               AND    NVL(P.END_CUSTOMER, ' ') = NVL(F.END_CUSTOMER, ' ')
3548	               AND    NVL(P.SCM_SITE_ID,  ' ') = NVL(F.SCM_SITE_ID,  ' ')
3549	               AND    NVL(P.ITEM_ID,      ' ') = NVL(F.ITEM_ID,      ' ')
3550	               AND    NVL(P.DOM_EXP,      ' ') = NVL(F.DOM_EXP,      ' ')
3551	               AND    NVL(P.FCST_TYPE,    ' ') = NVL(F.FCST_TYPE,    ' ')
3552	               AND    NVL(P.INCOTERMS,    ' ') = NVL(F.INCOTERMS,    ' ')
3553	               AND    NVL(P.PLANT_SITE_ID,'-') = NVL(F.PLANT_SITE_ID,'-')
3554	               AND    NVL(P.AS_FROM_SITE, ' ') = NVL(F.AS_FROM_SITE, ' '));

3556	    -- C_TITLE 커서 — 동적 컬럼 목록 생성
3557	    -- ====================================================================================================
3558	
3559	    V_COLUMN  := '';
3560	    V_GROUPBY := '';
3561	    V_ORDERBY := '';
3562	
3563	    OPEN C_TITLE;
3564	    LOOP
3565	        FETCH C_TITLE INTO V_CUR_PROGRAM_ID, V_CUR_SHEET_NAME, V_CUR_COLUMN_NAME, V_CUR_SORT_ORDER_NO;
3566	        EXIT WHEN C_TITLE%NOTFOUND;
3567	        IF V_COLUMN IS NULL THEN
3568	            V_COLUMN  := V_CUR_COLUMN_NAME;
3569	            V_GROUPBY := ' GROUP BY ' || V_CUR_COLUMN_NAME;
3570	            V_ORDERBY := ' ORDER BY SORT_KEY, ' || CASE WHEN V_CUR_COLUMN_NAME NOT IN ('SORT_KEY','EDIT_FLAG') THEN V_CUR_COLUMN_NAME ELSE '' END;
3571	        ELSE
3572	            V_COLUMN  := V_COLUMN  || ', ' || V_CUR_COLUMN_NAME;
3573	            V_GROUPBY := V_GROUPBY || ', ' || V_CUR_COLUMN_NAME;
3574	            V_ORDERBY := V_ORDERBY || CASE WHEN V_CUR_COLUMN_NAME NOT IN ('SORT_KEY','EDIT_FLAG') THEN ', ' || V_CUR_COLUMN_NAME ELSE '' END;
3575	        END IF;
3576	    END LOOP;
3577	    CLOSE C_TITLE;
3578	
3579	    --[E20240812-000094] NOTATION 컬럼 추가
3580	    -- C_TITLE 0행일 때 V_COLUMN NULL 방어 (leading comma 방지로 ORA-00936 회피)
3581	    V_COLUMN  := CASE WHEN V_COLUMN  IS NULL THEN 'NOTATION'              ELSE V_COLUMN  || ', NOTATION' END;
3582	    V_GROUPBY := CASE WHEN V_GROUPBY IS NULL THEN ' GROUP BY EDIT_FLAG, SORT_ORDER_NO' ELSE V_GROUPBY || ', EDIT_FLAG, SORT_ORDER_NO' END;
3583	
3584	    --[E20260822-KEYCOL] 저장 키 컬럼은 Column Setting 과 무관하게 항상 반환한다 (사용자 승인 A안, 2026-08-22).
3585	    --  C_TITLE 는 SCM_MST_TITLESET_USER('C') 를 읽으므로 사용자가 키 컬럼을 숨기면 GET 이 값을 안 준다.
3586	    --  그러면 FE 가 '' 를 보내 ①검증이 Common 으로 오판(수정 차단) ②MERGE ON 키 불일치(중복행) 가 난다.
3587	    --  실측: CELL_PROD_SITE_ID 를 숨긴 계정에서 기존 행 M 저장이
3588	    --        'Cell Prod Site* 를 Common으로 등록 할 수 없습니다' 로 차단됨.
3589	    --  키 목록 = SAVE MERGE ON 절 + FE save.ts keyOf. 이미 있으면 넣지 않는다(멱등).
3590	    --  ⚠ V_GROUPBY 에도 넣어야 한다 — SELECT 목록에만 넣으면 ORA-00979.
3591	    FOR K IN (SELECT REGEXP_SUBSTR('DIVISION_ID,PROJECT_CODE,END_CUSTOMER,SCM_SITE_ID,ITEM_ID,DOM_EXP,FCST_TYPE,INCOTERMS,MEASURE,PLANT_SITE_ID,CELL_PROD_SITE_ID,CURRENCY', '[^,]+', 1, LEVEL) AS C
3592	                FROM DUAL
3593	             CONNECT BY REGEXP_SUBSTR('DIVISION_ID,PROJECT_CODE,END_CUSTOMER,SCM_SITE_ID,ITEM_ID,DOM_EXP,FCST_TYPE,INCOTERMS,MEASURE,PLANT_SITE_ID,CELL_PROD_SITE_ID,CURRENCY', '[^,]+', 1, LEVEL) IS NOT NULL) LOOP
3594	        IF INSTR(',' || REPLACE(V_COLUMN, ' ', '') || ',', ',' || K.C || ',') = 0 THEN
3595	            V_COLUMN  := V_COLUMN  || ', ' || K.C;
3596	            V_GROUPBY := V_GROUPBY || ', ' || K.C;
3597	        END IF;
3598	    END LOOP;
3599	
3600	    V_ORDERBY := REPLACE(V_ORDERBY, 'MEASURE_GROUP', ' CAST(SORT_ORDER_NO AS NUMBER(10,2))');
3601	    V_ORDERBY := REPLACE(V_ORDERBY, ', MEASURE', '');
3602	
3603	    --[E20260901-MEASORDER] 메저 표시 순서 = MEASURE MODAL 선택 순서 보장 (사용자 지시 2026-09-01).
3604	    --   결함: 위 두 REPLACE 는 사용자 컬럼목록(titleset 'C')에 MEASURE_GROUP 이 있을 때만 정렬키가 남는다.
3605	    --   실측(사내·AS-IS 양쪽) C 목록에 MEASURE_GROUP 부재 → 치환 무발생 + ', MEASURE' 제거만 실행되어
3606	    --   ORDER BY 에서 **메저 정렬 기준이 소실**(표시 순서 비결정 = 사용자 신고 '순서 혼합').
3607	    --   정정: 정렬키 부재 시 titleset 'M' 순번(SORT_ORDER_NO — 모달 선택 순서 그대로 저장됨)을 말미에 추가.
3608	    --   SORT_ORDER_NO 는 PIVOT 소스 SELECT 에 실재(L4062/4073)하므로 PIVOT 이후 ORDER BY 사용 가능.
3609	    IF INSTR(UPPER(NVL(V_ORDERBY, ' ')), 'SORT_ORDER_NO') = 0 THEN
3610	        IF V_ORDERBY IS NULL OR LENGTH(TRIM(V_ORDERBY)) = 0 THEN
3611	            V_ORDERBY := ' ORDER BY CAST(SORT_ORDER_NO AS NUMBER(10,2))';
3612	        ELSE
3613	            V_ORDERBY := V_ORDERBY || ', CAST(SORT_ORDER_NO AS NUMBER(10,2))';
3614	        END IF;
3615	    END IF;
3616

3619	    -- ====================================================================================================

3625	    BEGIN
3626	        SELECT COUNT(1) INTO V_ADMIN_CNT
3627	        FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL
3628	        WHERE  LOOKUP_TYPE = 'GET_ADMIN_GROUP'
3629	        AND    VALID_YN    = 'Y'
3630	        AND (SEGMENT1 = p_user_group  --[AUTHUNION 2026-08-04] 보유그룹 합집합 판정 (사용자 확정)
3631	             OR SEGMENT1 IN (SELECT G.USER_GR_ID FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_USER_GR_MPG_M G
3632	                             WHERE G.USER_ID IN (p_user_group, p_created_by) AND G.USE_YN = 'Y'))
3633	        AND    SEGMENT2    = 'ALL';
3634
