3641	    -- TEMP_INTO_GROUP_MEASURE / TEMP_ADMIN_GROUP_MEASURE — 메저별 수정권한
3642	    -- [E20260819-LIMITMEAS] 사용자 승인(2026-08-19): SCM_FCST↔MKT_FCST 동시편집 제약 —
3643	    --   CURRENT(P_DMF0082_GET_MAIN_DATA_CURRENT) 적재부 완전 이식본으로 교체·통일:
3644	    --   ①요일 토큰 전개+당일 요일 필터 ②시간창(ATTRIBUTE2/3) ③LIMIT_MEASURE(ATTRIBUTE4) 강제 'N'.
3645	    --   INTO 스테이징 = CURRENT GTT(GTT_TEMP_INTO_GROUP_MEASURE, 7컬럼 실측) 재사용·사용 후 정리(donor 동일).
3646	    --   구 MAIN 2컬럼 GTT(GT_DMF0082_TEMP_INTO_GROUP_MEASURE) 적재는 소비처가 본 블록 EXISTS 1곳뿐(실측)이라 대체.
3647	    --   O_RC4 계약(MEASURE, EDIT_AUTH_YN, TODAY_LIMIT_YN, TODAY_DAY_LIST)·후속 EDIT_FLAG 판정은 원형 유지 —
3648	    --   EDIT_AUTH_YN 원천만 요일/시간창 반영 INTO 로 교체 + LIMIT 강제 'N' UPDATE 추가.
3649	    -- ====================================================================================================
3650	
3651	    DELETE FROM SCM_MGR.GTT_TEMP_INTO_GROUP_MEASURE;
3652	
3653	    --1). GET_ADMIN_GROUP_MEASURE 룩업에서 데이터를 TEMP로 담는다
3654	    --    (CROSS APPLY STRING_SPLIT × 2 → CROSS JOIN LATERAL REGEXP_SUBSTR CONNECT BY)
3655	    --    (C-11) DATEPART(WEEKDAY)/CONVERT(...,24) → TO_CHAR(SYSDATE,'DY','NLS_DATE_LANGUAGE=ENGLISH') / TO_CHAR(SYSDATE,'HH24MI')
3656	    --    (C-7)  ISNULL(REPLACE(x,' ',''),'')='' 빈값 판정 → REPLACE(x,' ','') IS NULL (빈값 = 제한 없음 = 행 유지)
3657	    INSERT INTO SCM_MGR.GTT_TEMP_INTO_GROUP_MEASURE
3658	    ( USER_GROUP, DIVISION_ID, MEASURE, DAYS, FROM_TIME, TO_TIME, LIMIT_MEASURE )
3659	    SELECT B.USER_GROUP, B.DIVISION_ID, B.MEASURE, B.DAYS, B.FROM_TIME, B.TO_TIME, LM.TOKEN AS LIMIT_MEASURE
3660	    FROM  (SELECT A.SEGMENT1 AS USER_GROUP, A.SEGMENT3 AS DIVISION_ID, A.SEGMENT4 AS MEASURE, DT.TOKEN AS DAYS
3661	                , A.ATTRIBUTE2 AS FROM_TIME, A.ATTRIBUTE3 AS TO_TIME, A.ATTRIBUTE4 AS LIMIT_MEASURE
3662	           FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL A
3663	           CROSS JOIN LATERAL (SELECT REGEXP_SUBSTR(A.ATTRIBUTE1, '[^,]+', 1, LEVEL) AS TOKEN
3664	                               FROM   DUAL
3665	                               CONNECT BY LEVEL <= NVL(REGEXP_COUNT(A.ATTRIBUTE1, ','), 0) + 1) DT
3666	           WHERE  A.LOOKUP_TYPE = 'GET_ADMIN_GROUP_MEASURE'
3667	           AND    A.VALID_YN = 'Y'
3668	           AND (A.SEGMENT1 = p_user_group  --[AUTHUNION 2026-08-04] 보유그룹 합집합 판정 (사용자 확정)
3669	             OR A.SEGMENT1 IN (SELECT G.USER_GR_ID FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_USER_GR_MPG_M G
3670	                             WHERE G.USER_ID IN (p_user_group, p_created_by) AND G.USE_YN = 'Y'))
3671	           AND    A.SEGMENT2 = VA_PROGRAM_ID
3672	           AND    A.SEGMENT3 = p_division_id
3673	          ) B
3674	    CROSS JOIN LATERAL (SELECT REGEXP_SUBSTR(B.LIMIT_MEASURE, '[^,]+', 1, LEVEL) AS TOKEN
3675	                        FROM   DUAL
3676	                        CONNECT BY LEVEL <= NVL(REGEXP_COUNT(B.LIMIT_MEASURE, ','), 0) + 1) LM
3677	    WHERE (REPLACE(B.DAYS, ' ', '') IS NULL
3678	           OR B.DAYS = TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'DY', 'NLS_DATE_LANGUAGE=ENGLISH'))   --일~토 = SUN~SAT
3679	    AND   (REPLACE(B.FROM_TIME, ' ', '') IS NULL
3680	           OR B.FROM_TIME <= TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'HH24MI'))
3681	    AND   (REPLACE(B.TO_TIME, ' ', '') IS NULL
3682	           OR B.TO_TIME >= TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'HH24MI'));
3683	
3684	    -- ====================================================================================================
3685	    -- TEMP_ADMIN_GROUP_MEASURE — 메저별 당일 수정권한 ([E20260819-LIMITMEAS] EDIT_AUTH_YN 원천 = 요일/시간창 반영 INTO)
3686	    -- ====================================================================================================
3687	
3688	    DELETE FROM SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_TEMP_ADMIN_GROUP_MEASURE;
3689	
3690	    INSERT INTO SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_TEMP_ADMIN_GROUP_MEASURE (MEASURE, EDIT_AUTH_YN, TODAY_LIMIT_YN, TODAY_DAY_LIST)
3691	    SELECT A.SEGMENT4 AS MEASURE
3692	         , CASE WHEN EXISTS (
3693	                    SELECT 1
3694	                    FROM   SCM_MGR.GTT_TEMP_INTO_GROUP_MEASURE B
3695	                    WHERE  B.MEASURE = A.SEGMENT4
3696	               ) THEN 'Y'
3697	                --[E20260826-AUTHVIEW] 시간창 因 잠금 구분값 'T' — 보유그룹의 시간창 정의 행(A1 요일)이 있으나
3698	                --  지금이 창 밖이라 INTO 에서 탈락한 경우. FE 는 'Y' 비교라 'T'=잠금 유지, MSG00477 표기에만 사용.
3699	                --  (그룹 미보유 因 잠금 'N' 과 구분 — DM Viewer 등에 시간 메시지 오표기 방지)
3700	                WHEN EXISTS (
3701	                    SELECT 1
3702	                    FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL T
3703	                    WHERE  T.LOOKUP_TYPE = 'GET_ADMIN_GROUP_MEASURE'
3704	                    AND    T.VALID_YN = 'Y'
3705	                    AND   (T.SEGMENT1 = p_user_group
3706	                           OR T.SEGMENT1 IN (SELECT G.USER_GR_ID FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_USER_GR_MPG_M G
3707	                                             WHERE G.USER_ID IN (p_user_group, p_created_by) AND G.USE_YN = 'Y'))
3708	                    AND    T.SEGMENT2 = VA_PROGRAM_ID
3709	                    AND    T.SEGMENT3 = p_division_id
3710	                    AND    T.SEGMENT4 = A.SEGMENT4
3711	                    AND    REPLACE(T.ATTRIBUTE1, ' ', '') IS NOT NULL
3712	               ) THEN 'T'
3713	                ELSE 'N' END AS EDIT_AUTH_YN
3714	         , CASE WHEN A.ATTRIBUTE1 IS NOT NULL THEN 'Y' ELSE 'N' END AS TODAY_LIMIT_YN
3715	         , A.ATTRIBUTE1 AS TODAY_DAY_LIST
3716	    FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL A
3717	    WHERE  A.LOOKUP_TYPE = 'GET_ADMIN_GROUP_MEASURE'
3718	    AND    A.VALID_YN    = 'Y'
3719	    AND    A.SEGMENT2    = VA_PROGRAM_ID
3720	    GROUP BY A.SEGMENT4, A.ATTRIBUTE1;
3721	
3722	    /* [E20260820-MEASRULE] measure 별 입력 게이트 명세 반영 (2026-08-20 PI 테스트 시트 — 사용자 확인 요청):
3723	       - LOC        : 항상 입력 가능 (룩업 그룹 통제 제외 — "Unit Price(LOC) 입력 가능해야 함")
3724	       - AS_In_Req  : 81 계열('81%')·82 + DM Planner (W/S) 소속일 때만 (AUTHUNION 동형 판정)
3725	       - VMI_In_Req : 40·82 + 권한 무관. 행 단위 "Sales Site VMI_YN='Y'" 조건은 행별 후보 콤보
3726	                      FcstPriceEditMeasure + DM_UI_EDITABLE_MEASURE VMI_COND 가 담당(실측 기구현) — 메저 게이트만 개방.
3727	       - MKT_FCST/SCM_FCST : 종전대로 GET_ADMIN_GROUP_MEASURE 룩업 통제(요일/시간창/LIMIT — E20260819-LIMITMEAS).
3728	       신규 division(81_JV 등)은 패턴으로 자동 커버. 근거 실측(2026-08-20): 81_JV 는 룩업·SITE·SITE_ITEM 전부 0행
3729	       (division 프로비저닝 미완) + 순수 W/S 계정은 40 룩업 행 부재로 전 메저 'N' — LIMITMEAS 회귀 아님(구 코드 동일 필터). */
3730	    /* [E20260825-SCPPLOC] SCP Planner = LOC 편집 불가 정정 (사용자 지적 2026-08-25).
3731	       AS-IS 파리티: DMF0082_FcstNPrice_Mgmt.cs L9172 은 bEDIT_AUTH_LOC 을 GET_ADMIN_GROUP_MEASURE
3732	       룽업 결과(Tables[3] MEASURE='LOC' 의 EDIT_FLAG) 그대로 읽고, L9229 case "LOC" 에서
3733	       권한이 없으면 언락하지 않아 셀이 잠긴다. 룽업상 SCP Planner 행은 SCM_FCST(MON 1500~1830) 1건뿐이라
3734	       AS-IS 에서 SCP Planner 는 LOC 를 못 고친다. 위 2026-08-20 measure 게이트 명세의 무조건 개방이 그 통제를
3735	       벗겨 SCP Planner 까지 편집 가능해졌다.
3736	       ⚠ 개방을 통째로 걷어내면 룽업 미프로비저닝 division(81/81_JV/82 = 룽업 0행)까지 잠겨
3737	       원장 REQ:1ST-41-1 / REQ:1ST-63-3 (81 에서 LOC 입력) 이 깨진다 → 두 조건을 모두 만족할 때만 제외한다:
3738	         (1) 해당 division 에 GET_ADMIN_GROUP_MEASURE 룽업이 존재(현재 40) 그리고
3739	         (2) 세션 보유그룹에 'SCP Planner' 포함(AUTHUNION 동형 판정 — 타 그룹 겸유 시는 룽업이 이미 'Y' 부여).
3740	       다른 그룹·다른 메저·다른 division 은 문장 불변(회귀 0). */
3741	    UPDATE SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_TEMP_ADMIN_GROUP_MEASURE
3742	    SET    EDIT_AUTH_YN = 'Y'
3743	    WHERE  MEASURE = 'LOC'
3744	    AND  ( NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1
3745	                       FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL L
3746	                       WHERE  L.LOOKUP_TYPE = 'GET_ADMIN_GROUP_MEASURE'
3747	                       AND    L.VALID_YN    = 'Y'
3748	                       AND    L.SEGMENT2    = VA_PROGRAM_ID
3749	                       AND    L.SEGMENT3    = p_division_id)   -- 룽업 미프로비저닝 division = 종전 개방 유지
3750	           --[E20260825-81AUTH] 81 계열은 룩업 전권 — 강제 개방 분기에서 제외 (LOC = 룩업 4그룹 통제, W/S 제외).
3751	           --  40 은 현행 유지(비 SCP 강제 개방 — REQ:1ST-36-1 계보).
3752	           OR ( p_division_id NOT LIKE '81%'
3753	                AND NOT ( p_user_group = 'SCP Planner'
3754	                    OR EXISTS (SELECT 1
3755	                               FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_USER_GR_MPG_M G
3756	                               WHERE  G.USER_ID    IN (p_user_group, p_created_by)
3757	                               AND    G.USER_GR_ID = 'SCP Planner'
3758	                               AND    G.USE_YN     = 'Y') )    -- SCP Planner 미보유 = 종전 개방 유지
3759	                --[E20260826-AUTHVIEW] DM Viewer = 편집 전면 불가 (사용자 지시 2026-08-26) — 강제 개방에서 제외.
3760	                --  AS-IS 파리티: 40 룩업 LOC 4그룹(ADMIN/DM ADMIN/DM Planner/SCM PI Admin)에 Viewer 없음 = bEDIT_AUTH_LOC false.
3761	                --  겸유자(Viewer+편집그룹)는 룩업 INTO 경유 기본 'Y' 라 회귀 없음.
3762	                AND NOT ( p_user_group = 'DM Viewer'
3763	                    OR EXISTS (SELECT 1
3764	                               FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_USER_GR_MPG_M G
3765	                               WHERE  G.USER_ID    IN (p_user_group, p_created_by)
3766	                               AND    G.USER_GR_ID = 'DM Viewer'
3767	                               AND    G.USE_YN     = 'Y') ) )
3768	         );
3769	
3770	    UPDATE SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_TEMP_ADMIN_GROUP_MEASURE
3771	    SET    EDIT_AUTH_YN = CASE WHEN (p_division_id LIKE '81%' OR p_division_id = '82')
3772	                                AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_USER_GR_MPG_M G
3773	                                            WHERE G.USER_ID IN (p_user_group, p_created_by)
3774	                                            AND   G.USER_GR_ID = 'DM Planner (W/S)' AND G.USE_YN = 'Y')
3775	                               THEN 'Y' ELSE 'N' END
3776	    WHERE  MEASURE = 'AS_In_Req';
3777	
3778	    UPDATE SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_TEMP_ADMIN_GROUP_MEASURE
3779	    SET    EDIT_AUTH_YN = CASE WHEN p_division_id IN ('40', '82')
3780	                                --[E20260826-AUTHVIEW] DM Viewer 단독(편집그룹 룩업 행 INTO 부재) = VMI 차단 (사용자 지시 2026-08-26).
3781	                                --  겸유자(Viewer+편집그룹)는 INTO 에 그 그룹의 VMI 행이 있어 개방 유지 — 회귀 없음.
3782	                                --[E20260826-SCPVMI] SCP Planner 도 제외 (사용자 지시 2026-08-26 "시간 밖 = 수정·INSERT/DELETE 전면 불가").
3783	                                --  AS-IS 파리티: 40 VMI 룩업 4그룹(ADMIN/DM ADMIN/DM Planner/SCM PI Admin)에 SCP 없음 = bEDIT_AUTH_VMI false.
3784	                                --  겸유자(편집그룹 겸유)는 INTO 에 그 그룹 VMI 행이 있어 개방 유지 — 회귀 없음.
3785	                                AND NOT ( ( p_user_group IN ('DM Viewer', 'SCP Planner')
3786	                                            OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_USER_GR_MPG_M G
3787	                                                       WHERE G.USER_ID IN (p_user_group, p_created_by)
3788	                                                       AND   G.USER_GR_ID IN ('DM Viewer', 'SCP Planner') AND G.USE_YN = 'Y') )
3789	                                          AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM SCM_MGR.GTT_TEMP_INTO_GROUP_MEASURE B
3790	                                                          WHERE B.MEASURE = 'VMI_In_Req') )
3791	                               THEN 'Y' ELSE 'N' END
3792	    WHERE  MEASURE = 'VMI_In_Req';
3793	
3794	    --3). [E20260819-LIMITMEAS] LIMIT_MEASURE 존재 시 해당 메저 강제 'N' (donor 이식 — EDIT_FLAG→EDIT_AUTH_YN 컬럼명만 상이)
3795	    /* [E20260907-LIMITMEAS-DIV] LIMIT_MEASURE 잠금 판정 = **division 범위** (사용자 지시 2026-09-07).
3796	       요건 원문 = 본 블록 말미 [E20250729-000433] "월요일 SCM FCST 입력 시 MKT FCST 수정 불가".
3797	       ⛔ 구 판정의 결함: 잠금 근거를 GTT_TEMP_INTO_GROUP_MEASURE(= **내 보유그룹 행만**)에서 찾았다.
3798	          40 의 SCM_FCST(MON 1000~1900, A4=MKT_FCST) 행은 ADMIN/SCM PI Admin/SCP Planner 3그룹에만
3799	          있어서, 그 그룹이 아닌 DM Planner 로 로그인하면 INTO 에 LIMIT_MEASURE 행이 0건 →
3800	          V_LIMIT_CNT=0 → 강제 'N' 이 아예 발동하지 않고 MKT_FCST 가 열린 채 남았다
3801	          (실증 2026-09-07 MON 16:35 · eunah.koo(DM Planner)/40 · INTO 3행 전부 A4 NULL).
3802	       ⭐ 잠금의 주체는 '내 그룹'이 아니라 '이 division 의 SCM_FCST 입력 시간창'이므로
3803	          SEGMENT1(그룹) 조건을 빼고 (SEGMENT2, SEGMENT3) 범위로 판정한다. 요일·시간창 판정식은
3804	          위 INTO 적재분과 **동일**(빈값 = 제한 없음)이라 그룹 축만 넓힌 변경이다.
3805	       ⚠ 81 계열의 ATTRIBUTE4 는 '메저명'이 아니라 잠금 유발 **그룹명**('SCP Planner')이다
3806	          ([E20260825-81AUTH], 아래 블록 담당) → 토큰이 같은 division 의 SEGMENT1(그룹명)과
3807	          일치하면 이 해석에서 제외한다. 두 해석은 상호배타.
3808	       실측(2026-09-07): DMF0082 의 A4 보유행은 전 division 이 SCM_FCST→'MKT_FCST' 21행뿐이고
3809	          그룹명 토큰은 0건이라, 이 정정의 실효 = "SCM_FCST 시간창 동안 MKT_FCST 전원 잠금". */
3810	    UPDATE SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_TEMP_ADMIN_GROUP_MEASURE A
3811	    SET    A.EDIT_AUTH_YN = 'N'
3812	    WHERE  EXISTS (SELECT 1
3813	                   FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL L
3814	                   CROSS JOIN LATERAL (SELECT REGEXP_SUBSTR(L.ATTRIBUTE4, '[^,]+', 1, LEVEL) AS TOKEN
3815	                                       FROM   DUAL
3816	                                       CONNECT BY LEVEL <= NVL(REGEXP_COUNT(L.ATTRIBUTE4, ','), 0) + 1) LM
3817	                   WHERE  L.LOOKUP_TYPE = 'GET_ADMIN_GROUP_MEASURE'
3818	                   AND    L.VALID_YN    = 'Y'
3819	                   AND    L.SEGMENT2    = VA_PROGRAM_ID
3820	                   AND    L.SEGMENT3    = p_division_id
3821	                   AND    REPLACE(L.ATTRIBUTE4, ' ', '') IS NOT NULL
3822	                   AND   (REPLACE(L.ATTRIBUTE1, ' ', '') IS NULL          -- 요일 미지정 = 상시
3823	                          OR INSTR(',' || REPLACE(L.ATTRIBUTE1, ' ', '') || ',',
3824	                                   ',' || TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'DY', 'NLS_DATE_LANGUAGE=ENGLISH') || ',') > 0)
3825	                   AND   (REPLACE(L.ATTRIBUTE2, ' ', '') IS NULL
3826	                          OR L.ATTRIBUTE2 <= TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'HH24MI'))
3827	                   AND   (REPLACE(L.ATTRIBUTE3, ' ', '') IS NULL
3828	                          OR L.ATTRIBUTE3 >= TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'HH24MI'))
3829	                   AND    TRIM(LM.TOKEN) = A.MEASURE
3830	                   AND    NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1                            -- 토큰=그룹명 → 81AUTH 해석
3831	                                      FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL L3
3832	                                      WHERE  L3.LOOKUP_TYPE = 'GET_ADMIN_GROUP_MEASURE'
3833	                                      AND    L3.VALID_YN    = 'Y'
3834	                                      AND    L3.SEGMENT2    = VA_PROGRAM_ID
3835	                                      AND    L3.SEGMENT3    = p_division_id
3836	                                      AND    L3.SEGMENT1    = TRIM(LM.TOKEN)));
3837	
3838	
3839	    /* [E20260825-81AUTH] LIMIT_AUTH(그룹 잠금) 해석 — AS-IS DMF0003 판 의미 이식 (사용자 확정 2026-08-25).
3840	       81 계열 룩업 행의 ATTRIBUTE4 는 "메저명"이 아니라 **잠금 유발 권한그룹명**('SCP Planner')이다:
3841	       그 그룹의 같은 (S2,S3,S4) 행이 지금 요일·시간창 안이면 내 메저는 편집 불가.
3842	       (원형 = PKG_DMF0003 AUTHGATE L10270~10296. 40 의 LIMIT_MEASURE(메저 잠금)과 공존 —
3843	        A4 값이 그룹 행(SEGMENT1)과 매칭될 때만 발동. 40 의 'MKT_FCST' 토큰은 그룹 미매칭 = 영향 0.)
3844	       합집합(AUTHUNION) 보존 — 보유그룹 행 중 잠기지 않은 행이 하나라도 있으면 Y 유지.
3845	       ⚠ 한계: A4 가 콤마 다중 그룹이면 토큰별 개별 판정(현 데이터 = 단일 'SCP Planner' 뿐). */
3846	    UPDATE SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_TEMP_ADMIN_GROUP_MEASURE A
3847	    SET    A.EDIT_AUTH_YN = 'N'
3848	    WHERE  A.EDIT_AUTH_YN = 'Y'
3849	    AND    EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM SCM_MGR.GTT_TEMP_INTO_GROUP_MEASURE B WHERE B.MEASURE = A.MEASURE)
3850	    AND    NOT EXISTS (
3851	             SELECT 1
3852	             FROM   SCM_MGR.GTT_TEMP_INTO_GROUP_MEASURE B
3853	             WHERE  B.MEASURE = A.MEASURE
3854	             AND    NOT EXISTS (
3855	                      SELECT 1
3856	                      FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL L2
3857	                      WHERE  L2.LOOKUP_TYPE = 'GET_ADMIN_GROUP_MEASURE'
3858	                      AND    L2.VALID_YN    = 'Y'
3859	                      AND    L2.SEGMENT2    = VA_PROGRAM_ID
3860	                      AND    L2.SEGMENT3    = p_division_id
3861	                      AND    L2.SEGMENT4    = B.MEASURE
3862	                      AND    L2.SEGMENT1    = B.LIMIT_MEASURE   -- LIMIT 토큰 = 그룹명일 때만 발동
3863	                      AND    REPLACE(L2.ATTRIBUTE1, ' ', '') IS NOT NULL  -- 시간창 정의 행만
3864	                      AND    INSTR(',' || REPLACE(L2.ATTRIBUTE1, ' ', '') || ',',
3865	                                   ',' || TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'DY', 'NLS_DATE_LANGUAGE=ENGLISH') || ',') > 0
3866	                      AND    L2.ATTRIBUTE2 <= TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'HH24MI')
3867	                      AND    L2.ATTRIBUTE3 >= TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'HH24MI')));
3868	
3869	    DELETE FROM SCM_MGR.GTT_TEMP_INTO_GROUP_MEASURE;

3874	    -- CAL_MONTH_END_DATE / CAL_WEEK_END_DATE
3875	    -- ====================================================================================================
3876	
3877	    DELETE FROM SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_CAL_MONTH_END_DATE;
3878	    DELETE FROM SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_CAL_WEEK_END_DATE;
3879	
3880	    INSERT INTO SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_CAL_MONTH_END_DATE (BASIS_YYYYMM, MONTH_END_DATE, M3_CUTOFF_DATE)
3881	    SELECT A.BASIS_YYYYMM
3882	         , MAX(A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD) AS MONTH_END_DATE
3883	         , TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(TO_DATE(MIN(A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD),'YYYYMMDD'), 3), 'YYYYMMDD') AS M3_CUTOFF_DATE
3884	    FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M A
3885	    WHERE  A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD BETWEEN p_from_yyyymmdd AND p_to_yyyymmdd
3886	    GROUP BY A.BASIS_YYYYMM;
3887	
3888	    INSERT INTO SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_CAL_WEEK_END_DATE (ISO_WEEK, BASIS_YYYYMM, WEEK_END_DATE, M3_CUTOFF_DATE)
3889	    SELECT A.ISO_WEEK
3890	         , A.BASIS_YYYYMM
3891	         , MAX(A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD) AS WEEK_END_DATE
3892	         , TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(TO_DATE(MIN(A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD),'YYYYMMDD'), 3), 'YYYYMMDD') AS M3_CUTOFF_DATE
3893	    FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M A
3894	    WHERE  A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD BETWEEN p_from_yyyymmdd AND p_to_yyyymmdd
3895	    GROUP BY A.ISO_WEEK, A.BASIS_YYYYMM;

3922	    , NEW_FLAG, AS_YN, VMI_STD_DIO)  --[E20260930-DMF0082QRYPERF-VMI82FOLD] VMI_STD_DIO INSERT 흡수 / --[E20260812-PERF] 전행 UPDATE 2회(redo/log 대기 93샘플) INSERT 시점 흡수
3923	    SELECT F.DIVISION_ID, F.PROJECT_CODE, F.END_CUSTOMER, F.SCM_SITE_ID, F.ITEM_ID
3924	         , F.DOM_EXP, F.FCST_TYPE, F.INCOTERMS, F.MEASURE, F.PLANT_SITE_ID
3925	         , F.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID

3935	         , MAX(F.PROJECT_STATUS), MAX(F.ITEM_NAME), MAX(F.VMI_YN)
3936	         , MAX(F.CUST_GROUP), MAX(F.CUST_GROUP_DTL), MAX(F.APPLICATION)

3947	         /* EDIT_FLAG: 수정권한 — MA.EDIT_AUTH_YN 기반 */
3948	         , CASE WHEN p_division_id = '82' THEN 'N'  --[E20260930-DMF0082QRYPERF-VMI82FOLD] 구 후행 UPDATE EDIT_FLAG='N'(82 조회전용) 흡수
3949	                WHEN V_GROUP_ID = 'ADMIN' THEN 'Y'
3950	                WHEN EXISTS (
3951	                    SELECT 1 FROM SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_TEMP_ADMIN_GROUP_MEASURE MA
3952	                    WHERE  MA.MEASURE = F.MEASURE
3953	                    AND    MA.EDIT_AUTH_YN = 'Y'
3954	                    AND   (MA.TODAY_LIMIT_YN = 'N'
3955	                           OR (MA.TODAY_LIMIT_YN = 'Y'
3956	                               AND INSTR(
3957	                                       REPLACE(TRIM(MA.TODAY_DAY_LIST),' ',''),
3958	                                       CASE TO_NUMBER(TO_CHAR(SYSDATE,'D'))
3959	                                           WHEN 1 THEN 'SUN' WHEN 2 THEN 'MON'
3960	                                           WHEN 3 THEN 'TUE' WHEN 4 THEN 'WED'
3961	                                           WHEN 5 THEN 'THU' WHEN 6 THEN 'FRI'
3962	                                           WHEN 7 THEN 'SAT'
3963	                                       END) > 0))
3964	                ) THEN 'Y'
3965	                ELSE 'N'
3966	           END AS EDIT_FLAG
3967	         /* SORT_KEY — [FIX 2026-07-22] AS-IS L5865 파리티: 상수 999.
3968	            구 변환(MEASURE_GROUP||SORT_ORDER_NO)은 ORDER BY 선두 SORT_KEY 를 measure 별 상이값으로

3970	         , 999 AS SORT_KEY
3971	         /* GROUP_ID from user profile (DM_MST_USER or SCM_MST_USER) */
3972	         , NULL AS GROUP_ID
3973	         /* BUCKET PIVOT 컬럼: SUN~SAT */
3974	         , PVT.SUN, PVT.MON, PVT.TUE, PVT.WED, PVT.THU, PVT.FRI, PVT.SAT
3975	         /* [FIX 2026-07-22] W/M 버킷 집계 분기용 파생 — AS-IS #MF_SP L5812~5846 파리티 */
3976	         , CAL.YYYYMM, CAL.YYYYMMWW, CAL.WEEK_END_YN, CAL.MONTH_END_YN
3977	         /* [E20260812-PERF] 구 후행 UPDATE 흡수 — NEW_FLAG='N' 고정(원 주석 정책 동일) /
3978	            AS_YN = p_scm_site_id 기반 비상관 판정(원 DECLARE 블록 등가 — MAX 는 GROUP BY 문법용) */
3979	         , MAX('N') AS NEW_FLAG
3980	         , MAX(CASE WHEN p_scm_site_id IS NULL OR p_scm_site_id = 'ALL' THEN 'N'
3981	                    WHEN EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM (SELECT TOKEN FROM SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_PARAM_TOKEN WHERE PARAM_NM = 'p_scm_site_id') SA
3982	                                 JOIN SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL SB
3983	                                   ON SA.TOKEN = SB.SEGMENT1 AND SB.LOOKUP_TYPE = 'AS_SITE_PLANT')
3984	                    THEN 'Y' ELSE 'N' END) AS AS_YN
3985	         , MAX(VD.VMI_STD_DIO) AS VMI_STD_DIO  --[E20260930-DMF0082QRYPERF-VMI82FOLD] 82 외 = NULL(조인 미성립)
3986	    FROM   SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_TEMP_FCST_N_PRICE F
3987	    INNER JOIN (
3988	        /* Daily bucket → date key */
3989	        SELECT D.BASIS_YYYYMMDD
3990	             , D.BASIS_YYYYMM || D.ISO_WEEK AS WEEK_KEY
3991	             , CASE WHEN D.BASIS_YYYYMM || D.ISO_WEEK = WE.ISO_WEEK || WE.BASIS_YYYYMM
3992	                    THEN WE.WEEK_END_DATE ELSE D.BASIS_YYYYMMDD END AS BUCKET_TYPE_KEY
3993	             , TO_CHAR(D.BASIS_YYYYMMDD) AS BUCKET_KEY
3994	             , 'D' AS BUCKET_TYPE
3995	             /* [FIX 2026-07-22] W/M 분기 파생 — AS-IS L5812(YYYYMM/YYYYMMWW)·L5845~5846(월말/주말 플래그) 파리티.
3996	                YYYYMMWW 는 C_BUCKET 컬럼키와 동일 포맷(LPAD 2) 의무 — 불일치 시 W_ 셀 전량 공백 */
3997	             , D.BASIS_YYYYMM AS YYYYMM
3998	             , D.BASIS_YYYYMM || LPAD(TRIM(D.ISO_WEEK), 2, '0') AS YYYYMMWW
3999	             , CASE WHEN WE2.WEEK_END_DATE  IS NOT NULL THEN 'Y' ELSE 'N' END AS WEEK_END_YN
4000	             , CASE WHEN ME.MONTH_END_DATE IS NOT NULL THEN 'Y' ELSE 'N' END AS MONTH_END_YN
4001	        FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M D
4002	        LEFT JOIN SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_CAL_WEEK_END_DATE WE
4003	            ON  WE.ISO_WEEK     = D.ISO_WEEK
4004	            AND WE.BASIS_YYYYMM = D.BASIS_YYYYMM
4005	        /* AS-IS #CAL_WEEK_END_DATE: 조회범위 내 (월,ISO주) 별 마지막 일자 */
4006	        LEFT JOIN (SELECT MAX(BASIS_YYYYMMDD) AS WEEK_END_DATE
4007	                   FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M
4008	                   WHERE  BASIS_YYYYMMDD BETWEEN p_from_yyyymmdd AND p_to_yyyymmdd
4009	                   GROUP BY BASIS_YYYYMM, ISO_WEEK) WE2
4010	            ON  WE2.WEEK_END_DATE = D.BASIS_YYYYMMDD
4011	        /* AS-IS #CAL_MONTH_END_DATE: 조회범위 내 월별 마지막 일자 */
4012	        LEFT JOIN (SELECT MAX(BASIS_YYYYMMDD) AS MONTH_END_DATE
4013	                   FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M
4014	                   WHERE  BASIS_YYYYMMDD BETWEEN p_from_yyyymmdd AND p_to_yyyymmdd
4015	                   GROUP BY BASIS_YYYYMM) ME
4016	            ON  ME.MONTH_END_DATE = D.BASIS_YYYYMMDD
4017	        WHERE  D.BASIS_YYYYMMDD BETWEEN p_from_yyyymmdd AND V_EXP_YYYYMMDD
4018	    ) CAL ON CAL.BASIS_YYYYMMDD = F.YYYYMMDD
4019	    /* BUCKET_PER PIVOT: DM_MST_BUCKET_CONVERSION (DIVISION+SCM_SITE+INCOTERMS 기준)
4020	       [E20260812-PERF] LATERAL(행별 PIVOT 재실행 — ASH direct path temp 156샘플 병목) → 조합별 선계산 후
4021	       일반 LEFT JOIN. PIVOT MAX ≡ MAX(CASE …) — 요일 행 부재 시 NULL 동일(등가) */
4022	    LEFT JOIN (
4023	        SELECT BC.DIVISION_ID, BC.SCM_SITE_ID, BC.INCOTERMS
4024	             , MAX(CASE WHEN BC.YYYYMMDD = 'SUN' THEN CASE WHEN BC.BUCKET_PER = 'Y' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END END) AS SUN
4025	             , MAX(CASE WHEN BC.YYYYMMDD = 'MON' THEN CASE WHEN BC.BUCKET_PER = 'Y' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END END) AS MON
4026	             , MAX(CASE WHEN BC.YYYYMMDD = 'TUE' THEN CASE WHEN BC.BUCKET_PER = 'Y' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END END) AS TUE
4027	             , MAX(CASE WHEN BC.YYYYMMDD = 'WED' THEN CASE WHEN BC.BUCKET_PER = 'Y' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END END) AS WED
4028	             , MAX(CASE WHEN BC.YYYYMMDD = 'THU' THEN CASE WHEN BC.BUCKET_PER = 'Y' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END END) AS THU
4029	             , MAX(CASE WHEN BC.YYYYMMDD = 'FRI' THEN CASE WHEN BC.BUCKET_PER = 'Y' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END END) AS FRI
4030	             , MAX(CASE WHEN BC.YYYYMMDD = 'SAT' THEN CASE WHEN BC.BUCKET_PER = 'Y' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END END) AS SAT
4031	        FROM   SCM_MGR.DM_MST_BUCKET_CONVERSION BC
4032	        GROUP BY BC.DIVISION_ID, BC.SCM_SITE_ID, BC.INCOTERMS
4033	    ) PVT ON  PVT.DIVISION_ID = F.DIVISION_ID
4034	          AND PVT.SCM_SITE_ID = F.SCM_SITE_ID
4035	          AND PVT.INCOTERMS   = F.INCOTERMS
4036	    LEFT JOIN ( --[E20260930-DMF0082QRYPERF-VMI82FOLD] 구 L4155 상관 스칼라 서브쿼리 → SCM_SITE_ID 별 선집계 (1회)
4037	        SELECT S.SCM_SITE_ID, MAX(S.VMI_YN || '^' || NVL(TO_CHAR(S.ATTRIBUTE5),'')) AS VMI_STD_DIO
4038	        FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_SITE S
4039	        WHERE  S.DIVISION_ID = '82'
4040	        GROUP BY S.SCM_SITE_ID
4041	    ) VD ON  VD.SCM_SITE_ID = F.SCM_SITE_ID
4042	         AND p_division_id  = '82'
4043	    GROUP BY F.DIVISION_ID, F.PROJECT_CODE, F.END_CUSTOMER, F.SCM_SITE_ID, F.ITEM_ID
4044	           , F.DOM_EXP, F.FCST_TYPE, F.INCOTERMS, F.MEASURE, F.PLANT_SITE_ID
4045	           , F.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, CAL.BUCKET_TYPE, CAL.BUCKET_KEY, F.MEASURE_GROUP
4046	           , F.SORT_ORDER_NO
4047	           , PVT.SUN, PVT.MON, PVT.TUE, PVT.WED, PVT.THU, PVT.FRI, PVT.SAT
4048	           , CAL.YYYYMM, CAL.YYYYMMWW, CAL.WEEK_END_YN, CAL.MONTH_END_YN;

4054	    -- ====================================================================================================
4055	    -- [UICATEGORY 2026-08-07] Tier3 파리티 컬럼 적재 (DMF0003 MF_BASE 파생 이식 — 값 정책은 work report 참조)
4056	    --   NEW_FLAG    : DMF0003 파생(수요/판가 FULL OUTER JOIN 신규 판정)은 본 SP 스테이징 구조에 부재 → 'N' 고정 (한계 명시)
4057	    --   AS_YN       : DMF0003 LP_AS_YN 파리티 — 조회 Sales Site 가 AS_SITE_PLANT 룩업 포함 시 전행 'Y'

4059	    --   VMI_STD_DIO : 82=SCM_MST_SITE(VMI_YN^ATTRIBUTE5) — 40 은 DMF0003 원문 조인키 사문(KEY=ITEM↔조인=SITE)으로 미적재

4065	    --[E20260812-PERF] AS_YN 전행 UPDATE 블록 제거 — INSERT 시점 비상관 CASE 로 흡수(등가)
4066	    NULL;
4067	
4068	    IF p_division_id = '40' THEN
4069	        -- ATTR1 (40) — DMF0003 GT_DMF0003_T_SSP_SYNC 적재 로직 이식 (DM_PLN_MKT_FCST MKT_FCST 일자 P/A 마커 31 PIVOT)
4070	        MERGE INTO SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_MF_SP T
4071	        USING (
4072	            SELECT DIVISION_ID, PROJECT_CODE, SCM_SITE_ID, ITEM_ID, DOM_EXP, FCST_TYPE, INCOTERMS
4073	                 , NVL(A1,'')  || '^' || NVL(A2,'')  || '^' || NVL(A3,'')  || '^' || NVL(A4,'')  || '^' || NVL(A5,'')
4074	                || '^' || NVL(A6,'')  || '^' || NVL(A7,'')  || '^' || NVL(A8,'')  || '^' || NVL(A9,'')  || '^' || NVL(A10,'')
4075	                || '^' || NVL(A11,'') || '^' || NVL(A12,'') || '^' || NVL(A13,'') || '^' || NVL(A14,'') || '^' || NVL(A15,'')
4076	                || '^' || NVL(A16,'') || '^' || NVL(A17,'') || '^' || NVL(A18,'') || '^' || NVL(A19,'') || '^' || NVL(A20,'')
4077	                || '^' || NVL(A21,'') || '^' || NVL(A22,'') || '^' || NVL(A23,'') || '^' || NVL(A24,'') || '^' || NVL(A25,'')
4078	                || '^' || NVL(A26,'') || '^' || NVL(A27,'') || '^' || NVL(A28,'') || '^' || NVL(A29,'') || '^' || NVL(A30,'')
4079	                || '^' || NVL(A31,'') AS ATTR1
4080	            FROM (
4081	                SELECT DIVISION_ID, PROJECT_CODE, SCM_SITE_ID, ITEM_ID, DOM_EXP, A.FCST_TYPE, INCOTERMS
4082	                     , YYYYMMDD || '/'
4083	                       || CASE
4084	                              WHEN REGEXP_LIKE(NVL(ATTRIBUTE3,'0'),'^[0-9.]+$')
4085	                               AND A.QTY >
4086	                                   CASE WHEN REGEXP_LIKE(NVL(ATTRIBUTE3,'0'),'^[0-9.]+$')
4087	                                        THEN TO_NUMBER(ATTRIBUTE3) ELSE 0 END
4088	                                 + CASE WHEN REGEXP_LIKE(NVL(ATTRIBUTE2,'0'),'^[0-9.]+$')
4089	                                        THEN TO_NUMBER(ATTRIBUTE2) ELSE 0 END
4090	                              THEN 'P'
4091	                              ELSE 'A'
4092	                          END AS SYNC_YN
4093	                     , 'A' || TO_CHAR(
4094	                           ROW_NUMBER() OVER (
4095	                               PARTITION BY DIVISION_ID, PROJECT_CODE, SCM_SITE_ID, ITEM_ID,
4096	                                            DOM_EXP, A.FCST_TYPE, INCOTERMS
4097	                               ORDER BY YYYYMMDD
4098	                           )) AS ROW_NUM
4099	                FROM   SCM_MGR.DM_PLN_MKT_FCST A
4100	                WHERE  A.DIVISION_ID = p_division_id
4101	                  AND  A.MEASURE     = 'MKT_FCST'
4102	                  AND  A.YYYYMMDD   BETWEEN V_FROM_YYYYMMDD AND V_TO_YYYYMMDD
4103	                  AND  ( A.ATTRIBUTE4 IS NOT NULL OR A.ATTRIBUTE5 IS NOT NULL )
4104	                  AND  A.QTY <> 0
4105	            ) A
4106	            PIVOT (
4107	                MAX(SYNC_YN) FOR ROW_NUM IN (
4108	                    'A1'  A1,  'A2'  A2,  'A3'  A3,  'A4'  A4,  'A5'  A5,
4109	                    'A6'  A6,  'A7'  A7,  'A8'  A8,  'A9'  A9,  'A10' A10,
4110	                    'A11' A11, 'A12' A12, 'A13' A13, 'A14' A14, 'A15' A15,
4111	                    'A16' A16, 'A17' A17, 'A18' A18, 'A19' A19, 'A20' A20,
4112	                    'A21' A21, 'A22' A22, 'A23' A23, 'A24' A24, 'A25' A25,
4113	                    'A26' A26, 'A27' A27, 'A28' A28, 'A29' A29, 'A30' A30,
4114	                    'A31' A31
4115	                )
4116	            )
4117	        ) S
4118	        ON (    T.DIVISION_ID  = S.DIVISION_ID
4119	            AND T.PROJECT_CODE = S.PROJECT_CODE
4120	            AND T.SCM_SITE_ID  = S.SCM_SITE_ID
4121	            AND T.ITEM_ID      = S.ITEM_ID
4122	            AND T.DOM_EXP      = S.DOM_EXP
4123	            AND T.FCST_TYPE    = S.FCST_TYPE
4124	            AND T.INCOTERMS    = S.INCOTERMS)
4125	        WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET T.ATTR1 = S.ATTR1;
4126	
4127	    ELSIF p_division_id = '82' THEN
4128	        -- ATTR1 (82) — DMF0003 GT_DMF0003_T_BNP 적재 로직 이식 (DM_PLN_PR_DETAIL 일자 A 마커 31 PIVOT)
4129	        MERGE INTO SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_MF_SP T
4130	        USING (
4131	            SELECT DIVISION_ID, PROJECT_CODE, SCM_SITE_ID, ITEM_ID, DOM_EXP, FCST_TYPE, INCOTERMS
4132	                 , NVL(A1,'')  || '^' || NVL(A2,'')  || '^' || NVL(A3,'')  || '^' || NVL(A4,'')  || '^' || NVL(A5,'')
4133	                || '^' || NVL(A6,'')  || '^' || NVL(A7,'')  || '^' || NVL(A8,'')  || '^' || NVL(A9,'')  || '^' || NVL(A10,'')
4134	                || '^' || NVL(A11,'') || '^' || NVL(A12,'') || '^' || NVL(A13,'') || '^' || NVL(A14,'') || '^' || NVL(A15,'')
4135	                || '^' || NVL(A16,'') || '^' || NVL(A17,'') || '^' || NVL(A18,'') || '^' || NVL(A19,'') || '^' || NVL(A20,'')
4136	                || '^' || NVL(A21,'') || '^' || NVL(A22,'') || '^' || NVL(A23,'') || '^' || NVL(A24,'') || '^' || NVL(A25,'')
4137	                || '^' || NVL(A26,'') || '^' || NVL(A27,'') || '^' || NVL(A28,'') || '^' || NVL(A29,'') || '^' || NVL(A30,'')
4138	                || '^' || NVL(A31,'') AS ATTR1
4139	            FROM (
4140	                SELECT DISTINCT DIVISION_ID, PROJECT_CODE, SCM_SITE_ID, ITEM_ID,
4141	                                DOM_EXP, FCST_TYPE, INCOTERMS
4142	                     , YYYYMMDD || '/A' AS SYNC_YN
4143	                     , 'A' || TO_CHAR(
4144	                           ROW_NUMBER() OVER (
4145	                               PARTITION BY DIVISION_ID, PROJECT_CODE, SCM_SITE_ID, ITEM_ID,
4146	                                            DOM_EXP, FCST_TYPE, INCOTERMS
4147	                               ORDER BY YYYYMMDD
4148	                           )) AS ROW_NUM
4149	                FROM   SCM_MGR.DM_PLN_PR_DETAIL A
4150	                WHERE  A.DIVISION_ID = p_division_id
4151	                  AND  A.YYYYMMDD   BETWEEN V_FROM_YYYYMMDD AND V_TO_YYYYMMDD
4152	            ) A
4153	            PIVOT (
4154	                MAX(SYNC_YN) FOR ROW_NUM IN (
4155	                    'A1'  A1,  'A2'  A2,  'A3'  A3,  'A4'  A4,  'A5'  A5,
4156	                    'A6'  A6,  'A7'  A7,  'A8'  A8,  'A9'  A9,  'A10' A10,
4157	                    'A11' A11, 'A12' A12, 'A13' A13, 'A14' A14, 'A15' A15,
4158	                    'A16' A16, 'A17' A17, 'A18' A18, 'A19' A19, 'A20' A20,
4159	                    'A21' A21, 'A22' A22, 'A23' A23, 'A24' A24, 'A25' A25,
4160	                    'A26' A26, 'A27' A27, 'A28' A28, 'A29' A29, 'A30' A30,
4161	                    'A31' A31
4162	                )
4163	            )
4164	        ) S
4165	        ON (    T.DIVISION_ID  = S.DIVISION_ID
4166	            AND T.PROJECT_CODE = S.PROJECT_CODE
4167	            AND T.SCM_SITE_ID  = S.SCM_SITE_ID
4168	            AND T.ITEM_ID      = S.ITEM_ID
4169	            AND T.DOM_EXP      = S.DOM_EXP
4170	            AND T.FCST_TYPE    = S.FCST_TYPE
4171	            AND T.INCOTERMS    = S.INCOTERMS)
4172	        WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET T.ATTR1 = S.ATTR1;
4173	
4174	        -- VMI_STD_DIO (82) — DMF0003 GT_DMF0003_VMI_DIO 82 분기 이식 (SCM_MST_SITE VMI_YN^ATTRIBUTE5, SCM_SITE_ID 키)
4175	        NULL;  --[E20260930-DMF0082QRYPERF-VMI82FOLD] VMI_STD_DIO 후행 UPDATE → MF_SP INSERT 흡수
4176	    END IF;
4177	
4178	    -- [UICATEGORY 2026-08-07] Division 82 = 조회 전용 (사용자 확정) — 편집 서버 권위 차단
4179	    IF p_division_id = '82' THEN
4180	        NULL;  --[E20260930-DMF0082QRYPERF-VMI82FOLD] EDIT_FLAG='N' 전행 UPDATE → MF_SP INSERT CASE 흡수
4181	    END IF;

4183	    /* [E20260825-AFSBLANK] AS_FROM_SITE NULL -> ' ' 정규화 (사용자 지시 2026-08-25).
4184	       증상: 81 조회에서 AS_FROM_SITE 컬럼을 켜면 같은 행이 실적/계획 2행으로 분리된다.
4185	       원인: 최종 커서가 동적 GROUP BY 로 표시 컬럼 전체를 묶는데(OPEN O_RC1 FOR 동적 SQL),
4186	             계획 arm 과 실적 arm 의 빈값 표현이 달라(공백 ' ' vs NULL) 같은 표시키가 서로 다른 그룹이 된다.
4187	             실적 원장은 PR_DM_PLN_MKT_FCST_SALES_RESULT_MAPPING 이 ATTRIBUTE10 을 적재하지 않아 항상 NULL
4188	             (INSERT 컬럼 목록에 ATTRIBUTE10 부재 — 실측).
4189	       주의: 실적 arm 에만 NVL 을 걸면 '계획 NULL vs 실적 공백' 이 새로 갈라진다(사내 실측 81 양쪽 NULL 4,801키).
4190	             그래서 두 arm 이 합류한 스테이징에서 한 번에 정규화한다 — 표현만 통일, 실제 사이트값은 불변.
4191	       한계: 값이 실제로 다른 경우(계획 'WA00_81-W' vs 실적 NULL)는 NVL 대상이 아니다 — 별도 판단 필요. */
4192	    UPDATE SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_MF_SP
4193	    SET    AS_FROM_SITE = ' '
4194	    WHERE  AS_FROM_SITE IS NULL;

4196	    /* [E20260825-CURRNORM] 수량 메저 행의 CURRENCY 표시키 통일 (사용자 신고 2026-08-25).
4197	       증상: CUST_FCST 계열 실적 행의 CURRENCY 가 '-'(센티널)·KRW/USD(실값)인데 계획 arm 은 ''(NULL)
4198	       → 같은 논리행이 CURRENCY 차이로 실적/계획 2행 분리 (AS_FROM_SITE 건과 동일 가족).
4199	       원인: 계획 원장(DM_PLN_MKT_FCST/_C)에는 CURRENCY 컴럼 자체가 없고(실측 ORA-904),
4200	       실적 원장(DM_PLN_SALES_PLAN_RESULT/_C)만 실컴럼 보유('-' 12,278행 포함 실측).
4201	       정정: 판가 메저(CURRENCY 가 실차원인 행)만 제외하고 수량 메저는 전행 ' ' 로 통일. */
4202	    UPDATE SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_MF_SP
4203	    SET    CURRENCY = ' '
4204	    WHERE  MEASURE NOT IN ('LOC', 'KRW', 'USD', 'MKT_AMT_LOC', 'MKT_AMT_KRW')
4205	    --[E20260901-CURRKEEP] 사용자 신고 2026-09-01: 수량 메저에도 '자신의 단가 CURRENCY' 표기 (AS-IS 동등).
4206	    --   구 조건 `CURRENCY IS NULL OR CURRENCY <> ' '` 은 **전 수량행을 무조건 공백으로 덮어써** 앞선 백필
4207	    --   MERGE(LOC 기준 8키, AS-IS L2284 동등)로 채운 실통화를 소실시켰다(GTT 양성테스트 실측).
4208	    --   정정: 실값 보존, NULL/공백/센티널('-')만 ' ' 통일 → CURRNORM 본래 목적(행 분리 방지) 유지.
4209	    --   VMI 계열은 AS-IS(IIF(MEASURE LIKE 'VMI%','',CURRENCY) · 백필 제외)와 동일하게 항상 공백.
4210	    AND    (   MEASURE LIKE 'VMI%'
4211	            OR CURRENCY IS NULL OR TRIM(CURRENCY) IS NULL OR TRIM(CURRENCY) = '-');

4215	    -- (SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M 에 요청 날짜 범위의 데이터가 없으면 V_PV_LIST 공백 → ORA-00936)
4216	    -- ====================================================================================================
4217	    IF DBMS_LOB.GETLENGTH(V_PV_LIST) = 0 THEN
4218	        BEGIN DBMS_LOB.FREETEMPORARY(V_PV_LIST);     EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN NULL; END;
4219	        BEGIN DBMS_LOB.FREETEMPORARY(V_PV_COL_LIST); EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN NULL; END;
4220	        BEGIN DBMS_LOB.FREETEMPORARY(V_QTY_SUM);     EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN NULL; END;
4221	        OPEN O_RC1 FOR SELECT NULL AS COL1 FROM DUAL WHERE 1 = 0;
4222	        OPEN O_RC2 FOR SELECT NULL AS COL1 FROM DUAL WHERE 1 = 0;
4223	        OPEN O_RC3 FOR SELECT NULL AS COL1 FROM DUAL WHERE 1 = 0;
4224	        OPEN O_RC4 FOR SELECT NULL AS COL1 FROM DUAL WHERE 1 = 0;
4225	        OPEN O_RC5 FOR SELECT NULL AS COL1 FROM DUAL WHERE 1 = 0;
4226	        OPEN O_RESULT_CURSOR FOR SELECT '1' AS RESULT_CODE, 'NO_BUCKET_DATA' AS MESSAGE, '0' AS EVENT FROM DUAL;
4227	        RETURN;
4228	    END IF;

4237	    -- [E20260918-KEYCOLGRAIN] 숨긴 저장키 컬럼이 행을 가르지 않게 대표값 통일
4238	    --
4239	    --   최종 커서는 PIVOT 이라 V_COLUMN 에 남은 모든 컬럼이 행 그레인이 된다.
4240	    --     AS-IS : @V_COLUMN = C_TITLE + NOTATION           → 숨긴 컬럼은 그레인 아님
4241	    --     TO-BE : + [E20260822-KEYCOL] 저장 키 12컬럼 강제 추가
4242	    --   그래서 Column Setting 에서 숨긴 컬럼이어도 TO-BE 만 행이 갈린다(AS-IS 부재 회귀).
4243	    --   실측(ZIOUI1 2026-09-18, Cell Prod Site 를 숨긴 계정 / DIV 40):
4244	    --     KRW·LOC·MKT_FCST·SCM_FCST 각 12 업무키, RTF_SYS_RP 11 업무키가 CELL_PROD_SITE_ID 로 2줄.
4245	    --   반대로 그 컬럼을 **표시**하는 계정은 AS-IS 도 2줄이므로 건드리면 안 된다
4246	    --     (클라우드 실측: 40/Huapu_CN_CGU/MCSS22531-B3 — NJ01-A 61행 + HQ06-A 1행(20260531) = AS-IS 파리티).
4247	    --
4248	    --   따라서 **C_TITLE 에 없을 때만** 업무키 단위 대표값으로 통일한다.
4249	    --   대표값 = 계획 구간 우선·최신 (편집·저장 대상이 계획 구간이므로 저장 키로도 이 값이 맞다).
4250	    -- ====================================================================================================
4251	    SELECT COUNT(*)
4252	      INTO V_USER_COL_CNT
4253	      FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_TITLESET_USER U
4254	     WHERE U.PROGRAM_ID = VA_PROGRAM_ID
4255	       AND U.SHEET_NAME = VA_SHEET_NAME
4256	       AND U.USER_ID    = p_created_by
4257	       AND U.USER_INQUIRY_COLUMN_TYPE_CODE = 'C'
4258	       AND U.COLUMN_NAME = 'CELL_PROD_SITE_ID';
4259	
4260	    IF V_USER_COL_CNT = 0 THEN   -- 화면에서 숨긴 컬럼일 때만 (표시 중이면 AS-IS 파리티 유지)
4261	        MERGE INTO SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_MF_SP T
4262	        USING (
4263	                   SELECT NVL(DIVISION_ID, '~') AS DIVISION_ID
4264	                        , NVL(PROJECT_CODE, '~') AS PROJECT_CODE
4265	                        , NVL(END_CUSTOMER, '~') AS END_CUSTOMER
4266	                        , NVL(SCM_SITE_ID, '~') AS SCM_SITE_ID
4267	                        , NVL(ITEM_ID, '~') AS ITEM_ID
4268	                        , NVL(DOM_EXP, '~') AS DOM_EXP
4269	                        , NVL(FCST_TYPE, '~') AS FCST_TYPE
4270	                        , NVL(INCOTERMS, '~') AS INCOTERMS
4271	                        , NVL(PLANT_SITE_ID, '~') AS PLANT_SITE_ID
4272	                        , NVL(MEASURE, '~') AS MEASURE
4273	                        , MAX(CELL_PROD_SITE_ID) KEEP (DENSE_RANK LAST
4274	                              ORDER BY CASE WHEN BUCKET_KEY >= V_TO_RESULT THEN 1 ELSE 0 END
4275	                                     , BUCKET_KEY) AS REP_CELL_PROD
4276	                   FROM   SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_MF_SP
4277	                   WHERE  CELL_PROD_SITE_ID IS NOT NULL
4278	                   GROUP BY NVL(DIVISION_ID, '~')
4279	                        , NVL(PROJECT_CODE, '~')
4280	                        , NVL(END_CUSTOMER, '~')
4281	                        , NVL(SCM_SITE_ID, '~')
4282	                        , NVL(ITEM_ID, '~')
4283	                        , NVL(DOM_EXP, '~')
4284	                        , NVL(FCST_TYPE, '~')
4285	                        , NVL(INCOTERMS, '~')
4286	                        , NVL(PLANT_SITE_ID, '~')
4287	                        , NVL(MEASURE, '~')
4288	                   HAVING COUNT(DISTINCT CELL_PROD_SITE_ID) > 1
4289	               ) S
4290	        ON ( NVL(T.DIVISION_ID, '~') = S.DIVISION_ID
4291	         AND NVL(T.PROJECT_CODE, '~') = S.PROJECT_CODE
4292	         AND NVL(T.END_CUSTOMER, '~') = S.END_CUSTOMER
4293	         AND NVL(T.SCM_SITE_ID, '~') = S.SCM_SITE_ID
4294	         AND NVL(T.ITEM_ID, '~') = S.ITEM_ID
4295	         AND NVL(T.DOM_EXP, '~') = S.DOM_EXP
4296	         AND NVL(T.FCST_TYPE, '~') = S.FCST_TYPE
4297	         AND NVL(T.INCOTERMS, '~') = S.INCOTERMS
4298	         AND NVL(T.PLANT_SITE_ID, '~') = S.PLANT_SITE_ID
4299	         AND NVL(T.MEASURE, '~') = S.MEASURE )
4300	        WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET T.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID = S.REP_CELL_PROD
4301	        WHERE  NVL(T.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, '~') <> S.REP_CELL_PROD;
4302	    END IF;

4309	    --

4320	     WHERE U.PROGRAM_ID = VA_PROGRAM_ID
4321	       AND U.SHEET_NAME = VA_SHEET_NAME

4325

4327	        MERGE INTO SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_MF_SP T
4328	        USING (
4329	                   SELECT NVL(DIVISION_ID, '~') AS DIVISION_ID
4330	                        , NVL(PROJECT_CODE, '~') AS PROJECT_CODE
4331	                        , NVL(END_CUSTOMER, '~') AS END_CUSTOMER
4332	                        , NVL(SCM_SITE_ID, '~') AS SCM_SITE_ID
4333	                        , NVL(ITEM_ID, '~') AS ITEM_ID
4334	                        , NVL(DOM_EXP, '~') AS DOM_EXP
4335	                        , NVL(FCST_TYPE, '~') AS FCST_TYPE
4336	                        , NVL(INCOTERMS, '~') AS INCOTERMS

4345	                        , NVL(PROJECT_CODE, '~')
4346	                        , NVL(END_CUSTOMER, '~')
4347	                        , NVL(SCM_SITE_ID, '~')
4348	                        , NVL(ITEM_ID, '~')
4349	                        , NVL(DOM_EXP, '~')
4350	                        , NVL(FCST_TYPE, '~')
4351	                        , NVL(INCOTERMS, '~')
4352	                        , NVL(CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, '~')
4353	                        , NVL(MEASURE, '~')
4354	                   HAVING COUNT(DISTINCT NVL(PLANT_SITE_ID, '~')) >= 1
4355	               ) S
4356	        ON ( NVL(T.DIVISION_ID, '~') = S.DIVISION_ID
4357	         AND NVL(T.PROJECT_CODE, '~') = S.PROJECT_CODE
4358	         AND NVL(T.END_CUSTOMER, '~') = S.END_CUSTOMER
4359	         AND NVL(T.SCM_SITE_ID, '~') = S.SCM_SITE_ID
4360	         AND NVL(T.ITEM_ID, '~') = S.ITEM_ID
4361	         AND NVL(T.DOM_EXP, '~') = S.DOM_EXP
4362	         AND NVL(T.FCST_TYPE, '~') = S.FCST_TYPE
4363	         AND NVL(T.INCOTERMS, '~') = S.INCOTERMS
4364	         AND NVL(T.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, '~') = S.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID

4371	    -- [FIX 2026-07-22] 최종 동적 SQL — AS-IS(L5965~6085) 5분기 파리티 복원
4372	    --   구 변환분: MF_SP 단일 소스 + PIVOT_BUCKET CASE(D/W/M) 구조였으나 MF_SP 적재가 전행

4374	    --   ① D_ 행단위  ② W_ SUM(BALANCE/VMI_Inv/AS_Inv=주말일만, 그 외 합계)  ③ W_ AVG(KRW/USD/LOC)
4375	    --   ④ M_ SUM(월말일 규칙 동일)  ⑤ M_ AVG(KRW/USD/LOC)  — QTY<>0 예외(SCM_FCST/FCST_GAP) AS-IS 동일.
4376	    --   placeholder REPLACE 방식 = DMF0082_GET_MAIN_DATA_CURRENT(정상 변환 선례) 동형.
4377	    --   (QTY 합계 보조컬럼은 기존 변환 유지 — FE 미사용 확인 2026-07-22)
4378	    -- ====================================================================================================
4379	    V_SQL := '
4380	    SELECT [@V_COLUMN]
4381	    , ([@V_QTY_SUM]) AS QTY
4382	    , [@V_PV_COL_LIST]
4383	    FROM (
4384	        SELECT [@V_COLUMN], SORT_ORDER_NO, ''D_'' || BUCKET_KEY AS PIVOT_BUCKET, QTY
4385	        FROM ( [@SRC0] ) F
4386	        WHERE ((MEASURE NOT IN (''SCM_FCST'',''FCST_GAP'') AND QTY <> 0) OR MEASURE IN (''SCM_FCST'',''FCST_GAP''))
4387	
4388	        UNION ALL
4389	
4390	        SELECT [@V_COLUMN], SORT_ORDER_NO, ''W_'' || YYYYMMWW AS PIVOT_BUCKET
4391	             , CASE WHEN MEASURE IN (''BALANCE'',''VMI_Inv'',''AS_Inv'')
4392	                    THEN CASE WHEN WEEK_END_YN = ''Y'' THEN SUM(QTY) ELSE 0 END
4393	                    ELSE SUM(QTY) END AS QTY
4394	        FROM ( [@SRC0] ) F
4395	        WHERE MEASURE NOT IN (''KRW'',''USD'',''LOC'')
4396	        AND ((MEASURE NOT IN (''SCM_FCST'',''FCST_GAP'') AND QTY <> 0) OR MEASURE IN (''SCM_FCST'',''FCST_GAP''))
4397	        [@V_GROUPBY], NOTATION, YYYYMMWW, WEEK_END_YN, MEASURE
4398	
4399	        UNION ALL
4400	
4401	        SELECT [@V_COLUMN], SORT_ORDER_NO, ''W_'' || YYYYMMWW AS PIVOT_BUCKET, AVG(QTY) AS QTY
4402	        FROM ( [@SRC0] ) F
4403	        WHERE MEASURE IN (''KRW'',''USD'',''LOC'')
4404	        AND ((MEASURE NOT IN (''SCM_FCST'',''FCST_GAP'') AND QTY <> 0) OR MEASURE IN (''SCM_FCST'',''FCST_GAP''))
4405	        [@V_GROUPBY], NOTATION, YYYYMMWW
4406	
4407	        UNION ALL
4408	
4409	        SELECT [@V_COLUMN], SORT_ORDER_NO, ''M_'' || YYYYMM AS PIVOT_BUCKET
4410	             , CASE WHEN MEASURE IN (''BALANCE'',''VMI_Inv'',''AS_Inv'')
4411	                    THEN CASE WHEN MONTH_END_YN = ''Y'' THEN SUM(QTY) ELSE 0 END
4412	                    ELSE SUM(QTY) END AS QTY
4413	        FROM ( [@SRC0] ) F
4414	        WHERE MEASURE NOT IN (''KRW'',''USD'',''LOC'')
4415	        AND ((MEASURE NOT IN (''SCM_FCST'',''FCST_GAP'') AND QTY <> 0) OR MEASURE IN (''SCM_FCST'',''FCST_GAP''))
4416	        [@V_GROUPBY], NOTATION, YYYYMM, MONTH_END_YN, MEASURE
4417	
4418	        UNION ALL
4419	
4420	        SELECT [@V_COLUMN], SORT_ORDER_NO, ''M_'' || YYYYMM AS PIVOT_BUCKET, AVG(QTY) AS QTY
4421	        FROM ( [@SRC0] ) F
4422	        WHERE MEASURE IN (''KRW'',''USD'',''LOC'')
4423	        AND ((MEASURE NOT IN (''SCM_FCST'',''FCST_GAP'') AND QTY <> 0) OR MEASURE IN (''SCM_FCST'',''FCST_GAP''))
4424	        [@V_GROUPBY], NOTATION, YYYYMM
4425	    ) SRC
4426	    PIVOT (SUM(QTY) FOR PIVOT_BUCKET IN ( [@V_PV_LIST] ))
4427	    [@V_ORDERBY]';
4428	
4429	    -- SRC0 = MF_SP alias 투영 (TEAM_ID/FORECAST_INFO_CELL_MODEL/LAST_UPDATE_DATE/BUCKET — 기존 wrapper 동일)
4430	    V_SQL := REPLACE(V_SQL, '[@SRC0]', 'SELECT MSP.*, MSP.DEPT_ID AS TEAM_ID, MSP.DEPT_NAME_KO AS TEAM_NAME_KO /*[UICATEGORY 2026-08-07]*/, MSP.CELL_MODEL AS FORECAST_INFO_CELL_MODEL, NVL(TO_CHAR(MSP.CREATE_DATE, ''YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS''), '' '') AS LAST_UPDATE_DATE, NVL(TO_CHAR(MSP.SUN),''0'')||NVL(TO_CHAR(MSP.MON),''0'')||NVL(TO_CHAR(MSP.TUE),''0'')||NVL(TO_CHAR(MSP.WED),''0'')||NVL(TO_CHAR(MSP.THU),''0'')||NVL(TO_CHAR(MSP.FRI),''0'')||NVL(TO_CHAR(MSP.SAT),''0'') AS BUCKET FROM SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_MF_SP MSP');
4431	    V_SQL := REPLACE(V_SQL, '[@V_QTY_SUM]',     V_QTY_SUM);
4432	    V_SQL := REPLACE(V_SQL, '[@V_PV_COL_LIST]', V_PV_COL_LIST);
4433	    V_SQL := REPLACE(V_SQL, '[@V_PV_LIST]',     V_PV_LIST);
4434	    V_SQL := REPLACE(V_SQL, '[@V_GROUPBY]',     V_GROUPBY);
4435	    V_SQL := REPLACE(V_SQL, '[@V_COLUMN]',      V_COLUMN);
4436	    V_SQL := REPLACE(V_SQL, '[@V_ORDERBY]',     V_ORDERBY);
4437	
4438	    OPEN O_RC1 FOR V_SQL;
4439	
4440	    DBMS_LOB.FREETEMPORARY(V_SQL);
4441	    DBMS_LOB.FREETEMPORARY(V_PV_LIST);
4442	    DBMS_LOB.FREETEMPORARY(V_PV_COL_LIST);
4443	    DBMS_LOB.FREETEMPORARY(V_QTY_SUM);
4444	
4445	    -- ====================================================================================================
4446	    -- Result Set 2: M+3 까지의 Monthly 캘린더 정보
4447	    -- ====================================================================================================
4448	
4449	    OPEN O_RC2 FOR
4450	        SELECT A.BASIS_YYYYMM
4451	             , MAX(A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD) AS MONTH_END_DATE
4452	             , TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(TRUNC(TO_DATE(MIN(A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD), 'YYYYMMDD'), 'MM'), 3), 'YYYYMMDD') AS M3_CUTOFF_DATE
4453	        FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M A
4454	        WHERE  A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD BETWEEN p_from_yyyymmdd AND p_to_yyyymmdd
4455	        GROUP BY A.BASIS_YYYYMM
4456	        ORDER BY A.BASIS_YYYYMM;
4457	
4458	    -- ====================================================================================================
4459	    -- Result Set 3: 현재 주차 / 오늘 시각 정보
4460	    -- ====================================================================================================
4461	
4462	    OPEN O_RC3 FOR
4463	        SELECT TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'YYYYMMDD') AS TODAY_DATE
4464	             , CASE TO_NUMBER(TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'D'))
4465	                   WHEN 1 THEN 'SUN' WHEN 2 THEN 'MON' WHEN 3 THEN 'TUE'
4466	                   WHEN 4 THEN 'WED' WHEN 5 THEN 'THU' WHEN 6 THEN 'FRI'
4467	                   WHEN 7 THEN 'SAT'
4468	               END AS TODAY_DAY
4469	             , SUBSTR(REPLACE(TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'HH24:MI:SS'), ':', ''), 1, 4) AS TODAY_TIME
4470	        FROM   DUAL;
4471	
4472	    -- ====================================================================================================
4473	    -- Result Set 4: 메저별 수정권한 (ADMIN GROUP MEASURE)
4474	    -- ====================================================================================================
4475	
4476	    OPEN O_RC4 FOR
4477	        SELECT MEASURE, EDIT_AUTH_YN, TODAY_LIMIT_YN, TODAY_DAY_LIST
4478	        FROM   SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_TEMP_ADMIN_GROUP_MEASURE
4479	        ORDER BY MEASURE;
4480	
4481	    -- ====================================================================================================
4482	    -- Result Set 5: 캘린더 주간 분포 (BUCKET CONVERSION)
4483	    -- ====================================================================================================
4484	
4485	    -- Result Set 5: 캘린더 주간 배분 정보 (M+4 월간 물량 배분용)
4486	    OPEN O_RC5 FOR
4487	        SELECT A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD
4488	             , A.BASIS_YYYYMM
4489	             , CASE WHEN SUBSTR(A.BASIS_START_DATE, -2) = '01' THEN 'Y' ELSE 'N' END AS FIRST_WEEK_FLAG
4490	             , CASE WHEN SUBSTR(TO_CHAR(TO_DATE(A.BASIS_START_DATE,'YYYYMMDD')+7,'YYYYMMDD'),1,6)
4491	                         = SUBSTR(A.BASIS_START_DATE,1,6)
4492	                    THEN 'N' ELSE 'Y' END AS END_MONTH_FLAG
4493	             , COUNT(*) OVER (PARTITION BY A.BASIS_YYYYMM) AS MONTH_DAY_CNT
4494	             , COUNT(*) OVER (PARTITION BY A.ISO_WEEK) AS WEEK_DAY_CNT
4495	             , A.ISO_WEEK
4496	        FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M A
4497	        WHERE  A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD BETWEEN p_from_yyyymmdd AND p_to_yyyymmdd
4498	        ORDER BY A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD;

4642	PROCEDURE P_DMF0082_POPUP_GET_FUTUREPRICE_INFO
4643	(
4644	    P_START_YYYYMMDD   IN  VARCHAR2,
4645	    P_END_YYYYMMDD     IN  VARCHAR2,
4646	    P_USER_ID          IN VARCHAR2, -- For EditYn
4647	    P_MNU_ID           IN VARCHAR2, -- For EditYn
4648	    O_RC1              OUT SYS_REFCURSOR,
4649	    O_RESULT_CURSOR    OUT SYS_REFCURSOR
4650	)
4651	AS
4652	/*
4653	 * Program ID   : DMF0020_GET_FUTUREPRICE_INFO
4654	 * Description  :
4655	 * Program History
4656	 *===================================================================================================
4657	 *   Date       In Charge      Description
4658	 *===================================================================================================
4659	 * 2025-02-25   dsiol     Initial Release [PJ2025-50017]LGES SCM DM 소형사업부 매출계획 동기화 개선
4660	 * 2026.03.24   jcnam        [PJ20264226] NextSCM Pilot 설계 및 개발
4661	 */
4662	    V_PROGRAM_NAME  VARCHAR2(200 CHAR);
4663	    V_PROC_INPUT    VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR);
4664	    V_BACKTRACE     VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR);
4665	    V_SQL_CODE      VARCHAR2(100 CHAR);
4666	    V_SQL_ERRMSG    VARCHAR2(1000 CHAR);
4667	    V_LOG_TYPE      VARCHAR2(100 CHAR);
4668	    V_LOG_MESSAGE   VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR);
4669	    V_DESCRIPTION   VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR);
4670	    N_LOG_COUNT     PLS_INTEGER := 0;
4671	    D_EVENT_DATE    DATE := SYSDATE;
4672	BEGIN
4673	    IF UTL_CALL_STACK.SUBPROGRAM(1).COUNT > 1 THEN
4674	        V_PROGRAM_NAME := $$PLSQL_UNIT || '.' || UTL_CALL_STACK.SUBPROGRAM(1)(2);
4675	    ELSE
4676	        V_PROGRAM_NAME := $$PLSQL_UNIT;
4677	    END IF;
4678	
4679	    OPEN O_RC1 FOR
4680	        SELECT YYYYMMDD,
4681	               SUBSTR(YYYYMMDD, 1, 6) AS YYYYMM,
4682	               t1.DIVISION_ID,
4683	               CURRENCY,
4684	               UNIT_CURRENCY,
4685	               EXCHANGE_PRICE,
4686	               NVL(T2.EDIT_YN,'N') AS EDIT_YN,
4687	               --[E20260915-EXLASTUPD] 환율 모달 그리드 Last Update Date / Last Update By 표시 (사용자 지시 2026-09-15).
4688	               --  문자열(YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS)로 반환 — FE 가 월 피벗 시 행별 최신값 판정(사전식 비교)에 그대로 사용.
4689	               TO_CHAR(t1.LAST_UPDATE_DATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS') AS LAST_UPDATE_DATE,
4690	               t1.LAST_UPDATED_BY
4691	          FROM SCM_MGR.DM_MST_EXCHANGE_RATE t1
4692	              LEFT JOIN (SELECT * -- EDIT_YN  /* 2026.08.15 AJS 신뢰도검사 발견2 : 권한 미등록 사용자 전량 소실 방지 (SELECT 절 NVL 과 정합) */
4693	                FROM SCM_ADM_USER_MNU_EDIT_AUTH_V
4694	                WHERE USER_ID = P_USER_ID
4695	                AND MNU_ID = P_MNU_ID
4696	                AND UNIT_TP_CD = 'D') T2
4697	                ON T1.DIVISION_ID = T2.UNIT_ID
4698	                     WHERE YYYYMMDD >= P_START_YYYYMMDD
4699	                       AND YYYYMMDD <= P_END_YYYYMMDD;
4700	
4701	    OPEN O_RESULT_CURSOR FOR
4702	        SELECT '0' AS CODE,
4703	               'SUCCESS' AS MSG,
4704	               CASE WHEN 1 = 2 THEN '-' ELSE NULL END AS EVENT
4705	          FROM DUAL;
4706	
4707	EXCEPTION
4708	    WHEN OTHERS THEN
4709	        ROLLBACK;
4710	
4711	        V_LOG_TYPE    := 'ERROR';
4712	        V_LOG_MESSAGE := 'EXCEPTION';
4713	        V_DESCRIPTION := '';
4714	        D_EVENT_DATE  := SYSDATE;
4715	        V_SQL_CODE    := TO_CHAR(SQLCODE);
4716	        V_SQL_ERRMSG  := SQLERRM;
4717	        V_PROC_INPUT  := JSON_OBJECT(
4718	                             'P_START_YYYYMMDD' VALUE P_START_YYYYMMDD,
4719	                             'P_END_YYYYMMDD'   VALUE P_END_YYYYMMDD,
4720	                             'P_USER_ID'        VALUE P_USER_ID,
4721	                             'P_MNU_ID'         VALUE P_MNU_ID
4722	                         );
4723	        V_BACKTRACE   := DBMS_UTILITY.FORMAT_ERROR_BACKTRACE;
4724	
4725	        BEGIN SCM_MGR.PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH(V_PROGRAM_NAME, V_LOG_TYPE, N_LOG_COUNT, V_LOG_MESSAGE, V_DESCRIPTION, D_EVENT_DATE, V_PROC_INPUT, V_SQL_CODE, V_SQL_ERRMSG, V_BACKTRACE); END;
4726	
4727	        RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(
4728	            -20001,
4729	            '[' || V_PROGRAM_NAME || '] SQLCODE=' || V_SQL_CODE ||
4730	            ' / SQLERRM=' || V_SQL_ERRMSG ||
4731	            ' / BACKTRACE=' || V_BACKTRACE ||
4732	            ' / PARAMS=' || V_PROC_INPUT
4733	        );

4746	 * Description  :
4747	 * Program History
4748	 *===================================================================================================
4749	 *   Date       In Charge      Description
4750	 *===================================================================================================
4751	 * 2025-02-25   dsiol     Initial Release [PJ2025-50017]LGES SCM DM 소형사업부 매출계획 동기화 개선

4755	    V_PROGRAM_NAME      VARCHAR2(200 CHAR);
4756	    V_LOG_TYPE          VARCHAR2(100 CHAR);
4757	    N_LOG_COUNT         PLS_INTEGER := 0;
4758	    V_LOG_MESSAGE       VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR);
4759	    V_DESCRIPTION       VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR);

4781	    , 99 AS DISPLAY_ALL

4792	             FROM   DUAL
4793	             CONNECT BY REGEXP_SUBSTR(T1.DIVISION_ID, '[^,]+', 1, LEVEL) IS NOT NULL

4802	    FROM SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL
4803	    WHERE VALID_YN = 'Y'

4807	    SELECT SEGMENT1 LOOKUP_CODE, SEGMENT2 DESCRIPTION, 99 AS DISPLAY_ALL,DISPLAY_SEQ
4808	    FROM SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL
4809	    WHERE VALID_YN = 'Y'
4810	    AND SEGMENT1 = 'LOC'
4811	    AND LOOKUP_TYPE = 'FUTUREMEASURE'
4812	    order by DISPLAY_SEQ
4813	    */
4814	
4815	    OPEN O_RESULT_CURSOR FOR
4816	    SELECT '0' AS CODE, 'SUCCESS' AS MSG, CASE WHEN 1 = 2 THEN '-' ELSE NULL END AS EVENT FROM DUAL;
4817	
4818	EXCEPTION
4819	    WHEN OTHERS THEN
4820	        ROLLBACK;
4821	
4822	        V_LOG_TYPE    := 'ERROR';
4823	        V_LOG_MESSAGE := 'EXCEPTION';
4824	        V_DESCRIPTION := '';
4825	        D_EVENT_DATE  := SYSDATE;
4826	        V_SQL_CODE    := TO_CHAR(SQLCODE);
4827	        V_SQL_ERRMSG  := SQLERRM;

4829	            'P_USER_ID'   VALUE P_USER_ID,
4830	            'P_MNU_ID'   VALUE P_MNU_ID
4831	        );

4962	PROCEDURE P_DMF0082_POPUP_GET_WEEKDATA
4963	(
4964	    P_YYYYMMDD          IN  VARCHAR2,
4965	    O_RC1               OUT SYS_REFCURSOR,
4966	    O_RESULT_CURSOR     OUT SYS_REFCURSOR
4967	)
4968	/*
4969	 * Program ID   : DMF0020_GET_WEEKDATA
4970	 * Description  :
4971	 * Program History
4972	 *===================================================================================================
4973	 *   Date       In Charge      Description
4974	 *===================================================================================================
4975	 * 2025-02-25   dsiol     Initial Release [PJ2025-50017]LGES SCM DM 소형사업부 매출계획 동기화 개선
4976	 * 2026.03.24   jcnam        [PJ20264226] NextSCM Pilot 설계 및 개발
4977	 */
4978	IS
4979	    -- LOG 용 변수
4980	    V_PROGRAM_NAME      VARCHAR2(200 CHAR);
4981	    V_LOG_TYPE          VARCHAR2(100 CHAR);
4982	    N_LOG_COUNT         PLS_INTEGER := 0;
4983	    V_LOG_MESSAGE       VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR);
4984	    V_DESCRIPTION       VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR);
4985	    D_EVENT_DATE        DATE := SYSDATE;
4986	
4987	    -- EXCEPTION 블록 필수 변수
4988	    V_PROC_INPUT        VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR);
4989	    V_BACKTRACE         VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR);
4990	    V_SQL_CODE          VARCHAR2(100 CHAR);
4991	    V_SQL_ERRMSG        VARCHAR2(1000 CHAR);
4992	BEGIN
4993	    -- 프로그램명 설정 (패키지 바디 이관 시 패키지명.프로시저명 자동 기록)
4994	    IF UTL_CALL_STACK.SUBPROGRAM(1).COUNT > 1 THEN
4995	        V_PROGRAM_NAME := $$PLSQL_UNIT || '.' || UTL_CALL_STACK.SUBPROGRAM(1)(2);
4996	    ELSE
4997	        V_PROGRAM_NAME := $$PLSQL_UNIT;
4998	    END IF;
4999	
5000	    OPEN O_RC1 FOR
5001	    SELECT BASIS_START_DATE, BASIS_YYYYMMDD
5002	    FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M
5003	    WHERE BASIS_YYYYMMDD <= P_YYYYMMDD
5004	    ORDER BY BASIS_YYYYMMDD DESC
5005	    FETCH FIRST 7 ROWS ONLY;
5006

5019	        V_SQL_ERRMSG  := SQLERRM;
5020	        V_PROC_INPUT  := JSON_OBJECT(
5021	            'P_YYYYMMDD' VALUE P_YYYYMMDD
5022	        );
5023	        V_BACKTRACE   := DBMS_UTILITY.FORMAT_ERROR_BACKTRACE;
5024	
5025	        BEGIN
5026	            SCM_MGR.PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH(
5027	                V_PROGRAM_NAME, V_LOG_TYPE, N_LOG_COUNT, V_LOG_MESSAGE,
5028	                V_DESCRIPTION, D_EVENT_DATE, V_PROC_INPUT,
5029	                V_SQL_CODE, V_SQL_ERRMSG, V_BACKTRACE
5030	            );

5042	PROCEDURE P_DMF0082_EXCEL_UPLOAD
5043	(
5044	    p_mode           IN  NUMBER
5045	  , p_created_by     IN  VARCHAR2
5046	  , p_program_id     IN  VARCHAR2 DEFAULT 'DMF0082'   --[5ARG 2026-08-12] 공통 validProc 계약(Excel.xml 5-arg)
5047	  , p_sheet_name     IN  VARCHAR2 DEFAULT 'FcstNPrice'
5048	  , O_RESULT_CURSOR  OUT SYS_REFCURSOR
5049	)
5050	/* ============================================================================================
5051	 * [E20260921-XLSFORMASIS] Excel Upload = **AS-IS DMF0020 파리티 전면 이식** (사용자 지시 2026-09-21)
5052	 *   원천 = PKG_DMF0020.P_DMF0020_EXCEL_UPLOAD (TO-BE 변환본, 사내 live 실측 2026-09-21 / 1,315줄)
5053	 *          본문을 원문 그대로 옮기고 **시그니처(5-arg)·프로시저명만** DMF0082 로 맞췄다.
5054	 *          AS-IS 원문에 DMF0020 리터럴은 주석 1곳뿐이고, PROGRAM_ID/SHEET_NAME 은 전 지점이
5055	 *          P_PROGRAM_ID / P_SHEET_NAME 파라미터 경유라 그대로 DMF0082/FcstNPrice 로 동작한다(실측).
5056	 *   슬롯 = ATTRIBUTE1 Div / 2 Team / 3 End Customer / 4 Project Code / 5 Currency / 6 Model
5057	 *          / 7~19 M0~M12  <- SCM_ADM_EXCEL_COLUMNS.HEADER_CONTENT 19토큰과 1:1 (02_META 동시 배포 의무)
5058	 *   적재 = SCM_MST_FUTURE_PRICE (MERGE) + SCM_MST_FUTURE_PRICE_H (INSERT), ATTRIBUTE8='EXCEL UPLOAD'
5059	 *   GTT  = GT_F020_* 6종 (사내 기존재 실측, SYS$SESSION · 저장분기 진입 시 DELETE 초기화)
5060	 *   구본 대비 = 구 DMF0082 업로드(ATTRIBUTE1~10 키 + 11~23 M, Sales Site/Incoterms/FCST Type/
5061	 *              Customs Handbook/Plant Site/Measure 축)는 이 회차로 폐기된다.
5062	 * ============================================================================================ */
5063	/*
5064	 * Program ID   : [DMF0020_EXCEL_UPLOAD]
5065	 * Description  : PR_DETAIL_AUTO 수정
5066	 * Program History
5067	 *===================================================================================================
5068	 *   Date       In Charge		Description
5069	 *===================================================================================================
5070	 * 2024.05.27   KJJ			최초 작성
5071	 * 2024.12.31	KJJ			[E20241217-000632]_Sales Price 화면 엑셀 업로드 시 중복데이터 입력 제한
5072	 * 2026.03.19   jcnam        [PJ20264226] NextSCM Pilot 설계 및 개발
5073	 * validation: 필수값, 모델중복, Currency 중복, Division 오류, TEAM 오류
5074	 *
5075	 */
5076	IS
5077	/*
5078	
5079	
5080	1.81 사업부만 대상임
5081	
5082	
5083	2. LOC 값이 없을 경우 0으로 간주하여 처리(skip 이 아님)
5084	=>기존 저장 SP(DMF0020_SAVE_SCM_MST_FUTURE_PRICE_ALL) 는 무조건 삭제 후 0 값이 아닌 것들(구분자 D 가 아닌것들)만 insert 하는 것으로 되어 있으나
5085	본 SP는 Merge into 구문으로 구현하여 0으로 처리
5086	
5087	
5088	=>2024.06.12 로직 변경
5089	0 으로 넣은 경우: Validation 에서 Invalid 처리
5090	빈값으로 넣은 경우: SKIP, 기존 Sales Price 데이터를 유지
5091

5095	=>1)동일모델 입력 방지 위해 Div / Team /	End Customer /	Project Code /	Model 로 중복체크
5096	=>2)위 체크조건 통과했을 경우 여러개 Currency 입력방지 위해 중복체크
5097

5100	4.ItemID가 입력되지 않은 것들과  ItemID가 입력된 건들을 나누어서 추출 후 병합(row순서는 고려하지 않음)
5101	=>
5102	81	자동차.마케팅.OTS.OTS2팀	Corvus Norway	A22CR1001	USD	 AMVAGBEVM-A4-D13  300	300	300	300	300
5103	81	자동차.마케팅.OTS.OTS2팀	Corvus Norway	A22CR1001	USD					   100	100	100	100	100
5104	
5105	
5106	81	자동차.마케팅.아시아.현대기아차팀	KIA	A22MZ1002	USD	ACHP2005I-A1T007	500	500	500	500
5107	
5108	
5109	순서로 입력되어 있어도 ItemID가 입력되지 않은 것 먼저 처리함(2024.05.29 박필수 책임 확인사항)
5110	
5111	=>이를 위해 ItemID 없는 건들 먼저 추출(전체 데이터셋)
5112	=>ItemID 있는 건들 추출(ItemID 데이터셋)
5113	=>merge into 처리
5114	
5115	
5116	5.SCM_MST_FUTURE_PRICE , SCM_MST_FUTURE_PRICE_H 저장시 화면에서 수동으로 등록한 것과 구분하기 위해 ATTRIBUTE8 컬럼에 EXCEL UPLOAD 표시
5117	
5118	*/

5154	       AND T.CREATED_BY = P_CREATED_BY;

5157	        V_LOG_TYPE    := 'END';
5158	        V_LOG_MESSAGE := '########## END of procedure ##########';

5165	        SELECT '-1' AS CODE, 'ERR_RTN: - Validation Check' AS MSG, V_DESCRIPTION AS EVENT FROM DUAL;
5166	        RETURN;
5167	    END IF;

5171	          INTO V_DIVISION
5172	          FROM SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD
5173	         WHERE PROGRAM_ID         = V_PROGRAM_ID
5174	           AND SHEET_NAME         = V_SHEET_NAME
5175	           AND CREATED_BY         = P_CREATED_BY

5248	        /* ATTRIBUTE2  : Team									*/
5249	        /* ATTRIBUTE3  : End Customer							*/

5256	             , MESSAGE_TEXT       = CASE WHEN NVL(ATTRIBUTE1, '') = '' THEN 'NULL DIVISION.'
5257	                                        WHEN NVL(ATTRIBUTE2, '') = '' THEN 'NULL TEAM.'
5258	                                        WHEN NVL(ATTRIBUTE3, '') = '' THEN 'NULL END CUSTOMER.'
5259	                                        WHEN NVL(ATTRIBUTE4, '') = '' THEN 'NULL PROJECT CODE.'
5260	                                        WHEN NVL(ATTRIBUTE5, '') = '' THEN 'NULL CURRENCY.'
5261	                                   END
5262	         WHERE PROGRAM_ID         = V_PROGRAM_ID
5263	           AND SHEET_NAME         = V_SHEET_NAME
5264	           AND CREATED_BY         = P_CREATED_BY
5265	           AND TO_CHAR(CREATION_DATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3') = V_CREATION_DATE
5266	           AND UPLOAD_STATUS_CODE = 'UPLOAD'
5267	           AND (
5268	                NVL(ATTRIBUTE1, '') = '' OR NVL(ATTRIBUTE2, '') = '' OR NVL(ATTRIBUTE3, '') = '' OR NVL(ATTRIBUTE4, '') = '' OR NVL(ATTRIBUTE5, '') = ''
5269	               );
5270

5278	        /* #################################################### */
5279	        /* 동일아이템 중복 체크									*/
5280	        /* ATTRIBUTE1  : Div									*/
5281	        /* ATTRIBUTE2  : Team									*/
5282	        /* ATTRIBUTE3  : End Customer							*/
5283	        /* ATTRIBUTE4 : Project Code							*/
5284	        /* ATTRIBUTE5 : Currency								*/
5285	        /* ATTRIBUTE6 : Model									*/
5286	        /* #################################################### */
5287	        UPDATE SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD
5288	           SET UPLOAD_STATUS_CODE = 'ERROR'
5289	             , MESSAGE_TEXT       = 'Duplication Model.'
5290	         WHERE PROGRAM_ID         = V_PROGRAM_ID
5291	           AND SHEET_NAME         = V_SHEET_NAME
5292	           AND CREATED_BY         = P_CREATED_BY
5293	           AND TO_CHAR(CREATION_DATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3') = V_CREATION_DATE
5294	           AND UPLOAD_STATUS_CODE = 'UPLOAD'
5295	           AND EXISTS (
5296	                SELECT 1
5297	                FROM (
5298	                    SELECT X.ATTRIBUTE1 AS COL1/* ATTRIBUTE1  : Div		*/
5299	                         , X.ATTRIBUTE2 AS COL2/* ATTRIBUTE2  : Team		*/
5300	                         , X.ATTRIBUTE3 AS COL3/* ATTRIBUTE3  : End Customer	*/
5301	                         , X.ATTRIBUTE4 AS COL4/* ATTRIBUTE4 : Project Code	*/
5302	                         , X.ATTRIBUTE6 AS COL6/*ATTRIBUTE6  : Model			*/
5303	                      FROM SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD X
5304	                     WHERE X.PROGRAM_ID    = V_PROGRAM_ID
5305	                       AND X.SHEET_NAME    = V_SHEET_NAME
5306	                       AND X.CREATED_BY    = P_CREATED_BY
5307	                       AND TO_CHAR(X.CREATION_DATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3') = V_CREATION_DATE
5308	                       AND X.UPLOAD_STATUS_CODE = 'UPLOAD'
5309	                     GROUP BY X.ATTRIBUTE1, X.ATTRIBUTE2, X.ATTRIBUTE3, X.ATTRIBUTE4, X.ATTRIBUTE6
5310	                    HAVING COUNT(*) > 1
5311	                ) T
5312	                WHERE T.COL1 = SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD.ATTRIBUTE1
5313	                  AND T.COL2 = SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD.ATTRIBUTE2
5314	                  AND T.COL3 = SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD.ATTRIBUTE3
5315	                  AND T.COL4 = SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD.ATTRIBUTE4
5316	                  AND T.COL6 = SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD.ATTRIBUTE6
5317	               );
5318	
5319	        ------------------------------------------------------------------------
5320	        --scm2_mgr.xLOG('ERROR Key Duplication ', SQL%ROWCOUNT, 4, TRUE);
5321	        V_LOG_TYPE    := '4';
5322	        V_LOG_MESSAGE := '########## ERROR Model Duplication ##########';
5323	        V_DESCRIPTION := '';
5324	        D_EVENT_DATE  := SYSDATE;
5325	        BEGIN SCM_MGR.PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH($$PLSQL_UNIT, V_LOG_TYPE, N_LOG_COUNT, V_LOG_MESSAGE, V_DESCRIPTION, D_EVENT_DATE); END;

5328	        /* #################################################### */
5329	        /* 위 체크 통과했을 경우 Currency 체크					*/
5330	        /*두 개 이상의 Currency가 중복으로 입력되면 안됨		*/
5331	        /* ATTRIBUTE1  : Div									*/
5332	        /* ATTRIBUTE2  : Team									*/
5333	        /* ATTRIBUTE3  : End Customer							*/
5334	        /* ATTRIBUTE4 : Project Code							*/
5335	        /* ATTRIBUTE5 : Currency								*/
5336	        /* ATTRIBUTE6 : Model									*/
5337	        /* #################################################### */
5338	        DECLARE
5339	            V_MODEL_DUP_EXISTS PLS_INTEGER;
5340	        BEGIN
5341	            SELECT 1 INTO V_MODEL_DUP_EXISTS FROM DUAL WHERE EXISTS (
5342	                SELECT 1
5343	                  FROM SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD X
5344	                 WHERE X.PROGRAM_ID    = V_PROGRAM_ID
5345	                   AND X.SHEET_NAME    = V_SHEET_NAME
5346	                   AND X.CREATED_BY    = P_CREATED_BY
5347	                   AND TO_CHAR(X.CREATION_DATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3') = V_CREATION_DATE
5348	                   AND X.UPLOAD_STATUS_CODE = 'UPLOAD'
5349	                 GROUP BY X.ATTRIBUTE1, X.ATTRIBUTE2, X.ATTRIBUTE3, X.ATTRIBUTE4, X.ATTRIBUTE6
5350	                HAVING COUNT(*) > 1
5351	            );
5352	            -- 모델 중복이 존재하면 Currency 체크 skip
5353	        EXCEPTION
5354	            WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN
5355	                -- 모델 중복 없을 때만 Multiple Currency 체크
5356	                UPDATE SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD
5357	                   SET UPLOAD_STATUS_CODE = 'ERROR'
5358	                     , MESSAGE_TEXT       = 'Multiple Currency.'
5359	                 WHERE PROGRAM_ID         = V_PROGRAM_ID
5360	                   AND SHEET_NAME         = V_SHEET_NAME
5361	                   AND CREATED_BY         = P_CREATED_BY
5362	                   AND TO_CHAR(CREATION_DATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3') = V_CREATION_DATE
5363	                   AND UPLOAD_STATUS_CODE = 'UPLOAD'
5364	                   AND EXISTS (
5365	                        SELECT 1
5366	                        FROM (
5367	                            SELECT COL1, COL2, COL3, COL4
5368	                            FROM (
5369	                                SELECT X.ATTRIBUTE1 AS COL1
5370	                                     , X.ATTRIBUTE2 AS COL2
5371	                                     , X.ATTRIBUTE3 AS COL3
5372	                                     , X.ATTRIBUTE4 AS COL4
5373	                                     , X.ATTRIBUTE5 AS COL5
5374	                                  FROM SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD X
5375	                                 WHERE X.PROGRAM_ID    = V_PROGRAM_ID
5376	                                   AND X.SHEET_NAME    = V_SHEET_NAME
5377	                                   AND X.CREATED_BY    = P_CREATED_BY
5378	                                   AND TO_CHAR(X.CREATION_DATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3') = V_CREATION_DATE
5379	                                   AND X.UPLOAD_STATUS_CODE = 'UPLOAD'
5380	                                 GROUP BY X.ATTRIBUTE1, X.ATTRIBUTE2, X.ATTRIBUTE3, X.ATTRIBUTE4, X.ATTRIBUTE5
5381	                            ) A
5382	                            GROUP BY COL1, COL2, COL3, COL4
5383	                           HAVING COUNT(*) > 1
5384	                        ) T
5385	                        WHERE T.COL1 = SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD.ATTRIBUTE1
5386	                          AND T.COL2 = SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD.ATTRIBUTE2
5387	                          AND T.COL3 = SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD.ATTRIBUTE3
5388	                          AND T.COL4 = SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD.ATTRIBUTE4
5389	                       );
5390	        END;
5391	
5392	        ------------------------------------------------------------------------
5393	        --scm2_mgr.xLOG('ERROR Key Duplication ', SQL%ROWCOUNT, 4, TRUE);
5394	        V_LOG_TYPE    := '4';
5395	        V_LOG_MESSAGE := '########## ERROR Multiple Currency ##########';
5396	        V_DESCRIPTION := '';
5397	        D_EVENT_DATE  := SYSDATE;
5398	        BEGIN SCM_MGR.PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH($$PLSQL_UNIT, V_LOG_TYPE, N_LOG_COUNT, V_LOG_MESSAGE, V_DESCRIPTION, D_EVENT_DATE); END;

5562	        /* #################################################### */
5563	        /* 숫자여부 체크										*/
5564	        /* #################################################### */
5565	        /* [E20260923-XLSUPQTY] 사용자 지시 2026-09-23 — M0~M12 비숫자는 'Invalid QTY' 로 안내.
5566	           ⛔ 구 판정 `NVL(ATTRIBUTEn,'') <> ''` 는 Oracle 에서 '' = NULL 이라 **항상 UNKNOWN**,
5567	              즉 이 UPDATE 가 단 1행도 잡지 못하는 사문이었다(hook #119).
5568	              그 탓에 비숫자 값이 저장 분기까지 흘러가 TO_NUMBER 에서 ORA-01722 원문이 노출됐다.
5569	              사내 실증 2026-09-23: 현행 조건 검출 0건 / IS NOT NULL 정정 조건 1건.
5570	           정정 = TRIM(x) IS NOT NULL (빈 셀·공백셀은 종전대로 제외) + 정규식 대상도 TRIM(x).
5571	           메시지 = 'Invalid QTY [<헤더라벨>: <입력값>]' — 헤더라벨은 HEADER_CONTENT 기간 토큰
5572	              M0~M12 (ATTRIBUTE7~19 와 1:1). 첫 위반 칸 1개를 표기한다(CASE 선평가). */
5573	        UPDATE SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD
5574	           SET UPLOAD_STATUS_CODE = 'ERROR'
5575	             , MESSAGE_TEXT       = 'Invalid QTY ['
5576	                                 || CASE
5577	                    WHEN TRIM(ATTRIBUTE7) IS NOT NULL AND NOT REGEXP_LIKE(TRIM(ATTRIBUTE7), '^[+-]?\d+\.?\d*$')
5578	                         THEN 'M0: ' || TRIM(ATTRIBUTE7)
5579	                    WHEN TRIM(ATTRIBUTE8) IS NOT NULL AND NOT REGEXP_LIKE(TRIM(ATTRIBUTE8), '^[+-]?\d+\.?\d*$')
5580	                         THEN 'M1: ' || TRIM(ATTRIBUTE8)
5581	                    WHEN TRIM(ATTRIBUTE9) IS NOT NULL AND NOT REGEXP_LIKE(TRIM(ATTRIBUTE9), '^[+-]?\d+\.?\d*$')
5582	                         THEN 'M2: ' || TRIM(ATTRIBUTE9)
5583	                    WHEN TRIM(ATTRIBUTE10) IS NOT NULL AND NOT REGEXP_LIKE(TRIM(ATTRIBUTE10), '^[+-]?\d+\.?\d*$')
5584	                         THEN 'M3: ' || TRIM(ATTRIBUTE10)
5585	                    WHEN TRIM(ATTRIBUTE11) IS NOT NULL AND NOT REGEXP_LIKE(TRIM(ATTRIBUTE11), '^[+-]?\d+\.?\d*$')
5586	                         THEN 'M4: ' || TRIM(ATTRIBUTE11)
5587	                    WHEN TRIM(ATTRIBUTE12) IS NOT NULL AND NOT REGEXP_LIKE(TRIM(ATTRIBUTE12), '^[+-]?\d+\.?\d*$')
5588	                         THEN 'M5: ' || TRIM(ATTRIBUTE12)
5589	                    WHEN TRIM(ATTRIBUTE13) IS NOT NULL AND NOT REGEXP_LIKE(TRIM(ATTRIBUTE13), '^[+-]?\d+\.?\d*$')
5590	                         THEN 'M6: ' || TRIM(ATTRIBUTE13)
5591	                    WHEN TRIM(ATTRIBUTE14) IS NOT NULL AND NOT REGEXP_LIKE(TRIM(ATTRIBUTE14), '^[+-]?\d+\.?\d*$')
5592	                         THEN 'M7: ' || TRIM(ATTRIBUTE14)
5593	                    WHEN TRIM(ATTRIBUTE15) IS NOT NULL AND NOT REGEXP_LIKE(TRIM(ATTRIBUTE15), '^[+-]?\d+\.?\d*$')
5594	                         THEN 'M8: ' || TRIM(ATTRIBUTE15)
5595	                    WHEN TRIM(ATTRIBUTE16) IS NOT NULL AND NOT REGEXP_LIKE(TRIM(ATTRIBUTE16), '^[+-]?\d+\.?\d*$')
5596	                         THEN 'M9: ' || TRIM(ATTRIBUTE16)
5597	                    WHEN TRIM(ATTRIBUTE17) IS NOT NULL AND NOT REGEXP_LIKE(TRIM(ATTRIBUTE17), '^[+-]?\d+\.?\d*$')
5598	                         THEN 'M10: ' || TRIM(ATTRIBUTE17)
5599	                    WHEN TRIM(ATTRIBUTE18) IS NOT NULL AND NOT REGEXP_LIKE(TRIM(ATTRIBUTE18), '^[+-]?\d+\.?\d*$')
5600	                         THEN 'M11: ' || TRIM(ATTRIBUTE18)
5601	                    WHEN TRIM(ATTRIBUTE19) IS NOT NULL AND NOT REGEXP_LIKE(TRIM(ATTRIBUTE19), '^[+-]?\d+\.?\d*$')
5602	                         THEN 'M12: ' || TRIM(ATTRIBUTE19)
5603	                                    END
5604	                                 || ']'
5605	         WHERE PROGRAM_ID         = V_PROGRAM_ID
5606	           AND SHEET_NAME         = V_SHEET_NAME
5607	           AND CREATED_BY         = P_CREATED_BY
5608	           AND TO_CHAR(CREATION_DATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3') = V_CREATION_DATE
5609	           AND UPLOAD_STATUS_CODE = 'UPLOAD'
5610	           AND (
5611	                (TRIM(ATTRIBUTE7) IS NOT NULL AND NOT REGEXP_LIKE(TRIM(ATTRIBUTE7), '^[+-]?\d+\.?\d*$')) OR--M0
5612	                (TRIM(ATTRIBUTE8) IS NOT NULL AND NOT REGEXP_LIKE(TRIM(ATTRIBUTE8), '^[+-]?\d+\.?\d*$')) OR--M1
5613	                (TRIM(ATTRIBUTE9) IS NOT NULL AND NOT REGEXP_LIKE(TRIM(ATTRIBUTE9), '^[+-]?\d+\.?\d*$')) OR--M2
5614	                (TRIM(ATTRIBUTE10) IS NOT NULL AND NOT REGEXP_LIKE(TRIM(ATTRIBUTE10), '^[+-]?\d+\.?\d*$')) OR--M3
5615	                (TRIM(ATTRIBUTE11) IS NOT NULL AND NOT REGEXP_LIKE(TRIM(ATTRIBUTE11), '^[+-]?\d+\.?\d*$')) OR--M4
5616	                (TRIM(ATTRIBUTE12) IS NOT NULL AND NOT REGEXP_LIKE(TRIM(ATTRIBUTE12), '^[+-]?\d+\.?\d*$')) OR--M5
5617	                (TRIM(ATTRIBUTE13) IS NOT NULL AND NOT REGEXP_LIKE(TRIM(ATTRIBUTE13), '^[+-]?\d+\.?\d*$')) OR--M6
5618	                (TRIM(ATTRIBUTE14) IS NOT NULL AND NOT REGEXP_LIKE(TRIM(ATTRIBUTE14), '^[+-]?\d+\.?\d*$')) OR--M7
5619	                (TRIM(ATTRIBUTE15) IS NOT NULL AND NOT REGEXP_LIKE(TRIM(ATTRIBUTE15), '^[+-]?\d+\.?\d*$')) OR--M8
5620	                (TRIM(ATTRIBUTE16) IS NOT NULL AND NOT REGEXP_LIKE(TRIM(ATTRIBUTE16), '^[+-]?\d+\.?\d*$')) OR--M9
5621	                (TRIM(ATTRIBUTE17) IS NOT NULL AND NOT REGEXP_LIKE(TRIM(ATTRIBUTE17), '^[+-]?\d+\.?\d*$')) OR--M10
5622	                (TRIM(ATTRIBUTE18) IS NOT NULL AND NOT REGEXP_LIKE(TRIM(ATTRIBUTE18), '^[+-]?\d+\.?\d*$')) OR--M11
5623	                (TRIM(ATTRIBUTE19) IS NOT NULL AND NOT REGEXP_LIKE(TRIM(ATTRIBUTE19), '^[+-]?\d+\.?\d*$'))   --M12
5624	               );
5625	
5626	        N_LOG_COUNT   := N_LOG_COUNT + 1;
5627	        V_LOG_TYPE    := 'UPDATE';
5628	        V_LOG_MESSAGE := '########## Invalid NUMERIC. ##########';
5629	        V_DESCRIPTION := TO_CHAR(SQL%ROWCOUNT);
5630	        D_EVENT_DATE  := SYSDATE;
5631	        BEGIN SCM_MGR.PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH($$PLSQL_UNIT, V_LOG_TYPE, N_LOG_COUNT, V_LOG_MESSAGE, V_DESCRIPTION, D_EVENT_DATE); END;
