5700	
5701	        --[E20241217-000632]_Sales Price 화면 엑셀 업로드 시 중복데이터 입력 제한 START
5702	        /* #################################################### */
5703	        /*  item별로 1개의 currency만 등록되게 기존 데이터 체크하여 튕겨냄					*/
5704	        /* ATTRIBUTE1  : Div									*/
5705	        /* ATTRIBUTE2  : Team									*/
5706	        /* ATTRIBUTE3  : End Customer							*/
5707	        /* ATTRIBUTE4 : Project Code							*/
5708	        /* ATTRIBUTE5 : Currency								*/
5709	        /* ATTRIBUTE6 : Model									*/
5710	        /* #################################################### */
5711	
5712	        --ITEM 입력 안된 경우는 프로젝트 하위의 전체 ITEM을 체크
5713	        -- M0~M12 각 월별 기존 FP 데이터와 Currency 불일치 여부를 UNION ALL 기반으로 체크
5714	        UPDATE SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD EU
5715	           SET EU.UPLOAD_STATUS_CODE = 'ERROR'
5716	             , EU.MESSAGE_TEXT       = '동일 기간에 두 개 이상의 Currency에 해당하는 Sales Price가 존재합니다.'
5717	         WHERE EU.PROGRAM_ID         = V_PROGRAM_ID
5718	           AND EU.SHEET_NAME         = V_SHEET_NAME
5719	           AND EU.CREATED_BY         = P_CREATED_BY
5720	           AND TO_CHAR(EU.CREATION_DATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3') = V_CREATION_DATE
5721	           AND EU.UPLOAD_STATUS_CODE = 'UPLOAD'
5722	           AND NVL(EU.ATTRIBUTE6, '') = '' --item_id 입력 안된건
5723	           AND EXISTS (
5724	                SELECT 1
5725	                FROM SCM_MST_SITE MS
5726	                INNER JOIN SCM_MST_SITE_ITEM ITM
5727	                    ON ITM.DIVISION_ID  = MS.DIVISION_ID
5728	                   AND ITM.PROJECT_CODE = EU.ATTRIBUTE4
5729	                   AND ITM.SCM_SITE_ID  = MS.SCM_SITE_ID
5730	                   AND ITM.VALID_YN     = 'Y'
5731	                INNER JOIN SCM_MST_FUTURE_PRICE FP
5732	                    ON FP.DIVISION_ID   = ITM.DIVISION_ID
5733	                   AND FP.PROJECT_CODE  = ITM.PROJECT_CODE
5734	                   AND FP.SCM_SITE_ID   = ITM.SCM_SITE_ID
5735	                   AND FP.MEASURE       = 'LOC'
5736	                   AND FP.ITEM_ID       = ITM.ITEM_ID
5737	                   AND FP.CURRENCY     <> EU.ATTRIBUTE5
5738	                WHERE MS.DIVISION_ID  = EU.ATTRIBUTE1
5739	                  AND MS.END_CUSTOMER = EU.ATTRIBUTE3
5740	                  AND MS.VALID_YN     = 'Y'
5741	                  AND (
5742	                       (NVL(EU.ATTRIBUTE7,  '') <> '' AND SUBSTR(FP.YYYYMMDD, 1, 6) = TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'YYYYMM'))                      --M0
5743	                    OR (NVL(EU.ATTRIBUTE8,  '') <> '' AND SUBSTR(FP.YYYYMMDD, 1, 6) = TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE,  1), 'YYYYMM'))  --M1
5744	                    OR (NVL(EU.ATTRIBUTE9,  '') <> '' AND SUBSTR(FP.YYYYMMDD, 1, 6) = TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE,  2), 'YYYYMM'))  --M2
5745	                    OR (NVL(EU.ATTRIBUTE10, '') <> '' AND SUBSTR(FP.YYYYMMDD, 1, 6) = TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE,  3), 'YYYYMM'))  --M3
5746	                    OR (NVL(EU.ATTRIBUTE11, '') <> '' AND SUBSTR(FP.YYYYMMDD, 1, 6) = TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE,  4), 'YYYYMM'))  --M4
5747	                    OR (NVL(EU.ATTRIBUTE12, '') <> '' AND SUBSTR(FP.YYYYMMDD, 1, 6) = TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE,  5), 'YYYYMM'))  --M5
5748	                    OR (NVL(EU.ATTRIBUTE13, '') <> '' AND SUBSTR(FP.YYYYMMDD, 1, 6) = TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE,  6), 'YYYYMM'))  --M6
5749	                    OR (NVL(EU.ATTRIBUTE14, '') <> '' AND SUBSTR(FP.YYYYMMDD, 1, 6) = TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE,  7), 'YYYYMM'))  --M7
5750	                    OR (NVL(EU.ATTRIBUTE15, '') <> '' AND SUBSTR(FP.YYYYMMDD, 1, 6) = TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE,  8), 'YYYYMM'))  --M8
5751	                    OR (NVL(EU.ATTRIBUTE16, '') <> '' AND SUBSTR(FP.YYYYMMDD, 1, 6) = TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE,  9), 'YYYYMM'))  --M9
5752	                    OR (NVL(EU.ATTRIBUTE17, '') <> '' AND SUBSTR(FP.YYYYMMDD, 1, 6) = TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE, 10), 'YYYYMM'))  --M10
5753	                    OR (NVL(EU.ATTRIBUTE18, '') <> '' AND SUBSTR(FP.YYYYMMDD, 1, 6) = TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE, 11), 'YYYYMM'))  --M11
5754	                    OR (NVL(EU.ATTRIBUTE19, '') <> '' AND SUBSTR(FP.YYYYMMDD, 1, 6) = TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE, 12), 'YYYYMM'))  --M12
5755	                  )
5756	           );
5757	
5758	        --ITEM 입력된 경우
5759	        UPDATE SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD EU
5760	           SET EU.UPLOAD_STATUS_CODE = 'ERROR'
5761	             , EU.MESSAGE_TEXT       = '동일 기간에 두 개 이상의 Currency에 해당하는 Sales Price가 존재합니다.'
5762	         WHERE EU.PROGRAM_ID         = V_PROGRAM_ID
5763	           AND EU.SHEET_NAME         = V_SHEET_NAME
5764	           AND EU.CREATED_BY         = P_CREATED_BY
5765	           AND TO_CHAR(EU.CREATION_DATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3') = V_CREATION_DATE
5766	           AND EU.UPLOAD_STATUS_CODE = 'UPLOAD'
5767	           AND NVL(EU.ATTRIBUTE6, '') <> '' --item_id 입력 된건
5768	           AND EXISTS (
5769	                SELECT 1
5770	                FROM SCM_MST_SITE MS
5771	                INNER JOIN SCM_MST_SITE_ITEM ITM
5772	                    ON ITM.DIVISION_ID  = MS.DIVISION_ID
5773	                   AND ITM.PROJECT_CODE = EU.ATTRIBUTE4
5774	                   AND ITM.SCM_SITE_ID  = MS.SCM_SITE_ID
5775	                   AND ITM.VALID_YN     = 'Y'
5776	                INNER JOIN SCM_MST_FUTURE_PRICE FP
5777	                    ON FP.DIVISION_ID   = ITM.DIVISION_ID
5778	                   AND FP.PROJECT_CODE  = ITM.PROJECT_CODE
5779	                   AND FP.SCM_SITE_ID   = ITM.SCM_SITE_ID
5780	                   AND FP.MEASURE       = 'LOC'
5781	                   AND FP.ITEM_ID       = ITM.ITEM_ID
5782	                   AND FP.ITEM_ID       = EU.ATTRIBUTE6
5783	                   AND FP.CURRENCY     <> EU.ATTRIBUTE5
5784	                WHERE MS.DIVISION_ID  = EU.ATTRIBUTE1
5785	                  AND MS.END_CUSTOMER = EU.ATTRIBUTE3
5786	                  AND MS.VALID_YN     = 'Y'
5787	                  AND (
5788	                       (NVL(EU.ATTRIBUTE7,  '') <> '' AND SUBSTR(FP.YYYYMMDD, 1, 6) = TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'YYYYMM'))
5789	                    OR (NVL(EU.ATTRIBUTE8,  '') <> '' AND SUBSTR(FP.YYYYMMDD, 1, 6) = TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE,  1), 'YYYYMM'))
5790	                    OR (NVL(EU.ATTRIBUTE9,  '') <> '' AND SUBSTR(FP.YYYYMMDD, 1, 6) = TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE,  2), 'YYYYMM'))
5791	                    OR (NVL(EU.ATTRIBUTE10, '') <> '' AND SUBSTR(FP.YYYYMMDD, 1, 6) = TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE,  3), 'YYYYMM'))
5792	                    OR (NVL(EU.ATTRIBUTE11, '') <> '' AND SUBSTR(FP.YYYYMMDD, 1, 6) = TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE,  4), 'YYYYMM'))
5793	                    OR (NVL(EU.ATTRIBUTE12, '') <> '' AND SUBSTR(FP.YYYYMMDD, 1, 6) = TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE,  5), 'YYYYMM'))
5794	                    OR (NVL(EU.ATTRIBUTE13, '') <> '' AND SUBSTR(FP.YYYYMMDD, 1, 6) = TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE,  6), 'YYYYMM'))
5795	                    OR (NVL(EU.ATTRIBUTE14, '') <> '' AND SUBSTR(FP.YYYYMMDD, 1, 6) = TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE,  7), 'YYYYMM'))
5796	                    OR (NVL(EU.ATTRIBUTE15, '') <> '' AND SUBSTR(FP.YYYYMMDD, 1, 6) = TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE,  8), 'YYYYMM'))
5797	                    OR (NVL(EU.ATTRIBUTE16, '') <> '' AND SUBSTR(FP.YYYYMMDD, 1, 6) = TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE,  9), 'YYYYMM'))
5798	                    OR (NVL(EU.ATTRIBUTE17, '') <> '' AND SUBSTR(FP.YYYYMMDD, 1, 6) = TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE, 10), 'YYYYMM'))
5799	                    OR (NVL(EU.ATTRIBUTE18, '') <> '' AND SUBSTR(FP.YYYYMMDD, 1, 6) = TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE, 11), 'YYYYMM'))
5800	                    OR (NVL(EU.ATTRIBUTE19, '') <> '' AND SUBSTR(FP.YYYYMMDD, 1, 6) = TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE, 12), 'YYYYMM'))
5801	                  )
5802	           );
5803	
5804	        --[E20241217-000632]_Sales Price 화면 엑셀 업로드 시 중복데이터 입력 제한 END
5805	
5806	        N_LOG_COUNT   := N_LOG_COUNT + 1;
5807	        V_LOG_TYPE    := 'UPDATE';
5808	        V_LOG_MESSAGE := '########## ERROR Multiple Currency ##########';
5809	        V_DESCRIPTION := TO_CHAR(SQL%ROWCOUNT);
5810	        D_EVENT_DATE  := SYSDATE;
5811	        BEGIN SCM_MGR.PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH($$PLSQL_UNIT, V_LOG_TYPE, N_LOG_COUNT, V_LOG_MESSAGE, V_DESCRIPTION, D_EVENT_DATE); END;
5812	
5813	        -- 정상(SUCCESS) 처리
5814	        UPDATE SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD
5815	           SET UPLOAD_STATUS_CODE = 'SUCCESS'
5816	         WHERE PROGRAM_ID         = V_PROGRAM_ID
5817	           AND SHEET_NAME         = V_SHEET_NAME
5818	           AND CREATED_BY         = P_CREATED_BY
5819	           AND TO_CHAR(CREATION_DATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3') = V_CREATION_DATE
5820	           AND UPLOAD_STATUS_CODE = 'UPLOAD';

6072	                AND A.ITEM_ID      = B.ITEM_ID
6073	                AND A.CURRENCY     = B.CURRENCY
6074	                AND A.INCOTERMS    = B.INCOTERMS
6075	                AND A.DOM_EXP      = B.DOM_EXP
6076	                AND A.PLANT_SITE_ID = B.PLANT_SITE_ID
6077	                AND A.FCST_TYPE    = B.FCST_TYPE
6078	                  )
6079	            WHEN MATCHED THEN
6080	                UPDATE SET
6081	                    A.M0  = CASE WHEN B.M0  = '0' THEN A.M0  ELSE B.M0  END
6082	                  , A.M1  = CASE WHEN B.M1  = '0' THEN A.M1  ELSE B.M1  END
6083	                  , A.M2  = CASE WHEN B.M2  = '0' THEN A.M2  ELSE B.M2  END
6084	                  , A.M3  = CASE WHEN B.M3  = '0' THEN A.M3  ELSE B.M3  END
6085	                  , A.M4  = CASE WHEN B.M4  = '0' THEN A.M4  ELSE B.M4  END
6086	                  , A.M5  = CASE WHEN B.M5  = '0' THEN A.M5  ELSE B.M5  END
6087	                  , A.M6  = CASE WHEN B.M6  = '0' THEN A.M6  ELSE B.M6  END
6088	                  , A.M7  = CASE WHEN B.M7  = '0' THEN A.M7  ELSE B.M7  END
6089	                  , A.M8  = CASE WHEN B.M8  = '0' THEN A.M8  ELSE B.M8  END
6090	                  , A.M9  = CASE WHEN B.M9  = '0' THEN A.M9  ELSE B.M9  END
6091	                  , A.M10 = CASE WHEN B.M10 = '0' THEN A.M10 ELSE B.M10 END
6092	                  , A.M11 = CASE WHEN B.M11 = '0' THEN A.M11 ELSE B.M11 END
6093	                  , A.M12 = CASE WHEN B.M12 = '0' THEN A.M12 ELSE B.M12 END
6094	            WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN
6095	                INSERT (DIVISION_ID, SCM_SITE_ID, PROJECT_CODE, ITEM_ID, CURRENCY,
6096	                        INCOTERMS, DOM_EXP, PLANT_SITE_ID, FCST_TYPE,
6097	                        M0, M1, M2, M3, M4, M5, M6, M7, M8, M9, M10, M11, M12)
6098	                VALUES (B.DIVISION_ID, B.SCM_SITE_ID, B.PROJECT_CODE, B.ITEM_ID, B.CURRENCY,
6099	                        B.INCOTERMS, B.DOM_EXP, B.PLANT_SITE_ID, B.FCST_TYPE,
6100	                        B.M0, B.M1, B.M2, B.M3, B.M4, B.M5, B.M6, B.M7, B.M8, B.M9, B.M10, B.M11, B.M12);
6101	
6102	            COMMIT;
6103	
6104	            --------------------------------------------------
6105	            -- 5. 추출된 PK 기준으로 LOC Measure의 가격을 Upload Master 기준 가격으로 Calendar Join해서 주차별 반영(Insert / Update, 52주 구간)
6106	            --------------------------------------------------
6107	            --월별로 UNPIVOT하여 실제 월 구하기(GT_F020_UPLOAD_MASTER_STEP2)
6108	            INSERT /*+ APPEND PARALLEL(4) */ INTO GT_F020_UPLOAD_MASTER_STEP2
6109	            (DIVISION_ID, SCM_SITE_ID, PROJECT_CODE, ITEM_ID, CURRENCY,
6110	             INCOTERMS, DOM_EXP, PLANT_SITE_ID, FCST_TYPE, LOC, MM, MM2)
6111	            SELECT DIVISION_ID, SCM_SITE_ID, PROJECT_CODE, ITEM_ID, CURRENCY,
6112	                   INCOTERMS, DOM_EXP, PLANT_SITE_ID, FCST_TYPE, LOC, MM,
6113	                   CASE MM
6114	                       WHEN 'M0'  THEN TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'YYYYMM')
6115	                       WHEN 'M1'  THEN TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE,  1), 'YYYYMM')
6116	                       WHEN 'M2'  THEN TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE,  2), 'YYYYMM')
6117	                       WHEN 'M3'  THEN TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE,  3), 'YYYYMM')
6118	                       WHEN 'M4'  THEN TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE,  4), 'YYYYMM')
6119	                       WHEN 'M5'  THEN TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE,  5), 'YYYYMM')
6120	                       WHEN 'M6'  THEN TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE,  6), 'YYYYMM')
6121	                       WHEN 'M7'  THEN TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE,  7), 'YYYYMM')
6122	                       WHEN 'M8'  THEN TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE,  8), 'YYYYMM')
6123	                       WHEN 'M9'  THEN TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE,  9), 'YYYYMM')
6124	                       WHEN 'M10' THEN TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE, 10), 'YYYYMM')
6125	                       WHEN 'M11' THEN TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE, 11), 'YYYYMM')
6126	                       WHEN 'M12' THEN TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE, 12), 'YYYYMM')
6127	                   END AS MM2
6128	              FROM GT_F020_UPLOAD_MASTER_STEP1
6129	            UNPIVOT (
6130	                LOC FOR MM IN (
6131	                    M0  AS 'M0',  M1  AS 'M1',  M2  AS 'M2',  M3  AS 'M3',
6132	                    M4  AS 'M4',  M5  AS 'M5',  M6  AS 'M6',  M7  AS 'M7',
6133	                    M8  AS 'M8',  M9  AS 'M9',  M10 AS 'M10', M11 AS 'M11',
6134	                    M12 AS 'M12'
6135	                )
6136	            );
6137	
6138	            COMMIT;
6139	
6140	            DBMS_STATS.GATHER_TABLE_STATS(
6141	                ownname => 'SCM_MGR', tabname => 'GT_F020_UPLOAD_MASTER_STEP2',
6142	                estimate_percent => DBMS_STATS.AUTO_SAMPLE_SIZE, method_opt => 'FOR ALL COLUMNS SIZE 1'
6143	            );
6144	
6145	            --5-1)Upload Master와 CALENDAR 테이블 조인하여 주차별로 ROW 생성
6146	            --52주차
6147	            INSERT /*+ APPEND PARALLEL(4) */ INTO GT_F020_UPLOAD_MASTER_FINAL
6148	            (DIVISION_ID, SCM_SITE_ID, PROJECT_CODE, ITEM_ID, CURRENCY,
6149	             INCOTERMS, DOM_EXP, PLANT_SITE_ID, FCST_TYPE, LOC, MM, MM2, BASIS_START_DATE, MEASURE)
6150	            SELECT DISTINCT
6151	                TMP.DIVISION_ID, TMP.SCM_SITE_ID, TMP.PROJECT_CODE, TMP.ITEM_ID, TMP.CURRENCY,
6152	                TMP.INCOTERMS, TMP.DOM_EXP, TMP.PLANT_SITE_ID, TMP.FCST_TYPE,
6153	                TMP.LOC, TMP.MM, TMP.MM2, CAL.BASIS_START_DATE,
6154	                'LOC' --엑셀 입력값은 LOC 메저로 간주
6155	              FROM GT_F020_UPLOAD_MASTER_STEP2 TMP
6156	             INNER JOIN (
6157	                SELECT DISTINCT
6158	                    SUBSTR(BASIS_START_DATE, 1, 6) AS YYYYMM,
6159	                    BASIS_START_DATE
6160	                  FROM SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M CAL
6161	                 WHERE CAL.BASIS_YYYYMMDD >= TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'YYYYMMDD')
6162	                   AND CAL.BASIS_YYYYMMDD <  TO_CHAR(SYSDATE + 52 * 7, 'YYYYMMDD')
6163	             ) CAL
6164	                ON TMP.MM2 = CAL.YYYYMM
6165	             WHERE TO_NUMBER(TMP.LOC) > 0; -- 0 이상만 처리 (빈값 제외)
6166	
6167	            COMMIT;
6168	
6169	            --5-2)환율데이터 생성
6170	            INSERT /*+ APPEND PARALLEL(4) */ INTO GT_F020_EXCH
6171	            (DIVISION_ID, YYYYMMDD, CURRENCY, UNIT_CURRENCY, EXCHANGE_PRICE)
6172	            SELECT DISTINCT A.DIVISION_ID, A.YYYYMMDD, A.CURRENCY, A.UNIT_CURRENCY, A.EXCHANGE_PRICE
6173	              FROM DM_MST_EXCHANGE_RATE A
6174	              JOIN GT_F020_UPLOAD_MASTER_STEP2 B
6175	                ON A.DIVISION_ID = B.DIVISION_ID
6176	               AND A.YYYYMMDD    = B.MM2 || '01'
6177	               AND A.CURRENCY    = 'KRW';
6178	
6179	            COMMIT;
6180	
6181	            DBMS_STATS.GATHER_TABLE_STATS(
6182	                ownname => 'SCM_MGR', tabname => 'GT_F020_UPLOAD_MASTER_FINAL',
6183	                estimate_percent => DBMS_STATS.AUTO_SAMPLE_SIZE, method_opt => 'FOR ALL COLUMNS SIZE 1'
6184	            );
6185	            DBMS_STATS.GATHER_TABLE_STATS(
6186	                ownname => 'SCM_MGR', tabname => 'GT_F020_EXCH',
6187	                estimate_percent => DBMS_STATS.AUTO_SAMPLE_SIZE, method_opt => 'FOR ALL COLUMNS SIZE 1'
6188	            );
6189	
6190	            --5-3) CURRENCY가 'KRW', 'USD'일 경우 해당 CURRENCY로 메저생성
6191	            --환율 계산할 필요 없으므로 LOC값 그대로 저장
6192	            INSERT /*+ APPEND PARALLEL(4) */ INTO GT_F020_UPLOAD_MASTER_FINAL
6193	            (DIVISION_ID, SCM_SITE_ID, PROJECT_CODE, ITEM_ID, CURRENCY,
6194	             INCOTERMS, DOM_EXP, PLANT_SITE_ID, FCST_TYPE, LOC, MM, MM2, BASIS_START_DATE, MEASURE)
6195	            SELECT
6196	                A.DIVISION_ID, A.SCM_SITE_ID, A.PROJECT_CODE, A.ITEM_ID, A.CURRENCY,
6197	                A.INCOTERMS, A.DOM_EXP, A.PLANT_SITE_ID, A.FCST_TYPE,
6198	                A.LOC, A.MM, A.MM2, A.BASIS_START_DATE,
6199	                A.CURRENCY AS MEASURE
6200	              FROM GT_F020_UPLOAD_MASTER_FINAL A
6201	             WHERE A.MEASURE  = 'LOC'
6202	               AND A.CURRENCY IN ('KRW', 'USD');
6203	
6204	            COMMIT;
6205	
6206	            --5-4) KRW 메저 생성(기준값)  : 환율금액을 곱하여 KRW 생성
6207	            INSERT /*+ APPEND PARALLEL(4) */ INTO GT_F020_UPLOAD_MASTER_FINAL
6208	            (DIVISION_ID, SCM_SITE_ID, PROJECT_CODE, ITEM_ID, CURRENCY,
6209	             INCOTERMS, DOM_EXP, PLANT_SITE_ID, FCST_TYPE, LOC, MM, MM2, BASIS_START_DATE, MEASURE)
6210	            SELECT
6211	                A.DIVISION_ID, A.SCM_SITE_ID, A.PROJECT_CODE, A.ITEM_ID, A.CURRENCY,
6212	                A.INCOTERMS, A.DOM_EXP, A.PLANT_SITE_ID, A.FCST_TYPE,
6213	                TO_CHAR(ROUND(TO_NUMBER(A.LOC) * B.EXCHANGE_PRICE, 3)),
6214	                A.MM, A.MM2, A.BASIS_START_DATE,
6215	                'KRW'
6216	              FROM GT_F020_UPLOAD_MASTER_FINAL A
6217	              JOIN GT_F020_EXCH B
6218	                ON B.UNIT_CURRENCY = A.CURRENCY
6219	               AND B.YYYYMMDD      = A.MM2 || '01'
6220	             WHERE A.MEASURE   = 'LOC'
6221	               AND A.CURRENCY <> 'KRW';
6222	
6223	            COMMIT;
6224	
6225	            --5-5) USD 메저 생성 : KRW 기준으로 계산
6226	            INSERT /*+ APPEND PARALLEL(4) */ INTO GT_F020_UPLOAD_MASTER_FINAL
6227	            (DIVISION_ID, SCM_SITE_ID, PROJECT_CODE, ITEM_ID, CURRENCY,
6228	             INCOTERMS, DOM_EXP, PLANT_SITE_ID, FCST_TYPE, LOC, MM, MM2, BASIS_START_DATE, MEASURE)
6229	            SELECT
6230	                A.DIVISION_ID, A.SCM_SITE_ID, A.PROJECT_CODE, A.ITEM_ID, A.CURRENCY,
6231	                A.INCOTERMS, A.DOM_EXP, A.PLANT_SITE_ID, A.FCST_TYPE,
6232	                TO_CHAR(ROUND(TO_NUMBER(A.LOC) / B.EXCHANGE_PRICE, 3)),
6233	                A.MM, A.MM2, A.BASIS_START_DATE,
6234	                'USD'
6235	              FROM GT_F020_UPLOAD_MASTER_FINAL A
6236	              JOIN GT_F020_EXCH B
6237	                ON B.UNIT_CURRENCY = 'USD'
6238	               AND B.YYYYMMDD      = A.MM2 || '01'
6239	             WHERE A.MEASURE   = 'KRW'
6240	               AND A.CURRENCY <> 'USD';
6241	
6242	            COMMIT;
6243	
6244	            DBMS_STATS.GATHER_TABLE_STATS(
6245	                ownname => 'SCM_MGR', tabname => 'GT_F020_UPLOAD_MASTER_FINAL',
6246	                estimate_percent => DBMS_STATS.AUTO_SAMPLE_SIZE, method_opt => 'FOR ALL COLUMNS SIZE 1'
6247	            );
6248	
6249	            ----------------------------------------------------
6250	            --6) SCM_MST_FUTURE_PRICE 저장
6251	            ----------------------------------------------------
6252	            MERGE INTO SCM_MST_FUTURE_PRICE A
6253	            USING GT_F020_UPLOAD_MASTER_FINAL B
6254	               ON (
6255	                    A.DIVISION_ID   = B.DIVISION_ID
6256	                AND A.PROJECT_CODE  = B.PROJECT_CODE
6257	                AND A.SCM_SITE_ID   = B.SCM_SITE_ID
6258	                AND A.PLANT_SITE_ID = B.PLANT_SITE_ID
6259	                AND A.ITEM_ID       = B.ITEM_ID
6260	                AND A.YYYYMMDD      = B.BASIS_START_DATE
6261	                AND A.CURRENCY      = B.CURRENCY
6262	                AND A.MEASURE       = B.MEASURE
6263	                AND A.DOM_EXP       = B.DOM_EXP
6264	                AND A.FCST_TYPE     = B.FCST_TYPE
6265	                AND A.INCOTERMS     = B.INCOTERMS
6266	                  )
6267	            WHEN MATCHED THEN
6268	                UPDATE SET
6269	                    A.PRICE            = TO_NUMBER(B.LOC)
6270	                  , A.LAST_UPDATED_BY  = P_CREATED_BY
6271	                  , A.LAST_UPDATE_DATE = SYSDATE
6272	                  , A.ATTRIBUTE8       = 'EXCEL UPLOAD' --엑셀업로드 여부 표시
6273	            WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN
6274	                INSERT (
6275	                    DIVISION_ID, PROJECT_CODE, SCM_SITE_ID, PLANT_SITE_ID,
6276	                    ITEM_ID, YYYYMMDD, CURRENCY, MEASURE, DOM_EXP, FCST_TYPE,
6277	                    INCOTERMS, PRICE, CREATED_BY, CREATION_DATE,
6278	                    LAST_UPDATED_BY, LAST_UPDATE_DATE, ATTRIBUTE8
6279	                )
6280	                VALUES (
6281	                    NVL(B.DIVISION_ID, '-'), NVL(B.PROJECT_CODE, '-'), NVL(B.SCM_SITE_ID, '-'), NVL(B.PLANT_SITE_ID, '-'),
6282	                    NVL(B.ITEM_ID, '-'), NVL(B.BASIS_START_DATE, '-'), NVL(B.CURRENCY, '-'), NVL(B.MEASURE, '-'), NVL(B.DOM_EXP, '-'), NVL(B.FCST_TYPE, '-'),
6283	                    NVL(B.INCOTERMS, '-'), TO_NUMBER(B.LOC), P_CREATED_BY, SYSDATE,
6284	                    P_CREATED_BY, SYSDATE, 'EXCEL UPLOAD' --엑셀업로드 여부 표시
6285	                );
6286	
6287	            N_LOG_COUNT := SQL%ROWCOUNT;
6288	
6289	            ----------------------------------------------------
6290	            --7) SCM_MST_FUTURE_PRICE_H 저장
6291	            ----------------------------------------------------
6292	            INSERT INTO SCM_MST_FUTURE_PRICE_H
6293	            (
6294	                DIVISION_ID, HISTORY_SEQ, PROJECT_CODE, SCM_SITE_ID, PLANT_SITE_ID,
6295	                ITEM_ID, YYYYMMDD, CURRENCY, MEASURE, DOM_EXP, FCST_TYPE,
6296	                INCOTERMS, PRICE, CREATED_BY, CREATION_DATE,
6297	                LAST_UPDATED_BY, LAST_UPDATE_DATE, ATTRIBUTE8
6298	            )
6299	            SELECT
6300	                DIVISION_ID,
6301	                SEQ1_SCM_MST_FUTURE_PRICE_H.NEXTVAL,
6302	                PROJECT_CODE, SCM_SITE_ID, PLANT_SITE_ID,
6303	                ITEM_ID, BASIS_START_DATE, CURRENCY, MEASURE, DOM_EXP, FCST_TYPE,
6304	                INCOTERMS, TO_NUMBER(LOC), P_CREATED_BY, SYSDATE,
6305	                P_CREATED_BY, SYSDATE,
6306	                'EXCEL UPLOAD' --엑셀업로드 여부 표시
6307	              FROM GT_F020_UPLOAD_MASTER_FINAL;
6308	
6309	            COMMIT;
6310	
6311	            EXECUTE IMMEDIATE 'ALTER SESSION DISABLE PARALLEL DML';
6312	
6313	            ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
6314	            --scm2_mgr.xLOG('SUCCESS              ', SQL%ROWCOUNT, 10, TRUE);
6315	            V_LOG_TYPE    := '21';
6316	            V_LOG_MESSAGE := '########## INSERT DMF0020_EXCEL_UPLOAD END ##########';
6317	            V_DESCRIPTION := TO_CHAR(P_MODE);
6318	            D_EVENT_DATE  := SYSDATE;
6319	            BEGIN SCM_MGR.PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH($$PLSQL_UNIT, V_LOG_TYPE, N_LOG_COUNT, V_LOG_MESSAGE, V_DESCRIPTION, D_EVENT_DATE); END;
6320	            ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
6321	
6322	        EXCEPTION
6323	            WHEN OTHERS THEN
6324	                ROLLBACK;
6325	                EXECUTE IMMEDIATE 'ALTER SESSION DISABLE PARALLEL DML';
6326	
6327	                V_SQL_CODE   := TO_CHAR(SQLCODE);
6328	                V_SQL_ERRMSG := SQLERRM;
6329	                V_BACKTRACE  := DBMS_UTILITY.FORMAT_ERROR_BACKTRACE;
6330	                V_PROC_INPUT := JSON_OBJECT(
6331	                    'P_MODE'       VALUE P_MODE,
6332	                    'P_CREATED_BY' VALUE P_CREATED_BY
6333	                );
6334	
6335	                BEGIN
6336	                    SCM_MGR.PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH(
6337	                        $$PLSQL_UNIT, 'ERROR', N_LOG_COUNT, 'EXCEPTION',
6338	                        '', SYSDATE, V_PROC_INPUT,
6339	                        V_SQL_CODE, V_SQL_ERRMSG, V_BACKTRACE
6340	                    );
6341	                END;
6342	
6343	                RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(-20001,
6344	                    '[' || $$PLSQL_UNIT || '] SQLCODE=' || V_SQL_CODE ||
6345	                    ' / SQLERRM=' || V_SQL_ERRMSG ||
6346	                    ' / BACKTRACE=' || DBMS_UTILITY.FORMAT_ERROR_BACKTRACE ||
6347	                    ' / PARAMS=' || V_PROC_INPUT
6348	                );
6349	                RAISE;
6350	        END;
6351	
6352	    END IF; -- END OF p_mode > 0
6353	
6354	    ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
6355	    --scm2_mgr.xLOG('SUCCESS              ', SQL%ROWCOUNT, 10, TRUE);
6356	    V_LOG_TYPE    := 'END';
6357	    V_LOG_MESSAGE := '########## INSERT DATA END ##########';
6358	    V_DESCRIPTION := TO_CHAR(P_MODE);
6359	    D_EVENT_DATE  := SYSDATE;
6360	    BEGIN SCM_MGR.PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH($$PLSQL_UNIT, V_LOG_TYPE, N_LOG_COUNT, V_LOG_MESSAGE, V_DESCRIPTION, D_EVENT_DATE); END;
6361	    ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
6362	
6363	    -- 결과 반환 분기
6364	    IF NVL(P_MODE, 0) = 0 THEN
6365	        -- 조회(Validation) 성공: EVENT는 NULL (Case 3 조회 성공 패턴)
6366	        OPEN O_RESULT_CURSOR FOR
6367	        SELECT '0' AS CODE, 'SUCCESS' AS MSG, CASE WHEN 1 = 2 THEN '-' ELSE NULL END AS EVENT FROM DUAL;
6368	    ELSE
6369	        -- DML 성공: EVENT에 처리 건수 반환
6370	        OPEN O_RESULT_CURSOR FOR
6371	        SELECT '0' AS CODE, 'SUCCESS' AS MSG, TO_CHAR(N_LOG_COUNT) AS EVENT FROM DUAL;
6372	    END IF;
6373	
6374	EXCEPTION
6375	    WHEN OTHERS THEN
6376	        ROLLBACK;
6377	
6378	        V_LOG_TYPE    := 'ERROR';
6379	        V_LOG_MESSAGE := 'EXCEPTION';
6380	        V_DESCRIPTION := '';
6381	        D_EVENT_DATE  := SYSDATE;
6382	        V_SQL_CODE    := TO_CHAR(SQLCODE);
6383	        V_SQL_ERRMSG  := SQLERRM;
6384	        V_PROC_INPUT  := JSON_OBJECT(
6385	            'P_MODE'       VALUE P_MODE,
6386	            'P_CREATED_BY' VALUE P_CREATED_BY
6387	        );
6388	        V_BACKTRACE   := DBMS_UTILITY.FORMAT_ERROR_BACKTRACE;
6389	
6390	        BEGIN
6391	            SCM_MGR.PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH(
6392	                $$PLSQL_UNIT, V_LOG_TYPE, N_LOG_COUNT, V_LOG_MESSAGE,
6393	                V_DESCRIPTION, D_EVENT_DATE, V_PROC_INPUT,
6394	                V_SQL_CODE, V_SQL_ERRMSG, V_BACKTRACE
6395	            );
6396	        END;
6397	
6398	        RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(-20001,
6399	            '[' || $$PLSQL_UNIT || '] SQLCODE=' || V_SQL_CODE ||
6400	            ' / SQLERRM=' || V_SQL_ERRMSG ||
6401	            ' / BACKTRACE=' || DBMS_UTILITY.FORMAT_ERROR_BACKTRACE ||
6402	            ' / PARAMS=' || V_PROC_INPUT
6403	        );
6404	        RAISE;
6405	END P_DMF0082_EXCEL_UPLOAD;

6407	
6408	PROCEDURE P_DMF0082_GET_EXCEL_UPLOAD_DATA
6409	(
6410	    p_created_by     IN  VARCHAR2
6411	  , O_RC1            OUT SYS_REFCURSOR   /* 최종 일자별 수량 (RECORD 형식) */
6412	  , O_RC2            OUT SYS_REFCURSOR   /* 최종 일자별 수량 (PIVOT 형식 — 동적 D 컬럼) */
6413	  , O_RESULT_CURSOR  OUT SYS_REFCURSOR   /* 표준 결과 커서 (RESULT_CODE/RESULT_MSG/EVENT) */
6414	)
6415	/*=====================================================================================================================================================
6416	 * Program ID  : DMF0082_GET_EXCEL_UPLOAD_DATA
6417	 * Description : Fcst&Price MGMT 화면에서 Excel Upload로 MKT FCST, LOC 메저를 월별로 업로드 한 후, Daily 로 풀어서 보여주는 프로시저
6418	 *                추후 Main 화면을 저장하는 프로시저 (DMF0082_SAVE_MAIN_DATA)를 호출하여 저장함.
6419	 * Usage       : Procedure 작성 시 참조하세요!
6420	 * Parameter   :
6421	 *=====================================================================================================================================================
6422	 * Date         In Charge    Description
6423	 *=====================================================================================================================================================
6424	 * 2025.05.xx   KJY          최초 작성 (MSSQL)
6425	 * 2026-07-12   Claude       MSSQL dbo.DMF0082_GET_EXCEL_UPLOAD_DATA → Oracle 19c 1:1 변환 (파일 산출물 — DB 미적용)
6426	 *=====================================================================================================================================================
6427	 * [변환 노트 — 부록 C 함정 처리]
6428	 *  - #temp 5종 → GTT 5종 (00_gtt_ddl.sql 신규: GT_DMF0082_FCST_PRICE_*). GTT 초기화 = DELETE FROM (C-2 TRUNCATE 금지 / C-5 INSERT 전 DELETE)
6429	 *  - MSSQL 마지막 SELECT 2개(RECORD/PIVOT) → O_RC1/O_RC2, 표준 O_RESULT_CURSOR 추가 (DMF0082_SAVE_MAIN_DATA Oracle 표준 준수)
6430	 *  - CONVERT(NVARCHAR(40), CREATION_DATE, 121) → TO_CHAR(CREATION_DATE,'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3') 명시 마스크 (C-11 NLS)
6431	 *  - FORMAT(DATEADD(MONTH,n,GETDATE()),'yyyyMM') → TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE,n),'YYYYMM') 명시 마스크 (C-11)
6432	 *  - UNPIVOT: MSSQL 은 ''(빈문자열) 행 유지 + IIF 로 0 처리 / Oracle 은 '' = NULL 이므로 UNPIVOT INCLUDE NULLS + NVL(QTY,'0') 로 동등화 (C-7)
6433	 *  - IIF(ISNULL(QTY,'')='','0',QTY) → NVL(A.QTY,'0') (C-7)
6434	 *  - CONVERT(NUMERIC(23,2), x) → CAST(TO_NUMBER(x) AS NUMBER(23,2)) — 변환 실패 시 MSSQL 과 동일하게 에러 발생(silent 무시 금지)
6435	 *  - EOMONTH → LAST_DAY / FORMAT(...,'dd') → TO_CHAR(...,'DD') 명시 마스크
6436	 *  - '' AS LAST_DAY_YN → NULL (C-7, Oracle 에서 '' = NULL)
6437	 *  - 동적 PIVOT IN 리스트 = CLOB + 커서 LOOP(FETCH 직후 EXIT WHEN %NOTFOUND — C-10) (hook #106 CLOB)
6438	 *  - SP_EXECUTESQL → OPEN O_RC2 FOR V_SQL(CLOB)
6439	 *  - MSSQL PRINT → DBMS_OUTPUT.PUT_LINE (SQL 원문 4000자 분할 PRINT 루프 포함 1:1 유지)
6440	 *  - WITH (NOLOCK) 제거 / [bracket] 제거 / dbo. 제거 / TOP 1 → FETCH FIRST 1 ROWS ONLY
6441	 *  - #FCST_PRICE_DAILY 의 BC 서브쿼리 DIVISION_ID = '40' 하드코딩은 AS-IS 원문 그대로 보존 (임의 수정 금지)
6442	 *===================================================================================================================================================*/
6443	IS
6444	    v_program_id     VARCHAR2(40 CHAR)  := 'DMF0082';
6445	    v_sheet_name     VARCHAR2(40 CHAR)  := 'FcstNPrice';
6446	    v_creation_date  VARCHAR2(40 CHAR);
6447	    v_start_yyyymm   VARCHAR2(6 CHAR)   := TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE, 4), 'YYYYMM');
6448	    v_end_yyyymm     VARCHAR2(6 CHAR)   := TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE, 16), 'YYYYMM');
6449	    v_division       VARCHAR2(10 CHAR);
6450	    v_defaultLocBucketDay VARCHAR2(10 CHAR);
6451	
6452	    /* 동적 PIVOT 변수 (hook #106 — 대량 문자열 누적 = CLOB) */
6453	    V_CONCAT_PIVOT   CLOB;              /* PIVOT IN 절: 'D20260101' AS D20260101, ... */
6454	    V_SQL            CLOB;
6455	    V_PIVOT_PIECE    VARCHAR2(100 CHAR);
6456	    V_PRINT_POS      PLS_INTEGER;
6457	    V_PRINT_LEN      PLS_INTEGER;
6458	
6459	    /* 표준 예외/로그 변수 */
6460	    V_PROC_INPUT     VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR);
6461	    V_SQL_CODE       VARCHAR2(20 CHAR);
6462	    V_SQL_ERRMSG     VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR);
6463	    V_BACKTRACE      VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR);
6464	
6465	    /* C_PIVOT : 조회구간 일자별 동적 PIVOT 컬럼 커서 */
6466	    CURSOR C_PIVOT IS
6467	        SELECT 'D' || A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD AS PV_KEY
6468	        FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M A
6469	        WHERE  1 = 1
6470	        AND    A.BASIS_YYYYMM BETWEEN v_start_yyyymm AND v_end_yyyymm
6471	        ORDER BY A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD;
6472	
6473	BEGIN
6474	        --[E20260812-DS2] GTT 무통계 클라우드 10배 지연 방어 표준
6475	        EXECUTE IMMEDIATE 'ALTER SESSION SET optimizer_dynamic_sampling = 2';
6476	
6477	
6478	    V_PROC_INPUT := JSON_OBJECT('rows' VALUE REGEXP_COUNT(p_created_by, '\^') + 1, 'p_created_by' VALUE SUBSTR(p_created_by, 1, 50));
6479	
6480	    SELECT TO_CHAR(MAX(T.CREATION_DATE), 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3')
6481	    INTO   v_creation_date
6482	    FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD T
6483	    WHERE  T.PROGRAM_ID = v_program_id
6484	    AND    T.SHEET_NAME = v_sheet_name
6485	    AND    T.CREATED_BY = p_created_by;
6486	
6487	    IF v_creation_date IS NULL THEN
6488	        DBMS_OUTPUT.PUT_LINE('NO EXCEL DATA');   /* AS-IS: PRINT 'NO EXCEL DATA' (실행은 계속) */
6489	    END IF;
6490	
6491	    ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
6492	    -- GTT 초기화 (AS-IS: IF OBJECT_ID ... DROP TABLE — Oracle: DELETE FROM, C-2/C-5)
6493	    ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
6494	    DELETE FROM SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_FCST_PRICE_EXCEL;
6495	    DELETE FROM SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_FCST_PRICE_UNPIVOT;
6496	    DELETE FROM SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_FCST_PRICE_BUCKET;
6497	    DELETE FROM SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_FCST_PRICE_WEEKLY;
6498	    DELETE FROM SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_FCST_PRICE_DAILY;
6499	
6500	    ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
6501	    --Excel Data
6502	    ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
6503	    INSERT INTO SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_FCST_PRICE_EXCEL
6504	    ( DIVISION_ID, END_CUSTOMER, SCM_SITE_ID, ITEM_ID, INCOTERMS
6505	    , FCST_TYPE, DOM_EXP, CELL_PROD_SITE, CURRENCY, MEASURE
6506	    , END_CUSTOMER_NAME, SCM_SITE_ID_NAME, FCST_TYPE_NAME, CELL_PROD_SITE_NAME, MEASURE_NAME
6507	    , M4, M5, M6, M7, M8, M9, M10, M11, M12, M13, M14, M15, M16)
6508	    SELECT A.ATTRIBUTE301 AS DIVISION_ID
6509	         , A.ATTRIBUTE302 AS END_CUSTOMER
6510	         , A.ATTRIBUTE303 AS SCM_SITE_ID
6511	         , A.ATTRIBUTE304 AS ITEM_ID
6512	         , A.ATTRIBUTE305 AS INCOTERMS
6513	         , A.ATTRIBUTE306 AS FCST_TYPE
6514	         , A.ATTRIBUTE307 AS DOM_EXP
6515	         , A.ATTRIBUTE308 AS CELL_PROD_SITE
6516	         , A.ATTRIBUTE309 AS CURRENCY
6517	         , A.ATTRIBUTE310 AS MEASURE
6518	         , A.ATTRIBUTE2   AS END_CUSTOMER_NAME
6519	         , A.ATTRIBUTE3   AS SCM_SITE_ID_NAME
6520	         , A.ATTRIBUTE6   AS FCST_TYPE_NAME
6521	         , A.ATTRIBUTE8   AS CELL_PROD_SITE_NAME
6522	         , A.ATTRIBUTE10  AS MEASURE_NAME
6523	         , A.ATTRIBUTE11  AS M4
6524	         , A.ATTRIBUTE12  AS M5
6525	         , A.ATTRIBUTE13  AS M6
6526	         , A.ATTRIBUTE14  AS M7
6527	         , A.ATTRIBUTE15  AS M8
6528	         , A.ATTRIBUTE16  AS M9
6529	         , A.ATTRIBUTE17  AS M10
6530	         , A.ATTRIBUTE18  AS M11
6531	         , A.ATTRIBUTE19  AS M12
6532	         , A.ATTRIBUTE20  AS M13
6533	         , A.ATTRIBUTE21  AS M14
6534	         , A.ATTRIBUTE22  AS M15
6535	         , A.ATTRIBUTE23  AS M16
6536	    FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_EXCEL_UPLOAD A
6537	    WHERE  1 = 1
6538	    AND    A.PROGRAM_ID = v_program_id
6539	    AND    A.SHEET_NAME = v_sheet_name
6540	    AND    TO_CHAR(A.CREATION_DATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS.FF3') = v_creation_date
6541	    AND    A.UPLOAD_STATUS_CODE = 'SUCCESS';
6542	
6543	    /* SELECT TOP 1 @v_division = DIVISION_ID FROM #FCST_PRICE_EXCEL */
6544	    BEGIN
6545	        SELECT DIVISION_ID
6546	        INTO   v_division
6547	        FROM   SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_FCST_PRICE_EXCEL
6548	        FETCH FIRST 1 ROWS ONLY;
6549	    EXCEPTION
6550	        WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN v_division := NULL;
6551	    END;
6552	
6553	    --LOC메저 기본 버킷 요일
6554	    v_defaultLocBucketDay := 'MON';
6555	
6556	    ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
6557	    --Unpivot
6558	    -- (C-7) MSSQL UNPIVOT 은 ''(빈문자열) 셀 행을 유지하고 IIF 로 0 처리.
6559	    --       Oracle 은 '' = NULL 이므로 INCLUDE NULLS + NVL(QTY,'0') 로 동일 결과 보장.
6560	    ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
6561	    INSERT INTO SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_FCST_PRICE_UNPIVOT
6562	    ( DIVISION_ID, END_CUSTOMER, SCM_SITE_ID, ITEM_ID, INCOTERMS
6563	    , FCST_TYPE, DOM_EXP, CELL_PROD_SITE, CURRENCY, MEASURE
6564	    , END_CUSTOMER_NAME, SCM_SITE_ID_NAME, FCST_TYPE_NAME, CELL_PROD_SITE_NAME, MEASURE_NAME
6565	    , KEY_VALUE, YYYYMM, QTY)
6566	    SELECT A.DIVISION_ID
6567	         , A.END_CUSTOMER
6568	         , A.SCM_SITE_ID
6569	         , A.ITEM_ID
6570	         , A.INCOTERMS
6571	         , A.FCST_TYPE
6572	         , A.DOM_EXP
6573	         , A.CELL_PROD_SITE
6574	         , A.CURRENCY
6575	         , A.MEASURE
6576	         , A.END_CUSTOMER_NAME
6577	         , A.SCM_SITE_ID_NAME
6578	         , A.FCST_TYPE_NAME
6579	         , A.CELL_PROD_SITE_NAME
6580	         , A.MEASURE_NAME
6581	         , A.KEY_VALUE
6582	         , CM.BASIS_YYYYMM AS YYYYMM
6583	         , A.QTY
6584	    FROM  (SELECT A.DIVISION_ID
6585	                , A.END_CUSTOMER
6586	                , A.SCM_SITE_ID
6587	                , A.ITEM_ID
6588	                , A.INCOTERMS
6589	                , A.FCST_TYPE
6590	                , A.DOM_EXP
6591	                , A.CELL_PROD_SITE
6592	                , A.CURRENCY
6593	                , A.MEASURE
6594	                , A.END_CUSTOMER_NAME
6595	                , A.SCM_SITE_ID_NAME
6596	                , A.FCST_TYPE_NAME
6597	                , A.CELL_PROD_SITE_NAME
6598	                , A.MEASURE_NAME
6599	                , A.DIVISION_ID || A.END_CUSTOMER || A.SCM_SITE_ID || A.ITEM_ID || A.INCOTERMS
6600	                  || A.FCST_TYPE || A.DOM_EXP || A.CELL_PROD_SITE || A.CURRENCY || A.MEASURE AS KEY_VALUE
6601	                , A.YYYYMM
6602	                /* CONVERT(NUMERIC(23,2), IIF(ISNULL(A.QTY,'')='', '0', A.QTY)) */
6603	                , CAST(TO_NUMBER(NVL(A.QTY, '0')) AS NUMBER(30,10)) AS QTY
6604	           /* Oracle UNPIVOT: 테이블과 UNPIVOT 사이 alias 불가 → alias 는 UNPIVOT 결과에 부여 */
6605	           FROM  SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_FCST_PRICE_EXCEL
6606	           UNPIVOT INCLUDE NULLS (QTY FOR YYYYMM IN
6607	                 ( M4  AS 'M4',  M5  AS 'M5',  M6  AS 'M6',  M7  AS 'M7'
6608	                 , M8  AS 'M8',  M9  AS 'M9',  M10 AS 'M10', M11 AS 'M11'
6609	                 , M12 AS 'M12', M13 AS 'M13', M14 AS 'M14', M15 AS 'M15', M16 AS 'M16')) A
6610	          ) A
6611	    JOIN  (SELECT DISTINCT A.BASIS_YYYYMM
6612	                , 'M' || TO_CHAR(DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY A.BASIS_YYYYMM) + 3) AS RN
6613	           FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M A
6614	           WHERE  1 = 1
6615	           AND    A.BASIS_YYYYMM BETWEEN v_start_yyyymm AND v_end_yyyymm) CM
6616	      ON  (CM.RN = A.YYYYMM);
6617	
6618	    ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
6619	    --[MKT_FCST]
6620	    --BUCKET 이 포함된 주차(=월별 수량을 나눠야할 대상 주차)
6621	    --SCM_SITE_ID/INCOTERMS별 BUCKET 이 정해진 대상우선 INSERT
6622	    ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
6623	    INSERT INTO SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_FCST_PRICE_BUCKET
6624	    ( DIVISION_ID, END_CUSTOMER, SCM_SITE_ID, ITEM_ID, INCOTERMS
6625	    , FCST_TYPE, DOM_EXP, CELL_PROD_SITE, CURRENCY, MEASURE
6626	    , KEY_VALUE, BASIS_YYYYMM, BASIS_YYYYMMDD, ISO_YYYYWW)
6627	    SELECT A.DIVISION_ID
6628	         , A.END_CUSTOMER
6629	         , A.SCM_SITE_ID
6630	         , A.ITEM_ID
6631	         , A.INCOTERMS
6632	         , A.FCST_TYPE
6633	         , A.DOM_EXP
6634	         , A.CELL_PROD_SITE
6635	         , A.CURRENCY
6636	         , A.MEASURE
6637	         , A.KEY_VALUE
6638	         , CM.BASIS_YYYYMM
6639	         , CM.BASIS_YYYYMMDD
6640	         , CM.ISO_YYYYWW
6641	    FROM   SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_FCST_PRICE_UNPIVOT A
6642	    JOIN  (SELECT A.BASIS_YYYYMM
6643	                , A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD
6644	                , A.ISO_YYYYWW
6645	                , A.DAYOFWEEK
6646	           FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M A
6647	           WHERE  1 = 1
6648	           AND    A.BASIS_YYYYMM BETWEEN v_start_yyyymm AND v_end_yyyymm
6649	          ) CM
6650	      ON  (A.YYYYMM = CM.BASIS_YYYYMM)
6651	    JOIN  (SELECT BC.DIVISION_ID
6652	                , BC.SCM_SITE_ID
6653	                , BC.INCOTERMS
6654	                , BC.BUCKET_PER
6655	                , BC.YYYYMMDD
6656	                , COUNT(BC.BUCKET_PER) OVER (PARTITION BY BC.DIVISION_ID, BC.SCM_SITE_ID, BC.INCOTERMS) AS BUCKET_CNT
6657	           FROM   SCM_MGR.DM_MST_BUCKET_CONVERSION BC
6658	           WHERE  1 = 1
6659	           AND    BC.DIVISION_ID = v_division) BC
6660	      ON  (BC.DIVISION_ID = A.DIVISION_ID
6661	     AND   BC.SCM_SITE_ID = A.SCM_SITE_ID
6662	     AND   BC.INCOTERMS   = A.INCOTERMS
6663	     AND   BC.YYYYMMDD    = CM.DAYOFWEEK
6664	     AND   BC.BUCKET_PER  = 'Y')
6665	    WHERE  1 = 1
6666	    AND    A.MEASURE = 'MKT_FCST'
6667	
6668	    UNION ALL
6669	
6670	    --DEFAULT BUCKET INSERT (SCM_SITE_ID/INCOTERMS별 BUCKET 이 없는 대상 기준)
6671	    SELECT A.DIVISION_ID
6672	         , A.END_CUSTOMER
6673	         , A.SCM_SITE_ID
6674	         , A.ITEM_ID
6675	         , A.INCOTERMS
6676	         , A.FCST_TYPE
6677	         , A.DOM_EXP
6678	         , A.CELL_PROD_SITE
6679	         , A.CURRENCY
6680	         , A.MEASURE
6681	         , A.KEY_VALUE
6682	         , CM.BASIS_YYYYMM
6683	         , CM.BASIS_YYYYMMDD
6684	         , CM.ISO_YYYYWW
6685	    FROM   SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_FCST_PRICE_UNPIVOT A
6686	    JOIN  (SELECT A.BASIS_YYYYMM
6687	                , A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD
6688	                , A.ISO_YYYYWW
6689	                , A.DAYOFWEEK
6690	           FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M A
6691	           WHERE  1 = 1
6692	           AND    A.BASIS_YYYYMM BETWEEN v_start_yyyymm AND v_end_yyyymm
6693	          ) CM
6694	      ON  (A.YYYYMM = CM.BASIS_YYYYMM)
6695	    JOIN  (SELECT L.SEGMENT3 AS DAYOFWEEK
6696	           FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL L
6697	           WHERE  1 = 1
6698	           AND    L.LOOKUP_TYPE = 'DEFAULT_BUCKET_DAY'
6699	           AND    L.VALID_YN    = 'Y'
6700	           AND    L.SEGMENT1    = v_division
6701	           AND    L.SEGMENT2    = 'MONTHLY') BC
6702	      ON  (BC.DAYOFWEEK = CM.DAYOFWEEK)
6703	    WHERE  1 = 1
6704	    AND    A.MEASURE = 'MKT_FCST'
6705	    AND    NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1
6706	                       FROM   SCM_MGR.DM_MST_BUCKET_CONVERSION X
6707	                       WHERE  1 = 1
6708	                       AND    X.DIVISION_ID = A.DIVISION_ID
6709	                       AND    X.SCM_SITE_ID = A.SCM_SITE_ID
6710	                       AND    X.INCOTERMS   = A.INCOTERMS
6711	                       AND    X.BUCKET_PER  = 'Y');
6712	
6713	    ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
6714	    --[MKT_FCST]
6715	    --WEEK 별 수량 나누기
6716	    ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
6717	    INSERT INTO SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_FCST_PRICE_WEEKLY
6718	    ( DIVISION_ID, END_CUSTOMER, SCM_SITE_ID, ITEM_ID, INCOTERMS
6719	    , FCST_TYPE, DOM_EXP, CELL_PROD_SITE, CURRENCY, MEASURE
6720	    , KEY_VALUE, YYYYMM, ISO_YYYYWW, WEEK_DAY_CNT, MONTHLY_QTY, WEEKLY_QTY)
6721	    WITH W_WEEKLY AS (
6722	        SELECT A.DIVISION_ID
6723	             , A.END_CUSTOMER
6724	             , A.SCM_SITE_ID
6725	             , A.ITEM_ID
6726	             , A.INCOTERMS
6727	             , A.FCST_TYPE
6728	             , A.DOM_EXP
6729	             , A.CELL_PROD_SITE
6730	             , A.CURRENCY
6731	             , A.MEASURE
6732	             , A.KEY_VALUE
6733	             , A.YYYYMM
6734	             , CM.ISO_YYYYWW
6735	             , CM.WEEK_DAY_CNT
6736	             , A.QTY AS MONTHLY_QTY
6737	             , FLOOR(A.QTY / CM.EOM * CM.WEEK_DAY_CNT) AS TEMP_WEEKLY_QTY
6738	             , SUM(FLOOR(A.QTY / CM.EOM * CM.WEEK_DAY_CNT))
6739	                   OVER (PARTITION BY A.KEY_VALUE, A.YYYYMM
6740	                         ORDER BY A.KEY_VALUE, A.YYYYMM, CM.ISO_YYYYWW
6741	                         ROWS BETWEEN UNBOUNDED PRECEDING AND 1 PRECEDING) AS CUM_SUM
6742	             , CASE WHEN LEAD(A.YYYYMM, 1)
6743	                        OVER (PARTITION BY A.KEY_VALUE, A.YYYYMM
6744	                              ORDER BY A.KEY_VALUE, A.YYYYMM, CM.ISO_YYYYWW) IS NULL
6745	                    THEN 'Y' ELSE 'N' END AS LAST_WEEK_YN
6746	        FROM   SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_FCST_PRICE_UNPIVOT A
6747	        JOIN  (SELECT A.BASIS_YYYYMM
6748	                    , A.ISO_YYYYWW
6749	                    , COUNT(1) AS WEEK_DAY_CNT
6750	                    /* CONVERT(INT, FORMAT(EOMONTH(MAX(A.BASIS_DATE)),'dd')) → 명시 마스크 (C-11) */
6751	                    , TO_NUMBER(TO_CHAR(LAST_DAY(MAX(A.BASIS_DATE)), 'DD')) AS EOM
6752	               FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M A
6753	               WHERE  1 = 1
6754	               AND    A.BASIS_YYYYMM BETWEEN v_start_yyyymm AND v_end_yyyymm
6755	               GROUP BY A.BASIS_YYYYMM
6756	                      , A.ISO_YYYYWW
6757	              ) CM
6758	          ON  (A.YYYYMM = CM.BASIS_YYYYMM)
6759	        JOIN  (SELECT DISTINCT PB.KEY_VALUE
6760	                    , PB.BASIS_YYYYMM
6761	                    , PB.ISO_YYYYWW
6762	               FROM   SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_FCST_PRICE_BUCKET PB) PB --버켓 요일이 있는 주차만 대상으로 함.
6763	          ON  (A.KEY_VALUE  = PB.KEY_VALUE
6764	         AND   A.YYYYMM     = PB.BASIS_YYYYMM
6765	         AND   CM.ISO_YYYYWW = PB.ISO_YYYYWW)
6766	        WHERE  1 = 1
6767	        AND    A.MEASURE = 'MKT_FCST'
6768	    )
6769	    SELECT A.DIVISION_ID
6770	         , A.END_CUSTOMER
6771	         , A.SCM_SITE_ID
6772	         , A.ITEM_ID
6773	         , A.INCOTERMS
6774	         , A.FCST_TYPE
6775	         , A.DOM_EXP
6776	         , A.CELL_PROD_SITE
6777	         , A.CURRENCY
6778	         , A.MEASURE
6779	         , A.KEY_VALUE
6780	         , A.YYYYMM
6781	         , A.ISO_YYYYWW
6782	         , A.WEEK_DAY_CNT
6783	         , A.MONTHLY_QTY
6784	         , CASE WHEN A.LAST_WEEK_YN = 'Y' THEN A.MONTHLY_QTY - A.CUM_SUM
6785	                ELSE A.TEMP_WEEKLY_QTY END AS WEEKLY_QTY
6786	    FROM   W_WEEKLY A
6787	    WHERE  1 = 1
6788	    AND    A.MEASURE = 'MKT_FCST'
6789	    ORDER BY A.KEY_VALUE, A.YYYYMM;
6790	
6791	    ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
6792	    --[MKT_FCST]
6793	    --DAILY 별 수량 나누기
6794	    ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
6795	    INSERT INTO SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_FCST_PRICE_DAILY
6796	    ( DIVISION_ID, END_CUSTOMER, SCM_SITE_ID, ITEM_ID, INCOTERMS
6797	    , FCST_TYPE, DOM_EXP, CELL_PROD_SITE, CURRENCY, MEASURE
6798	    , KEY_VALUE, YYYYMM, BASIS_YYYYMMDD, ISO_YYYYWW
6799	    , BUCKET_CNT, MONTHLY_QTY, WEEKLY_QTY, LAST_DAY_YN, DAILY_QTY)
6800	    WITH W_DAILY AS (
6801	        --SCM_SITE_ID별 BUCKET
6802	        SELECT A.DIVISION_ID
6803	             , A.END_CUSTOMER
6804	             , A.SCM_SITE_ID
6805	             , A.ITEM_ID
6806	             , A.INCOTERMS
6807	             , A.FCST_TYPE
6808	             , A.DOM_EXP
6809	             , A.CELL_PROD_SITE
6810	             , A.CURRENCY
6811	             , A.MEASURE
6812	             , A.KEY_VALUE
6813	             , A.YYYYMM
6814	             , CM.BASIS_YYYYMMDD
6815	             , CM.ISO_YYYYWW
6816	             , BC.BUCKET_CNT
6817	             , A.MONTHLY_QTY
6818	             , A.WEEKLY_QTY
6819	             , FLOOR(A.WEEKLY_QTY / BC.BUCKET_CNT) AS TEMP_DAILY_QTY
6820	             , SUM(FLOOR(A.WEEKLY_QTY / BC.BUCKET_CNT))
6821	                   OVER (PARTITION BY A.KEY_VALUE, A.YYYYMM, A.ISO_YYYYWW
6822	                         ORDER BY A.KEY_VALUE, A.YYYYMM, CM.BASIS_YYYYMMDD
6823	                         ROWS BETWEEN UNBOUNDED PRECEDING AND 1 PRECEDING) AS CUM_SUM
6824	             , CASE WHEN LEAD(A.ISO_YYYYWW, 1)
6825	                        OVER (PARTITION BY A.KEY_VALUE, A.YYYYMM, A.ISO_YYYYWW
6826	                              ORDER BY A.KEY_VALUE, A.YYYYMM, CM.BASIS_YYYYMMDD) IS NULL
6827	                    THEN 'Y' ELSE 'N' END AS LAST_DAY_YN
6828	        FROM   SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_FCST_PRICE_WEEKLY A
6829	        JOIN  (SELECT A.BASIS_YYYYMM
6830	                    , A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD
6831	                    , A.ISO_YYYYWW
6832	                    , A.DAYOFWEEK
6833	               FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M A
6834	               WHERE  1 = 1
6835	               AND    A.BASIS_YYYYMM BETWEEN v_start_yyyymm AND v_end_yyyymm
6836	              ) CM
6837	          ON  (A.YYYYMM     = CM.BASIS_YYYYMM
6838	         AND   A.ISO_YYYYWW = CM.ISO_YYYYWW)
6839	        JOIN  (SELECT BC.DIVISION_ID
6840	                    , BC.SCM_SITE_ID
6841	                    , BC.INCOTERMS
6842	                    , BC.BUCKET_PER
6843	                    , BC.YYYYMMDD
6844	                    , COUNT(BC.BUCKET_PER) OVER (PARTITION BY BC.DIVISION_ID, BC.SCM_SITE_ID, BC.INCOTERMS) AS BUCKET_CNT
6845	               FROM   SCM_MGR.DM_MST_BUCKET_CONVERSION BC
6846	               WHERE  1 = 1
6847	               AND    BC.DIVISION_ID = v_division) BC   /* [E20260819-XLS81LOC] 구 '40' 하드코딩 → v_division (형제 스테이지 동형 — 81 BUCKET_CONVERSION 491행 실존: '40' 유지 시 81 커스텀 버킷 행이 BC/DEFAULT 양 분기 모두 탈락) */
6848	          ON  (BC.DIVISION_ID = A.DIVISION_ID
6849	         AND   BC.SCM_SITE_ID = A.SCM_SITE_ID
6850	         AND   BC.INCOTERMS   = A.INCOTERMS
6851	         AND   BC.YYYYMMDD    = CM.DAYOFWEEK
6852	         AND   BC.BUCKET_PER  = 'Y')
6853	
6854	        UNION ALL
6855	
6856	        --DEFAULT BUCKET
6857	        SELECT A.DIVISION_ID
6858	             , A.END_CUSTOMER
6859	             , A.SCM_SITE_ID
6860	             , A.ITEM_ID
6861	             , A.INCOTERMS
6862	             , A.FCST_TYPE
6863	             , A.DOM_EXP
6864	             , A.CELL_PROD_SITE
6865	             , A.CURRENCY
6866	             , A.MEASURE
6867	             , A.KEY_VALUE
6868	             , A.YYYYMM
6869	             , CM.BASIS_YYYYMMDD
6870	             , CM.ISO_YYYYWW
6871	             , 1 AS BUCKET_CNT
6872	             , A.MONTHLY_QTY
6873	             , A.WEEKLY_QTY
6874	             , FLOOR(A.WEEKLY_QTY) AS TEMP_DAILY_QTY
6875	             , SUM(FLOOR(A.WEEKLY_QTY))
6876	                   OVER (PARTITION BY A.KEY_VALUE, A.YYYYMM, A.ISO_YYYYWW
6877	                         ORDER BY A.KEY_VALUE, A.YYYYMM, CM.BASIS_YYYYMMDD
6878	                         ROWS BETWEEN UNBOUNDED PRECEDING AND 1 PRECEDING) AS CUM_SUM
6879	             , CASE WHEN LEAD(A.ISO_YYYYWW, 1)
6880	                        OVER (PARTITION BY A.KEY_VALUE, A.YYYYMM, A.ISO_YYYYWW
6881	                              ORDER BY A.KEY_VALUE, A.YYYYMM, CM.BASIS_YYYYMMDD) IS NULL
6882	                    THEN 'Y' ELSE 'N' END AS LAST_DAY_YN
6883	        FROM   SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_FCST_PRICE_WEEKLY A
6884	        JOIN  (SELECT A.BASIS_YYYYMM
6885	                    , A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD
6886	                    , A.ISO_YYYYWW
6887	                    , A.DAYOFWEEK
6888	               FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M A
6889	               WHERE  1 = 1
6890	               AND    A.BASIS_YYYYMM BETWEEN v_start_yyyymm AND v_end_yyyymm
6891	              ) CM
6892	          ON  (A.YYYYMM     = CM.BASIS_YYYYMM
6893	         AND   A.ISO_YYYYWW = CM.ISO_YYYYWW)
6894	        JOIN  (SELECT L.SEGMENT3 AS DAYOFWEEK
6895	               FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL L
6896	               WHERE  1 = 1
6897	               AND    L.LOOKUP_TYPE = 'DEFAULT_BUCKET_DAY'
6898	               AND    L.VALID_YN    = 'Y'
6899	               AND    L.SEGMENT1    = v_division
6900	               AND    L.SEGMENT2    = 'MONTHLY') BC
6901	          ON  (BC.DAYOFWEEK = CM.DAYOFWEEK)
6902	        WHERE  1 = 1
6903	        AND    A.MEASURE = 'MKT_FCST'
6904	        AND    NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1
6905	                           FROM   SCM_MGR.DM_MST_BUCKET_CONVERSION X
6906	                           WHERE  1 = 1
6907	                           AND    X.DIVISION_ID = A.DIVISION_ID
6908	                           AND    X.SCM_SITE_ID = A.SCM_SITE_ID
6909	                           AND    X.INCOTERMS   = A.INCOTERMS
6910	                           AND    X.BUCKET_PER  = 'Y')
6911	    )
6912	    SELECT A.DIVISION_ID
6913	         , A.END_CUSTOMER
6914	         , A.SCM_SITE_ID
6915	         , A.ITEM_ID
6916	         , A.INCOTERMS
6917	         , A.FCST_TYPE
6918	         , A.DOM_EXP
6919	         , A.CELL_PROD_SITE
6920	         , A.CURRENCY
6921	         , A.MEASURE
6922	         , A.KEY_VALUE
6923	         , A.YYYYMM
6924	         , A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD
6925	         , A.ISO_YYYYWW
6926	         , A.BUCKET_CNT
6927	         , A.MONTHLY_QTY
6928	         , A.WEEKLY_QTY
6929	         , A.LAST_DAY_YN
6930	         , CASE WHEN A.LAST_DAY_YN = 'Y' THEN A.WEEKLY_QTY - NVL(A.CUM_SUM, 0)
6931	                ELSE A.TEMP_DAILY_QTY END AS DAILY_QTY
6932	    FROM   W_DAILY A
6933	    WHERE  1 = 1
6934	    ORDER BY A.KEY_VALUE, A.YYYYMM;
6935	
6936	    ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
6937	    --[LOC]
6938	    --DAILY 별 PRICE생성
6939	    ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
6940	    INSERT INTO SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_FCST_PRICE_DAILY
6941	    ( DIVISION_ID
6942	    , END_CUSTOMER
6943	    , SCM_SITE_ID
6944	    , ITEM_ID
6945	    , INCOTERMS
6946	    , FCST_TYPE
6947	    , DOM_EXP
6948	    , CELL_PROD_SITE
6949	    , CURRENCY
6950	    , MEASURE
6951	    , KEY_VALUE
6952	    , YYYYMM
6953	    , BASIS_YYYYMMDD
6954	    , ISO_YYYYWW
6955	    , BUCKET_CNT
6956	    , MONTHLY_QTY
6957	    , WEEKLY_QTY
6958	    , LAST_DAY_YN
6959	    , DAILY_QTY
6960	    )
6961	    SELECT A.DIVISION_ID
6962	         , A.END_CUSTOMER
6963	         , A.SCM_SITE_ID
6964	         , A.ITEM_ID
6965	         , A.INCOTERMS
6966	         , A.FCST_TYPE
6967	         , A.DOM_EXP
6968	         , A.CELL_PROD_SITE
6969	         , A.CURRENCY
6970	         , A.MEASURE
6971	         , A.KEY_VALUE
6972	         , CM.BASIS_YYYYMM AS YYYYMM
6973	         , CM.BASIS_YYYYMMDD
6974	         , CM.ISO_YYYYWW
6975	         , NULL AS BUCKET_CNT
6976	         , NULL AS MONTHLY_QTY
6977	         , NULL AS WEEKLY_QTY
6978	         , NULL AS LAST_DAY_YN     /* AS-IS: '' AS LAST_DAY_YN → Oracle '' = NULL (C-7) */
6979	         , CAST(A.QTY AS NUMBER(30,10)) AS DAILY_QTY
6980	    FROM   SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_FCST_PRICE_UNPIVOT A
6981	    JOIN  (SELECT A.BASIS_YYYYMM
6982	                , A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD
6983	                , A.ISO_YYYYWW
6984	                , A.DAYOFWEEK
6985	           FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M A
6986	           WHERE  1 = 1
6987	           AND    A.BASIS_YYYYMM BETWEEN v_start_yyyymm AND v_end_yyyymm
6988	           AND    (A.DAYOFWEEK = v_defaultLocBucketDay OR A.CALENDAR_DAY = '01')
6989	          ) CM
6990	      ON  (A.YYYYMM = CM.BASIS_YYYYMM)
6991	    WHERE  1 = 1
6992	    AND    A.MEASURE = 'LOC';
6993	
6994	    ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
6995	    --최종 일자별 수량 Select (RECORD 형식)---------------------------
6996	    ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
6997	    OPEN O_RC1 FOR
6998	        --MKT_FCST
6999	        SELECT A.DIVISION_ID
7000	             , A.END_CUSTOMER
7001	             , A.SCM_SITE_ID
7002	             , A.ITEM_ID
7003	             , A.INCOTERMS
7004	             , A.FCST_TYPE
7005	             , A.DOM_EXP
7006	             , A.CELL_PROD_SITE
7007	             , A.CURRENCY
7008	             , A.MEASURE
7009	             , A.KEY_VALUE
7010	             , A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD
7011	             , CAST(A.DAILY_QTY AS NUMBER(30,10)) AS DAILY_QTY
7012	        FROM   SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_FCST_PRICE_DAILY A
7013	        UNION ALL
7014	        --LOC
7015	        SELECT A.DIVISION_ID
7016	             , A.END_CUSTOMER
7017	             , A.SCM_SITE_ID
7018	             , A.ITEM_ID
7019	             , A.INCOTERMS
7020	             , A.FCST_TYPE
7021	             , A.DOM_EXP
7022	             , A.CELL_PROD_SITE
7023	             , A.CURRENCY
7024	             , A.MEASURE
7025	             , A.KEY_VALUE
7026	             , CM.BASIS_YYYYMMDD
7027	             , CAST(A.QTY AS NUMBER(30,10)) AS DAILY_QTY
7028	        FROM   SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_FCST_PRICE_UNPIVOT A
7029	        JOIN  (SELECT A.BASIS_YYYYMM
7030	                    , A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD
7031	                    , A.ISO_YYYYWW
7032	                    , A.DAYOFWEEK
7033	               FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M A
7034	               WHERE  1 = 1
7035	               AND    A.BASIS_YYYYMM BETWEEN v_start_yyyymm AND v_end_yyyymm
7036	               AND    (A.DAYOFWEEK = v_defaultLocBucketDay OR A.CALENDAR_DAY = '01')
7037	              ) CM
7038	          ON  (A.YYYYMM = CM.BASIS_YYYYMM)
7039	        WHERE  1 = 1
7040	        AND    A.MEASURE = 'LOC'
7041	        ORDER BY KEY_VALUE, BASIS_YYYYMMDD;
7042	    --최종 일자별 Select (RECORD 형식) END ---------------------------
7043	
7044	    ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
7045	    --최종 일자별 Select (PIVOT 형식) START ---------------------------
7046	    -- 동적 PIVOT IN 리스트 구성 (커서 LOOP — FETCH 직후 EXIT WHEN %NOTFOUND, C-10)
7047	    ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
7048	    DBMS_LOB.CREATETEMPORARY(V_CONCAT_PIVOT, TRUE);
7049	
7050	    OPEN C_PIVOT;
7051	    LOOP
7052	        FETCH C_PIVOT INTO V_PIVOT_PIECE;
7053	        EXIT WHEN C_PIVOT%NOTFOUND;
7054	        IF DBMS_LOB.GETLENGTH(V_CONCAT_PIVOT) = 0 THEN
7055	            V_CONCAT_PIVOT := '''' || V_PIVOT_PIECE || ''' AS ' || V_PIVOT_PIECE;
7056	        ELSE
7057	            V_CONCAT_PIVOT := V_CONCAT_PIVOT || ',''' || V_PIVOT_PIECE || ''' AS ' || V_PIVOT_PIECE;
7058	        END IF;
7059	    END LOOP;
7060	    CLOSE C_PIVOT;
7061	
7062	    /* [DEFECT_GUARD] 캘린더 미적재 등으로 PIVOT IN 리스트가 비면 ORA-00936 — 빈 커서 반환 */
7063	    IF DBMS_LOB.GETLENGTH(V_CONCAT_PIVOT) = 0 THEN
7064	        BEGIN DBMS_LOB.FREETEMPORARY(V_CONCAT_PIVOT); EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN NULL; END;
7065	        OPEN O_RC2 FOR SELECT NULL AS COL1 FROM DUAL WHERE 1 = 0;
7066	        OPEN O_RESULT_CURSOR FOR
7067	            SELECT '1' AS RESULT_CODE, 'NO_CALENDAR_DATA' AS RESULT_MSG, '0' AS EVENT FROM DUAL;
7068	        RETURN;
7069	    END IF;
7070	
7071	    DBMS_LOB.CREATETEMPORARY(V_SQL, TRUE);
7072	
7073	    /* Oracle PIVOT: 서브쿼리와 PIVOT 사이 alias 불가 / PIVOT 집계 안 alias 참조 불가 → 최종 alias A 로 이동 */
7074	    V_SQL := '
7075	    SELECT A.*
7076	      FROM (
7077	    SELECT A.DIVISION_ID
7078	         , A.END_CUSTOMER
7079	         , B.END_CUSTOMER_NAME
7080	         , A.SCM_SITE_ID
7081	         , A.ITEM_ID
7082	         , A.INCOTERMS
7083	         , A.FCST_TYPE
7084	         , B.FCST_TYPE_NAME
7085	         , A.DOM_EXP
7086	         , A.CELL_PROD_SITE
7087	         , B.CELL_PROD_SITE_NAME
7088	         , A.CURRENCY
7089	         , A.MEASURE
7090	         , B.MEASURE_NAME
7091	         , ''D'' || A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD AS BASIS_YYYYMMDD
7092	         , A.DAILY_QTY
7093	      FROM SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_FCST_PRICE_DAILY A
7094	      JOIN SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_FCST_PRICE_UNPIVOT B
7095	        ON (A.KEY_VALUE = B.KEY_VALUE))
7096	      PIVOT (MAX(DAILY_QTY) FOR BASIS_YYYYMMDD IN (' || V_CONCAT_PIVOT || '
7097	    )) A';
7098	
7099	    OPEN O_RC2 FOR V_SQL;
7100	
7101	    /* AS-IS: WHILE LEN(@V_SQL) > 0 ... PRINT LEFT(@V_SQL, 4000) — 4,000자씩 출력 (디버그 출력 1:1 유지) */
7102	    V_PRINT_POS := 1;
7103	    V_PRINT_LEN := NVL(DBMS_LOB.GETLENGTH(V_SQL), 0);
7104	    WHILE V_PRINT_POS <= V_PRINT_LEN
7105	    LOOP
7106	        DBMS_OUTPUT.PUT_LINE(DBMS_LOB.SUBSTR(V_SQL, 4000, V_PRINT_POS));
7107	        V_PRINT_POS := V_PRINT_POS + 4000;
7108	    END LOOP;
7109	
7110	    BEGIN DBMS_LOB.FREETEMPORARY(V_SQL);          EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN NULL; END;
7111	    BEGIN DBMS_LOB.FREETEMPORARY(V_CONCAT_PIVOT); EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN NULL; END;
7112	    --최종 일자별 Select (PIVOT 형식) END ---------------------------
7113	
7114	    OPEN O_RESULT_CURSOR FOR
7115	        SELECT '0' AS RESULT_CODE, 'SUCCESS' AS RESULT_MSG, '0' AS EVENT FROM DUAL;
7116	
7117	EXCEPTION
7118	    WHEN OTHERS THEN
7119	        ROLLBACK;
7120	        BEGIN DBMS_LOB.FREETEMPORARY(V_SQL);          EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN NULL; END;
7121	        BEGIN DBMS_LOB.FREETEMPORARY(V_CONCAT_PIVOT); EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN NULL; END;
7122	        V_SQL_CODE   := TO_CHAR(SQLCODE);
7123	        V_SQL_ERRMSG := SUBSTR(SQLERRM, 1, 4000);
7124	        V_BACKTRACE  := SUBSTR(DBMS_UTILITY.FORMAT_ERROR_BACKTRACE, 1, 4000);
7125	        BEGIN
7126	            SCM_MGR.PR_DM_SYS_LOG_BATCH(
7127	                $$PLSQL_UNIT, 'ERROR', 0
7128	              , '########## FAIL END of procedure ##########', ''
7129	              , SYSDATE, p_created_by, V_PROC_INPUT, V_SQL_CODE, V_SQL_ERRMSG, V_BACKTRACE);
7130	        EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN NULL; END;
7131	        RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(-20001, '[' || $$PLSQL_UNIT || '] ' || V_SQL_ERRMSG || CHR(10) || V_BACKTRACE);
7132	
7133	END P_DMF0082_GET_EXCEL_UPLOAD_DATA;

7136	PROCEDURE P_DMF0082_GET_MAIN_DATA_CURRENT
7137	/* [DIV81] 2026-07-14 admin: Division 81(LIKE '81%') 시 GTT 스테이징 INSERT 만 81 테이블(DM_PLN_MKT_FCST(_C)/SCM_MST_FUTURE_PRICE(_C))로 분기. END_CUSTOMER/CELL_PROD_SITE_ID 는 '-' 리터럴 (컬럼 부재 — ALL_TAB_COLUMNS 실측). 하류(pivot/커서) 공통. 40 원문 무수정. */
7138	(
7139	    p_division_id    IN  VARCHAR2
7140	  , p_team_id        IN  VARCHAR2
7141	  , p_customer       IN  VARCHAR2
7142	  , p_biz_type       IN  VARCHAR2
7143	  , p_from_yyyymmdd  IN  VARCHAR2
7144	  , p_to_yyyymmdd    IN  VARCHAR2
7145	  , p_scm_site_id    IN  VARCHAR2
7146	  , p_item_id        IN  VARCHAR2
7147	  , p_version_type   IN  VARCHAR2
7148	  , p_qty_yn         IN  VARCHAR2
7149	  , p_created_by     IN  VARCHAR2
7150	  , p_user_group     IN  VARCHAR2
7151	  , p_plant_site_id  IN  VARCHAR2 DEFAULT 'ALL'  /* [PLANT_SITE 2026-08-04] 조회조건 Plant Site (81 전용 — FE 40/82 비활성 'ALL') */
7152	  , p_project_code   IN  VARCHAR2 DEFAULT 'ALL'  /* [UICATEGORY 2026-08-07] 조회조건 Project (다중 콤마 토큰) */
7153	  , p_owner          IN  VARCHAR2 DEFAULT 'ALL'  /* [UICATEGORY 2026-08-07] 조회조건 Owner (다중 콤마 토큰) */
7154	  , p_sender_code    IN  VARCHAR2 DEFAULT 'ALL'  /* [UICATEGORY 2026-08-07 → E20260819-SENDERJOIN] 조회조건 Sender Code — 2026-08-19 사용자 재확정: 실적용(무값 행 제외) */
7155	  , O_RC1            OUT SYS_REFCURSOR   /* 1) 동적 PIVOT 메인 데이터 */
7156	  , O_RC2            OUT SYS_REFCURSOR   /* 2) M+3 월 MONTH_NAME/ISO_WEEK (Monthly 입력제한 기준) */
7157	  , O_RC3            OUT SYS_REFCURSOR   /* 3) 현재/전주 주차 정보 */
7158	  , O_RC4            OUT SYS_REFCURSOR   /* 4) 메저별 EDIT_FLAG (GET_ADMIN_GROUP_MEASURE) */
7159	  , O_RC5            OUT SYS_REFCURSOR   /* 5) 조회구간 캘린더 분배 정보 (M+4 월간 물량 배분용) */
7160	  , O_RESULT_CURSOR  OUT SYS_REFCURSOR   /* 표준 결과 커서 (RESULT_CODE/RESULT_MSG/EVENT) */
7161	)
7162	/*=====================================================================================================================================================
7163	 * Program ID  : DMF0082_GET_MAIN_DATA_CURRENT
7164	 * Description : Procedure 작성 가이드
7165	 * Usage       : Procedure 작성 시 참조하세요!
7166	 * Parameter   :
7167	 *=====================================================================================================================================================
7168	 * Date             In Charge    Description
7169	 *=====================================================================================================================================================
7170	 * 2026.01.09       park.choyi   Initial Release (MSSQL)
7171	 * 2026-07-12       Claude       MSSQL dbo.DMF0082_GET_MAIN_DATA_CURRENT → Oracle 19c 1:1 변환 (파일 산출물 — DB 미적용)
7172	 *
7173	 * EXEC DMF0082_GET_MAIN_DATA_CURRENT '40','ALL','ALL','ALL','20260101','20260731','ALL','','Current','Y','park.choyi','ADMIN'
7174	 *=====================================================================================================================================================
7175	 * [변환 노트 — 부록 C 함정 처리 / GTT 재사용]
7176	 *  - #temp 13종 → 기존 GTT_* family 13종 전량 재사용 (컬럼 1:1 실측 일치 — 00_gtt_ddl.sql 헤더 참조. 신규 GTT 불필요):
7177	 *      #TEMP_DM_MST_ITEM_SINGLE_V → GTT_TEMP_DM_MST_ITEM_SINGLE_V / #TempSiteItemAll → GTT_TEMPSITEITEMALL / #TempSiteItem → GTT_TEMPSITEITEM
7178	 *      #TempSelectMeasure → GTT_TEMPSELECTMEASURE / #T_LAST_DATA_SETUP_FCST → GTT_T_LAST_DATA_SETUP_FCST / #T_LAST_DATA_SETUP_PRICE → GTT_T_LAST_DATA_SETUP_PRICE
7179	 *      #T_WS_FCST → GTT_T_WS_FCST / #TempFcstNPrice → GTT_TEMPFCSTNPRICE / #TEMP_INTO_GROUP_MEASURE → GTT_TEMP_INTO_GROUP_MEASURE
7180	 *      #TEMP_ADMIN_GROUP_MEASURE → GTT_TEMP_ADMIN_GROUP_MEASURE / #CAL_MONTH_END_DATE → GTT_CAL_MONTH_END_DATE / #CAL_WEEK_END_DATE → GTT_CAL_WEEK_END_DATE
7181	 *      #MF_SP → GTT_MF_SP
7182	 *  - GTT 초기화 = DELETE FROM (C-2: TRUNCATE 금지 — mutex hang / C-5: INSERT 전 DELETE 의무)
7183	 *  - @@FETCH_STATUS WHILE 루프 2개(C_BUCKET/C_TITLE) → LOOP + FETCH 직후 EXIT WHEN %NOTFOUND (C-10 — 무한루프 방지)
7184	 *  - 동적 SQL/PIVOT 리스트 누적 = CLOB (hook #106) + [@...] placeholder REPLACE 방식은 AS-IS 그대로 유지.
7185	 *    단, AS-IS 는 '[@v_PV_COL_LIST]' 를 @v_PV_LIST 로 치환(MSSQL 에선 두 형태 동일 문자열이라 무해) —
7186	 *    Oracle 은 PIVOT IN 리스트('D_x' AS D_x)와 SELECT 컬럼 리스트(D_x)가 다르므로 각각 올바르게 치환 (원문 버그 교정, 결과 동일)
7187	 *  - MSSQL PIVOT ([D_x],...) → Oracle PIVOT ('D_x' AS D_x, ...) / 서브쿼리·PIVOT 사이 alias 불가 → alias 는 PIVOT 뒤로 이동
7188	 *  - OPTION (ORDER GROUP) MSSQL 힌트 제거
7189	 *  - SUBSTRING(F.OWNER, 0, CHARINDEX...) 첫 토큰 추출 → CASE WHEN INSTR=0 THEN OWNER ELSE SUBSTR(OWNER,1,INSTR-1) END (C-1 SUBSTR-0 함정 회피)
7190	 *  - STUFF(...FOR XML PATH(''))(OWNER 이름 결합) → LISTAGG / STRING_SPLIT → REGEXP_SUBSTR CONNECT BY (룰 4/5)
7191	 *  - CASE WHEN @P = 'ALL' THEN @P ELSE col END IN (SPLIT(@P)) → (@P = 'ALL' OR col IN (SPLIT(@P))) 동치 변환
7192	 *  - ISNULL(x,'')='' / <>'' → IS NULL / IS NOT NULL, ISNULL(x,'') 표시값 → x (Oracle '' = NULL, C-7)
7193	 *  - '' AS CURRENCY / '' AS LAST_DAY_YN 류 → NULL (C-7)
7194	 *  - ISNULL(T2.FORECAST_INFO_CELL_MODEL,'') = T4.ITEM_ID → T2.FORECAST_INFO_CELL_MODEL = T4.ITEM_ID (''=NULL 은 어떤 값과도 불일치 — 결과 동일)
7195	 *  - LIKE '%CURRENT%' (MSSQL 대소문자 무시 collation) → UPPER(p_version_type) LIKE '%CURRENT%'
7196	 *  - DATEPART(WEEKDAY,GETDATE()) 1~7 → 요일 3문자 매핑 → TO_CHAR(SYSDATE,'DY','NLS_DATE_LANGUAGE=ENGLISH') (C-11: 세션 NLS 비의존)
7197	 *  - CONVERT(CHAR(8),GETDATE(),24)/112, FORMAT(...,'yyyyMM'), DATEADD → TO_CHAR/ADD_MONTHS 명시 마스크 (C-11)
7198	 *  - DATEADD(YY,-1,@p_from_yyyymmdd)(문자열 암묵 DATE 변환) → ADD_MONTHS(TO_DATE(p_from_yyyymmdd,'YYYYMMDD'),-12) 명시 (C-11)
7199	 *  - DATEADD(WEEK,50,@p_from_yyyymmdd) → TO_DATE(p_from_yyyymmdd,'YYYYMMDD') + 50*7 명시 (C-11)
7200	 *  - OUTER APPLY (SELECT TOP(1) ... ORDER BY) → LEFT JOIN LATERAL (... ORDER BY ... FETCH FIRST 1 ROWS ONLY) ON 1=1
7201	 *  - UPDATE ... FROM JOIN (CURRENCY 일괄 UPDATE / ADMIN_GROUP EDIT_FLAG UPDATE) → MERGE(GROUP BY+MAX 로 ORA-30926 방지) / EXISTS (룰 #30)
7202	 *  - MSSQL UPDATE JOIN 다중매치 임의채택 → MAX() 결정적 채택 (주석 명시)
7203	 *  - AS-IS C_TITLE 2번째 UNION 의 C.DEFAULT_FLAG = '' → C.DEFAULT_FLAG IS NULL (C-7. 사내 실측: DEFAULT_FLAG NULL 11건 존재)
7204	 *  - #FCST 일부 서브쿼리의 DIVISION_ID = '40' 하드코딩 (LOOKA/BC) 은 AS-IS 원문 그대로 보존 (임의 수정 금지)
7205	 *  - #TempSiteItemAll 의 K(SSO_DEPT_INFO) 조인 ELSE 분기 S.DEPT_ID: 사내 SCM_MST_SITE.DEPT_ID 컬럼 삭제(Case 39)로
7206	 *    기존 변환 표준(DMF0082_GET_MAIN_DATA)과 동일하게 F.DEPT_ID 사용. (K 는 SELECT 미참조 — 결과 영향 0)
7207	 *  - MSSQL WHILE 커서 결과 PRINT 류 → DBMS_OUTPUT.PUT_LINE (A00x 진행 로그는 원문 유지 목적상 주석으로 보존)
7208	 *  - EXEC PR_DM_SYS_LOG_BATCH 7-인자 → SCM_MGR.PR_DM_SYS_LOG_BATCH(...) 동일 7-인자 (8~11번째 default)
7209	 *  - 표준 O_RESULT_CURSOR + WHEN OTHERS 핸들러 = DMF0082_SAVE_MAIN_DATA / DMF0082_GET_MAIN_DATA Oracle 표준 준수
7210	 *===================================================================================================================================================*/
7211	IS
7212	    --=============================================================================================================================
7213	    -- LOG 용 변수 설정 -- [E20250915-000105]_로그를 찍기위해서 LOG용 변수 추가
7214	    --=============================================================================================================================
7215	    V_LOG_TYPE          VARCHAR2(30 CHAR);
7216	    N_LOG_COUNT         NUMBER := 0;
7217	    V_LOG_MESSAGE       VARCHAR2(1000 CHAR);
7218	    V_DESCRIPTION       VARCHAR2(500 CHAR);
7219	    D_EVENT_DATE        DATE;
7220	
7221	    --=============================================================================================================================
7222	    -- 사용자 컬럼 및 메저 세팅 관련 변수
7223	    --=============================================================================================================================
7224	    V_USER_COL_CNT      NUMBER := 0;
7225	    V_USER_MEA_CNT      NUMBER := 0;
7226	    VA_PROGRAM_ID       VARCHAR2(50 CHAR) := 'DMF0082';
7227	    VA_SHEET_NAME       VARCHAR2(50 CHAR) := 'FcstNPrice';
7228	
7229	    --=============================================================================================================================
7230	    -- BUCKET 관련 변수 (hook #106 — PIVOT 리스트 누적 = CLOB)
7231	    --=============================================================================================================================
7232	    V_EXP_YYYYMMDD      VARCHAR2(8 CHAR);
7233	    V_PV_LIST           CLOB;               /* PIVOT IN 절: 'D_20260101' AS D_20260101, ... */
7234	    V_PV_COL_LIST       CLOB;               /* 외부 SELECT 컬럼 리스트: D_20260101, ...      */
7235	    V_PV_COLUMN_NAME    VARCHAR2(200 CHAR);
7236	    V_PV_NAME           VARCHAR2(200 CHAR);
7237	    V_SORT_ORDER_NO     VARCHAR2(50 CHAR);
7238	
7239	    /* 조회 할 때 쓸 날짜 관련 변수 */
7240	    V_FROM_YYYYMMDD     VARCHAR2(8 CHAR);
7241	    V_TO_YYYYMMDD       VARCHAR2(8 CHAR);
7242	    V_FROM_RESULT       VARCHAR2(8 CHAR);
7243	    V_TO_RESULT         VARCHAR2(8 CHAR);
7244	    V_USER_TIME_ZONE    VARCHAR2(50 CHAR);
7245	
7246	    /* C_TITLE 커서에서만 사용 될 지역변수 */
7247	    V_CUR_COLUMN_NAME   VARCHAR2(200 CHAR);
7248	    V_CUR_PROGRAM_ID    VARCHAR2(50 CHAR);
7249	    V_CUR_SHEET_NAME    VARCHAR2(100 CHAR);
7250	    V_CUR_SORT_ORDER_NO VARCHAR2(50 CHAR);
7251	
7252	    /* C_TITLE 커서에서 완성 된 후 동적쿼리에서 쓰여질 변수 */
7253	    V_COLUMN            VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR);
7254	    V_GROUPBY           VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR);
7255	    V_ORDERBY           VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR);
7256	
7257	    /* @v_group_id 권한 그룹 */
7258	    V_GROUP_ID          VARCHAR2(50 CHAR) := '';
7259	    V_ADMIN_CNT         NUMBER := 0;
7260	    V_MEASURE_CNT       NUMBER := 0;
7261	    V_LIMIT_CNT         NUMBER := 0;
7262	
7263	    /* 최종적으로 실행하는 동적쿼리 */
7264	    V_SQL_TEXT          CLOB;
7265	
7266	    /* 표준 예외 변수 */
7267	    V_PROC_INPUT        VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR);
7268	    V_SQL_CODE          VARCHAR2(20 CHAR);
7269	    V_SQL_ERRMSG        VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR);
7270	    V_BACKTRACE         VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR);
7271	
7272	    --=============================================================================================================================
7273	    -- C_BUCKET : 선택한 bucket(D/W/M)의 동적 PIVOT 컬럼을 만드는 커서
7274	    --  (MSSQL: '[D_x] AS D_x' / '[D_x]' → Oracle: 컬럼명 'D_x' / PIVOT IN piece '''D_x'' AS D_x')
7275	    --  [PJ2025-50188]소형 매출계획 동기화 개선 고도화 : CURSOR 선언에 LOCAL 추가 (Oracle 커서는 기본 local)
7276	    --=============================================================================================================================
7277	    CURSOR C_BUCKET IS
7278	        SELECT 'D_' || A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD                                            AS COLUMN_NAME
7279	             , '''D_' || A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD || ''' AS D_' || A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD        AS PV_NAME
7280	             , A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD || '_31'                                           AS SORT_ORDER_NO
7281	        FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M A
7282	        WHERE  A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD >= p_from_yyyymmdd
7283	        AND    A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD <= V_EXP_YYYYMMDD
7284	        UNION ALL
7285	        SELECT 'W_' || A.BASIS_YYYYMM || A.ISO_WEEK                                AS COLUMN_NAME
7286	             , '''W_' || A.BASIS_YYYYMM || A.ISO_WEEK || ''' AS W_' || A.BASIS_YYYYMM || A.ISO_WEEK AS PV_NAME
7287	             , A.BASIS_START_DATE || '_21'                                         AS SORT_ORDER_NO
7288	        FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M A
7289	        WHERE  A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD >= p_from_yyyymmdd
7290	        AND    A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD <= p_to_yyyymmdd
7291	        GROUP BY A.BASIS_YYYYMM, A.ISO_WEEK, A.BASIS_START_DATE
7292	        UNION ALL
7293	        SELECT 'M_' || A.BASIS_YYYYMM                                              AS COLUMN_NAME
7294	             , '''M_' || A.BASIS_YYYYMM || ''' AS M_' || A.BASIS_YYYYMM            AS PV_NAME
7295	             , MIN(A.BASIS_START_DATE) || '_01'                                    AS SORT_ORDER_NO
7296	        FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M A
7297	        WHERE  A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD >= p_from_yyyymmdd
7298	        AND    A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD <= p_to_yyyymmdd
7299	        GROUP BY A.BASIS_YYYYMM
7300	        ORDER BY SORT_ORDER_NO;
7301	
7302	    --=============================================================================================================================
7303	    -- C_TITLE : 선택한 bucket의 값인 MONTH 혹은 WEEK를 이용해서 동적인 컬럼을 만드는 커서
7304	    --=============================================================================================================================
7305	    CURSOR C_TITLE IS
7306	        SELECT * FROM ( --[SORTFIX 2026-08-04] 인라인뷰 (UNION ORDER BY 식 제약 ORA-01785 회피)
7307	        SELECT U.PROGRAM_ID
7308	             , U.SHEET_NAME
7309	             , U.COLUMN_NAME
7310	             , CASE WHEN U.COLUMN_NAME = 'LAST_UPDATE_DATE' THEN '800'
7311	                    WHEN U.COLUMN_NAME = 'LAST_UPDATED_BY'  THEN '560'
7312	                    ELSE TO_CHAR(U.SORT_ORDER_NO) END AS SORT_ORDER_NO
7313	        FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_TITLESET_USER U
7314	             , SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_SHEET_COLUMNS C
7315	        WHERE  1 = 1
7316	        AND    U.PROGRAM_ID  = C.PROGRAM_ID
7317	        AND    U.SHEET_NAME  = C.SHEET_NAME
7318	        AND    U.COLUMN_NAME = C.COLUMN_NAME
7319	        AND    U.PROGRAM_ID  = VA_PROGRAM_ID
7320	        AND    U.SHEET_NAME  = VA_SHEET_NAME
7321	        AND    U.USER_ID     = p_created_by
7322	        AND    U.USER_INQUIRY_COLUMN_TYPE_CODE = 'C'
7323	        -- [UICATEGORY 2026-08-07] GTT_MF_SP 산출 whitelist — titleset 'C' 오염(미산출 컬럼) 유입 시 동적 SQL ORA-00904 차단
7324	        --   (DMF0003 TITLESET IMMUNIZE 파리티. 목록 = GTT_MF_SP 전 컬럼 — GTT ALTER 시 동기 의무)
7325	        AND    U.COLUMN_NAME IN (
7326	                 'DIVISION_ID','SCM_SITE_ID','PLANT_SITE_ID','PROJECT_CODE','ITEM_ID','INCOTERMS','FCST_TYPE','DOM_EXP'
7327	                ,'MEASURE','END_CUSTOMER','NICK_NAME','DMS_SALES','DMS_CUSTOMER','YYYYMM','YYYYMMWW','YYYYMMDD','DAYOFWEEK'
7328	                ,'FORECAST_INFO_CELL_MODEL','TEAM_ID','TEAM_NAME_KO','ITEM_TYPE','PROJECT_NAME','PROJECT_OWNER','OWNER'
7329	                ,'SOLDTO_CUSTOMER','COUNTRY_NAME','CITY_NAME','ITEM_NAME','VMI_YN','CELL_PROD_SITE_ID'
7330	                ,'CUST_GROUP','CUST_GROUP_DTL','APPLICATION','ROUTE','MDM_PROJECT_NAME','MIN_CAPACITY'
7331	                ,'FORECAST_INFO_BIZ_TYPE','CELL_TYPE','FORECAST_INFO_CELL_PACK_COUNT'
7332	                ,'FORECAST_INFO_ALL','FORECAST_INFO_SPEC','FORECAST_INFOR_VERSION','CELL_MODEL_NICK_NAME'
7333	                ,'QTY','MONTH_END_YN','WEEK_END_YN','EDIT_FLAG','SORT_ORDER_NO','MEASURE_GROUP','NEW_FLAG','BUCKET','SORT_KEY'
7334	                ,'LAST_UPDATE_DATE','LAST_UPDATED_BY','NOTATION','CURRENCY','VERSION_NO','CUTOFF_YYYYMMDD'
7335	                ,'AS_YN','ATTR1','VMI_STD_DIO','BIZ_TYPE','PROJECT_STATUS','ENERGE','CELL_QUANTITY'
7336	                ,'CELL_PER_CAR','CELL_PER_MODULE','MODULE_PER_CAR','SENDER_CODE','SENDER_NAME','PROJECT_S_OP_REPORT','AS_FROM_SITE' )
7337	        UNION ALL
7338	        /* 2024/05/17   kcw3408     이동계획 판가 매출GROUP_ID( Item은 Group 기준으로 정렬) 추가) */
7339	        SELECT C.PROGRAM_ID
7340	             , C.SHEET_NAME
7341	             , C.COLUMN_NAME
7342	             , TO_CHAR(CASE WHEN C.COLUMN_NAME = 'GROUP_ID' THEN NVL(U.SORT_ORDER_NO, 85) - 0.5
7343	                            ELSE C.SORT_ORDER_NO END) AS SORT_ORDER_NO
7344	        FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_SHEET_COLUMNS C
7345	        LEFT OUTER JOIN SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_TITLESET_USER U
7346	            ON  U.PROGRAM_ID  = C.PROGRAM_ID
7347	            AND U.SHEET_NAME  = C.SHEET_NAME
7348	            AND U.USER_INQUIRY_COLUMN_TYPE_CODE = 'C'
7349	            AND U.COLUMN_NAME IN ('ITEM_ID')
7350	            AND U.USER_ID     = p_created_by
7351	        WHERE  1 = 1
7352	        AND    C.PROGRAM_ID   = VA_PROGRAM_ID
7353	        AND    C.SHEET_NAME   = VA_SHEET_NAME
7354	        -- [E20260907-DEFFLAG] AS-IS: AND    C.DEFAULT_FLAG = ' '  /* [UICATEGORY 2026-08-07 FIX] 구 "IS NULL" = 사문(DEFAULT_FLAG NOT NULL, 실값 ' ') → 고정컬럼(SORT_KEY 등) V_COLUMN 미포함
7355	        -- [E20260907-DEFFLAG] AS-IS: → ORDER BY SORT_KEY 가 상시 ORA-00904 (Current 조회 전면 파손 — 2026-08-07 실측). GET 의 L162 [FIX 2026-07-12] 와 규약 통일 */
7356	        AND    NVL(TRIM(C.DEFAULT_FLAG), '-') = '-'  -- [E20260907-DEFFLAG] 구 "= ' '" → **센티널 정규화**. AS-IS 원문은 DEFAULT_FLAG='' (=빈 값) 이고 ' '(클라우드) 와 '-'(ZIOUI1) 는 같은 센티널의 표기 변형이다(hook #119). 'N' 은 별개 실값이라 제외 — 이렇게 해야 양 DB 가 동일 컬럼셋(실측 10건)을 낸다. 구 조건은 ZIOUI1 에서 항상 0행이라 SORT_KEY 누락 → ORDER BY SORT_KEY 가 ORA-00904
7357	        -- [E20260907-DEFFLAG] branch2 에도 branch1 과 **동일한** MF_SP 산출 whitelist 적용. 판정 완화로 비산출 컬럼(실측 DEPT_NAME)이 유입돼 ORA-00904 → branch1 비대칭 해소
7358	        AND    C.COLUMN_NAME IN (
7359	                 'DIVISION_ID','SCM_SITE_ID','PLANT_SITE_ID','PROJECT_CODE','ITEM_ID','INCOTERMS','FCST_TYPE','DOM_EXP'
7360	                ,'MEASURE','END_CUSTOMER','NICK_NAME','DMS_SALES','DMS_CUSTOMER','YYYYMM','YYYYMMWW','YYYYMMDD','DAYOFWEEK'
7361	                ,'FORECAST_INFO_CELL_MODEL','TEAM_ID','TEAM_NAME_KO','ITEM_TYPE','PROJECT_NAME','PROJECT_OWNER','OWNER'
7362	                ,'SOLDTO_CUSTOMER','COUNTRY_NAME','CITY_NAME','ITEM_NAME','VMI_YN','CELL_PROD_SITE_ID'
7363	                ,'CUST_GROUP','CUST_GROUP_DTL','APPLICATION','ROUTE','MDM_PROJECT_NAME','MIN_CAPACITY'
7364	                ,'FORECAST_INFO_BIZ_TYPE','CELL_TYPE','FORECAST_INFO_CELL_PACK_COUNT'
7365	                ,'FORECAST_INFO_ALL','FORECAST_INFO_SPEC','FORECAST_INFOR_VERSION','CELL_MODEL_NICK_NAME'
7366	                ,'QTY','MONTH_END_YN','WEEK_END_YN','EDIT_FLAG','SORT_ORDER_NO','MEASURE_GROUP','NEW_FLAG','BUCKET','SORT_KEY'
7367	                ,'LAST_UPDATE_DATE','LAST_UPDATED_BY','NOTATION','CURRENCY','VERSION_NO','CUTOFF_YYYYMMDD'
7368	                ,'AS_YN','ATTR1','VMI_STD_DIO','BIZ_TYPE','PROJECT_STATUS','ENERGE','CELL_QUANTITY'
7369	                ,'CELL_PER_CAR','CELL_PER_MODULE','MODULE_PER_CAR','SENDER_CODE','SENDER_NAME','PROJECT_S_OP_REPORT','AS_FROM_SITE' )
7370	        AND    C.VALID_YN     = 'Y'
7371	        -- [UICATEGORY 2026-08-07] branch1(사용자 titleset 'C') 중복 컬럼 제외 — GET 동형 (V_COLUMN 중복 = ORA-00918 방어)
7372	        AND    NOT EXISTS (
7373	                   SELECT 1 FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_TITLESET_USER U2
7374	                   WHERE  U2.PROGRAM_ID  = C.PROGRAM_ID
7375	                   AND    U2.SHEET_NAME  = C.SHEET_NAME
7376	                   AND    U2.COLUMN_NAME = C.COLUMN_NAME
7377	                   AND    U2.USER_ID     = p_created_by
7378	                   AND    U2.USER_INQUIRY_COLUMN_TYPE_CODE = 'C'
7379	               )
7380	        ) ORDER BY TO_NUMBER(SORT_ORDER_NO); --[SORTFIX 2026-08-04] UI Category Setup 순번 수치정렬 (TO_CHAR 문자정렬 결함 교정 — DMF0003 TO_NUMBER 원형 파리티)
7381	
7382	BEGIN
7383	
7384	    -- [E20260907-PERF2] GTT undo -> temp (redo 제거). 실측 redo 93.8MB -> 6.6MB / 12.3s -> 10.9s.
7385	    --   이 SP 는 GT_DMF0082_TEMP_FCST_N_PRICE 에 12만행 규모를 적재/삭제하며, 최대 병목의 대기가
7386	    --   `log buffer space` 였다. 결과·로직 불변 — 순수 I/O 비용 절감.
7387	    --   ⚠ 트랜잭션 중 임시테이블을 이미 썼으면 변경 불가 → 그 경우 조용히 건너뛴다(최적화 힌트).
7388	    BEGIN
7389	        EXECUTE IMMEDIATE 'ALTER SESSION SET TEMP_UNDO_ENABLED = TRUE';
7390	    EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN NULL;  -- 설정 실패는 성능만 영향, 결과 무영향
7391	    END;
7392	
7393	    /* ⭐ [E20260815-SPLIT2GTT] hook #129 §3.0 (2순위) — 테이블 함수 → GTT 선적재.
7394	
7395	       `TABLE(UF_FX_String_SplitForIN(p_x,','))` 는 카디널리티 추정이 고정값으로 굳고,
7396	
7397	       같은 파라미터를 여러 곳에서 쓰면 그때마다 PIPELINED 함수가 다시 돈다(패키지 내 19곳 실측).
7398	
7399	       파라미터당 **1회만** 쪼개 GTT 에 넣고, 호출부는 GTT 를 읽는다.
7400	
7401	       ⚠ 이 블록은 **어떤 IF 안에도 두지 않는다** — 조건 블록에 들어가면 일반 조회에서
7402	
7403	         통째로 건너뛰어 토큰이 비고 조회가 전멸한다(2026-08-15 센티널 패치 실패 계보). */
