7538	    /* SET @V_EXP_YYYYMMDD = CONVERT(NVARCHAR(8), DATEADD(WEEK, 50, @p_from_yyyymmdd), 112);  (C-11 명시 마스크) */
7539	    --[E20260908-GETRANGE] 52주 이후 조회/저장 개방 (사용자 지시 2026-09-08).
7540	    --   구 구현은 D_(일자) 버킷만 'p_from + 50주' 로 잘렸다(W_/M_ 는 p_to_yyyymmdd 전 구간) →
7541	    --   50주 이후는 **헤더는 생기는데 값이 안 내려오는** 상태가 되고, 그 구간에 LOC 를 입력하면
7542	    --   일자 셀이 0 으로 남아 저장 SP 의 부분주 합계 판정이 ACTION='D' 로 뒤집혀
7543	    --   검증 #18(Sales Price 삭제 시 52주 이후 FCST 확인)이 수정인데 발동, 저장이 막혔다.
7544	    --   ⇒ D_ 상한을 조회종료일까지 넓힌다. GREATEST 로 구 하한(from+50주)을 보존해
7545	    --     짧은 조회구간의 종전 동작은 그대로 둔다(비회귀). 52주 저장 RULE(#1/#10/#18)은 무변경.
7546	    V_EXP_YYYYMMDD := GREATEST(TO_CHAR(TO_DATE(p_from_yyyymmdd, 'YYYYMMDD') + (50 * 7), 'YYYYMMDD')
7547	                             , p_to_yyyymmdd);

7549	    DBMS_LOB.CREATETEMPORARY(V_PV_LIST, TRUE);
7550	    DBMS_LOB.CREATETEMPORARY(V_PV_COL_LIST, TRUE);

7553	    LOOP
7554	        FETCH C_BUCKET INTO V_PV_COLUMN_NAME, V_PV_NAME, V_SORT_ORDER_NO;
7555	        EXIT WHEN C_BUCKET%NOTFOUND;          /* C-10: FETCH 직후 종료 판정 의무 */
7556	        IF DBMS_LOB.GETLENGTH(V_PV_COL_LIST) = 0 THEN
7557	            V_PV_COL_LIST := V_PV_COLUMN_NAME;
7558	            V_PV_LIST     := V_PV_NAME;
7559	        ELSE
7560	            V_PV_COL_LIST := V_PV_COL_LIST || ',' || V_PV_COLUMN_NAME;
7561	            V_PV_LIST     := V_PV_LIST     || ',' || V_PV_NAME;
7562	        END IF;
7563	    END LOOP;
7564	    CLOSE C_BUCKET;

7626	    INSERT INTO SCM_MGR.GTT_TEMPSITEITEMALL
7627	    ( DIVISION_ID, PROJECT_CODE, SCM_SITE_ID, ITEM_ID, NICK_NAME, PROJECT_NAME
7628	    , CELL_MODEL, END_CUSTOMER, SOLDTO_CUSTOMER
7629	    , COUNTRY, COUNTRY_NAME, CITY, CITY_NAME
7630	    , OWNER, PROJECT_OWNER, USER_ID, ITEM_NAME, ITEM_TYPE

7632	    , MIN_CAPACITY, FORECAST_INFO_BIZ_TYPE, FORECAST_INFO_PRODUCT_GROUP, CELL_TYPE, FORECAST_INFO_CELL_PACK_COUNT
7633	    , FORECAST_INFO_ALL, FORECAST_INFO_SPEC, FORECAST_INFOR_VERSION
7634	    , CELL_MODEL_NICK_NAME, VMI_YN, DMS_SALES, DMS_CUSTOMER, DEPT_ID, DEPT_NAME_KO
7635	    , ITEM_VALID_YN )
7636	    SELECT F.DIVISION_ID
7637	         , F.PROJECT_CODE
7638	         , F.SCM_SITE_ID
7639	         , F.ITEM_ID
7640	         , T2.NICK_NAME
7641	         , NVL(MP.PROJECT_NAME, F.PROJECT_CODE) AS PROJECT_NAME  --[E20260819-PJTNAME]
7642	         , F.ATTRIBUTE5                                     AS CELL_MODEL
7643	         , S.END_CUSTOMER
7644	         , S.SOLDTO_CUSTOMER
7645	         , I.COUNTRY
7646	         , I.COUNTRY_NAME
7647	         , H.CITY
7648	         , H.CITY_NAME
7649	         /* STUFF( ... FOR XML PATH('') ) OWNER 다중 사용자 이름 결합 → LISTAGG (AS-IS 로직 동일) */
7650	         , (SELECT LISTAGG(XA.USER_NAME_EN, ',') WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY XA.USER_NAME_EN)
7651	            FROM  (SELECT DISTINCT ZA.USER_NAME_EN
7652	                   FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_SSO_USER_INFO ZA
7653	                   WHERE  ZA.USER_ID IN (SELECT REGEXP_SUBSTR(F.OWNER, '[^,]+', 1, LEVEL)
7654	                                         FROM   DUAL
7655	                                         CONNECT BY LEVEL <= NVL(REGEXP_COUNT(F.OWNER, ','), 0) + 1)) XA
7656	           )                                                AS OWNER
7657	         , J.USER_NAME_EN                                   AS PROJECT_OWNER
7658	         , J.USER_ID
7659	         , T2.ITEM_NAME
7660	         , T2.ITEM_TYPE
7661	         , F.CUST_GROUP
7662	         , F.CUST_GROUP_DTL
7663	         , F.APPLICATION
7664	         , F.ROUTE
7665	         , T2.MDM_PROJECT_NAME
7666	         , T2.MIN_CAPACITY
7667	         , CASE WHEN F.ITEM_ID = LOOKA.MODEL THEN LOOKA.BIZ_TYPE
7668	                ELSE T2.FORECAST_INFO_BIZ_TYPE END          AS FORECAST_INFO_BIZ_TYPE
7669	         , T2.FORECAST_INFO_PRODUCT_GROUP
7670	         , LOOK.NAME                                        AS CELL_TYPE
7671	         , CASE WHEN F.ITEM_ID = LOOKA.MODEL
7672	                THEN TO_NUMBER(LOOKA.CELL_PACK_COUNT DEFAULT NULL ON CONVERSION ERROR)
7673	                ELSE T2.FORECAST_INFO_CELL_PACK_COUNT END   AS FORECAST_INFO_CELL_PACK_COUNT
7674	         , T2.FORECAST_INFO_SPEC || T2.FORECAST_INFOR_VERSION AS FORECAST_INFO_ALL
7675	         , T2.FORECAST_INFO_SPEC                            AS FORECAST_INFO_SPEC
7676	         , T2.FORECAST_INFOR_VERSION                        AS FORECAST_INFOR_VERSION
7677	         , T4.NICK_NAME                                     AS CELL_MODEL_NICK_NAME   /* ISNULL(T4.NICK_NAME,'') → '' = NULL (C-7) */
7678	         , S.VMI_YN
7679	         , F.ATTRIBUTE1                                     AS DMS_SALES              /* ISNULL(F.ATTRIBUTE1,'') → '' = NULL (C-7) */
7680	         , F.ATTRIBUTE2                                     AS DMS_CUSTOMER           /* ISNULL(F.ATTRIBUTE2,'') → '' = NULL (C-7) */
7681	         , TEAM.DEPT_ID
7682	         , TEAM.DEPT_NAME_KO
7683	         , F.VALID_YN                                       AS ITEM_VALID_YN
7684	    FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_SITE_ITEM F
7685	    INNER JOIN SCM_MGR.GTT_TEMP_DM_MST_ITEM_SINGLE_V T2
7686	        ON  F.DIVISION_ID = T2.DIVISION_ID
7687	        AND F.ITEM_ID     = T2.ITEM_ID
7688	    INNER JOIN (SELECT DISTINCT '%' || TOKEN || '%' AS TST
7689	                FROM  (SELECT REGEXP_SUBSTR(p_item_id, '[^,]+', 1, LEVEL) AS TOKEN
7690	                       FROM   DUAL
7691	                       CONNECT BY LEVEL <= NVL(REGEXP_COUNT(p_item_id, ','), 0) + 1)) B
7692	        ON  (F.ITEM_ID LIKE B.TST OR T2.NICK_NAME LIKE B.TST)
7693	    INNER JOIN SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_SITE S
7694	        ON  F.DIVISION_ID = S.DIVISION_ID
7695	        AND F.SCM_SITE_ID = S.SCM_SITE_ID
7696	        AND S.SITE_TYPE   = 'SALES'
7697	    INNER JOIN SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_CITY H
7698	        ON  S.CITY = H.CITY
7699	    INNER JOIN SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_COUNTRY I
7700	        ON  S.COUNTRY = I.COUNTRY
7701	    --[E20260819-PJTNAME] Project 명 원천 — SCM_MST_PROJECT (미등재 코드=코드 폴백 LEFT)
7702	    LEFT JOIN SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_PROJECT MP
7703	        ON  F.DIVISION_ID  = MP.DIVISION_ID
7704	        AND F.PROJECT_CODE = MP.PROJECT_CODE
7705	    -- [hook#150 2026-09-06 AJS] AS-IS: LEFT JOIN (SELECT B2.USER_ID, B2.USER_NAME, B2.USER_NAME_EN, B2.DEPT_ID, C.DEPT_NAME_KO
7706	               -- [hook#150 2026-09-06 AJS] AS-IS: FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_SSO_USER_INFO B2
7707	               -- [hook#150 2026-09-06 AJS] AS-IS: INNER JOIN SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_SSO_DEPT_INFO C
7708	                   -- [hook#150 2026-09-06 AJS] AS-IS: ON B2.DEPT_ID = C.DEPT_ID) TEAM
7709	        -- [hook#150 2026-09-06 AJS] AS-IS: /* (룰 #5) TEAM.USER_ID IN (SELECT TOP 1 TOKEN FROM STRING_SPLIT(F.OWNER,',')) → 첫 토큰 비교 */
7710	        -- [hook#150 2026-09-06 AJS] AS-IS: ON TEAM.USER_ID = CASE WHEN INSTR(F.OWNER, ',') = 0 THEN F.OWNER
7711	                               -- [hook#150 2026-09-06 AJS] AS-IS: ELSE SUBSTR(F.OWNER, 1, INSTR(F.OWNER, ',') - 1) END
7712	    -- 2026-09-06 hook#150 AJS: DEPT_ID 원천 = SCM_MST_SITE_ITEM.DEPT_ID (SCM_MST_SITE.DEPT_ID 삭제 예정 / OWNER 경유 폐기)
7713	    LEFT JOIN SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_SSO_DEPT_INFO TEAM
7714	        ON TEAM.DEPT_ID = F.DEPT_ID
7715	    LEFT JOIN SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_SSO_USER_INFO J
7716	        /* (C-1) SUBSTRING(F.OWNER, 0, CHARINDEX...) 첫 토큰 → SUBSTR(s,1,INSTR-1) 보정 */
7717	        ON J.USER_ID = CASE WHEN INSTR(F.OWNER, ',') = 0 THEN F.OWNER
7718	                            ELSE SUBSTR(F.OWNER, 1, INSTR(F.OWNER, ',') - 1) END
7719	    LEFT JOIN SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_SSO_DEPT_INFO K
7720	        /* AS-IS ELSE S.DEPT_ID — 사내 SCM_MST_SITE.DEPT_ID 삭제(Case 39) → F.DEPT_ID (K 는 SELECT 미참조) */
7721	        ON K.DEPT_ID = F.DEPT_ID  -- 2026-09-06 hook#150 AJS (AS-IS: CASE WHEN F.DIVISION_ID IN ('40') THEN J.DEPT_ID ELSE F.DEPT_ID END)
7722	    LEFT JOIN (SELECT SEGMENT1 AS CODE, SEGMENT2 AS NAME
7723	               FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL
7724	               WHERE  LOOKUP_TYPE = 'CELL_TYPE') LOOK
7725	        ON LOOK.CODE = T2.CELL_TYPE
7726	    LEFT JOIN SCM_MGR.GTT_TEMP_DM_MST_ITEM_SINGLE_V T4
7727	        /* ISNULL(T2.FORECAST_INFO_CELL_MODEL,'') = T4.ITEM_ID → 직접 비교 (C-7, 결과 동일) */
7728	        ON  T2.DIVISION_ID = T4.DIVISION_ID
7729	        AND T2.FORECAST_INFO_CELL_MODEL = T4.ITEM_ID
7730	    LEFT JOIN (SELECT A.MODEL, A.BIZ_TYPE, A.CELL_PACK_COUNT, A.MODEL_RANK
7731	               FROM  (SELECT SEGMENT2 AS MODEL
7732	                           , SEGMENT3 AS BIZ_TYPE
7733	                           , SEGMENT4 AS CELL_PACK_COUNT
7734	                           , RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY SEGMENT2 ORDER BY DISPLAY_SEQ DESC) AS MODEL_RANK
7735	                      FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL
7736	                      WHERE  LOOKUP_TYPE = 'DM_BIZ_TYPE_40'
7737	                      AND    SEGMENT1    = '40') A
7738	               WHERE  A.MODEL_RANK = 1) LOOKA
7739	        ON LOOKA.MODEL = F.ITEM_ID
7740	    WHERE  F.DIVISION_ID = '40'                    /* AS-IS 원문 하드코딩('40') 그대로 보존 */
7741	    /* CASE WHEN @P='ALL' THEN @P ELSE col END IN (SPLIT(@P)) → OR 동치 변환 */
7742	    AND    (p_scm_site_id = 'ALL' OR F.SCM_SITE_ID IN (
7743	                SELECT REGEXP_SUBSTR(p_scm_site_id, '[^,]+', 1, LEVEL)
7744	                FROM   DUAL
7745	                CONNECT BY LEVEL <= NVL(REGEXP_COUNT(p_scm_site_id, ','), 0) + 1))
7746	    AND    (p_customer = 'ALL' OR F.PROJECT_CODE IN (
7747	                SELECT REGEXP_SUBSTR(p_customer, '[^,]+', 1, LEVEL)
7748	                FROM   DUAL
7749	                CONNECT BY LEVEL <= NVL(REGEXP_COUNT(p_customer, ','), 0) + 1))
7750	    AND    (p_biz_type = 'ALL' OR
7751	                CASE WHEN F.ITEM_ID = LOOKA.MODEL THEN LOOKA.BIZ_TYPE
7752	                     ELSE T2.FORECAST_INFO_BIZ_TYPE END IN (
7753	                SELECT REGEXP_SUBSTR(p_biz_type, '[^,]+', 1, LEVEL)
7754	                FROM   DUAL
7755	                CONNECT BY LEVEL <= NVL(REGEXP_COUNT(p_biz_type, ','), 0) + 1));
7756	    -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
7757	    -- #TempSiteItemAll 만들기 (END)
7758	    -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
7759	    --[E20260812-PERF-NOSTATS] DBMS_STATS.GATHER_TABLE_STATS(OWNNAME => 'SCM_MGR', TABNAME => 'GTT_TEMPSITEITEMALL', NO_INVALIDATE => FALSE);  -- hook #129: SP 내 통계수집 금지(ASH 병목 실측 — undo/COL$ 대기. DS2 dynamic sampling 이 대체)
7760	
7761	    -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
7762	    -- #TempSiteItem 만들기 (START)
7763	    -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
7764	    INSERT INTO SCM_MGR.GTT_TEMPSITEITEM
7765	    ( DIVISION_ID, PROJECT_CODE, SCM_SITE_ID, ITEM_ID, NICK_NAME, PROJECT_NAME
7766	    , CELL_MODEL, END_CUSTOMER, SOLDTO_CUSTOMER
7767	    , COUNTRY, COUNTRY_NAME, CITY, CITY_NAME
7768	    , OWNER, PROJECT_OWNER, USER_ID, ITEM_NAME, ITEM_TYPE
7769	    , CUST_GROUP, CUST_GROUP_DTL, APPLICATION, ROUTE, MDM_PROJECT_NAME
7770	    , MIN_CAPACITY, FORECAST_INFO_BIZ_TYPE, FORECAST_INFO_PRODUCT_GROUP, CELL_TYPE, FORECAST_INFO_CELL_PACK_COUNT
7771	    , FORECAST_INFO_ALL, FORECAST_INFO_SPEC, FORECAST_INFOR_VERSION
7772	    , CELL_MODEL_NICK_NAME, VMI_YN, DMS_SALES, DMS_CUSTOMER, DEPT_ID, DEPT_NAME_KO
7773	    , ITEM_VALID_YN )
7774	    SELECT DIVISION_ID, PROJECT_CODE, SCM_SITE_ID, ITEM_ID, NICK_NAME, PROJECT_NAME
7775	         , MAX(CELL_MODEL)                     AS CELL_MODEL
7776	         , MAX(END_CUSTOMER)                   AS END_CUSTOMER
7777	         , MAX(SOLDTO_CUSTOMER)                AS SOLDTO_CUSTOMER
7778	         , MAX(COUNTRY)                        AS COUNTRY
7779	         , MAX(COUNTRY_NAME)                   AS COUNTRY_NAME
7780	         , MAX(CITY)                           AS CITY
7781	         , MAX(CITY_NAME)                      AS CITY_NAME
7782	         , MAX(OWNER)                          AS OWNER
7783	         , MAX(PROJECT_OWNER)                  AS PROJECT_OWNER
7784	         , MAX(USER_ID)                        AS USER_ID
7785	         , MAX(ITEM_NAME)                      AS ITEM_NAME
7786	         , MAX(ITEM_TYPE)                      AS ITEM_TYPE
7787	         , MAX(CUST_GROUP)                     AS CUST_GROUP
7788	         , MAX(CUST_GROUP_DTL)                 AS CUST_GROUP_DTL
7789	         , MAX(APPLICATION)                    AS APPLICATION
7790	         , MAX(ROUTE)                          AS ROUTE
7791	         , MAX(MDM_PROJECT_NAME)               AS MDM_PROJECT_NAME
7792	         , MAX(MIN_CAPACITY)                   AS MIN_CAPACITY
7793	         , MAX(FORECAST_INFO_BIZ_TYPE)         AS FORECAST_INFO_BIZ_TYPE
7794	         , MAX(FORECAST_INFO_PRODUCT_GROUP)    AS FORECAST_INFO_PRODUCT_GROUP
7795	         , MAX(CELL_TYPE)                      AS CELL_TYPE
7796	         , MAX(FORECAST_INFO_CELL_PACK_COUNT)  AS FORECAST_INFO_CELL_PACK_COUNT
7797	         , MAX(FORECAST_INFO_ALL)              AS FORECAST_INFO_ALL
7798	         , MAX(FORECAST_INFO_SPEC)             AS FORECAST_INFO_SPEC
7799	         , MAX(FORECAST_INFOR_VERSION)         AS FORECAST_INFOR_VERSION
7800	         , MAX(CELL_MODEL_NICK_NAME)           AS CELL_MODEL_NICK_NAME
7801	         , MAX(VMI_YN)                         AS VMI_YN
7802	         , MAX(DMS_SALES)                      AS DMS_SALES
7803	         , MAX(DMS_CUSTOMER)                   AS DMS_CUSTOMER
7804	         , MAX(DEPT_ID)                        AS DEPT_ID
7805	         , MAX(DEPT_NAME_KO)                   AS DEPT_NAME_KO
7806	         , MAX(ITEM_VALID_YN)                  AS ITEM_VALID_YN
7807	    FROM   SCM_MGR.GTT_TEMPSITEITEMALL
7808	    GROUP BY DIVISION_ID, PROJECT_CODE, SCM_SITE_ID, ITEM_ID, NICK_NAME, PROJECT_NAME;

7815	    --=============================================================================================================================
7816	
7817	    /* 조회 할 때 쓸 날짜 변수 세팅 시작 */
7818	    V_FROM_YYYYMMDD := '';  --MKT FCST PLAN FROM 주차 월요일
7819	    V_TO_YYYYMMDD   := '';  --MKT FCST PLAN TO 주차 월요일
7820	    V_FROM_RESULT   := '';  --MKT FCST RESULT FROM 주차 월요일
7821	    V_TO_RESULT     := '';  --MKT FCST RESULT TO 주차 월요일
7822	    V_USER_TIME_ZONE := '';
7823	
7824	    BEGIN
7825	        SELECT U.TIMEZONE
7826	        INTO   V_USER_TIME_ZONE
7827	        FROM   SCM_MGR.TB_FX_USER U
7828	        WHERE  U.USERID = p_created_by;
7829	    EXCEPTION
7830	        WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN V_USER_TIME_ZONE := NULL;
7831	        WHEN TOO_MANY_ROWS THEN V_USER_TIME_ZONE := NULL;
7832	    END;
7833	
7834	    -- @V_FROM_YYYYMMDD : (USER 시차를 고려한) 당주 월요일
7835	    BEGIN
7836	        SELECT MIN(C.BASIS_YYYYMMDD)
7837	        INTO   V_FROM_YYYYMMDD
7838	        FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M C
7839	             , (SELECT DISTINCT ISO_YYYYWW
7840	                FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M
7841	                WHERE  1 = 1
7842	                /* CONVERT(CHAR(8), DBO.FN_MULTY_TIME(@V_USER_TIME_ZONE), 112) → 명시 마스크 (C-11) */
7843	                AND    BASIS_YYYYMMDD = TO_CHAR(SCM_MGR.FN_MULTY_TIME(V_USER_TIME_ZONE), 'YYYYMMDD')
7844	               ) T
7845	        WHERE  1 = 1
7846	        AND    C.ISO_YYYYWW = T.ISO_YYYYWW;
7847	    EXCEPTION
7848	        WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN V_FROM_YYYYMMDD := NULL;
7849	    END;
7850	
7851	    -- FROM 실적 : FROM 조회 구간 월요일부터
7852	    BEGIN
7853	        SELECT C.BASIS_START_DATE
7854	        INTO   V_FROM_RESULT
7855	        FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M C
7856	        WHERE  1 = 1
7857	        AND    C.BASIS_YYYYMMDD = p_from_yyyymmdd;
7858	    EXCEPTION
7859	        WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN V_FROM_RESULT := NULL;
7860	    END;
7861	
7862	    -- TO 실적 : TO 조회 구간 월요일 전까지
7863	    BEGIN
7864	        SELECT C.BASIS_START_DATE
7865	        INTO   V_TO_RESULT
7866	        FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M C
7867	        WHERE  1 = 1
7868	        AND    C.BASIS_YYYYMMDD = p_to_yyyymmdd;
7869	    EXCEPTION
7870	        WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN V_TO_RESULT := NULL;
7871	    END;
7872	
7873	    IF V_FROM_YYYYMMDD > V_FROM_RESULT THEN
7874	        -- 실적 보여줌
7875	        V_TO_YYYYMMDD := p_to_yyyymmdd;
7876	        V_TO_RESULT   := V_FROM_YYYYMMDD;   -- TO 실적 : 당주 월요일 전까지
7877	    ELSIF V_FROM_YYYYMMDD <= V_FROM_RESULT THEN
7878	        -- 실적 없음
7879	        V_TO_YYYYMMDD := p_to_yyyymmdd;
7880	        V_FROM_RESULT := '19000101';        --CONVERT(CHAR(8), DATEADD(MM, 6, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP), 112);
7881	        V_TO_RESULT   := '19000101';        --CONVERT(CHAR(8), DATEADD(MM, -6, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP), 112);
7882	    ELSIF V_FROM_YYYYMMDD > V_TO_RESULT THEN
7883	        -- 실적만 보여줌
7884	        V_FROM_YYYYMMDD := '19000101';
7885	        V_TO_YYYYMMDD   := '19000101';
7886	        V_FROM_RESULT   := p_from_yyyymmdd; -- FROM 실적 : FROM 조회 구간부터
7887	        V_TO_RESULT     := p_to_yyyymmdd;   -- TO 실적 : TO 조회 구간까지
7888	    ELSIF V_FROM_YYYYMMDD = V_TO_RESULT THEN
7889	        -- 실적 없음
7890	        V_TO_YYYYMMDD := V_FROM_YYYYMMDD;
7891	        V_FROM_RESULT := p_from_yyyymmdd;
7892	        V_TO_RESULT   := p_to_yyyymmdd;
7893	    END IF;
7894	    /* 조회 할 때 쓸 날짜 변수 세팅 종료 */
7895	
7896	    --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
7897	    -- #TempSelectMeasure 만들기 시작
7898	    --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
7899	    INSERT INTO SCM_MGR.GTT_TEMPSELECTMEASURE
7900	    ( PROGRAM_ID, SHEET_NAME, USER_ID, MEASURE_GROUP, MEASURE, RESULT_MEASURE, SORT_ORDER_NO )
7901	    SELECT DISTINCT U.PROGRAM_ID                        AS PROGRAM_ID
7902	         , U.SHEET_NAME                                 AS SHEET_NAME
7903	         , U.USER_ID                                    AS USER_ID
7904	         , U.COLUMN_NAME                                AS MEASURE_GROUP
7905	         , U.COLUMN_NAME                                AS MEASURE
7906	         , NVL(SMSM.ATTRIBUTE5, U.COLUMN_NAME)          AS RESULT_MEASURE  -- 2025.03.13 RESULT_MEASURE 추가
7907	         , TO_CHAR(U.SORT_ORDER_NO)                     AS SORT_ORDER_NO
7908	    FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_TITLESET_USER U              /* AS-IS INDEX(PK_SCM_MST_TITLESET_USER) 힌트 제거 */
7909	    JOIN   SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_SHEET_MEASURE SMSM
7910	        ON  U.PROGRAM_ID                    = SMSM.PROGRAM_ID
7911	        AND U.SHEET_NAME                    = SMSM.SHEET_NAME
7912	        AND U.COLUMN_NAME                   = SMSM.COLUMN_NAME
7913	        AND U.USER_INQUIRY_COLUMN_TYPE_CODE = 'M'
7914	        AND SMSM.VALID_YN                   = 'Y'
7915	    WHERE  1 = 1
7916	    AND    U.USER_INQUIRY_COLUMN_TYPE_CODE = 'M'
7917	    AND    U.PROGRAM_ID = VA_PROGRAM_ID  --'DMF0082'
7918	    AND    U.SHEET_NAME = VA_SHEET_NAME  --'FcstNPrice'
7919	    AND    U.USER_ID    = p_created_by;
7920	
7921	    ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
7922	    --[PJ2025-50188]소형 매출계획 동기화 개선 고도화 : 실적 Unit Price 로직 변경 (MKT Fcst AMT/QTY)
7923	    --실적구간 LOC,KRW를 계산하기 위해 필수 메저 RTF_SYS_RP, MKT_AMT_LOC, MKT_AMT_KRW 를 메저에 강제 등록 START
7924	    ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
7925	    SELECT COUNT(*)
7926	    INTO   V_MEASURE_CNT
7927	    FROM   SCM_MGR.GTT_TEMPSELECTMEASURE A
7928	    WHERE  1 = 1
7929	    AND    A.MEASURE IN ('LOC', 'KRW', 'FCST_GAP');  --'FCST_GAP' 추가 [PJ2025-50188] FCST Gap 메저 추가 (MKT FCST - SCM FCST)
7930	
7931	    IF V_MEASURE_CNT > 0 THEN
7932	        INSERT INTO SCM_MGR.GTT_TEMPSELECTMEASURE
7933	        ( PROGRAM_ID, SHEET_NAME, USER_ID, MEASURE_GROUP, MEASURE, RESULT_MEASURE, SORT_ORDER_NO )
7934	        WITH W_TARGET_MEASURE AS (
7935	            SELECT A.PROGRAM_ID
7936	                 , A.SHEET_NAME
7937	                 , 'LOC' AS TARGET_MEASURE
7938	                 , A.COLUMN_NAME AS MEASURE_GROUP
7939	                 , A.COLUMN_NAME AS MEASURE
7940	                 , A.COLUMN_NAME AS RESULT_MEASURE
7941	                 , TO_CHAR(A.SORT_ORDER_NO) AS SORT_ORDER_NO
7942	            FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_SHEET_MEASURE A
7943	            WHERE  1 = 1
7944	            AND    A.PROGRAM_ID = VA_PROGRAM_ID
7945	            AND    A.SHEET_NAME = VA_SHEET_NAME
7946	            AND    A.COLUMN_NAME IN ('RTF_SYS_RP', 'MKT_AMT_LOC')
7947	            UNION ALL
7948	            SELECT A.PROGRAM_ID
7949	                 , A.SHEET_NAME
7950	                 , 'KRW' AS TARGET_MEASURE
7951	                 , A.COLUMN_NAME AS MEASURE_GROUP
7952	                 , A.COLUMN_NAME AS MEASURE
7953	                 , A.COLUMN_NAME AS RESULT_MEASURE
7954	                 , TO_CHAR(A.SORT_ORDER_NO) AS SORT_ORDER_NO
7955	            FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_SHEET_MEASURE A
7956	            WHERE  1 = 1
7957	            AND    A.PROGRAM_ID = VA_PROGRAM_ID
7958	            AND    A.SHEET_NAME = VA_SHEET_NAME
7959	            AND    A.COLUMN_NAME IN ('RTF_SYS_RP', 'MKT_AMT_KRW')
7960	            -- [PJ2025-50188]소형 매출계획 동기화 개선 고도화 : FCST Gap 메저 추가 (MKT FCST - SCM FCST)  START -----
7961	            UNION ALL
7962	            SELECT A.PROGRAM_ID
7963	                 , A.SHEET_NAME
7964	                 , 'FCST_GAP' AS TARGET_MEASURE
7965	                 , A.COLUMN_NAME AS MEASURE_GROUP
7966	                 , A.COLUMN_NAME AS MEASURE
7967	                 , A.COLUMN_NAME AS RESULT_MEASURE
7968	                 , TO_CHAR(A.SORT_ORDER_NO) AS SORT_ORDER_NO
7969	            FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_SHEET_MEASURE A
7970	            WHERE  1 = 1
7971	            AND    A.PROGRAM_ID = VA_PROGRAM_ID
7972	            AND    A.SHEET_NAME = VA_SHEET_NAME
7973	            AND    A.COLUMN_NAME IN ('MKT_FCST', 'SCM_FCST')
7974	            -- [PJ2025-50188]소형 매출계획 동기화 개선 고도화 : FCST Gap 메저 추가 (MKT FCST - SCM FCST)  END -----
7975	        )
7976	        SELECT DISTINCT
7977	               W.PROGRAM_ID
7978	             , W.SHEET_NAME
7979	             , 'DM_ADMIN'       AS USER_ID  --USER_ID를 기준으로 단가 계산후 메저를 삭제함(사용자가 선택하지 않은 메저가 화면에 조회되면 안되므로)
7980	             , W.MEASURE_GROUP
7981	             , W.MEASURE
7982	             , W.RESULT_MEASURE
7983	             , W.SORT_ORDER_NO
7984	        FROM   W_TARGET_MEASURE W
7985	        JOIN   SCM_MGR.GTT_TEMPSELECTMEASURE T
7986	            ON  (W.PROGRAM_ID     = T.PROGRAM_ID
7987	           AND   W.SHEET_NAME     = T.SHEET_NAME
7988	           AND   W.TARGET_MEASURE = T.MEASURE)
7989	        WHERE  1 = 1
7990	        AND    NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1
7991	                           FROM   SCM_MGR.GTT_TEMPSELECTMEASURE X
7992	                           WHERE  1 = 1
7993	                           AND    X.MEASURE = W.MEASURE);
7994	    END IF;
7995	    ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
7996	    --실적구간 LOC,KRW를 계산하기 위해 필수 메저 RTF_SYS_RP, MKT_AMT_LOC, MKT_AMT_KRW 를 메저에 강제 등록 END
7997	    ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
7998	
7999	    --[PJ2025-50188]소형 매출계획 동기화 개선 고도화 : 속도개선 START----------------------------------------------
8000	    --LOC를 기준으로 CURRENCY를 일괄 업데이트 하기 때문에 무조건 해당 메저 INSERT
8001	    --DM_PLN_CFM_SELLING_PRICE 테이블로 JOIN 해서 업데이트 하면 속도가 너무 느림
8002	    INSERT INTO SCM_MGR.GTT_TEMPSELECTMEASURE
8003	    ( PROGRAM_ID, SHEET_NAME, USER_ID, MEASURE_GROUP, MEASURE, RESULT_MEASURE, SORT_ORDER_NO )
8004	    SELECT A.PROGRAM_ID
8005	         , A.SHEET_NAME
8006	         , 'DM_ADMIN'

8017	                       FROM   SCM_MGR.GTT_TEMPSELECTMEASURE X
8018	                       WHERE  1 = 1
8019	                       AND    X.MEASURE = A.COLUMN_NAME);

8686	             , C.FORECAST_INFO_SPEC
8687	             , C.FORECAST_INFOR_VERSION
8688	             , C.CELL_MODEL_NICK_NAME
8689	             , B.SORT_ORDER_NO
8690	             , LD.CREATE_DATE
8691	             , LD.LAST_UPDATED_BY
8692	             , A.CURRENCY
8693	             , 'CURRENT' AS VERSION_NO
8694	             , NULL      AS CUTOFF_YYYYMMDD
8695	             , CASE WHEN A.MEASURE IN ('KRW', 'USD', 'LOC','MKT_AMT_LOC', 'MKT_AMT_KRW') THEN 'POINT3' ELSE 'INTEGER' END AS NOTATION
8696	        FROM   SCM_MGR.DM_PLN_CFM_SELLING_PRICE A
8697	        INNER JOIN SCM_MGR.GTT_TEMPSELECTMEASURE B
8698	            ON  A.MEASURE = B.MEASURE
8699	        LEFT JOIN LATERAL (SELECT LD.CREATE_DATE, LD.LAST_UPDATED_BY
8700	                           FROM   SCM_MGR.GTT_T_LAST_DATA_SETUP_PRICE LD
8701	                           WHERE  LD.DIVISION_ID = A.DIVISION_ID AND LD.PROJECT_CODE = A.PROJECT_CODE AND LD.SCM_SITE_ID = A.SCM_SITE_ID AND LD.ITEM_ID = A.ITEM_ID
8702	                           AND    LD.DOM_EXP = A.DOM_EXP AND LD.FCST_TYPE = A.FCST_TYPE AND LD.INCOTERMS = A.INCOTERMS AND LD.MEASURE = A.MEASURE
8703	                           ORDER BY LD.CREATE_DATE DESC
8704	                           FETCH FIRST 1 ROWS ONLY) LD ON 1 = 1
8705	        INNER JOIN SCM_MGR.GTT_TEMPSITEITEM C
8706	            ON  A.DIVISION_ID   = C.DIVISION_ID
8707	            AND A.PROJECT_CODE  = C.PROJECT_CODE
8708	            AND A.SCM_SITE_ID   = C.SCM_SITE_ID
8709	            AND A.ITEM_ID       = C.ITEM_ID
8710	            AND C.ITEM_VALID_YN = 'Y'
8711	        INNER JOIN SCM_MGR.GTT_T_WS_FCST D
8712	            ON A.FCST_TYPE = D.FCST_TYPE
8713	        WHERE  1 = 1
8714	        AND    A.DIVISION_ID = p_division_id
8715	        AND    A.YYYYMMDD BETWEEN V_TO_RESULT AND V_TO_YYYYMMDD --계획구간만 : [PJ2025-50188] 실적 Unit Price 로직 변경
8716	        --AND A.QTY <> 0
8717	
8718	        UNION ALL
8719	
8720	        /* DM_PLN_MKT_FCST_RESULT  테이블 기준 MEASURE */
8721	        -- 2025.03.13 실적 (MKT_AMT_KRW,MKT_AMT_LOC Measure는 DM_PLN_SALES_PLAN_RESULT 테이블에서 가져옴) Start
8722	        SELECT A.DIVISION_ID
8723	             , A.PROJECT_CODE
8724	             , A.PROJECT_CODE AS END_CUSTOMER
8725	             , A.SCM_SITE_ID
8726	             , A.ITEM_ID
8727	             , A.DOM_EXP
8728	             , A.FCST_TYPE
8729	             , A.INCOTERMS
8730	             , B.MEASURE AS MEASURE -- 확인필요
8731	             , A.PLANT_SITE_ID
8732	             , A.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID           -- [PJ2025-50188] 매출실적 CELL_PROD_SITE_ID 추가 ('-' ==> CELL_PROD_SITE_ID 추가됨)
8733	             , A.YYYYMMDD
8734	             , A.QTY
8735	             , B.MEASURE_GROUP
8736	             , C.CELL_MODEL
8737	             , C.NICK_NAME
8738	             , C.DMS_SALES
8739	             , C.DMS_CUSTOMER
8740	             , C.OWNER
8741	             , C.DEPT_ID
8742	             , C.DEPT_NAME_KO
8743	             , C.FORECAST_INFO_CELL_PACK_COUNT
8744	             , C.ITEM_TYPE
8745	             , C.PROJECT_NAME
8746	             , C.PROJECT_OWNER
8747	             , C.SOLDTO_CUSTOMER
8748	             , C.COUNTRY_NAME
8749	             , C.CITY_NAME
8750	             , C.ITEM_NAME
8751	             , C.VMI_YN
8752	             , C.CUST_GROUP
8753	             , C.CUST_GROUP_DTL
8754	             , C.APPLICATION
8755	             , C.ROUTE
8756	             , C.MDM_PROJECT_NAME
8757	             , C.MIN_CAPACITY
8758	             , C.FORECAST_INFO_BIZ_TYPE
8759	             , C.CELL_TYPE
8760	             , C.FORECAST_INFO_ALL
8761	             , C.FORECAST_INFO_SPEC
8762	             , C.FORECAST_INFOR_VERSION
8763	             , C.CELL_MODEL_NICK_NAME
8764	             , B.SORT_ORDER_NO
8765	             , LD.CREATE_DATE
8766	             , LD.LAST_UPDATED_BY
8767	             /* IIF(A.MEASURE LIKE 'VMI%', '', A.CURRENCY) → CASE + NULL (C-7) — 실적데이터도 통화를 보여주기 위해 테이블 변경 [PJ2025-50188] */
8768	             , CASE WHEN A.MEASURE LIKE 'VMI%' THEN NULL ELSE A.CURRENCY END AS CURRENCY
8769	             , 'CURRENT' AS VERSION_NO
8770	             , NULL      AS CUTOFF_YYYYMMDD
8771	             , CASE WHEN A.MEASURE IN ('KRW', 'USD', 'LOC', 'MKT_AMT_LOC', 'MKT_AMT_KRW') THEN 'POINT3' ELSE 'INTEGER' END AS NOTATION
8772	        FROM   SCM_MGR.DM_PLN_SALES_PLAN_RESULT A --DM_PLN_MKT_FCST_RESULT → 실적데이터도 통화를 보여주기 위해 테이블 변경 [PJ2025-50188]
8773	        INNER JOIN SCM_MGR.GTT_TEMPSELECTMEASURE B
8774	            ON  A.MEASURE = B.RESULT_MEASURE
8775	        LEFT JOIN LATERAL (SELECT LD.CREATE_DATE, LD.LAST_UPDATED_BY
8776	                           FROM   SCM_MGR.GTT_T_LAST_DATA_SETUP_FCST LD
8777	                           WHERE  LD.DIVISION_ID = A.DIVISION_ID AND LD.PROJECT_CODE = A.PROJECT_CODE AND LD.SCM_SITE_ID = A.SCM_SITE_ID AND LD.ITEM_ID = A.ITEM_ID
8778	                           AND    LD.DOM_EXP = A.DOM_EXP AND LD.FCST_TYPE = A.FCST_TYPE AND LD.INCOTERMS = A.INCOTERMS AND LD.MEASURE = A.MEASURE
8779	                           ORDER BY LD.CREATE_DATE DESC
8780	                           FETCH FIRST 1 ROWS ONLY) LD ON 1 = 1
8781	        INNER JOIN SCM_MGR.GTT_TEMPSITEITEM C
8782	            ON  A.DIVISION_ID   = C.DIVISION_ID
8783	            AND A.PROJECT_CODE  = C.PROJECT_CODE
8784	            AND A.SCM_SITE_ID   = C.SCM_SITE_ID
8785	            AND A.ITEM_ID       = C.ITEM_ID
8786	            AND C.ITEM_VALID_YN = 'Y'
8787	        INNER JOIN SCM_MGR.GTT_T_WS_FCST D
8788	            ON A.FCST_TYPE = D.FCST_TYPE
8789	        WHERE  1 = 1
8790	        AND    A.DIVISION_ID = p_division_id
8791	        AND    A.YYYYMMDD >= p_from_yyyymmdd AND A.YYYYMMDD < V_TO_RESULT
8792	        AND    A.QTY <> 0;
8793	        END IF;  --[DIV81]
8794	
8795	        --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
8796	        -- CURRENCY 일괄 UPDATE (MSSQL UPDATE ... FROM JOIN → MERGE. GROUP BY+MAX 로 ORA-30926 방지 — MSSQL 임의채택의 결정적 대체)
8797	        --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
8798	        MERGE INTO SCM_MGR.GTT_TEMPFCSTNPRICE A
8799	        USING (SELECT SP.DIVISION_ID, SP.PROJECT_CODE, SP.SCM_SITE_ID, SP.PLANT_SITE_ID, SP.ITEM_ID
8800	                    , SP.INCOTERMS, SP.DOM_EXP, SP.FCST_TYPE, SP.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID
8801	                    , MAX(SP.CURRENCY) AS CURRENCY
8802	               --FROM DBO.DM_PLN_CFM_SELLING_PRICE SP WITH(NOLOCK)
8803	               FROM   SCM_MGR.GTT_TEMPFCSTNPRICE SP -- [PJ2025-50188]소형 매출계획 동기화 개선 고도화 : 속도개선
8804	               WHERE  1 = 1
8805	               AND    SP.DIVISION_ID = p_division_id
8806	               AND    SP.YYYYMMDD BETWEEN V_TO_RESULT AND V_TO_YYYYMMDD
8807	               AND    SP.MEASURE = 'LOC'
8808	               GROUP BY SP.DIVISION_ID, SP.PROJECT_CODE, SP.SCM_SITE_ID, SP.PLANT_SITE_ID, SP.ITEM_ID
8809	                    , SP.INCOTERMS, SP.DOM_EXP, SP.FCST_TYPE, SP.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID) SP
8810	        ON (    A.DIVISION_ID       = SP.DIVISION_ID
8811	            AND A.PROJECT_CODE      = SP.PROJECT_CODE
8812	            AND A.SCM_SITE_ID       = SP.SCM_SITE_ID
8813	            AND A.PLANT_SITE_ID     = SP.PLANT_SITE_ID
8814	            AND A.ITEM_ID           = SP.ITEM_ID
8815	            AND A.INCOTERMS         = SP.INCOTERMS
8816	            AND A.DOM_EXP           = SP.DOM_EXP
8817	            AND A.FCST_TYPE         = SP.FCST_TYPE
8818	            AND A.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID = SP.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID)
8819	        WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE
8820	            SET A.CURRENCY = SP.CURRENCY
8821	            WHERE A.YYYYMMDD BETWEEN p_from_yyyymmdd AND V_TO_YYYYMMDD -- [PJ2025-50188] 속도개선
8822	            AND   A.CURRENCY IS NULL                                   /* ISNULL(A.CURRENCY,'')='' → IS NULL (C-7) */
8823	            AND   A.MEASURE NOT LIKE 'VMI%';
8824	
8825	        -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
8826	        -- #TempFcstNPrice 에 INSERT 한 메저들을 이용하여 판가 실적 계산
8827	        -- LOC = MKT_AMT_LOC/RTF_SYS_RP
8828	        -- KRW = MKT_AMT_KRW/RTF_SYS_RP
8829	        -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
8830	        INSERT INTO SCM_MGR.GTT_TEMPFCSTNPRICE
8831	        ( DIVISION_ID       , PROJECT_CODE                  , END_CUSTOMER           , SCM_SITE_ID            , ITEM_ID              , DOM_EXP            , FCST_TYPE          , INCOTERMS        , MEASURE                 , PLANT_SITE_ID
8832	        , CELL_PROD_SITE_ID , YYYYMMDD                      , QTY                    , MEASURE_GROUP          , CELL_MODEL           , NICK_NAME          , DMS_SALES          , DMS_CUSTOMER     , OWNER                   , DEPT_ID
8833	        , DEPT_NAME_KO      , FORECAST_INFO_CELL_PACK_COUNT , ITEM_TYPE              , PROJECT_NAME           , PROJECT_OWNER
8834	        , SOLDTO_CUSTOMER   , COUNTRY_NAME                  , CITY_NAME
8835	        , ITEM_NAME         , VMI_YN                        , CUST_GROUP             , CUST_GROUP_DTL         , APPLICATION          , ROUTE              , MDM_PROJECT_NAME   , MIN_CAPACITY     , FORECAST_INFO_BIZ_TYPE  , CELL_TYPE
8836	        , FORECAST_INFO_ALL , FORECAST_INFO_SPEC            , FORECAST_INFOR_VERSION , CELL_MODEL_NICK_NAME
8837	        , SORT_ORDER_NO
8838	        , CREATE_DATE       , LAST_UPDATED_BY
8839	        , CURRENCY          , VERSION_NO                    , CUTOFF_YYYYMMDD        , NOTATION )
8840	        --기존 소스 개선 및 FCST GAP 추가 [PJ2025-50188] : FCST Gap 메저 추가 (MKT FCST - SCM FCST) START -------------------
8841	        SELECT A.DIVISION_ID
8842	             , A.PROJECT_CODE
8843	             , A.PROJECT_CODE AS END_CUSTOMER
8844	             , A.SCM_SITE_ID
8845	             , A.ITEM_ID
8846	             , A.DOM_EXP
8847	             , A.FCST_TYPE
8848	             , A.INCOTERMS
8849	             , D.MEASURE
8850	             , A.PLANT_SITE_ID
8851	             , A.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID          -- [PJ2025-50188] 매출실적 CELL_PROD_SITE_ID 추가
8852	             , A.YYYYMMDD
8853	             /* IIF → CASE (분모 0 방어 포함, AS-IS 동일) */
8854	             , CASE D.MEASURE
8855	                    WHEN 'LOC' THEN CASE WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN A.MEASURE = 'RTF_SYS_RP' THEN A.QTY ELSE 0 END) <> 0
8856	                                         THEN SUM(CASE WHEN A.MEASURE = 'MKT_AMT_LOC' THEN A.QTY ELSE 0 END)
8857	                                              / SUM(CASE WHEN A.MEASURE = 'RTF_SYS_RP' THEN A.QTY ELSE 0 END)
8858	                                         ELSE 0 END
8859	                    WHEN 'KRW' THEN CASE WHEN SUM(CASE WHEN A.MEASURE = 'RTF_SYS_RP' THEN A.QTY ELSE 0 END) <> 0
8860	                                         THEN SUM(CASE WHEN A.MEASURE = 'MKT_AMT_KRW' THEN A.QTY ELSE 0 END)
8861	                                              / SUM(CASE WHEN A.MEASURE = 'RTF_SYS_RP' THEN A.QTY ELSE 0 END)
8862	                                         ELSE 0 END
8863	               END AS QTY
8864	             , MAX(D.MEASURE_GROUP) AS MEASURE_GROUP
8865	             , MAX(A.CELL_MODEL) AS CELL_MODEL
8866	             , MAX(A.NICK_NAME) AS NICK_NAME
8867	             , MAX(A.DMS_SALES) AS DMS_SALES
8868	             , MAX(A.DMS_CUSTOMER) AS DMS_CUSTOMER
8869	             , MAX(A.OWNER) AS OWNER
8870	             , MAX(A.DEPT_ID) AS DEPT_ID
8871	             , MAX(A.DEPT_NAME_KO) AS DEPT_NAME_KO
8872	             , MAX(A.FORECAST_INFO_CELL_PACK_COUNT) AS FORECAST_INFO_CELL_PACK_COUNT
8873	             , MAX(A.ITEM_TYPE) AS ITEM_TYPE
8874	             , MAX(A.PROJECT_NAME) AS PROJECT_NAME
8875	             , MAX(A.PROJECT_OWNER) AS PROJECT_OWNER
8876	             , MAX(A.SOLDTO_CUSTOMER) AS SOLDTO_CUSTOMER
8877	             , MAX(A.COUNTRY_NAME) AS COUNTRY_NAME
8878	             , MAX(A.CITY_NAME) AS CITY_NAME
8879	             , MAX(A.ITEM_NAME) AS ITEM_NAME
8880	             , MAX(A.VMI_YN) AS VMI_YN
8881	             , MAX(A.CUST_GROUP) AS CUST_GROUP
8882	             , MAX(A.CUST_GROUP_DTL) AS CUST_GROUP_DTL
8883	             , MAX(A.APPLICATION) AS APPLICATION
8884	             , MAX(A.ROUTE) AS ROUTE
8885	             , MAX(A.MDM_PROJECT_NAME) AS MDM_PROJECT_NAME
8886	             , MAX(A.MIN_CAPACITY) AS MIN_CAPACITY
8887	             , MAX(A.FORECAST_INFO_BIZ_TYPE) AS FORECAST_INFO_BIZ_TYPE
8888	             , MAX(A.CELL_TYPE) AS CELL_TYPE
8889	             , MAX(A.FORECAST_INFO_ALL) AS FORECAST_INFO_ALL
8890	             , MAX(A.FORECAST_INFO_SPEC) AS FORECAST_INFO_SPEC
8891	             , MAX(A.FORECAST_INFOR_VERSION) AS FORECAST_INFOR_VERSION
8892	             , MAX(A.CELL_MODEL_NICK_NAME) AS CELL_MODEL_NICK_NAME
8893	             , MAX(D.SORT_ORDER_NO) AS SORT_ORDER_NO
8894	             , MAX(A.CREATE_DATE) AS CREATE_DATE
8895	             , MAX(A.LAST_UPDATED_BY) AS LAST_UPDATED_BY
8896	             , MAX(A.CURRENCY) AS CURRENCY
8897	             , 'CURRENT' AS VERSION_NO
8898	             , NULL      AS CUTOFF_YYYYMMDD
8899	             , CASE WHEN D.MEASURE IN ('KRW', 'USD', 'LOC','MKT_AMT_LOC', 'MKT_AMT_KRW') THEN 'POINT3' ELSE 'INTEGER' END AS NOTATION
8900	        FROM   SCM_MGR.GTT_TEMPFCSTNPRICE A
8901	        JOIN   SCM_MGR.GTT_TEMPSELECTMEASURE D
8902	            ON D.MEASURE IN ('LOC', 'KRW')
8903	        WHERE  1 = 1
8904	        AND    A.MEASURE IN ('RTF_SYS_RP', 'MKT_AMT_LOC', 'MKT_AMT_KRW')
8905	        AND    A.YYYYMMDD >= p_from_yyyymmdd
8906	        AND    A.YYYYMMDD <  V_TO_RESULT
8907	        GROUP BY A.DIVISION_ID, A.PROJECT_CODE, A.SCM_SITE_ID
8908	               , A.ITEM_ID, A.DOM_EXP, A.FCST_TYPE, A.INCOTERMS, A.PLANT_SITE_ID
8909	               , A.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID          -- [PJ2025-50188] 매출실적 CELL_PROD_SITE_ID 추가
8910	               , A.CURRENCY, A.YYYYMMDD, D.MEASURE;
8911	        -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
8912	        -- [PJ2025-50188]소형 매출계획 동기화 개선 고도화 : 실적 Unit Price 로직 변경 (MKT FCST Amt. /RTF[R+P])  END
8913	        -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
8914	    END IF;
8915	    --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
8916	    -- #TempFcstNPrice 만들기 종료 - VERSION - CURRENT
8917	    --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
8918	
8919	    -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
8920	    --[PJ2025-50188]소형 매출계획 동기화 개선 고도화 : 실적 Unit Price 로직 변경 START
8921	    --Unit Price 계산 후 불필요 MEASURE 삭제 (삭제하지 않으면 화면에서 보임)
8922	    -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
8923	    DELETE FROM SCM_MGR.GTT_TEMPFCSTNPRICE A
8924	    WHERE  EXISTS (SELECT 1
8925	                   FROM   SCM_MGR.GTT_TEMPSELECTMEASURE B
8926	                   WHERE  A.MEASURE = B.MEASURE
8927	                   AND    B.USER_ID = 'DM_ADMIN');
8928	    -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
8929	    --[PJ2025-50188]소형 매출계획 동기화 개선 고도화 : 실적 Unit Price 로직 변경 END
8930	    -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
8931	
8932	    /* [PLANT_SITE 2026-08-04] 조회조건 Plant Site 필터 (81 전용 — FE 40/82 비활성 = 'ALL') — GTT 단일 지점 */
8933	        /* [E20260819-MULTIJOIN] 조회조건 SCM Site — 구 스테이징 INSERT 별 인라인 OR분기(REGEXP 분해 10지점(MERGE 포함))를
8934	       PLANT_SITE 동형의 스테이징 후 단일 지점 GTT+NOT EXISTS 로 통합(그레인에 SCM_SITE_ID 포함 — 파생 메저 GROUP BY 실측, 최종 내용 동일). */
8935	    IF p_scm_site_id IS NOT NULL AND p_scm_site_id <> 'ALL' THEN
8936	        DELETE FROM SCM_MGR.GTT_TEMPFCSTNPRICE F
8937	        WHERE  NOT EXISTS (
8938	                   SELECT 1
8939	                   FROM   SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_PARAM_TOKEN T
8940	                   WHERE  T.PARAM_NM = 'p_scm_site_id'
8941	                   AND    T.TOKEN    = F.SCM_SITE_ID);
8942	    END IF;
8943	
8944	    IF p_plant_site_id IS NOT NULL AND p_plant_site_id <> 'ALL' THEN
8945	        DELETE FROM SCM_MGR.GTT_TEMPFCSTNPRICE
8946	        WHERE  PLANT_SITE_ID NOT IN (
8947	                   SELECT TOKEN
8948	                   FROM   (SELECT TOKEN FROM SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_PARAM_TOKEN WHERE PARAM_NM = 'p_plant_site_id'));
8949	    END IF;
8950	
8951	    /* [UICATEGORY 2026-08-07] 조회조건 Project / Owner 필터 (PLANT_SITE 동형 — GTT 단일 지점).
8952	       Sender Code 는 본 스테이징에 SENDER_CODE 컬럼 원천 부재 → GTT_MF_SP 적재 후 지점에서 처리 ([E20260819-SENDERJOIN] 실적용 — 무값 행 제외) */
8953	    IF p_project_code IS NOT NULL AND p_project_code <> 'ALL' THEN
8954	        DELETE FROM SCM_MGR.GTT_TEMPFCSTNPRICE
8955	        WHERE  NVL(PROJECT_CODE, ' ') NOT IN (
8956	                   SELECT TOKEN
8957	                   FROM   (SELECT TOKEN FROM SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_PARAM_TOKEN WHERE PARAM_NM = 'p_project_code'));
8958	    END IF;
8959	
8960	    IF p_owner IS NOT NULL AND p_owner <> 'ALL' THEN
8961	        DELETE FROM SCM_MGR.GTT_TEMPFCSTNPRICE
8962	        WHERE  NVL(OWNER, ' ') NOT IN (
8963	                   SELECT TOKEN
8964	                   FROM   (SELECT TOKEN FROM SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_PARAM_TOKEN WHERE PARAM_NM = 'p_owner'));
8965	    END IF;
