12418	    --==========================================================================================================================
12419	    -- 1. 40 Validation 필요 대상 : Sales Price 해당 주차 확인 (START)   --[E20260916-PRICEWEEK]
12420	    --==========================================================================================================================
12421	    ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
12422	    -- [E20250912-001720]_수정 저장 VALIDATION 추가 (START)
12423	    ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
12424	    INSERT INTO SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_TEMP_DAYINFO (DAYOFWEEKS)
12425	    SELECT DAYOFWEEK
12426	    FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M
12427	    WHERE BASIS_YYYYMMDD = TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'YYYYMMDD');
12428	
12429	    DBMS_STATS.GATHER_TABLE_STATS(OWNNAME => 'SCM_MGR', TABNAME => 'GT_PRDM1_TEMP_DAYINFO');
12430	
12431	    BEGIN
12432	        SELECT A.ATTRIBUTE1, A.ATTRIBUTE2, A.ATTRIBUTE3, A.ATTRIBUTE4
12433	        INTO   V_TARGET_DATE, V_FROM_TIME, V_TO_TIME, V_MEASURE_VAR
12434	        FROM (
12435	            SELECT B.SEGMENT3, B.ATTRIBUTE1, B.ATTRIBUTE2, B.ATTRIBUTE3, ATTRIBUTE4
12436	            FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL B
12437	            WHERE B.LOOKUP_TYPE = 'GET_ADMIN_GROUP_MEASURE'
12438	            AND B.VALID_YN = 'Y'
12439	            AND B.SEGMENT2 = 'DMF0082'
12440	            AND B.ATTRIBUTE4 IS NOT NULL
12441	            AND (B.ATTRIBUTE1 = 'ALL' OR B.ATTRIBUTE1 IN (SELECT DAYOFWEEKS FROM SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_TEMP_DAYINFO))
12442	            AND TRUNC(TO_NUMBER(REPLACE(SUBSTR(TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'HH24:MI:SS'), 1, 5), ':', '')))
12443	                BETWEEN TRUNC(TO_NUMBER(B.ATTRIBUTE2 DEFAULT NULL ON CONVERSION ERROR))
12444	                    AND TRUNC(TO_NUMBER(B.ATTRIBUTE3 DEFAULT NULL ON CONVERSION ERROR))
12445	            AND B.SEGMENT3 = V_DIVISION_ID
12446	        ) A
12447	        GROUP BY A.ATTRIBUTE1, A.ATTRIBUTE2, A.ATTRIBUTE3, ATTRIBUTE4;
12448	    EXCEPTION
12449	        WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN
12450	            V_TARGET_DATE := NULL; V_FROM_TIME := NULL; V_TO_TIME := NULL; V_MEASURE_VAR := NULL;
12451	        WHEN TOO_MANY_ROWS THEN
12452	            V_TARGET_DATE := NULL; V_FROM_TIME := NULL; V_TO_TIME := NULL; V_MEASURE_VAR := NULL;
12453	    END;
12454	
12455	    SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_cnt_check
12456	    FROM SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_T_MAIN A
12457	    INNER JOIN (
12458	        SELECT A.SEGMENT3 AS DIVISION_ID, A.ATTRIBUTE1 AS TARGET_DATE, A.ATTRIBUTE2 AS FROM_TIME, A.ATTRIBUTE3 AS TO_TIME
12459	             , REGEXP_SUBSTR(A.ATTRIBUTE4, '[^,]+', 1, LEVEL) AS MEASURE
12460	        FROM (
12461	            SELECT B.SEGMENT3, B.ATTRIBUTE1, B.ATTRIBUTE2, B.ATTRIBUTE3, B.ATTRIBUTE4
12462	            FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL B
12463	            WHERE B.LOOKUP_TYPE = 'GET_ADMIN_GROUP_MEASURE'
12464	            AND B.VALID_YN = 'Y'
12465	            AND B.SEGMENT2 = 'DMF0082'
12466	            AND B.ATTRIBUTE4 IS NOT NULL
12467	        ) A
12468	        CONNECT BY LEVEL <= REGEXP_COUNT(A.ATTRIBUTE4, ',') + 1
12469	             AND PRIOR A.SEGMENT3 = A.SEGMENT3 AND PRIOR A.ATTRIBUTE1 = A.ATTRIBUTE1
12470	             AND PRIOR SYS_GUID() IS NOT NULL
12471	    ) C ON A.DIVISION_ID = C.DIVISION_ID
12472	          AND A.MEASURE  = C.MEASURE
12473	    WHERE A.QTY_PRICE <> 0
12474	    AND (C.TARGET_DATE = 'ALL' OR C.TARGET_DATE IN (SELECT DAYOFWEEKS FROM SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_TEMP_DAYINFO))
12475	    AND TRUNC(TO_NUMBER(REPLACE(SUBSTR(TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'HH24:MI:SS'), 1, 5), ':', '')))
12476	        BETWEEN TRUNC(TO_NUMBER(C.FROM_TIME DEFAULT NULL ON CONVERSION ERROR))
12477	            AND TRUNC(TO_NUMBER(C.TO_TIME   DEFAULT NULL ON CONVERSION ERROR));
12478	
12479	    IF v_cnt_check > 1 THEN
12480	        V_LOG_TYPE    := 'INVALID';
12481	        V_DESCRIPTION := V_MEASURE_VAR || ' 메져는 "' || V_TARGET_DATE || '" 요일의 '
12482	                      || SUBSTR(V_FROM_TIME, 1, 2) || ':' || SUBSTR(V_FROM_TIME, 3, 2)
12483	                      || ' ~ ' || SUBSTR(V_TO_TIME, 1, 2) || ':' || SUBSTR(V_TO_TIME, 3, 2)
12484	                      || ' 시간에는 수정 불가능합니다.';
12485	        RAISE e_validation_error;
12486	    END IF;
12487	    ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
12488	    -- [E20250912-001720]_수정 저장 VALIDATION 추가 (END)
12489	    ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
12490	    ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
12491	    -- 1.1). SALES PRICE 미리 수집
12492	    ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
12493	    BEGIN
12494	        --[E20260916-PRICEWEEK] 판가 수집 구간 = **52주 고정 → 입력행이 속한 주차 범위** (사용자 확정 2026-09-16).
12495	        --  TO-BE RULE: "단가 입력 메저 값 입력 시 **해당 주차**의 Unit Price 가 없으면 오류".
12496	        --  판정이 행별 주차로 바뀌므로 수집도 52주 윈도우가 아니라 입력이 닿는 주차만 있으면 된다.
12497	        --  (구 [E20250825-000297] 52주 구간 산식 폐지 — 52주 단가 RULE 전면 삭제 지시)
12498	        --  범위(BETWEEN)로 잡는 이유: 40/81 두 수집 경로가 V_MIN_DATE/V_MAX_DATE 를 그대로 쓰기 때문.
12499	        --  실제 판정은 아래 검증 10 이 **주차 정확 일치**로 하므로 범위가 연속 구간이어도 결과 불변.
12500	        SELECT MIN(C.BASIS_START_DATE), MAX(C.BASIS_END_DATE)
12501	        INTO   V_MIN_DATE, V_MAX_DATE
12502	        FROM   SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_T_MAIN A
12503	        JOIN   SCM_MGR.SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M C ON C.BASIS_YYYYMMDD = A.YYYYMMDD;
12504	    EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN V_MIN_DATE := NULL; V_MAX_DATE := NULL;
12505	    END;
12506	
12507	    BEGIN SCM_MGR.PR_DM_SYS_LOG_BATCH($$PLSQL_UNIT, 'ING', N_LOG_COUNT,
12508	        '■ : A003--' || TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS'), '', SYSDATE,
12509	        v_created_by,
12510	        V_PROC_INPUT, NULL, NULL, NULL); END;
12511	
12512	    ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
12513	    -- 1.2). Sales Price **해당 주차** 데이터 TEMP 테이블 INSERT (START)   --[E20260916-PRICEWEEK] 구 '52주 구간'
12514	    -- 판가 확인은 UI에서 입력한 값이든, 이미 DB에 있던 값이든 상관없이 그 주차에 데이터가 있으면 통과한다.
12515	    ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
12516	    IF v_is_81 = 'N' THEN  --[DIV81] 판가 수집 분기  --[E20260916-PRICEWEEK] 구 '52주'
12517	    INSERT INTO SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_T_PRICE
12518	    ( DIVISION_ID, PROJECT_CODE, END_CUSTOMER, SCM_SITE_ID, PLANT_SITE_ID, CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, ITEM_ID
12519	    , YYYYMMDD, CURRENCY, MEASURE, DOM_EXP, FCST_TYPE, INCOTERMS, PRICE )
12520	    SELECT DIVISION_ID, PROJECT_CODE, END_CUSTOMER, SCM_SITE_ID, PLANT_SITE_ID, CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, ITEM_ID
12521	         , YYYYMMDD, CURRENCY, MEASURE, DOM_EXP, FCST_TYPE, INCOTERMS, PRICE
12522	    FROM (
12523	        SELECT DIVISION_ID, PROJECT_CODE, END_CUSTOMER, SCM_SITE_ID, PLANT_SITE_ID, CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, ITEM_ID
12524	             , YYYYMMDD, CURRENCY, MEASURE, DOM_EXP, FCST_TYPE, INCOTERMS, SUM(PRICE) AS PRICE
12525	        FROM (
12526	            -- DB에 있는 원래 데이터
12527	            SELECT DISTINCT B.DIVISION_ID, B.PROJECT_CODE, B.END_CUSTOMER, B.SCM_SITE_ID, B.PLANT_SITE_ID, B.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, B.ITEM_ID
12528	                          , B.YYYYMMDD, B.CURRENCY, B.MEASURE, B.DOM_EXP, B.FCST_TYPE, B.INCOTERMS, B.PRICE AS PRICE
12529	            FROM SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_T_MAIN A
12530	            JOIN SCM_MGR.DM_PLN_CFM_SELLING_PRICE B
12531	              ON A.DIVISION_ID       = B.DIVISION_ID
12532	             AND A.PROJECT_CODE      = B.PROJECT_CODE
12533	             AND A.END_CUSTOMER      = B.END_CUSTOMER
12534	             AND A.SCM_SITE_ID       = B.SCM_SITE_ID
12535	             --[E20260820-SENTINELDDL] 구 SENTINEL3 변형 동일 처리 삭제 — 원장 정규화·DDL·배치 정정 완료로 단순 등호
12536	             AND A.PLANT_SITE_ID     = B.PLANT_SITE_ID
12537	             AND A.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID = B.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID
12538	             AND A.ITEM_ID           = B.ITEM_ID
12539	             AND A.DOM_EXP           = B.DOM_EXP
12540	             AND A.FCST_TYPE         = B.FCST_TYPE
12541	             AND A.INCOTERMS         = B.INCOTERMS
12542	            WHERE B.DIVISION_ID  = A.DIVISION_ID
12543	              AND B.YYYYMMDD BETWEEN V_MIN_DATE AND V_MAX_DATE
12544	              AND B.PRICE <> 0
12545	              AND B.MEASURE = 'LOC'
12546	            UNION
12547	            ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
12548	            -- UI에서 입력한 신규 데이터 혹은 기존 DB 값을 UI에서 수정한 데이터
12549	            -- MKT Fcst 와 같이 입력되는 Price의 경우 Validation에 포함 (UI에서 MKT_FCST MEASURE랑 LOC MEASURE를 동시에 입력하는 경우를 말함)
12550	            ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
12551	            SELECT DISTINCT A.DIVISION_ID, A.PROJECT_CODE, A.END_CUSTOMER, A.SCM_SITE_ID, A.PLANT_SITE_ID, A.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, A.ITEM_ID
12552	                          , A.YYYYMMDD, A.CURRENCY, A.MEASURE, A.DOM_EXP, A.FCST_TYPE, A.INCOTERMS, A.QTY_PRICE AS PRICE
12553	            FROM SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_T_MAIN A
12554	            WHERE A.YYYYMMDD BETWEEN V_MIN_DATE AND V_MAX_DATE
12555	              AND A.QTY_PRICE <> 0
12556	              AND A.MEASURE = 'LOC'
12557	        ) A
12558	        GROUP BY DIVISION_ID, PROJECT_CODE, END_CUSTOMER, SCM_SITE_ID, PLANT_SITE_ID, CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, ITEM_ID
12559	               , YYYYMMDD, CURRENCY, MEASURE, DOM_EXP, FCST_TYPE, INCOTERMS
12560	    ) AA;
12561	    ELSE
12562	        --[DIV81] 1.2-81). Sales Price 52주 구간 수집 — SCM_MST_FUTURE_PRICE (정본 DMF0003 L619 이식)
12563	        --  81 조인키 8종 (END_CUSTOMER/CELL_PROD_SITE_ID 제외, PLANT_SITE_ID 조인 포함 — 81 정본 규약)
12564	        INSERT INTO SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_T_PRICE
12565	        ( DIVISION_ID, PROJECT_CODE, END_CUSTOMER, SCM_SITE_ID, PLANT_SITE_ID, CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, ITEM_ID
12566	        , YYYYMMDD, CURRENCY, MEASURE, DOM_EXP, FCST_TYPE, INCOTERMS, PRICE )
12567	        SELECT DIVISION_ID, PROJECT_CODE, END_CUSTOMER, SCM_SITE_ID, PLANT_SITE_ID, CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, ITEM_ID
12568	             , YYYYMMDD, CURRENCY, MEASURE, DOM_EXP, FCST_TYPE, INCOTERMS, SUM(PRICE) AS PRICE
12569	        FROM (
12570	            -- DB에 있는 원래 데이터 (81: SCM_MST_FUTURE_PRICE, placeholder '-' 부여)
12571	            SELECT DISTINCT B.DIVISION_ID, B.PROJECT_CODE, '-' AS END_CUSTOMER, B.SCM_SITE_ID, B.PLANT_SITE_ID
12572	                          , '-' AS CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, B.ITEM_ID
12573	                          , B.YYYYMMDD, B.CURRENCY, B.MEASURE, B.DOM_EXP, B.FCST_TYPE, B.INCOTERMS, B.PRICE AS PRICE
12574	            FROM SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_T_MAIN A
12575	            JOIN SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_FUTURE_PRICE B
12576	              ON A.DIVISION_ID   = B.DIVISION_ID
12577	             AND A.PROJECT_CODE  = B.PROJECT_CODE
12578	             AND A.SCM_SITE_ID   = B.SCM_SITE_ID
12579	             AND A.PLANT_SITE_ID = B.PLANT_SITE_ID
12580	             AND A.ITEM_ID       = B.ITEM_ID
12581	             AND A.DOM_EXP       = B.DOM_EXP
12582	             AND A.FCST_TYPE     = B.FCST_TYPE
12583	             AND A.INCOTERMS     = B.INCOTERMS
12584	            WHERE B.YYYYMMDD BETWEEN V_MIN_DATE AND V_MAX_DATE
12585	              AND B.PRICE <> 0
12586	              AND B.MEASURE = 'LOC'
12587	            UNION
12588	            -- UI에서 입력한 신규/수정 데이터 ('-' 정규화 값 그대로)
12589	            SELECT DISTINCT A.DIVISION_ID, A.PROJECT_CODE, A.END_CUSTOMER, A.SCM_SITE_ID, A.PLANT_SITE_ID
12590	                          , A.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, A.ITEM_ID
12591	                          , A.YYYYMMDD, A.CURRENCY, A.MEASURE, A.DOM_EXP, A.FCST_TYPE, A.INCOTERMS, A.QTY_PRICE AS PRICE
12592	            FROM SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_T_MAIN A
12593	            WHERE A.YYYYMMDD BETWEEN V_MIN_DATE AND V_MAX_DATE
12594	              AND A.QTY_PRICE <> 0
12595	              AND A.MEASURE = 'LOC'
12596	        ) AA81
12597	        GROUP BY DIVISION_ID, PROJECT_CODE, END_CUSTOMER, SCM_SITE_ID, PLANT_SITE_ID, CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, ITEM_ID
12598	               , YYYYMMDD, CURRENCY, MEASURE, DOM_EXP, FCST_TYPE, INCOTERMS;
12599	    END IF;  --[DIV81] 판가 수집 분기
12600	
12601	    DBMS_STATS.GATHER_TABLE_STATS(OWNNAME => 'SCM_MGR', TABNAME => 'GT_PRDM1_T_PRICE');

12612	    -- 1.3). 입력된 데이터의 기존 셀 생산지 데이터 수집 (START)
12613	    -- 셀 생산지가 Common인 경우에 다른 생산지는 입력 불가하도록 Validation 추가
12614	    ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
12615	    IF v_is_81 = 'N' THEN  --[DIV81] 1.3 은 CELL_PROD 검증(#8) 전용 — 81 제외
12616	    INSERT INTO SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_T_FCST
12617	    ( DIVISION_ID, PROJECT_CODE, END_CUSTOMER, SCM_SITE_ID, PLANT_SITE_ID, CELL_PROD_SITE_ID
12618	    , ITEM_ID, YYYYMMDD, MEASURE, DOM_EXP, FCST_TYPE, INCOTERMS )
12619	    SELECT DISTINCT B.DIVISION_ID, B.PROJECT_CODE, B.END_CUSTOMER, B.SCM_SITE_ID, B.PLANT_SITE_ID, B.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID
12620	                  , B.ITEM_ID, B.YYYYMMDD, B.MEASURE, B.DOM_EXP, B.FCST_TYPE, B.INCOTERMS
12621	    FROM SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_T_MAIN A
12622	    JOIN SCM_MGR.DM_PLN_CFM_MKT_DEMAND_PLAN B
12623	      ON A.DIVISION_ID  = B.DIVISION_ID
12624	     AND A.PROJECT_CODE = B.PROJECT_CODE
12625	     AND A.END_CUSTOMER = B.END_CUSTOMER
12626	     AND A.SCM_SITE_ID  = B.SCM_SITE_ID
12627	     AND A.PLANT_SITE_ID= B.PLANT_SITE_ID
12628	     --AND A.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID = B.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID
12629	     AND A.ITEM_ID      = B.ITEM_ID
12630	     AND A.DOM_EXP      = B.DOM_EXP
12631	     AND A.FCST_TYPE    = B.FCST_TYPE
12632	     AND A.INCOTERMS    = B.INCOTERMS
12633	     AND A.YYYYMMDD     = B.YYYYMMDD
12634	     AND A.MEASURE      = B.MEASURE
12635	    WHERE B.DIVISION_ID = A.DIVISION_ID
12636	      AND B.QTY <> 0
12637	      --AND B.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID = '-'
12638	      AND A.MEASURE IN ('MKT_FCST', 'VMI_In_Req', 'SCM_FCST'); --[PJ2025-50188] SCM_FCST - MEASURE 추가
12639	
12640	    DBMS_STATS.GATHER_TABLE_STATS(OWNNAME => 'SCM_MGR', TABNAME => 'GT_PRDM1_T_FCST');
12641	    END IF;  --[DIV81] 1.3 게이트
12642	    ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

12684	    -- 3. 40 Validation 필요 대상 : 중장기 구간 확인 (START)
12685	    --==========================================================================================================================
12686	    BEGIN
12687	        SELECT MIN(C.BASIS_YYYYMMDD), MAX(C.BASIS_YYYYMMDD)
12688	        INTO   V_FROM_DATE, V_TO_DATE
12689	        FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL L
12690	        JOIN SCM_MGR.SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M C
12691	          ON C.BASIS_YYYYMM = L.ATTRIBUTE1 OR C.BASIS_YYYYMM = L.ATTRIBUTE2
12692	        WHERE L.LOOKUP_TYPE = 'DM_LONG_PLAN' AND L.SEGMENT1 = '40' AND L.SEGMENT2 = 'MKT FCST' AND L.VALID_YN = 'Y';
12693	    EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN V_FROM_DATE := NULL; V_TO_DATE := NULL;
12694	    END;
12695	
12696	    ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
12697	    -- 소형일 경우 중장기계획 관련 2022년 입력 마감여부 확인
12698	    -- 소형이고 2022년이 마감되어있는데, 2022년 데이터가 들어있으면 에러처리
12699	    ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
12700	    IF V_DIVISION_ID = '40' THEN
12701	        SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_cnt_check
12702	        FROM SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_T_MAIN R
12703	        WHERE R.YYYYMMDD < V_FROM_DATE OR R.YYYYMMDD > V_TO_DATE;
12704	
12705	        IF v_cnt_check > 0 THEN
12706	            V_LOG_TYPE    := 'INVALID';
12707	            V_DESCRIPTION := V_FROM_DATE || ' ~ ' || V_TO_DATE || ' 구간 내의 데이터만 수정할 수 있습니다.';
12708	            RAISE e_validation_error;
12709	        END IF;
12710	    END IF;
12711	    --==========================================================================================================================
12712	    -- 3. 40 Validation 필요 대상 : 중장기 구간 확인 (END)
12713	    --==========================================================================================================================

12715	    BEGIN SCM_MGR.PR_DM_SYS_LOG_BATCH($$PLSQL_UNIT, 'ING', N_LOG_COUNT,
12716	        '■ : A007--' || TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS'), '', SYSDATE,
12717	        v_created_by,
12718	        V_PROC_INPUT, NULL, NULL, NULL); END;

12724	        SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_cnt_check

12741	
12742	    --==========================================================================================================================
12743	    -- 4. 40 Validation 필요 대상 : MKT Fcst Exception Model 대상 확인 (START)
12744	    --==========================================================================================================================
12745	    s_BLOCK_Model := '';
12746	    BEGIN
12747	        SELECT ITEM_ID INTO s_BLOCK_Model
12748	        FROM (
12749	            SELECT A.ITEM_ID
12750	            FROM SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_T_MAIN A

12753	                WHERE LOOKUP_TYPE = 'FCST_EXCEPT_MODEL' AND SEGMENT1 = '40' AND VALID_YN = 'Y' AND SEGMENT2 = A.ITEM_ID
12754	            ) > 0
12755	            AND A.QTY_PRICE <> 0
12756	            FETCH FIRST 1 ROWS ONLY
12757	        );
12758	    EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN s_BLOCK_Model := '';
12759	    END;
12760

12765	    END IF;

12774	
12775	    --==========================================================================================================================
12776	    -- 5. 40 Validation 필요 대상 :  Site/Item Master Valid 여부 확인 (START)
12777	    --==========================================================================================================================
12778	    BEGIN
12779	        v_valid_item_id := NULL;
12780	        BEGIN
12781	            SELECT A.ITEM_ID INTO v_valid_item_id
12782	            FROM SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_T_MAIN A
12783	            WHERE NOT EXISTS (
12784	                SELECT ITEM.DIVISION_ID
12785	                FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_SITE_ITEM ITEM
12786	                   , SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_ITEM_SN SN
12787	                WHERE ITEM.DIVISION_ID = A.DIVISION_ID
12788	                  AND ITEM.SCM_SITE_ID = A.SCM_SITE_ID
12789	                  AND ITEM.ITEM_ID     = A.ITEM_ID
12790	                  AND ITEM.DIVISION_ID = SN.DIVISION_ID
12791	                  AND ITEM.ITEM_ID     = SN.ITEM_ID
12792	                  AND SN.ACTIVE_FLAG   = 'Y'
12793	                  AND ITEM.VALID_YN    = 'Y'
12794	            )
12795	            FETCH FIRST 1 ROWS ONLY;
12796	        EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN v_valid_item_id := NULL;
12797	        END;
12798	
12799	        IF v_valid_item_id IS NOT NULL THEN
12800	            V_LOG_TYPE    := 'INVALID';
12801	            V_DESCRIPTION := '[' || v_valid_item_id || '] Invalid Site Item ID !';
12802	            RAISE e_validation_error;
12803	        END IF;
12804	    END;

12813	
12814	    --==========================================================================================================================
12815	    -- 6. 40 Validation 필요 대상 : BIZ_TYPE 존재여부 확인 (START)
12816	    --==========================================================================================================================
12817	    v_BIZ_TYPE_CHECK := NULL;
12818	    --[E20250714-000331]_[공급생산 SCM PI팀] FCSTPrice MGMT화면 전극(AP) Item id 입력 시 Biz Type Validation 제외 요청 (STRAT)
12819	
12820	    IF v_is_81 = 'N' THEN  --[DIV81] BIZ_TYPE 검증(#6)은 40 전용 (81 정본 DMF0003 에 없음)
12821	    ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
12822	    -- 6.1). 룩업: EXCEPTION_BIZ_TYPE 데이터를 #TEMP_EXCEPTION_BIZ_TYPE에 담는다.
12823	    ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
12824	    INSERT INTO SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_TEMP_EXCEPTION_BIZ_TYPE
12825	    ( DIVISION_ID, ITEM_ID, ITEM_TYPE, SEQ )
12826	    SELECT SEGMENT1 AS DIVISION_ID, SEGMENT2 AS ITEM_ID, SEGMENT3 AS ITEM_TYPE
12827	         , ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY ROWNUM) AS SEQ
12828	    FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL
12829	    WHERE LOOKUP_TYPE = 'EXCEPTION_BIZ_TYPE'
12830	    AND VALID_YN = 'Y'
12831	    AND SEGMENT1 = V_DIVISION_ID;
12832	
12833	    DBMS_STATS.GATHER_TABLE_STATS(OWNNAME => 'SCM_MGR', TABNAME => 'GT_PRDM1_TEMP_EXCEPTION_BIZ_TYPE');
12834	
12835	    ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
12836	    -- 6.2). 룩업에 저장된 데이터가 1건 이상인 경우가 존재할 수 있으므로, LOOP 실행하여,
12837	    --       한건씩 매핑된 데이터로 #TEMP_EXCEPTION_ITEM_LIST에 데이터 적재
12838	    --       ITEM_ID 일부로 시작하는 것으로 요청했으므로 뒷쪽만 LIKE 처리하여 데이터 처리
12839	    ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
12840	    V_ROWCNT := SQL%ROWCOUNT;
12841	
12842	    BEGIN
12843	        SELECT 1, COUNT(*) INTO V_I, V_CNT
12844	        FROM SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_TEMP_EXCEPTION_BIZ_TYPE;
12845	    EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN V_I := 1; V_CNT := 0;
12846	    END;
12847	
12848	    V_EXCEPTION_COUNT := 0;
12849	
12850	    WHILE V_I <= V_CNT LOOP
12851	        BEGIN
12852	            SELECT A.DIVISION_ID, A.ITEM_ID, A.ITEM_TYPE
12853	            INTO V_EXCEPTION_DIVISION_ID, V_EXCEPTION_ITEM_ID, V_EXCEPTION_ITEM_TYPE
12854	            FROM SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_TEMP_EXCEPTION_BIZ_TYPE A
12855	            WHERE SEQ = V_I;
12856	        EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN NULL;
12857	        END;
12858	
12859	        INSERT INTO SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_TEMP_EXCEPTION_ITEM_LIST
12860	        ( DIVISION_ID, ITEM_ID, ITEM_TYPE )
12861	        SELECT DISTINCT A.DIVISION_ID, A.ITEM_ID, V_EXCEPTION_ITEM_TYPE AS ITEM_TYPE
12862	        FROM SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_T_MAIN A
12863	        WHERE A.DIVISION_ID = V_EXCEPTION_DIVISION_ID
12864	        AND   A.ITEM_ID LIKE '' || V_EXCEPTION_ITEM_ID || '%';
12865	
12866	        V_I := V_I + 1;
12867	    END LOOP;
12868	
12869	    DBMS_STATS.GATHER_TABLE_STATS(OWNNAME => 'SCM_MGR', TABNAME => 'GT_PRDM1_TEMP_EXCEPTION_ITEM_LIST');
12870	
12871	    ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
12872	    -- 6.3).  #TEMP_EXCEPTION_ITEM_LIST에 존재하는 데이터는 NOT EXISTS 체크하여 VALIDATION 체크한다.
12873	    ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
12874	    BEGIN
12875	        v_BIZ_TYPE_CHECK := NULL;
12876	        BEGIN
12877	            SELECT A.ITEM_ID INTO v_BIZ_TYPE_CHECK
12878	            FROM (
12879	                SELECT T1.DIVISION_ID, T1.ITEM_ID, T2.ITEM_TYPE, T2.FORECAST_INFO_BIZ_TYPE
12880	                FROM SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_T_MAIN T1
12881	                LEFT JOIN LATERAL (
12882	                    SELECT T2.ITEM_ID, T2.ITEM_TYPE, T2.FORECAST_INFO_BIZ_TYPE
12883	                    FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_ITEM_M_SN T2
12884	                    WHERE T1.DIVISION_ID = T2.DIVISION_ID
12885	                      AND T1.ITEM_ID     = T2.ITEM_ID
12886	                      AND T2.ACTIVE_FLAG = 'Y'
12887	                ) T2 ON 1=1
12888	                WHERE T1.ACTION = 'I'
12889	                AND T2.FORECAST_INFO_BIZ_TYPE IS NULL
12890	                GROUP BY T1.DIVISION_ID, T1.ITEM_ID, T2.ITEM_TYPE, T2.FORECAST_INFO_BIZ_TYPE
12891	            ) A
12892	            WHERE NOT EXISTS (
12893	                SELECT B.ITEM_ID
12894	                FROM SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_TEMP_EXCEPTION_ITEM_LIST B
12895	                WHERE A.DIVISION_ID = B.DIVISION_ID
12896	                  AND A.ITEM_ID     = B.ITEM_ID
12897	                  AND A.ITEM_TYPE   = B.ITEM_TYPE
12898	            )
12899	            GROUP BY A.ITEM_ID
12900	            FETCH FIRST 1 ROWS ONLY;
12901	        EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN v_BIZ_TYPE_CHECK := NULL;
12902	        END;
12903	
12904	        --[E20250714-000331]_[공급생산 SCM PI팀] FCSTPrice MGMT화면 전극(AP) Item id 입력 시 Biz Type Validation 제외 요청 (END)
12905	        IF v_BIZ_TYPE_CHECK IS NOT NULL THEN
12906	            --BIZ_TYPE 이 없어서 저장을 할 수 없습니다.
12907	            --[E20260608-000294]_문구변경 요청으로 인하여 문구 변경 진행 (START)
12908	            --V_DESCRIPTION := 'Model*: ' || v_BIZ_TYPE_CHECK || '는 BIZ_TYPE 이 없어서 저장을 할 수 없습니다.'; --[E20260608-000294]_원소스 주석
12909	            V_DESCRIPTION := '1. MDM에서 Biz Type을 입력하시기 바랍니다.' || CHR(10)
12910	                          || '2. MDM에 Biz Type이 입력된 상태면 SCM_FP에 CSR 요청하시기 바랍니다.';
12911	            --[E20260608-000294]_문구변경 요청으로 인하여 문구 변경 진행 (END)
12912	            V_LOG_TYPE    := 'INVALID';
12913	            RAISE e_validation_error;
12914	        END IF;
12915	    END;
12916	    END IF;  --[DIV81] #6 게이트
12917	    --==========================================================================================================================
12918	    -- 6. 40 Validation 필요 대상 : BIZ_TYPE 존재여부 확인 (END)
12919	    --==========================================================================================================================
12920	
12921	    BEGIN SCM_MGR.PR_DM_SYS_LOG_BATCH($$PLSQL_UNIT, 'ING', N_LOG_COUNT,
12922	        '■ : A010--' || TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS'), '', SYSDATE,
12923	        v_created_by,
12924	        V_PROC_INPUT, NULL, NULL, NULL); END;
12925	
12926	    --==========================================================================================================================
12927	    -- 7. 40 Validation 필요 대상 : ITEM_ID가 CELL인 경우 CELL_PROD_SITE_ID를 Common으로 등록 할 수 없습니다 (START)
12928	    --==========================================================================================================================
12929	    v_CELL_TYPE_CHECK := NULL;
12930	
12931	    IF v_is_81 = 'N' THEN  --[DIV81] CELL_PROD_SITE_ID 검증(#7)은 40 전용 (81 은 컬럼 부재 — '-' 정규화)
12932	    BEGIN
12933	        v_CELL_TYPE_CHECK := NULL;
12934	        BEGIN
12935	            SELECT LISTAGG(A.ITEM_ID, ',') WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY A.ITEM_ID)
12936	            INTO v_CELL_TYPE_CHECK
12937	            FROM (
12938	                SELECT T1.ITEM_ID
12939	                FROM SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_T_MAIN T1
12940	                LEFT JOIN SCM_MGR.SCM_ITEM_M_SN T2
12941	                  ON T1.DIVISION_ID = T2.DIVISION_ID AND T1.ITEM_ID = T2.ITEM_ID
12942	                WHERE (T2.FORECAST_INFO_BIZ_TYPE = 'CELL' OR T1.ITEM_ID LIKE 'MC%')
12943	                  AND T1.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID = '-'
12944	                  AND T2.ACTIVE_FLAG = 'Y'
12945	                  AND NVL(T1.QTY_PRICE, 0) <> 0
12946	                  AND (T1.ACTION = 'I'
12947	                    OR NOT EXISTS (
12948	                        SELECT 1 FROM SCM_MGR.DM_PLN_CFM_MKT_DEMAND_PLAN X
12949	                        WHERE T1.DIVISION_ID       = X.DIVISION_ID
12950	                          AND T1.PROJECT_CODE      = X.PROJECT_CODE
12951	                          AND T1.END_CUSTOMER      = X.END_CUSTOMER
12952	                          AND T1.SCM_SITE_ID       = X.SCM_SITE_ID
12953	                          AND T1.PLANT_SITE_ID     = X.PLANT_SITE_ID
12954	                          AND T1.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID = X.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID
12955	                          AND T1.ITEM_ID           = X.ITEM_ID
12956	                          AND T1.DOM_EXP           = X.DOM_EXP
12957	                          AND T1.FCST_TYPE         = X.FCST_TYPE
12958	                          AND T1.INCOTERMS         = X.INCOTERMS
12959	                          AND X.MEASURE            = 'MKT_FCST'
12960	                          AND X.YYYYMMDD           >= v_plan_start_yyyymmdd
12961	                          AND X.QTY                <> 0
12962	                    ))
12963	                GROUP BY T1.ITEM_ID
12964	                FETCH FIRST 10 ROWS ONLY
12965	            ) A;
12966	        EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN v_CELL_TYPE_CHECK := NULL;
12967	        END;
12968	
12969	        IF v_CELL_TYPE_CHECK IS NOT NULL THEN
12970	            V_DESCRIPTION := 'Model*: ' || v_CELL_TYPE_CHECK || '는 Cell Prod Site* 를 Common으로 등록 할 수 없습니다.';
12971	            V_LOG_TYPE    := 'INVALID';
12972	            RAISE e_validation_error;
12973	        END IF;
12974	    END;
12975	    END IF;  --[DIV81] #7 게이트
12976	    --==========================================================================================================================
12977	    -- 7. 40 Validation 필요 대상 : ITEM_ID가 CELL인 경우 CELL_PROD_SITE_ID를 Common으로 등록 할 수 없습니다 (END)
12978	    --==========================================================================================================================
12979	
12980	    BEGIN SCM_MGR.PR_DM_SYS_LOG_BATCH($$PLSQL_UNIT, 'ING', N_LOG_COUNT,
12981	        '■ : A011--' || TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS'), '', SYSDATE,
12982	        v_created_by,
12983	        V_PROC_INPUT, NULL, NULL, NULL); END;
12984	
12985	    --==========================================================================================================================
12986	    -- 8. 40 Validation 필요 대상 : 셀 생산지가 Common인 경우에 다른 생산지는 입력 불가하도록 Validation 추가 (START)
12987	    --==========================================================================================================================
12988	    s_cell_prod_valid := NULL;
12989	
12990	    IF v_is_81 = 'N' THEN  --[DIV81] CELL_PROD 검증(#8)은 40 전용
12991	    BEGIN
12992	        BEGIN
12993	            SELECT A.ITEM_ID INTO s_cell_prod_valid
12994	            FROM (
12995	                SELECT P.DIVISION_ID, P.PROJECT_CODE, P.END_CUSTOMER, P.SCM_SITE_ID, P.PLANT_SITE_ID, P.ITEM_ID, P.YYYYMMDD
12996	                     , P.MEASURE, P.DOM_EXP, P.FCST_TYPE, P.INCOTERMS, P.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID AS CELL_PROD_SITE_FCST, T.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID AS CELL_PROD_SITE_MAIN
12997	                FROM SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_T_FCST P
12998	                INNER JOIN SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_T_MAIN T
12999	                  ON T.DIVISION_ID      = P.DIVISION_ID
13000	                 AND T.PROJECT_CODE     = P.PROJECT_CODE
13001	                 AND T.END_CUSTOMER     = P.END_CUSTOMER
13002	                 AND T.SCM_SITE_ID      = P.SCM_SITE_ID
13003	                 AND T.PLANT_SITE_ID    = P.PLANT_SITE_ID
13004	                 AND T.ITEM_ID          = P.ITEM_ID
13005	                 AND T.YYYYMMDD         = P.YYYYMMDD
13006	                 AND T.MEASURE          = P.MEASURE
13007	                 AND T.DOM_EXP          = P.DOM_EXP
13008	                 AND T.FCST_TYPE        = P.FCST_TYPE
13009	                 AND T.INCOTERMS        = P.INCOTERMS
13010	                 AND T.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID <> P.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID
13011	                WHERE T.ACTION = 'I'
13012	                GROUP BY P.DIVISION_ID, P.PROJECT_CODE, P.END_CUSTOMER, P.SCM_SITE_ID, P.PLANT_SITE_ID
13013	                       , P.ITEM_ID, P.YYYYMMDD, P.MEASURE, P.DOM_EXP, P.FCST_TYPE, P.INCOTERMS, P.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, T.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID
13014	                HAVING P.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID = '-' OR T.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID = '-'
13015	            ) A
13016	            FETCH FIRST 1 ROWS ONLY;
13017	        EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN s_cell_prod_valid := NULL;
13018	        END;
13019	
13020	        IF s_cell_prod_valid IS NOT NULL THEN
13021	            V_DESCRIPTION := 'Common과 다른 Cell Prod SIte를 중복으로 입력할 수 없습니다.';
13022	            V_LOG_TYPE    := 'INVALID';
13023	            RAISE e_validation_error;
13024	        END IF;
13025	    END;
13026	    END IF;  --[DIV81] #8 게이트
13027	    --==========================================================================================================================

13029	    --==========================================================================================================================
13030	
13031	    BEGIN SCM_MGR.PR_DM_SYS_LOG_BATCH($$PLSQL_UNIT, 'ING', N_LOG_COUNT,
13032	        '■ : A012--' || TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS'), '', SYSDATE,
13033	        v_created_by,
13034	        V_PROC_INPUT, NULL, NULL, NULL); END;

13038	    -- Common 식별을 위해 Common의 Dash 값은 XX로 치환하고 XX 를 포함 두개 이상의 Cell Prod Site가 입력되었는지를 확인.
13039	    --==========================================================================================================================
13040	    s_cell_prod_valid := NULL;
13041	
13042	    IF v_is_81 = 'N' THEN  --[DIV81] CELL_PROD 검증(#9)은 40 전용
13043	    BEGIN
13044	        BEGIN
13045	            SELECT 'N' INTO s_cell_prod_valid
13046	            FROM (
13047	                SELECT T.DIVISION_ID, T.PROJECT_CODE, T.END_CUSTOMER, T.SCM_SITE_ID, T.PLANT_SITE_ID
13048	                     , T.ITEM_ID, T.YYYYMMDD, T.MEASURE, T.DOM_EXP, T.FCST_TYPE, T.INCOTERMS
13049	                     , LISTAGG(CASE WHEN T.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID = '-' THEN 'XX' ELSE T.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID END, ', ')
13050	                        WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY T.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID) AS CELL_PROD_ALL
13051	                FROM SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_T_MAIN T
13052	                WHERE T.ACTION = 'I'
13053	                GROUP BY T.DIVISION_ID, T.PROJECT_CODE, T.END_CUSTOMER, T.SCM_SITE_ID, T.PLANT_SITE_ID
13054	                       , T.ITEM_ID, T.YYYYMMDD, T.MEASURE, T.DOM_EXP, T.FCST_TYPE, T.INCOTERMS
13055	                HAVING COUNT(*) > 1
13056	            ) A
13057	            WHERE INSTR(A.CELL_PROD_ALL, 'XX') > 0
13058	            FETCH FIRST 1 ROWS ONLY;
13059	        EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN s_cell_prod_valid := NULL;
13060	        END;

13076	        v_created_by,

13079	    --==========================================================================================================================
13080	    -- 10. 40 Validation 필요 대상 : Sales Price 해당 주차 확인 (START)   --[E20260916-PRICEWEEK]
13081	    --==========================================================================================================================
13082	    /* @s_unit_price_valid 변수내에는 Null이 발생하는 컬럼값이 존재하면 안됨 (변수전체가 Null로 저장됨)  */
13083	    INSERT INTO SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_MSHYCHU1 (SEGMENT2)
13084	    SELECT MST.SEGMENT2
13085	    FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL MST  -- SCM_FCST - MEASURE 추가, 아래의 룩업에 SCM_FCST 정보를 추가했습니다.
13086	    WHERE MST.LOOKUP_TYPE = 'MKT_FCST_VALIDATION_PRICE'
13087	    AND MST.SEGMENT1      = 'MEASURE'
13088	    AND MST.VALID_YN      = 'Y';
13089	
13090	    DBMS_STATS.GATHER_TABLE_STATS(OWNNAME => 'SCM_MGR', TABNAME => 'GT_PRDM1_MSHYCHU1');
13091	
13092	    INSERT INTO SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_MSHYCHU2 (SEGMENT2)
13093	    SELECT MST.SEGMENT2
13094	    FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL MST
13095	    WHERE MST.LOOKUP_TYPE = 'MKT_FCST_VALIDATION_PRICE'
13096	    AND MST.SEGMENT1      = 'FCST_TYPE'
13097	    AND MST.VALID_YN      = 'Y';
13098	
13099	    DBMS_STATS.GATHER_TABLE_STATS(OWNNAME => 'SCM_MGR', TABNAME => 'GT_PRDM1_MSHYCHU2');
13100	
13101	    s_unit_price_valid := NULL;
13102	    BEGIN
13103	        --[E20260922-MSGUNDERSCORE] 라벨 뒤 '_' 길이맞춤 제거 (사용자 요청 2026-09-22) — 라벨 9개(End Customer ~ YYYYMMDD) 를 '라벨 : 값' 형태로
13104	        SELECT 'End Customer : '   || NVL(A.END_CUSTOMER,  '')
13105	            || CHR(10) || 'Sales site : ' || NVL(A.SCM_SITE_ID,   '')
13106	            || CHR(10) || 'Model : '   || NVL(A.ITEM_ID,       '')
13107	            || CHR(10) || 'Customs Handbook : '      || NVL(A.DOM_EXP,       '')
13108	            || CHR(10) || 'Fcst Type : '  || NVL((
13109	                SELECT SEGMENT2 FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL MST
13110	                WHERE MST.LOOKUP_TYPE = 'SALES_TYPE_CODE' AND MST.SEGMENT1 = A.FCST_TYPE
13111	                FETCH FIRST 1 ROWS ONLY), '')
13112	            || CHR(10) || 'Incoterms : '   || NVL(A.INCOTERMS,     '')
13113	            || CHR(10) || 'Plant Site : '   || NVL(A.PLANT_SITE_ID, '')
13114	            || CHR(10) || 'Cell Prod Site : '|| NVL(A.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, '')
13115	            || CHR(10) || 'YYYYMMDD : '       || NVL(MIN(M.BASIS_START_DATE), '') || ' ~ ' || NVL(MAX(M.BASIS_START_DATE), '')
13116	            || CHR(10) || CHR(10)
13117	        INTO s_unit_price_valid
13118	        FROM SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_T_MAIN A
13119	        --[E20260915-PRICEPJTGAP] INNER JOIN SCM_MST_SITE_ITEM(Project Code 일치 행만 검사) 제거 → 아래 WHERE NOT EXISTS 로 전환.
13120	        --  종전(=AS-IS DMF0082_SAVE_MAIN_DATA L1185-1191 동일)에는 #5 Site/Item 검증이 Site+Model 로만 통과시키고
13121	        --  여기서는 Project 까지 맞는 마스터 행이 있어야 검사해, 다른 Project 로만 등록된 품목이 Unit Price 없이 저장됐다.
13122	        --  사용자 지시(2026-09-15): 이 경우도 누락되지 않도록 — AS-IS 대비 의도적 강화.
13123	        --[E20260916-PRICEWEEK] 52주 전체 주차와의 CROSS JOIN(M ON 1=1) 폐지 → **그 행이 속한 주차** 1건과만 대조.
13124	        --  AS-IS: 입력행 × 52주 전부 → 52주 중 한 주라도 판가가 없으면 저장 차단(전 구간 완비 요구).
13125	        --  TO-BE: 값을 입력하는 그 주차에 판가가 있으면 통과 (사용자 확정 2026-09-16).
13126	        --  ⚠ 날짜 동등비교가 아니라 **캘린더 주차 매핑**이다 — 입력행(T_MAIN)은 일 단위 행이 실재하고
13127	        --    (FE 가 주 단가를 월=값·화~일=0 으로 전송), 판가 원장은 주 시작일로 적재되기 때문이다.
13128	        JOIN SCM_MGR.SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M M
13129	          ON M.BASIS_YYYYMMDD = A.YYYYMMDD
13130	        LEFT JOIN SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_T_PRICE P
13131	               ON A.DIVISION_ID       = P.DIVISION_ID
13132	              AND A.PROJECT_CODE      = P.PROJECT_CODE
13133	              AND A.END_CUSTOMER      = P.END_CUSTOMER
13134	              AND A.SCM_SITE_ID       = P.SCM_SITE_ID
13135	              --[E20260820-SENTINELDDL] 구 SENTINEL3 변형 동일 처리 삭제 — 단순 등호
13136	              AND A.PLANT_SITE_ID     = P.PLANT_SITE_ID
13137	              AND A.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID = P.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID
13138	              AND A.ITEM_ID           = P.ITEM_ID
13139	              AND A.DOM_EXP           = P.DOM_EXP
13140	              AND A.FCST_TYPE         = P.FCST_TYPE
13141	              AND A.INCOTERMS         = P.INCOTERMS
13142	              AND P.YYYYMMDD          = M.BASIS_START_DATE
13143	              AND P.PRICE             > 0
13144	        WHERE P.YYYYMMDD IS NULL  -- PRICE값이 없는경우
13145	          AND A.MEASURE IN (SELECT SEGMENT2 FROM SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_MSHYCHU1)
13146	          AND A.QTY_PRICE <> 0
13147	          --[E20260915-PRICEPJTGAP] 전 행 검사 기본 — 마스터(Division+Project+Site+Model)에서
13148	          --  판매계획 조정 제외(SALES_PLAN_ADJUST_YN='N')로 명시된 경우만 제외(종전 제외 규칙 보존).
13149	          AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1
13150	                            FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_SITE_ITEM Z
13151	                           WHERE Z.DIVISION_ID  = A.DIVISION_ID
13152	                             AND Z.PROJECT_CODE = A.PROJECT_CODE
13153	                             AND Z.SCM_SITE_ID  = A.SCM_SITE_ID
13154	                             AND Z.ITEM_ID      = A.ITEM_ID
13155	                             AND Z.VALID_YN     = 'Y'
13156	                             AND Z.SALES_PLAN_ADJUST_YN = 'N')
13157	          --[E20260814-FCSTNAME] 통합테스트 No.49 — "수량·판가를 입력했는데 판가 미입력 팝업" 오탐 정정.
13158	          --   제외 목록(MKT_FCST_VALIDATION_PRICE / SEGMENT1='FCST_TYPE')은 **코드**(ASF·SDF·SMF·SPF·
13159	          --   TST·WSF)로만 적재되는데, FCST_TYPE 이 **명칭**('Spare Parts(FREE)' 등)으로 들어온 행은
13160	          --   NOT IN 을 그대로 통과해 52주 판가 게이트에 걸렸다.
13161	          --   실측(2026-08-14 게이트 시뮬): 같은 데이터셋이 'SPF' → 위반 0건(정상 제외) /
13162	          --                                'Spare Parts(FREE)' → 위반 62주(오탐 차단).
13163	          --   명칭 유입은 가정이 아니라 원장 실재다 — DM_PLN_CFM_SELLING_PRICE(div 40)에
13164	          --   FCST_TYPE='Mass' 585행(AS-IS 원장에도 105행) 존재. 즉 AS-IS 부터의 혼재이므로
13165	          --   입력측 교정만으로는 기존 행을 구제하지 못한다 ⇒ 판정측에서 코드·명칭 양쪽을 제외한다.
13166	          AND A.FCST_TYPE NOT IN (
13167	                  SELECT X.SEGMENT2 FROM SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_MSHYCHU2 X          /* 코드 (원 조건) */
13168	                  UNION ALL
13169	                  SELECT L.SEGMENT2                                            /* 그 코드의 명칭 */
13170	                  FROM   SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_MSHYCHU2 X
13171	                  JOIN   SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL L
13172	                         ON L.LOOKUP_TYPE = 'SALES_TYPE_CODE'
13173	                        AND L.VALID_YN    = 'Y'
13174	                        AND L.SEGMENT1    = X.SEGMENT2 )
13175	        GROUP BY A.DIVISION_ID, A.PROJECT_CODE, A.END_CUSTOMER, A.SCM_SITE_ID, A.ITEM_ID, A.DOM_EXP, A.FCST_TYPE
13176	               , A.INCOTERMS, A.MEASURE, A.PLANT_SITE_ID, A.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, P.YYYYMMDD
13177	        ORDER BY A.DIVISION_ID, A.PROJECT_CODE, A.END_CUSTOMER, A.SCM_SITE_ID, A.ITEM_ID, A.DOM_EXP, A.FCST_TYPE
13178	               , A.INCOTERMS, A.MEASURE, A.PLANT_SITE_ID, A.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, P.YYYYMMDD
13179	        FETCH FIRST 1 ROWS ONLY;
13180	    EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN s_unit_price_valid := NULL;
13181	    END;
13182	
13183	    IF s_unit_price_valid IS NOT NULL THEN
13184	        V_LOG_TYPE    := 'INVALID';
13185	        --[E20260916-PRICEWEEK] 52주 문구 삭제 — 판정이 '해당 주차' 단위로 바뀌었다.
13186	        V_DESCRIPTION := s_unit_price_valid || 'Unit Price Not Found !' || CHR(10)
13187	                      || '해당주차의 Sales Price 값을 입력하시기 바랍니다.';
13188	        RAISE e_validation_error;
13189	    END IF;

13242	        v_created_by,

13244	
13245	    --==========================================================================================================================
13246	    -- 13. 40 Validation 필요 대상 :  BOM Valid 여부 확인 (START)
13247	    -- 저장하려는 ITEM의 11주 구간에 BOM이 없으면 값을 저장하지 못하도록 함
13248	    --==========================================================================================================================
13249	    UPDATE SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_T_MAIN A
13250	    SET V_BOM_YN = 'N'
13251	    WHERE EXISTS (
13252	        SELECT 1
13253	        FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL B
13254	        JOIN SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_ITEM_SN C ON A.DIVISION_ID = C.DIVISION_ID AND A.ITEM_ID = C.ITEM_ID
13255	        JOIN SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL D ON D.LOOKUP_TYPE = 'DM_BOM_EXCEPT_CLASS' AND D.VALID_YN = 'Y' AND C.EVAL_CLASS = D.SEGMENT1
13256	        WHERE B.LOOKUP_TYPE = 'AS_SITE_PLANT'
13257	          AND B.SEGMENT3    = V_DIVISION_ID
13258	          AND B.VALID_YN    = 'Y'
13259	          AND A.V_ATTRIBUTE10 = B.SEGMENT2
13260	    )
13261	    AND A.DIVISION_ID = V_DIVISION_ID
13262	    AND A.MEASURE IN ('MKT_FCST', 'SCM_FCST'); --SCM_FCST - MEASURE 추가
13263	
13264	    SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_cnt_check FROM SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_T_MAIN WHERE V_BOM_YN IS NULL;
13265	
13266	    IF v_cnt_check > 0 THEN
13267	        -- 저장전 변수 초기화
13268	        v_valid_bom  := NULL;
13269	        v_valid_shipto := NULL;
13270	
13271	        BEGIN
13272	            SELECT A.ITEM_ID INTO v_valid_bom
13273	            FROM SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_T_MAIN A
13274	            INNER JOIN SCM_MGR.DM_MST_ITEM_SINGLE_V C
13275	                    ON A.ITEM_ID       = C.ITEM_ID
13276	                   AND A.DIVISION_ID   = C.DIVISION_ID
13277	                   AND C.ITEM_TYPE IN ('P', 'H')
13278	                   AND C.ACTIVE_FLAG   = 'Y'
13279	            LEFT JOIN SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_BOM_ENG_SN X
13280	                   ON A.DIVISION_ID = X.DIVISION_ID AND A.ITEM_ID = X.PARENT_ITEM_ID
13281	            LEFT JOIN (
13282	                SELECT DIVISION_ID
13283	                     , CASE WHEN DIVISION_ID IN ('82','40') THEN '-' ELSE FROM_SITE END FROM_SITE  /* 2026.08.15 AJS 하이픈통일 02 작업지시 1순위 4자리 + MSSQL 원본 1305/1308/1348/1351 등가('82' 누락 정정) */
13284	                     , TO_SITE, INCOTERMS, MIN(LT_TOTAL) AS LT_TOTAL
13285	                FROM SCM_MGR.SCP_MST_BOD_V
13286	                WHERE PRIORITY = 1
13287	                GROUP BY DIVISION_ID
13288	                       , CASE WHEN DIVISION_ID IN ('82','40') THEN '-' ELSE FROM_SITE END  /* 2026.08.15 AJS 하이픈통일 02 작업지시 1순위 4자리 + MSSQL 원본 1305/1308/1348/1351 등가('82' 누락 정정) */
13289	                       , TO_SITE, INCOTERMS
13290	            ) BOD ON A.DIVISION_ID  = BOD.DIVISION_ID
13291	                 AND A.PLANT_SITE_ID = BOD.FROM_SITE
13292	                 AND A.SCM_SITE_ID   = BOD.TO_SITE
13293	                 AND A.INCOTERMS     = BOD.INCOTERMS
13294	            WHERE A.MEASURE IN ('MKT_FCST', 'Vmi_In_Req', 'SCM_FCST') --SCM_FCST - MEASURE 추가
13295	              AND A.YYYYMMDD >= TO_CHAR(SCM_MGR.FN_NEXT_DAY(SYSDATE, 'MON') - 7, 'YYYYMMDD')   -- 당주 월요일부터
13296	              AND A.YYYYMMDD  < TO_CHAR(SCM_MGR.FN_NEXT_DAY(SYSDATE, 'MON') + NVL(BOD.LT_TOTAL, 0) + 7 * 10, 'YYYYMMDD')   -- 당주 월요일 + 11주 까지
13297	              AND A.QTY_PRICE > 0
13298	              AND X.PARENT_ITEM_ID IS NULL  -- 존재하지 않는대상
13299	              AND A.ITEM_ID NOT IN ('ECE0074N5-01')
13300	              AND A.ITEM_ID NOT IN ('MCCH120022B2-K', 'MCCH120022B2-GD')
13301	              AND A.V_BOM_YN IS NULL
13302	            FETCH FIRST 1 ROWS ONLY;
13303	        EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN v_valid_bom := NULL;
13304	        END;
13305	
13306	        IF v_valid_bom IS NOT NULL THEN
13307	            V_LOG_TYPE    := 'INVALID';
13308	            V_DESCRIPTION := '[' || v_valid_bom || '] BOM does not exist !';
13309	            RAISE e_validation_error;
13310	        END IF;
13311	    END IF;

13338	                     , CASE WHEN DIVISION_ID IN ('82','40') THEN '-' ELSE FROM_SITE END FROM_SITE  /* 2026.08.15 AJS 하이픈통일 02 작업지시 1순위 4자리 + MSSQL 원본 1305/1308/1348/1351 등가('82' 누락 정정) */
13339	                     , TO_SITE, INCOTERMS, MIN(LT_TOTAL) AS LT_TOTAL
13340	                FROM SCM_MGR.SCP_MST_BOD_V
13341	                WHERE PRIORITY = 1
13342	                GROUP BY DIVISION_ID
13343	                       , CASE WHEN DIVISION_ID IN ('82','40') THEN '-' ELSE FROM_SITE END  /* 2026.08.15 AJS 하이픈통일 02 작업지시 1순위 4자리 + MSSQL 원본 1305/1308/1348/1351 등가('82' 누락 정정) */

13345	            ) BOD ON A.DIVISION_ID  = BOD.DIVISION_ID
13346	                 AND A.PLANT_SITE_ID = BOD.FROM_SITE
13347	                 AND A.SCM_SITE_ID   = BOD.TO_SITE
13348	                 AND A.INCOTERMS     = BOD.INCOTERMS
13349	            WHERE A.MEASURE IN ('MKT_FCST', 'Vmi_In_Req', 'SCM_FCST') --[PJ2025-50188] SCM_FCST - MEASURE 추가
13350	              AND A.YYYYMMDD >= TO_CHAR(SCM_MGR.FN_NEXT_DAY(SYSDATE, 'MON') - 7, 'YYYYMMDD')   -- 당주 월요일부터
13351	              AND A.YYYYMMDD  < TO_CHAR(SCM_MGR.FN_NEXT_DAY(SYSDATE, 'MON') + NVL(BOD.LT_TOTAL, 0) + 7 * 3, 'YYYYMMDD')   -- BOD LT + 4W
13352	              AND A.QTY_PRICE > 0
13353	              AND X.SHIPTO_CODE IS NULL

13395	              AND A.DOM_EXP                = RSLT.DOM_EXP

13397	              AND A.INCOTERMS              = RSLT.INCOTERMS
13398	              AND A.PLANT_SITE_ID          = RSLT.PLANT_SITE_ID
13399	              AND A.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID      = RSLT.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID
13400	              AND ITEM.ACTIVE_FLAG         = 'Y'
13401	              AND RSLT.FCST_TYPE           = 'RMA'
13402	      );

13408	    , ATTRIBUTE7, ATTRIBUTE8, ATTRIBUTE9, ATTRIBUTE10, CELL_PROD_SITE_ID )

13410	         , RSLT.DOM_EXP, RSLT.FCST_TYPE, RSLT.INCOTERMS, 'RTF_SYS_RP' AS MEASURE
13411	         , RSLT.PLANT_SITE_ID, RSLT.YYYYMMDD
13412	         --[E20260309-000271]_ISNULL처리 추가 (START)

13418	         , RSLT.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID

13422	           AND RSLT.ITEM_ID      = ITEM.ITEM_ID

13424	           AND RSLT.FCST_TYPE    = 'RMA'

13426	      AND RSLT.YYYYMMDD >= v_plan_start_yyyymmdd; -- 당주 월요일이후
13427	    END IF;  --[DIV81] #16 게이트

13439	    -- 17. 40 Validation 필요 대상 :  Site/Item Master Valid 여부 확인 (START)
13440	    -- DM_PLN_CFM_SELLING_PRICE Validation Check
13441	    --==========================================================================================================================

13443	    FROM SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_T_MAIN

13452	                         ON ITEM.DIVISION_ID = A.DIVISION_ID
13453	                        AND ITEM.SCM_SITE_ID = A.SCM_SITE_ID
13454	                        AND ITEM.ITEM_ID     = A.ITEM_ID
13455	                        AND ITEM.VALID_YN    = 'Y'
13456	            LEFT OUTER JOIN SCM_MGR.DM_MST_ITEM_SINGLE_V SN
13457	                         ON ITEM.DIVISION_ID = SN.DIVISION_ID
13458	                        AND ITEM.ITEM_ID     = SN.ITEM_ID
13459	                        AND SN.ACTIVE_FLAG   = 'Y'
13460	            WHERE A.QTY_PRICE <> 0
13461	              AND (ITEM.ITEM_ID IS NULL OR SN.ITEM_ID IS NULL)
13462	            FETCH FIRST 1 ROWS ONLY;

13476

13478	        '■ : A019--' || TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS'), '', SYSDATE,
13479	        v_created_by,
13480	        V_PROC_INPUT, NULL, NULL, NULL); END;
13481	
13482	    --==========================================================================================================================
13483	    -- Measure가 LOC이면서 , 같은 월의 같은 주차의 합이 0이라면 D로 업데이트 한다. 그 외는 M으로 업데이트 한다. (START)
13484	    --==========================================================================================================================
13485	    --[E20260822-LOCWEEKSUM] 주차 합계가 '일 합계' 로 축소된 변환 결함 정정 (2026-08-22).
13486	    --   구 구현은 바깥 행 날짜에 맞추는 상관 조건을 **서브쿼리 안**에 두어, 윈도우 함수가 돌기 전에
13487	    --   행이 그 날짜 1건으로 좁혀졌다 → PARTITION BY 주차가 무의미해지고 **하루치 합계**로 판정됐다.
13488	    --   실사고: FE 가 주 단가를 (월=5301, 화~일=0) 로 보내면 0 인 6일이 각각 ACTION='D' 가 되어
13489	    --   검증 #18(SALES PRICE 삭제 시 52주 FCST 확인)이 **수정인데** 발동, 저장이 막혔다.
13490	    --   AS-IS(MSSQL)는 파생테이블에서 전체 LOC 행에 파티션 합계를 먼저 구하고 **바깥에서** 날짜로 조인한다:
13491	    --       UPDATE #T_MAIN SET [ACTION] = DD.[ACTION]
13492	    --       FROM ( SELECT CASE WHEN SUM(A.QTY_PRICE) OVER (PARTITION BY D.ISO_YYYYWW + D.PARTIAL_WEEK_NAME) = 0
13493	    --                          THEN 'D' ELSE 'M' END AS [ACTION], D.BASIS_YYYYMMDD
13494	    --              FROM #T_MAIN A JOIN SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M D ON A.YYYYMMDD = D.BASIS_YYYYMMDD
13495	    --              WHERE A.MEASURE='LOC' AND A.[ACTION] <> 'I' ) DD
13496	    --       WHERE YYYYMMDD = DD.BASIS_YYYYMMDD AND MEASURE='LOC'
13497	    --   그 구조를 그대로 살린다. MAX(ACT) 는 중복 제거용 — 같은 날짜는 같은 주차라 ACT 가 행마다 동일하다.
13498	    --   `A.ACTION <> 'I'` 는 ON 이 아니라 UPDATE 의 WHERE 에 둔다(Oracle 은 ON 절 컬럼을 갱신 못 함).
13499	    MERGE INTO SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_T_MAIN A
13500	    USING (
13501	        SELECT BASIS_YYYYMMDD, MAX(ACT) AS ACT
13502	        FROM (
13503	            SELECT D.BASIS_YYYYMMDD
13504	                 , CASE WHEN SUM(B.QTY_PRICE) OVER (PARTITION BY D.ISO_YYYYWW || D.PARTIAL_WEEK_NAME) = 0
13505	                        THEN 'D' ELSE 'M' END AS ACT
13506	            FROM SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_T_MAIN B
13507	            JOIN SCM_MGR.SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M D ON B.YYYYMMDD = D.BASIS_YYYYMMDD
13508	            WHERE B.MEASURE = 'LOC' AND B.ACTION <> 'I'
13509	        )
13510	        GROUP BY BASIS_YYYYMMDD
13511	    ) S
13512	    ON (A.YYYYMMDD = S.BASIS_YYYYMMDD AND A.MEASURE = 'LOC')
13513	    WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET A.ACTION = S.ACT WHERE A.ACTION <> 'I';
13514	    --==========================================================================================================================
13515	    -- Measure가 LOC이면서 , 같은 월의 같은 주차의 합이 0이라면 D로 업데이트 한다. 그 외는 M으로 업데이트 한다.(END)
13516	    --==========================================================================================================================
13517	
13518	    BEGIN SCM_MGR.PR_DM_SYS_LOG_BATCH($$PLSQL_UNIT, 'ING', N_LOG_COUNT,
13519	        '■ : A020--' || TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS'), '', SYSDATE,
13520	        v_created_by,
13521	        V_PROC_INPUT, NULL, NULL, NULL); END;
13522	
13523	    --==========================================================================================================================
13524	    -- 18. 40 Validation 필요 대상 : SALES PRICE 삭제 시 **해당 주차** MKT FCST or RTF 데이터 확인 (START)   --[E20260916-PRICEWEEK]
13525	    --==========================================================================================================================
13526	    v_invalid_Site := NULL;
13527	    n_Dflag_chk    := 0;
13528	
13529	    -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
13530	    -- ACTIVE_FLAG가 한건이라도 D인경우에만 처리한다.
13531	    -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
13532	    SELECT COUNT(*) INTO n_Dflag_chk
13533	    FROM SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_T_MAIN
13534	    WHERE MEASURE = 'LOC' AND ACTION = 'D';  -- Delete 가능 - UI에서 MKT_FCST를 D로 보내는 경우가 있어서 작성함.
13535	
13536	    IF n_Dflag_chk > 0 THEN
13537	      IF v_is_81 = 'N' THEN  --[DIV81] #18 분기: 40=DM_PLN_CFM_MKT_DEMAND_PLAN / 81=DM_PLN_MKT_FCST(하단 ELSE)
13538	        -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
13539	        -- 52주 이후의 데이터의 FLAG를 0으로 변경하여 삭제하려고 하는 경우는 아래 로직으로 데이터를 확인한다.
13540	        -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
13541	        BEGIN
13542	            v_invalid_Site := NULL;
13543	            BEGIN
13544	                SELECT '● Sales Site : ' || F.SCM_SITE_ID || ' / ● Model : ' || F.ITEM_ID
13545	                INTO v_invalid_Site
13546	                FROM SCM_MGR.DM_PLN_CFM_MKT_DEMAND_PLAN F
13547	                JOIN SCM_MGR.SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M B ON F.YYYYMMDD = B.BASIS_YYYYMMDD
13548	                WHERE EXISTS (
13549	                    SELECT A.DIVISION_ID
13550	                    FROM SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_T_MAIN A
13551	                    JOIN SCM_MGR.SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M D ON A.YYYYMMDD = D.BASIS_YYYYMMDD
13552	                    WHERE A.ACTION         = 'D'
13553	                      AND F.DIVISION_ID    = A.DIVISION_ID
13554	                      AND F.PROJECT_CODE   = A.PROJECT_CODE
13555	                      AND F.SCM_SITE_ID    = A.SCM_SITE_ID
13556	                      AND F.PLANT_SITE_ID  = A.PLANT_SITE_ID
13557	                      AND F.ITEM_ID        = A.ITEM_ID
13558	                      AND F.DOM_EXP        = A.DOM_EXP
13559	                      AND F.FCST_TYPE      = A.FCST_TYPE
13560	                      AND F.INCOTERMS      = A.INCOTERMS
13561	                      AND A.MEASURE        = 'LOC'
13562	                      AND F.MEASURE IN ('MKT_FCST', 'RTF_SCP', 'AS_In_Req', 'SCM_FCST') -- SCM_FCST - MEASURE 추가
13563	                      AND F.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID = A.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID
13564	                      --[E20260916-PRICEWEEK] '52주 이후' 윈도우 폐지 (사용자 확정 2026-09-16 — 52주 단가 RULE 전면 삭제).
13565	                      --  남은 상관조건(B.ISO_YYYYWW = D.ISO_YYYYWW)이 곧 TO-BE 룰이다:
13566	                      --  "단가 입력 메저 값이 존재하는 **그 주차**의 Unit Price 는 삭제 불가".
13567	                      --  구 (b) '52주 이내(주차 무관)' 블록은 이 규칙에 흡수되어 제거했다.
13568	                      AND B.BASIS_START_DATE = D.BASIS_START_DATE
13569	                      AND F.QTY <> 0  -- 제로값은 제외함
13570	                      AND F.FCST_TYPE NOT IN (
13571	                            SELECT SEGMENT1 FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL MST
13572	                            WHERE MST.LOOKUP_TYPE = 'SALES_TYPE_CODE' AND MST.SEGMENT4 = 'FREE'
13573	                            GROUP BY SEGMENT1
13574	                      )  -- 무상제공인경우는 Validation 에서 제외
13575	                )
13576	                  --[E20260917-SAMESAVEDEL] 같은 저장에서 그 FCST 도 0 으로 지워지는 중이면 '존재'로 보지 않는다.
13577	                  --  사용자 확인 2026-09-17: 2단계(메저 삭제·저장 → 단가 삭제)는 되고 1회 저장만 막혔다.
13578	                  --  원장은 검증 시점에 아직 옛 값이라, 이번 저장분(GT_PRDM1_T_MAIN)의 0 행을 차감한다.
13579	                  --  등록 가드 #10 이 '입력값 + DB값' 합집합을 보는 것과 축을 맞춘 것이다.
13580	                  AND NOT EXISTS (
13581	                        SELECT 1
13582	                          FROM SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_T_MAIN Z
13583	                         WHERE Z.DIVISION_ID       = F.DIVISION_ID
13584	                           AND Z.PROJECT_CODE      = F.PROJECT_CODE
13585	                           AND Z.SCM_SITE_ID       = F.SCM_SITE_ID
13586	                           AND Z.PLANT_SITE_ID     = F.PLANT_SITE_ID
13587	                           AND Z.ITEM_ID           = F.ITEM_ID
13588	                           AND Z.DOM_EXP           = F.DOM_EXP
13589	                           AND Z.FCST_TYPE         = F.FCST_TYPE
13590	                           AND Z.INCOTERMS         = F.INCOTERMS
13591	                           AND Z.MEASURE           = F.MEASURE
13592	                           AND Z.YYYYMMDD          = F.YYYYMMDD
13593	                           AND Z.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID = F.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID
13594	                           AND NVL(Z.QTY_PRICE, 0) = 0)
13595	                FETCH FIRST 1 ROWS ONLY;
13596	            EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN v_invalid_Site := NULL;
13597	            END;
13598	
13599	            IF v_invalid_Site IS NOT NULL THEN
13600	                V_DESCRIPTION := 'MKT FCST or RTF or AS_In_Req or SCM_FCST 정보가 해당 주차에 존재하므로 Sales Price 삭제가 불가합니다.' || CHR(10) || v_invalid_Site;
13601	                V_LOG_TYPE    := 'INVALID';
13602	                RAISE e_validation_error;
13603	            END IF;
13604	        END;
13605	
13606	        --[E20260916-PRICEWEEK] 구 '52주 이내' 블록 삭제 (사용자 확정 2026-09-16).
13607	        --  그 블록은 키만 맞으면 **주차 상관없이** 52주 윈도우 안의 FCST 존재만으로 삭제를 막았다.
13608	        --  TO-BE 는 '값이 존재하는 그 주차' 기준이므로 위 (a) 블록(동일 ISO 주차)이 유일한 판정이다.
13609	      ELSE
13610	        --[DIV81] 18-81). Price 삭제 시 52주 FCST 확인 — DM_PLN_MKT_FCST 기준 (정본 DMF0020 L545~670 이식)
13611	        --  키 8종 (END_CUSTOMER/CELL_PROD 제외), 대상 MEASURE = MKT_FCST/RTF_SCP/AS_In_Req (81 정본 — SCM_FCST 없음)
13612	        -- (a) 52주 이후 동일 주차 존재 확인
13613	        BEGIN
13614	            v_invalid_Site := NULL;
13615	            BEGIN
13616	                SELECT '● Sales Site : ' || F.SCM_SITE_ID || ' / ● Model : ' || F.ITEM_ID
13617	                INTO v_invalid_Site
13618	                FROM SCM_MGR.DM_PLN_MKT_FCST F
13619	                JOIN SCM_MGR.SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M B ON F.YYYYMMDD = B.BASIS_YYYYMMDD
13620	                WHERE EXISTS (
13621	                    SELECT A.DIVISION_ID
13622	                    FROM SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_T_MAIN A
13623	                    JOIN SCM_MGR.SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M D ON A.YYYYMMDD = D.BASIS_YYYYMMDD
13624	                    WHERE A.ACTION         = 'D'
13625	                      AND F.DIVISION_ID    = A.DIVISION_ID
13626	                      AND F.PROJECT_CODE   = A.PROJECT_CODE
13627	                      AND F.SCM_SITE_ID    = A.SCM_SITE_ID
13628	                      AND F.PLANT_SITE_ID  = A.PLANT_SITE_ID
13629	                      AND F.ITEM_ID        = A.ITEM_ID
13630	                      AND F.DOM_EXP        = A.DOM_EXP
13631	                      AND F.FCST_TYPE      = A.FCST_TYPE
13632	                      AND F.INCOTERMS      = A.INCOTERMS
13633	                      AND A.MEASURE        = 'LOC'
13634	                      AND F.MEASURE IN ('MKT_FCST', 'RTF_SCP', 'AS_In_Req')
13635	                      --[E20260916-PRICEWEEK] '52주 이후' 윈도우 폐지 — 40 경로와 동일 (동일 ISO 주차 상관조건만 유지)
13636	                      AND B.BASIS_START_DATE = D.BASIS_START_DATE
13637	                      AND F.QTY <> 0
13638	                      AND F.FCST_TYPE NOT IN (
13639	                            SELECT SEGMENT1 FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL MST
13640	                            WHERE MST.LOOKUP_TYPE = 'SALES_TYPE_CODE' AND MST.SEGMENT4 = 'FREE'
13641	                            GROUP BY SEGMENT1
13642	                      )
13643	                )
13644	                  --[E20260917-SAMESAVEDEL] 같은 저장에서 그 FCST 도 0 으로 지워지는 중이면 '존재'로 보지 않는다.
13645	                  --  사용자 확인 2026-09-17: 2단계(메저 삭제·저장 → 단가 삭제)는 되고 1회 저장만 막혔다.
13646	                  --  원장은 검증 시점에 아직 옛 값이라, 이번 저장분(GT_PRDM1_T_MAIN)의 0 행을 차감한다.
13647	                  --  등록 가드 #10 이 '입력값 + DB값' 합집합을 보는 것과 축을 맞춘 것이다.
13648	                  AND NOT EXISTS (
13649	                        SELECT 1
13650	                          FROM SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_T_MAIN Z
13651	                         WHERE Z.DIVISION_ID       = F.DIVISION_ID
13652	                           AND Z.PROJECT_CODE      = F.PROJECT_CODE
13653	                           AND Z.SCM_SITE_ID       = F.SCM_SITE_ID
13654	                           AND Z.PLANT_SITE_ID     = F.PLANT_SITE_ID
13655	                           AND Z.ITEM_ID           = F.ITEM_ID
13656	                           AND Z.DOM_EXP           = F.DOM_EXP
13657	                           AND Z.FCST_TYPE         = F.FCST_TYPE
13658	                           AND Z.INCOTERMS         = F.INCOTERMS
13659	                           AND Z.MEASURE           = F.MEASURE
13660	                           AND Z.YYYYMMDD          = F.YYYYMMDD
13661	                           AND NVL(Z.QTY_PRICE, 0) = 0)
13662	                FETCH FIRST 1 ROWS ONLY;
13663	            EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN v_invalid_Site := NULL;
13664	            END;
13665	
13666	            IF v_invalid_Site IS NOT NULL THEN
13667	                V_DESCRIPTION := 'MKT FCST or RTF or AS_In_Req 정보가 해당 주차에 존재하므로 Sales Price 삭제가 불가합니다.' || CHR(10) || v_invalid_Site;
13668	                V_LOG_TYPE    := 'INVALID';
13669	                RAISE e_validation_error;
13670	            END IF;
13671	        END;
13672	
13673	        --[E20260916-PRICEWEEK] 구 (b) '52주 이내' 블록 삭제 — 40 경로와 동일 사유 (주차 무관 판정 폐지)
13674	      END IF;  --[DIV81] #18 분기
13675	    END IF;
13676

13686	    --==========================================================================================================================
13687	    --[DIV81] 19. 81 전용 Validation (V81-01 ~ V81-11) — 정본: DMF0003_SAVE_MKT_FCST / DMF0020_SAVE_SCM_MST_FUTURE_PRICE_ALL (START)
13688	    --==========================================================================================================================
13689	    IF v_is_81 = 'Y' THEN
13690	
13691	        -- V81-01. 필수 컬럼 + PLANT_SITE_ID 필수 (DMF0003 L566 이식 + plant site 요구사항 2026-07-14)
13692	        SELECT COUNT(*) INTO v_cnt_check
13693	        FROM SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_T_MAIN A
13694	        --[E20260820-SENTINELDDL] NVL 언랩 — GTT 키 컬럼 DEFAULT ON NULL '-' NOT NULL 승격으로 NULL 불가
13695	        WHERE A.DIVISION_ID   = '-'
13696	           OR A.PROJECT_CODE  = '-'
13697	           OR A.SCM_SITE_ID   = '-'
13698	           OR A.ITEM_ID       = '-'
13699	           OR A.DOM_EXP       = '-'
13700	           OR A.FCST_TYPE     = '-'
13701	           OR A.INCOTERMS     = '-'
13702	           OR A.MEASURE       = '-'
13703	           OR A.PLANT_SITE_ID = '-';
13704	
13705	        IF v_cnt_check > 0 THEN
13706	            V_LOG_TYPE    := 'INVALID';
13707	            V_DESCRIPTION := ' Check Mandatory Columns ( Fcst type or Incoterms or Customs Handbook or Plant Site ) !';
13708	            RAISE e_validation_error;
13709	        END IF;
13710	
13711	        -- V81-02. AS PLANT 로 가는 DP 의 INCOTERMS 제한 (DMF0003 L731 이식 — C 조인 무필터 포함 정본 그대로)
13712	        SELECT COUNT(1) INTO v_cnt_check
13713	        FROM   SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_T_MAIN A
13714	        JOIN   SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL B
13715	          ON   A.DIVISION_ID  = B.SEGMENT3
13716	         AND   A.SCM_SITE_ID  = B.SEGMENT1
13717	         AND   B.LOOKUP_TYPE  = 'AS_SITE_PLANT'
13718	         AND   B.VALID_YN     = 'Y'
13719	        LEFT   JOIN SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL C
13720	          ON   A.DIVISION_ID  = C.SEGMENT1
13721	         AND   A.INCOTERMS    = C.SEGMENT2
13722	         AND   C.VALID_YN     = 'Y'
13723	        WHERE  C.SEGMENT1 IS NULL;
13724	
13725	        IF v_cnt_check > 0 THEN
13726	            V_LOG_TYPE    := 'INVALID';
13727	            V_DESCRIPTION := 'AS In Request 메저는 지정된 Incoterms 만 사용가능합니다.';
13728	            RAISE e_validation_error;
13729	        END IF;
13730	
13731	        -- V81-03. Invalid Project Code (DMF0003 L847 ≡ DMF0020 L280 이식)
13732	        V_VALID_PROJECT_CODE := NULL;
13733	        BEGIN
13734	            SELECT A.PROJECT_CODE INTO V_VALID_PROJECT_CODE
13735	            FROM   SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_T_MAIN A
13736	            WHERE  NOT EXISTS (
13737	                       SELECT C.DIVISION_ID
13738	                       FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_PROJECT C
13739	                       WHERE  A.DIVISION_ID  = C.DIVISION_ID
13740	                         AND  A.PROJECT_CODE = C.PROJECT_CODE
13741	                         AND  C.DIVISION_ID <> '40'
13742	                   )
13743	              AND  ROWNUM = 1;
13744	        EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN V_VALID_PROJECT_CODE := NULL;
13745	        END;
13746	
13747	        IF V_VALID_PROJECT_CODE IS NOT NULL THEN
13748	            V_LOG_TYPE    := 'INVALID';
13749	            V_DESCRIPTION := '[' || V_VALID_PROJECT_CODE || '] Invalid Project Code !';
13750	            RAISE e_validation_error;
13751	        END IF;
13752	
13753	        -- V81-04. Cell/Module/Pack 인데 CELL_PER_CAR 미등록 (DMF0003 L1123 이식 — 스테이징 GTT 대신 뷰 직조인)
13754	        --   [E20260820-XLSSAVEPERF] DM_MST_ITEM_SINGLE_V 직조인 폐지 — 소비 컬럼(ITEM_CLASS_NAME)은
13755	        --   뷰의 T1 축(SCM_MST_ITEM_SN GROUP BY DIVISION_ID, ITEM_ID 의 MAX)에서만 오므로 뷰 내부
13756	        --   T2(SUBSTR 셀프조인)가 불필요. 뷰 직조인은 JPPD 시 키별 PK SKIP SCAN(15키 407s),
13757	        --   NO_MERGE 힌트 시 뷰 전체 실체화(30.6s) — 둘 다 병목 (ASH 실측 2026-08-20).
13758	        --   행 존재성 동치 근거: 뷰의 T2 는 동일 테이블 셀프집계 INNER JOIN 이라 T1 행이 있으면 항상 매칭.
13759	        v_valid_item_id := NULL;
13760	        BEGIN
13761	            SELECT A.ITEM_ID INTO v_valid_item_id
13762	            FROM   SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_T_MAIN A
13763	            LEFT   JOIN SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_SITE_ITEM F
13764	              ON   A.DIVISION_ID  = F.DIVISION_ID
13765	             AND   A.ITEM_ID      = F.ITEM_ID
13766	             AND   A.PROJECT_CODE = F.PROJECT_CODE
13767	             AND   A.SCM_SITE_ID  = F.SCM_SITE_ID
13768	            JOIN   (SELECT SN.DIVISION_ID, SN.ITEM_ID, MAX(SN.ITEM_CLASS_NAME) AS ITEM_CLASS_NAME
13769	                    FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_ITEM_SN SN
13770	                    GROUP  BY SN.DIVISION_ID, SN.ITEM_ID) X
13771	              ON   A.DIVISION_ID = X.DIVISION_ID
13772	             AND   A.ITEM_ID     = X.ITEM_ID
13773	            WHERE  CASE WHEN INSTR(X.ITEM_CLASS_NAME,'Cell')   > 0 AND NVL(F.CELL_PER_CAR,0) = 0 THEN 1
13774	                        WHEN INSTR(X.ITEM_CLASS_NAME,'Module') > 0 AND NVL(F.CELL_PER_CAR,0) = 0 THEN 1
13775	                        WHEN INSTR(X.ITEM_CLASS_NAME,'Pack')   > 0 AND NVL(F.CELL_PER_CAR,0) = 0 THEN 1
13776	                        ELSE 0
13777	                   END = 1
13778	              AND  ROWNUM = 1;
13779	        EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN v_valid_item_id := NULL;
13780	        END;
13781	
13782	        IF v_valid_item_id IS NOT NULL THEN
13783	            BEGIN
13784	                SELECT CASE WHEN INSTR(X.ITEM_CLASS_NAME,'Module') > 0 THEN 'Cell/Car or Cell/Module'
13785	                            ELSE 'Cell/Car' END
13786	                INTO   V_VALID_TYPE
13787	                FROM   (SELECT MAX(SN.ITEM_CLASS_NAME) AS ITEM_CLASS_NAME  --[E20260820-XLSSAVEPERF] 뷰 대체(T1 축 동치)
13788	                        FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_ITEM_SN SN
13789	                        WHERE  SN.ITEM_ID = v_valid_item_id
13790	                        GROUP  BY SN.DIVISION_ID) X
13791	                WHERE  ROWNUM = 1;
13792	            EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN V_VALID_TYPE := '-';
13793	            END;
13794	            V_LOG_TYPE    := 'INVALID';
13795	            V_DESCRIPTION := '[' || v_valid_item_id || '] ' || V_VALID_TYPE || ' does not exist !';
13796	            RAISE e_validation_error;
13797	        END IF;
13798	
13799	        -- V81-05. Kwh/Cell(ATTRIBUTE8) / CELL_TYPE(ATTRIBUTE10) / CELL_PER_CAR 필수 (DMF0003 L1170 이식)
13800	        V_DESC_81 := NULL;
13801	        BEGIN
13802	            SELECT V_DESCRIPTION_81 INTO V_DESC_81
13803	            FROM (
13804	                SELECT '[' || RTRIM(A.ITEM_ID) || '] '
13805	                    || CASE WHEN (NOT REGEXP_LIKE(NVL(B.ATTRIBUTE8,'-'), '^[0-9.]+$')
13806	                                  OR TO_NUMBER(CASE WHEN REGEXP_LIKE(NVL(B.ATTRIBUTE8,'0'), '^[0-9.]+$') THEN B.ATTRIBUTE8 ELSE '0' END) <= 0)
13807	                            THEN 'Kwh/Cell does not exist !'
13808	                            WHEN B.ATTRIBUTE10 NOT IN (
13809	                                     SELECT SEGMENT2 FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL
13810	                                     WHERE LOOKUP_TYPE = 'CELL_TYPE_INFO' AND (SEGMENT1 LIKE '81%' OR SEGMENT1 = '82') AND VALID_YN = 'Y'
13811	                                 )
13812	                            THEN 'CELL_TYPE does not exist !'
13813	                            WHEN NVL(B.CELL_PER_CAR,0) <= 0
13814	                            THEN 'Cell/Car does not exist !'
13815	                       END
13816	                    || CHR(10) || 'Sales Site Project Model 에서 해당 항목을 채워주세요.' AS V_DESCRIPTION_81
13817	                FROM SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_T_MAIN A
13818	                JOIN SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_SITE_ITEM B
13819	                  ON A.DIVISION_ID  = B.DIVISION_ID
13820	                 AND A.PROJECT_CODE = B.PROJECT_CODE
13821	                 AND A.SCM_SITE_ID  = B.SCM_SITE_ID
13822	                 AND A.ITEM_ID      = B.ITEM_ID
13823	                JOIN SCM_MGR.DM_MST_ITEM_SINGLE_V C
13824	                  ON A.DIVISION_ID  = C.DIVISION_ID
13825	                 AND A.ITEM_ID      = C.ITEM_ID
13826	                 AND C.ITEM_TYPE    = 'P'
13827	                WHERE (
13828	                        ( NOT REGEXP_LIKE(NVL(B.ATTRIBUTE8,'-'), '^[0-9.]+$')
13829	                          OR TO_NUMBER(CASE WHEN REGEXP_LIKE(NVL(B.ATTRIBUTE8,'0'), '^[0-9.]+$') THEN B.ATTRIBUTE8 ELSE '0' END) <= 0 )
13830	                        OR B.ATTRIBUTE10 NOT IN (
13831	                               SELECT SEGMENT2 FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL
13832	                               WHERE LOOKUP_TYPE = 'CELL_TYPE_INFO' AND (SEGMENT1 LIKE '81%' OR SEGMENT1 = '82') AND VALID_YN = 'Y'
13833	                           )
13834	                        OR NVL(B.CELL_PER_CAR,0) <= 0
13835	                      )
13836	                ORDER BY A.ITEM_ID
13837	                FETCH FIRST 1 ROW ONLY
13838	            );
13839	        EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN V_DESC_81 := NULL;
13840	        END;
13841	
13842	        IF V_DESC_81 IS NOT NULL THEN
13843	            V_DESCRIPTION := V_DESC_81;
13844	            V_LOG_TYPE    := 'INVALID';
13845	            RAISE e_validation_error;
13846	        END IF;
13847	
13848	        -- V81-06. Invalid Item ID (DMF0020 L324 이식 — ITEM_SN 존재)
13849	        v_valid_item_id := NULL;
13850	        BEGIN
13851	            SELECT A.ITEM_ID INTO v_valid_item_id
13852	            FROM   SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_T_MAIN A
13853	            WHERE  NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_ITEM_SN I WHERE I.ITEM_ID = A.ITEM_ID)
13854	              AND  ROWNUM = 1;
13855	        EXCEPTION WHEN NO_DATA_FOUND THEN v_valid_item_id := NULL;
13856	        END;
13857	
13858	        IF v_valid_item_id IS NOT NULL THEN
13859	            V_LOG_TYPE    := 'INVALID';
13860	            V_DESCRIPTION := '[' || v_valid_item_id || '] Invalid Item ID !';
13861	            RAISE e_validation_error;
13862	        END IF;
13863	
13864	        -- V81-07. Invalid Sales Site (DMF0020 L365 [V20260708] 이식 — 조회 JOIN 정합. price 행 한정 + 삭제행 제외)
13865	        SELECT COUNT(1) INTO v_cnt_check
13866	        FROM   SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_T_MAIN R
13867	        WHERE  R.ACTION <> 'D'
13868	          AND  R.MEASURE = 'LOC'
13869	          AND  NOT EXISTS (
13870	                   SELECT 1 FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_SITE S
13871	                   WHERE  S.DIVISION_ID = R.DIVISION_ID
13872	                     AND  S.SCM_SITE_ID = R.SCM_SITE_ID
13873	                     AND  S.SITE_TYPE   = 'SALES'
13874	                     AND  S.VALID_YN    = 'Y'
13875	               );
13876	
13877	        IF v_cnt_check > 0 THEN
13878	            V_LOG_TYPE    := 'INVALID';
13879	            V_DESCRIPTION := 'Invalid Sales Site ! (Sales Site 마스터 미등록/무효 - Sales Price 는 저장해도 조회되지 않아 저장 차단)';
13880	            RAISE e_validation_error;
13881	        END IF;
13882	
13883	        -- V81-08. Invalid Item for Division (DMF0020 L421 이식 — 사업부 Item 마스터. 삭제행 제외)
13884	        SELECT COUNT(1) INTO v_cnt_check
13885	        FROM   SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_T_MAIN R
13886	        WHERE  R.ACTION <> 'D'
13887	          AND  NOT EXISTS (
13888	                   SELECT 1 FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_ITEM_SN I
13889	                   WHERE  I.DIVISION_ID = R.DIVISION_ID
13890	                     AND  I.ITEM_ID     = R.ITEM_ID
13891	               );
13892	
13893	        IF v_cnt_check > 0 THEN
13894	            V_LOG_TYPE    := 'INVALID';
13895	            V_DESCRIPTION := 'Invalid Item for Division ! (해당 사업부 Item 마스터 미등록 - 저장 차단)';
13896	            RAISE e_validation_error;
13897	        END IF;
13898	
13899	        -- V81-09. 동일 기간 Currency 중복 (DMF0020 L675 이식 — KRW/USD 파생 전이므로 LOC 기준)
13900	        SELECT COUNT(1) INTO v_cnt_check
13901	        FROM (
13902	            SELECT 1 AS DUP
13903	            FROM SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_T_MAIN
13904	            WHERE MEASURE = 'LOC' AND NVL(QTY_PRICE, 0) <> 0
13905	            GROUP BY DIVISION_ID, PROJECT_CODE, SCM_SITE_ID, PLANT_SITE_ID, ITEM_ID, YYYYMMDD, DOM_EXP, FCST_TYPE, INCOTERMS, MEASURE
13906	            HAVING COUNT(CURRENCY) > 1
13907	        );
13908	
13909	        IF v_cnt_check > 0 THEN
13910	            V_LOG_TYPE    := 'INVALID';
13911	            V_DESCRIPTION := '동일 기간에 두 개 이상의 Currency에 해당하는 Sales Price가 존재합니다.';
13912	            RAISE e_validation_error;
13913	        END IF;
13914	
13915	        -- V81-10. 신규(I) Price 행이 SCM_MST_FUTURE_PRICE 에 기존재 (DMF0020 L710 이식)
13916	        SELECT COUNT(1) INTO v_cnt_check
13917	        FROM   SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_FUTURE_PRICE S
13918	        WHERE  EXISTS (
13919	                   SELECT 1 FROM SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_T_MAIN R
13920	                   WHERE  S.DIVISION_ID   = R.DIVISION_ID
13921	                     AND  S.PROJECT_CODE  = R.PROJECT_CODE
13922	                     AND  S.SCM_SITE_ID   = R.SCM_SITE_ID
13923	                     AND  S.PLANT_SITE_ID = R.PLANT_SITE_ID
13924	                     AND  S.ITEM_ID       = R.ITEM_ID
13925	                     AND  S.YYYYMMDD      = R.YYYYMMDD
13926	                     AND  S.MEASURE       = R.MEASURE
13927	                     AND  S.DOM_EXP       = R.DOM_EXP
13928	                     AND  S.FCST_TYPE     = R.FCST_TYPE
13929	                     AND  S.INCOTERMS     = R.INCOTERMS
13930	                     AND  R.ACTION        = 'I'
13931	                     AND  R.MEASURE       = 'LOC'
13932	               )
13933	          AND  ROWNUM = 1;
13934	
13935	        IF v_cnt_check > 0 THEN
13936	            V_LOG_TYPE    := 'INVALID';
13937	            V_DESCRIPTION := '동일 기간에 두 개 이상의 Currency에 해당하는 Sales Price가 존재합니다.';
13938	            RAISE e_validation_error;
13939	        END IF;
13940	
13941	        -- V81-11. AS 계열 FCST TYPE 신규(I) — AS FROM SITE 미전달 행만 차단([E20260805-AFS] p_as_from_site 신설로 완화).
13942	        --  고객향 사이트(AS_SITE_PLANT 미등재) + AS 계열 + V_ATTRIBUTE10 공백 = 불완전 저장 차단 (DMF0003 MSG00476 계보).
13943	        SELECT COUNT(1) INTO v_cnt_check
13944	        FROM   SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_T_MAIN A
13945	        JOIN   SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL C
13946	          ON   A.FCST_TYPE   = C.SEGMENT1
13947	         AND   C.LOOKUP_TYPE = 'SALES_TYPE_CODE'
13948	         AND   C.ATTRIBUTE2  = 'Y'
13949	        WHERE  A.ACTION = 'I'
13950	          AND  (A.V_ATTRIBUTE10 IS NULL OR TRIM(A.V_ATTRIBUTE10) = '')  --[E20260805-AFS] AS FROM SITE 전달 시 신규 허용
13951	          AND  NOT EXISTS (
13952	                   SELECT 1 FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL B
13953	                   WHERE  A.SCM_SITE_ID = B.SEGMENT1
13954	                     AND  B.LOOKUP_TYPE = 'AS_SITE_PLANT'
13955	                     AND  B.SEGMENT3    = A.DIVISION_ID
13956	                     AND  B.VALID_YN    = 'Y'
13957	               );
13958	
13959	        IF v_cnt_check > 0 THEN
13960	            V_LOG_TYPE    := 'INVALID';
13961	            V_DESCRIPTION := 'AS 관련 FCST TYPE 의 신규 입력은 AS FROM SITE 지정이 필요합니다.';  --[E20260805-AFS]
13962	            RAISE e_validation_error;
13963	        END IF;
13964	
13965	    END IF;

13967	    --[DIV81] 19. 81 전용 Validation (END)

14198	             , T.CREATED_BY, '' AS V_ATTRIBUTE1, '' AS V_ATTRIBUTE2, '' AS V_ATTRIBUTE3, '' AS V_ATTRIBUTE4
14199	             , '' AS V_ATTRIBUTE5, '' AS V_ATTRIBUTE6, '' AS V_ATTRIBUTE7, '' AS V_ATTRIBUTE8
14200	             , '' AS V_ATTRIBUTE9, '' AS V_ATTRIBUTE10, '' AS V_BOM_YN, '' AS V_VMI_YN
14201	        FROM (SELECT * FROM SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_T_MAIN WHERE QTY_PRICE = 0 AND MEASURE = 'MKT_FCST') T,
14202	        (SELECT 'MKT_AMT_KRW' MEASURE FROM DUAL
14203	         UNION
14204	         SELECT 'MKT_AMT_LOC' MEASURE FROM DUAL
14205	        ) B
14206	        --[E20251208-000309]_0인 경우를 처리하는 로직이 존재하지 않아서, 0인 경우에 MKT FCST AMT 생성하도록 추가 (END)
14207	    ) AMT
14208	    GROUP BY AMT.ACT, AMT.DIVISION_ID, AMT.PROJECT_CODE, AMT.END_CUSTOMER, AMT.SCM_SITE_ID, AMT.ITEM_ID
14209	           , AMT.DOM_EXP, AMT.FCST_TYPE, AMT.INCOTERMS, AMT.MEASURE, AMT.PLANT_SITE_ID, AMT.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, AMT.YYYYMMDD;

14212	    ELSE

14264	                                                  , M.FCST_TYPE, M.INCOTERMS ORDER BY ROWNUM) AS RN

14268	          AND B.QTY_PRICE = 0
