14488	    AND A.FCST_TYPE         = B.FCST_TYPE
14489	    AND A.INCOTERMS         = B.INCOTERMS
14490	    AND A.MEASURE           = B.MEASURE
14491	    AND A.PLANT_SITE_ID     = B.PLANT_SITE_ID
14492	    AND A.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID = B.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID
14493	    AND A.YYYYMMDD          = B.YYYYMMDD)
14494	    WHEN MATCHED THEN
14495	    UPDATE
14496	    SET A.QTY              = B.QTY_PRICE
14497	      , A.LAST_UPDATED_BY  = B.CREATED_BY
14498	      , A.LAST_UPDATE_DATE = V_GETDATE
14499	    WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN
14500	    INSERT
14501	    ( DIVISION_ID, PROJECT_CODE, END_CUSTOMER, SCM_SITE_ID, ITEM_ID, DOM_EXP, FCST_TYPE, INCOTERMS, MEASURE, PLANT_SITE_ID
14502	    , CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, YYYYMMDD, QTY, CREATED_BY, CREATION_DATE, LAST_UPDATED_BY, LAST_UPDATE_DATE )
14503	    VALUES
14504	    ( B.DIVISION_ID, B.PROJECT_CODE, B.END_CUSTOMER, B.SCM_SITE_ID, B.ITEM_ID, B.DOM_EXP, B.FCST_TYPE, B.INCOTERMS, B.MEASURE, B.PLANT_SITE_ID
14505	    , B.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, B.YYYYMMDD, B.QTY_PRICE, B.CREATED_BY, V_GETDATE, B.CREATED_BY, V_GETDATE);
14506	    ELSE
14507	        --[DIV81] 수요 DML 81: MERGE DM_PLN_MKT_FCST — ON 키 10종. MERGE 채택 사유: DMF0003 정본은 DELETE+INSERT 이나
14508	        --  기존 ATTRIBUTE(CONFIRM 계열) 보존을 위해 UPDATE 는 QTY/감사컬럼만 갱신. DMF0003 과 동일하게 QTY=0 행도 물리 저장.
14509	        --[E20260822-DIV81DEL] 81 수요 행 삭제 — TO-BE 는 81 편집 권한이 있어 실삭제가 필요하다(사용자 지시 2026-08-22).
14510	        --  40 처럼 'QTY=0 → 삭제' 를 쓸 수 없다: 81 은 QTY=0 행을 물리 저장하는 설계(바로 위 주석)라
14511	        --  값을 0 으로 고친 것까지 지워져 DMF0003 정본과 어긋난다.
14512	        --  그래서 FE 행 삭제 플래그를 기준으로 한다 — GT_PRDM1_T_MAIN.ACTION = p_active_flag (L11592).
14513	        --  키 10종 = 아래 MERGE ON 절과 동일 (DM_PLN_MKT_FCST 에는 END_CUSTOMER·CELL_PROD_SITE_ID 없음).
14514	        DELETE FROM SCM_MGR.DM_PLN_MKT_FCST A
14515	        WHERE A.MEASURE IN ('MKT_FCST', 'VMI_In_Req', 'AS_In_Req', 'SCM_FCST')   --[E20260826-ASINSREQ] W/S AS_In_Req 입력 개방 (AS-IS 저장은 메저 무필터 — 파리티)  --[E20260902-81SCMFCST]
14516	          AND EXISTS (
14517	              SELECT 1
14518	              FROM SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_T_MAIN B
14519	              WHERE B.ACTION = 'D'
14520	                AND B.MEASURE IN ('MKT_FCST', 'VMI_In_Req', 'AS_In_Req', 'SCM_FCST')   --[E20260826-ASINSREQ]  --[E20260902-81SCMFCST]
14521	                AND B.DIVISION_ID   = A.DIVISION_ID
14522	                AND B.PROJECT_CODE  = A.PROJECT_CODE
14523	                AND B.SCM_SITE_ID   = A.SCM_SITE_ID
14524	                AND B.ITEM_ID       = A.ITEM_ID
14525	                AND B.DOM_EXP       = A.DOM_EXP
14526	                AND B.FCST_TYPE     = A.FCST_TYPE
14527	                AND B.INCOTERMS     = A.INCOTERMS
14528	                AND B.MEASURE       = A.MEASURE
14529	                AND B.PLANT_SITE_ID = A.PLANT_SITE_ID
14530	                AND B.YYYYMMDD      = A.YYYYMMDD
14531	          );
14532	
14533	        MERGE INTO SCM_MGR.DM_PLN_MKT_FCST A
14534	        USING (SELECT * FROM (
14535	                   SELECT M.*
14536	                        , ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY M.DIVISION_ID, M.PROJECT_CODE
14537	                                                         , M.SCM_SITE_ID, M.ITEM_ID, M.DOM_EXP
14538	                                                         , M.FCST_TYPE, M.INCOTERMS, M.MEASURE
14539	                                                         , M.PLANT_SITE_ID, M.YYYYMMDD ORDER BY ROWNUM) AS RN
14540	                   FROM SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_T_MAIN M
14541	                   WHERE M.MEASURE IN ('MKT_FCST', 'VMI_In_Req', 'AS_In_Req', 'SCM_FCST')   --[E20260826-ASINSREQ]  --[E20260902-81SCMFCST]
14542	                     AND NVL(M.ACTION, 'X') <> 'D')   --[E20260822-DIV81DEL] 삭제행은 MERGE 대상에서 제외 (안 하면 지운 행이 QTY=0 으로 되살아난다)
14543	               WHERE RN = 1) B
14544	        ON (A.DIVISION_ID       = B.DIVISION_ID
14545	        AND A.PROJECT_CODE      = B.PROJECT_CODE
14546	        AND A.SCM_SITE_ID       = B.SCM_SITE_ID
14547	        AND A.ITEM_ID           = B.ITEM_ID
14548	        AND A.DOM_EXP           = B.DOM_EXP
14549	        AND A.FCST_TYPE         = B.FCST_TYPE
14550	        AND A.INCOTERMS         = B.INCOTERMS
14551	        AND A.MEASURE           = B.MEASURE
14552	        AND A.PLANT_SITE_ID     = B.PLANT_SITE_ID
14553	        AND A.YYYYMMDD          = B.YYYYMMDD)
14554	        WHEN MATCHED THEN
14555	        UPDATE
14556	        SET A.QTY              = NVL(B.QTY_PRICE, 0)
14557	          , A.LAST_UPDATED_BY  = B.CREATED_BY
14558	          , A.LAST_UPDATE_DATE = V_GETDATE
14559	          , A.ATTRIBUTE10      = B.V_ATTRIBUTE10  --[E20260820-AFSNULL] NVL 보존 처리 폐지 — 전달값 그대로(빈값=NULL, 사용자 지시)
14560	        WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN
14561	        INSERT
14562	        ( DIVISION_ID, PROJECT_CODE, SCM_SITE_ID, ITEM_ID, DOM_EXP, FCST_TYPE, INCOTERMS, MEASURE, PLANT_SITE_ID, YYYYMMDD
14563	        , QTY, CREATED_BY, CREATION_DATE, LAST_UPDATED_BY, LAST_UPDATE_DATE
14564	        , ATTRIBUTE1, ATTRIBUTE2, ATTRIBUTE3, ATTRIBUTE4, ATTRIBUTE5
14565	        , ATTRIBUTE6, ATTRIBUTE7, ATTRIBUTE8, ATTRIBUTE9, ATTRIBUTE10 )
14566	        VALUES
14567	        ( B.DIVISION_ID, B.PROJECT_CODE, B.SCM_SITE_ID, B.ITEM_ID, B.DOM_EXP, B.FCST_TYPE, B.INCOTERMS, B.MEASURE, B.PLANT_SITE_ID, B.YYYYMMDD
14568	        , NVL(B.QTY_PRICE, 0), B.CREATED_BY, V_GETDATE, B.CREATED_BY, V_GETDATE
14569	        , NULL, NULL, NULL, NULL, NULL
14570	        , NULL, NULL, NULL, NULL, B.V_ATTRIBUTE10 );  --[E20260820-AFSNULL] 빈값=NULL 그대로
14571	    END IF;  --[DIV81] 수요 DML 분기
14572	
14573	    n_update_cnt := SQL%ROWCOUNT; -- [E20250915-000105]_ROW_COUNT 확인
14574

14576	        'MERGE INTO DM_PLN_CFM_MKT_DEMAND_PLAN END', '', SYSDATE,
14577	        v_created_by,
14578	        V_PROC_INPUT, NULL, NULL, NULL); END;

14610	    --[E20251209-000712]_전극의 경우 SCP RTF를 받아오지 않도록 처리했기때문에, MKT FCST 수정시 -> MKT FCST 메져로 RTF 생성 / SCM FCST 수정시 -> SCM FCST 메져로 RTF 생성하도록 수정 (START)
14611	    --==========================================================================================================================
14612	    -- 전극인 경우에는 RTF를 생성해준다 (SCP RTF를 반영하지 않기때문에) (START)
14613	    --==========================================================================================================================
14614	    n_EE_chk := 0;
14615	    IF v_is_81 = 'N' THEN  --[DIV81] 전극 RTF 생성은 40 전용 (40 테이블 DML)
14616	    SELECT COUNT(*) INTO n_EE_chk
14617	    FROM SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_T_MAIN RSLT
14618	       , SCM_MGR.DM_MST_ITEM_SINGLE_V ITEM
14619	    WHERE RSLT.DIVISION_ID = ITEM.DIVISION_ID
14620	      AND RSLT.ITEM_ID     = ITEM.ITEM_ID
14621	      AND ITEM.ACTIVE_FLAG = 'Y'
14622	      --↓전극인 경우에 RTF를 MKT_FCST와 동일하게 생성
14623	      AND RSLT.ITEM_ID IN (
14624	            SELECT K.ITEM_ID FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_ITEM_SN K
14625	            WHERE K.DIVISION_ID = RSLT.DIVISION_ID AND K.SCP_OPERATION_GROUP = 'E' AND K.ACTIVE_FLAG = 'Y'
14626	      );
14627	
14628	    IF n_EE_chk > 0 THEN
14629	        DELETE FROM SCM_MGR.DM_PLN_CFM_MKT_DEMAND_PLAN A
14630	        WHERE A.MEASURE  = 'RTF_SYS_RP'
14631	          AND A.YYYYMMDD >= v_plan_start_yyyymmdd -- 당주 월요일이후
14632	          AND EXISTS (
14633	                SELECT RSLT.DIVISION_ID
14634	                FROM SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_T_MAIN RSLT
14635	                   , SCM_MGR.DM_MST_ITEM_SINGLE_V ITEM
14636	                WHERE RSLT.DIVISION_ID         = ITEM.DIVISION_ID
14637	                  AND RSLT.ITEM_ID             = ITEM.ITEM_ID
14638	                  AND A.DIVISION_ID            = RSLT.DIVISION_ID
14639	                  AND A.PROJECT_CODE           = RSLT.PROJECT_CODE
14640	                  AND A.END_CUSTOMER           = RSLT.END_CUSTOMER
14641	                  AND A.SCM_SITE_ID            = RSLT.SCM_SITE_ID
14642	                  AND A.ITEM_ID                = RSLT.ITEM_ID
14643	                  AND A.DOM_EXP                = RSLT.DOM_EXP
14644	                  AND A.FCST_TYPE              = RSLT.FCST_TYPE
14645	                  AND A.INCOTERMS              = RSLT.INCOTERMS
14646	                  AND A.PLANT_SITE_ID          = RSLT.PLANT_SITE_ID
14647	                  AND A.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID      = RSLT.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID
14648	                  AND ITEM.ACTIVE_FLAG         = 'Y'
14649	                  --↓전극인 경우에 RTF를 MKT_FCST와 동일하게 생성
14650	                  AND RSLT.ITEM_ID IN (
14651	                        SELECT K.ITEM_ID FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_ITEM_SN K
14652	                        WHERE K.DIVISION_ID = RSLT.DIVISION_ID AND K.SCP_OPERATION_GROUP = 'E' AND K.ACTIVE_FLAG = 'Y'
14653	                  )  --20180720 ACTIVE_FLAG 조건추가
14654	          );
14655	
14656	        INSERT INTO SCM_MGR.DM_PLN_CFM_MKT_DEMAND_PLAN
14657	        ( DIVISION_ID, PROJECT_CODE, END_CUSTOMER, SCM_SITE_ID, ITEM_ID, DOM_EXP, FCST_TYPE, INCOTERMS, MEASURE
14658	        , PLANT_SITE_ID, YYYYMMDD, QTY, CREATED_BY, CREATION_DATE, LAST_UPDATED_BY, LAST_UPDATE_DATE
14659	        , ATTRIBUTE1, ATTRIBUTE2, ATTRIBUTE3, ATTRIBUTE4, ATTRIBUTE5, ATTRIBUTE6
14660	        , ATTRIBUTE7, ATTRIBUTE8, ATTRIBUTE9, ATTRIBUTE10, CELL_PROD_SITE_ID )
14661	        SELECT A.DIVISION_ID, A.PROJECT_CODE, A.END_CUSTOMER, A.SCM_SITE_ID, A.ITEM_ID, A.DOM_EXP, A.FCST_TYPE, A.INCOTERMS
14662	             , 'RTF_SYS_RP' AS MEASURE
14663	             , A.PLANT_SITE_ID, A.YYYYMMDD, A.QTY, 'DMF0082_SYSTEM' AS CREATED_BY, SYSDATE AS CREATION_DATE
14664	             , 'DMF0082_SYSTEM' AS LAST_UPDATED_BY, SYSDATE AS LAST_UPDATE_DATE
14665	             , A.ATTRIBUTE1, A.ATTRIBUTE2, A.ATTRIBUTE3, A.ATTRIBUTE4, A.ATTRIBUTE5, A.ATTRIBUTE6
14666	             , A.ATTRIBUTE7, A.ATTRIBUTE8, A.ATTRIBUTE9, A.ATTRIBUTE10, A.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID
14667	        FROM SCM_MGR.DM_PLN_CFM_MKT_DEMAND_PLAN A
14668	        WHERE A.MEASURE = (SELECT MEASURE FROM SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_T_MAIN WHERE MEASURE IN ('SCM_FCST', 'MKT_FCST') GROUP BY MEASURE FETCH FIRST 1 ROWS ONLY)
14669	          AND A.YYYYMMDD >= v_plan_start_yyyymmdd -- 당주 월요일이후
14670	          AND EXISTS (
14671	                SELECT RSLT.DIVISION_ID
14672	                FROM SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_T_MAIN RSLT
14673	                   , SCM_MGR.DM_MST_ITEM_SINGLE_V ITEM
14674	                WHERE RSLT.DIVISION_ID    = ITEM.DIVISION_ID
14675	                  AND RSLT.ITEM_ID        = ITEM.ITEM_ID
14676	                  AND A.DIVISION_ID       = RSLT.DIVISION_ID
14677	                  AND A.PROJECT_CODE      = RSLT.PROJECT_CODE
14678	                  AND A.END_CUSTOMER      = RSLT.END_CUSTOMER
14679	                  AND A.SCM_SITE_ID       = RSLT.SCM_SITE_ID
14680	                  AND A.ITEM_ID           = RSLT.ITEM_ID
14681	                  AND A.DOM_EXP           = RSLT.DOM_EXP
14682	                  AND A.FCST_TYPE         = RSLT.FCST_TYPE
14683	                  AND A.INCOTERMS         = RSLT.INCOTERMS
14684	                  AND A.PLANT_SITE_ID     = RSLT.PLANT_SITE_ID
14685	                  AND A.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID = RSLT.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID
14686	                  AND ITEM.ACTIVE_FLAG    = 'Y'
14687	                  --↓전극인 경우에 RTF를 MKT_FCST와 동일하게 생성
14688	                  AND RSLT.ITEM_ID IN (
14689	                        SELECT K.ITEM_ID FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_ITEM_SN K
14690	                        WHERE K.DIVISION_ID = RSLT.DIVISION_ID AND K.SCP_OPERATION_GROUP = 'E' AND K.ACTIVE_FLAG = 'Y'
14691	                  )  --20180720 ACTIVE_FLAG 조건추가
14692	          );
14693	
14694	        --[E20260107-001651]_룩업에 설정된 값으로 ATTRIBUTE2 UPDATE (BPS I/F시 FROM_SITE_ID 전송하기 위함) START
14695	        UPDATE SCM_MGR.DM_PLN_CFM_MKT_DEMAND_PLAN A
14696	        SET A.ATTRIBUTE2 = (SELECT LOOK.SEGMENT3 FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL LOOK
14697	                             WHERE LOOK.LOOKUP_TYPE = 'DM_ELECTROD_FROM_SITE_ID' AND LOOK.VALID_YN = 'Y'
14698	                               AND A.DIVISION_ID = LOOK.SEGMENT1 AND ROWNUM = 1)
14699	        WHERE A.MEASURE  = 'RTF_SYS_RP'
14700	          AND A.YYYYMMDD >= v_plan_start_yyyymmdd
14701	          AND EXISTS (
14702	                SELECT 1 FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL LOOK
14703	                WHERE LOOK.LOOKUP_TYPE = 'DM_ELECTROD_FROM_SITE_ID' AND LOOK.VALID_YN = 'Y'
14704	                  AND A.DIVISION_ID = LOOK.SEGMENT1
14705	                  AND A.ITEM_ID LIKE '' || NVL(LOOK.ATTRIBUTE1, A.ITEM_ID) || '%'
14706	                  AND A.ITEM_ID IN (
14707	                        SELECT K.ITEM_ID FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_ITEM_SN K
14708	                        WHERE K.DIVISION_ID = A.DIVISION_ID AND K.SCP_OPERATION_GROUP = LOOK.SEGMENT2 AND K.ACTIVE_FLAG = 'Y'
14709	                  )
14710	          );
14711	        --[E20260107-001651]_룩업에 설정된 값으로 ATTRIBUTE2 UPDATE (BPS I/F시 FROM_SITE_ID 전송하기 위함) END
14712	
14713	        n_update_cnt := SQL%ROWCOUNT; -- [E20250915-000105]_ROW_COUNT 확인

14720	        N_LOG_COUNT   := n_update_cnt;
14721	        V_DESCRIPTION := '';
14722	        D_EVENT_DATE  := SYSDATE;

14763	             , CURRENCY, MEASURE, DOM_EXP, FCST_TYPE, INCOTERMS, NVL(QTY_PRICE, 0), CREATED_BY, V_GETDATE
14764	             , CREATED_BY, V_GETDATE

14974	-- ============================================================================
14975	-- [E20260813-EXCELFORM] DMF0082 Excel Form 조회 SP 신설 — BODY 구현부
14976	--
14977	--  ⭐ 설계 요지 (근거)
14978	--   1) 조회조건 필터는 **MAIN GET 을 그대로 호출**해 재사용한다. MAIN 이 스테이징하는
14979	--      GT_DMF0082_TEMP_FCST_N_PRICE 가 키 10 + 정보성 컬럼 전량을 이미 보유하므로,
14980	--      필터 로직을 복제하지 않고도 "MAIN 조회조건에 맞는 데이터 전체"가 정의상 보장된다.
14981	--      (사내 실측 2026-08-13: div40 3개월 = GET 6.9s / GTT 938,540행 / DISTINCT 키 2,559)
14982	--   2) measure 는 화면 개인화(titleset)와 **무관**하게 LOOKUP 'DM_UI_EDITABLE_MEASURE'
14983	--      (SEGMENT1='DMF0082', ATTRIBUTE1='Y' = INSERT_YN)로 CROSS JOIN 한다 — 사용자 요구
14984	--      "입력 가능 메저 전체 대상". VMI 조건(ATTRIBUTE3)은 검증 SP L4711~4716 과 동일 술어를 쓴다.
14985	--   3) 각 컬럼 값은 **검증 SP 가 기대하는 표기**로 내린다 (검증 SP 원문 실측):
14986	--        Div              = 코드 그대로                              (L4330)
14987	--        End Customer     = END_CUSTOMER lookup SEGMENT3(명칭)        (L4394 EC.NAME = ATTRIBUTE2)
14988	--        Sales Site       = SCM_SITE_ID 코드 그대로                   (L4433)
14989	--        Model            = ITEM_ID 코드 그대로                       (L4513)
14990	--        Incoterms        = INCOTERMS lookup SEGMENT2(=SEGMENT1)      (L4552)
14991	--        FCST Type        = SALES_TYPE_CODE lookup SEGMENT2(명칭)     (L4591)
14992	--        Customs Handbook = DM_CUSTOMS_HANDBOOK_40 SEGMENT2(=SEG1)    (L4629)
14993	--        Cell Prod Site   = SP_CELL_PROD_SITE ATTRIBUTE1(명칭)        (L4667)
14994	--        Currency         = CURRENCY_LIST SEGMENT2(=SEGMENT1)         (L4761, LOC 행만 검증)
14995	--        Measure          = DM_UI_EDITABLE_MEASURE SEGMENT4(표시명)   (L4722)
14996	--      → 본 SP 는 검증 SP 의 역함수. 양식→업로드 왕복이 구조적으로 보장된다.
14997	--   4) 정보성 컬럼은 키별 MAX() 로 축약한다. DISTINCT 에 넣으면 정보성 값 변형 1건이
14998	--      키를 쪼개 행이 불어난다(중복 양식 행 = 업로드 중복키 ERROR 유발).
14999	-- ============================================================================
15000	PROCEDURE P_DMF0082_GET_EXCEL_FORM_DATA (
15001	    p_division_id               IN  VARCHAR2
15002	  , p_dept_id                   IN  VARCHAR2
15003	  , p_end_customer              IN  VARCHAR2
15004	  , p_biz_type                  IN  VARCHAR2
15005	  , p_from_yyyymmdd             IN  VARCHAR2
15006	  , p_to_yyyymmdd               IN  VARCHAR2
15007	  , p_sales_site                IN  VARCHAR2
15008	  , p_item_id                   IN  VARCHAR2
15009	  , p_version_type              IN  VARCHAR2
15010	  , p_qty_yn                    IN  VARCHAR2
15011	  , p_ses_user_id               IN  VARCHAR2
15012	  , p_user_group                IN  VARCHAR2
15013	  , p_plant_site_id             IN  VARCHAR2 DEFAULT 'ALL'
15014	  , p_project_code              IN  VARCHAR2 DEFAULT 'ALL'
15015	  , p_owner                     IN  VARCHAR2 DEFAULT 'ALL'
15016	  , p_sender_code               IN  VARCHAR2 DEFAULT 'ALL'
15017	  , o_rc1                       OUT SYS_REFCURSOR
15018	  , o_result_cursor             OUT SYS_REFCURSOR
15019	)
15020	AS
15021	/* ============================================================================================
15022	 * [E20260921-XLSFORMASIS] Excel Form = **AS-IS DMF0020 파리티 전면 이식** (사용자 지시 2026-09-21)
15023	 *
15024	 *   지시 : "EXCEL FORM, UPLOAD 을 AS-IS DMF0020 의 EXCEL FORM, UPLOAD 와 동일하게 기능 구현"
15025	 *          "EXCEL FORM COLUMN - Div / Team / End Customer / Project Code / Currency / Model / M0 ~ M12"
15026	 *          "to-be 도 m0 - m12 까지는 빈칸"
15027	 *
15028	 *   원천 = AS-IS `DMF0020_SalesPriceTemplatePopup` 이 호출하는
15029	 *          `DM.DMF0020_GET_SALES_PRICE_TEMPLATE` (TO-BE 변환본 = PKG_DMF0020.P_DMF0020_GET_SALES_PRICE_TEMPLATE,
15030	 *          사내 live VALID 실측 2026-09-21). 본문 SQL 을 그 원문 그대로 옮겼다.
15031	 *
15032	 *   ⭐ 구 구현과의 차이 (의도된 교체 — 사용자 확정):
15033	 *     (1) 행 원천 : SCM_MST_FUTURE_PRICE(단가 원장 키) -> **DM_PLN_SALES_PLAN_C ∪ DM_PLN_SALES_PLAN_RESULT_C**
15034	 *                   (MEASURE='RTF_SYS_RP', QTY<>0, DIV 최신 SP_W 버전, 52주 창) = AS-IS 원문
15035	 *     (2) 출력 축 : 23컬럼(Sales Site/Plant Site/Incoterms/FCST Type/Customs Handbook/Measure/
15036	 *                   Project/Project Owner/FORM_WARN 포함) -> **AS-IS 19컬럼 + EDIT_YN**
15037	 *     (3) M0~M12  : 구 구현은 LOC 원장 월별 대표단가를 프리필했다(사용자 지적 "조회 시 값 보임").
15038	 *                   AS-IS 는 `'' AS M0 … '' AS M12` -> **전부 빈 문자열**로 환원. LP 프리필 조인 제거.
15039	 *     (4) 선택 필터: AS-IS 양식은 division 단위 전량이라 조회조건 필터가 없다.
15040	 *                   p_sales_site/p_item_id/p_end_customer/p_plant_site_id/p_project_code 는
15041	 *                   **AS-IS 파리티를 위해 미적용**(시그니처는 PROC_M 배선 보존 목적으로 그대로 둔다).
15042	 *
15043	 *   ⚠ [NLSFIX 2026-09-21] AS-IS 변환 원본(PKG_DMF0020.P_DMF0020_GET_SALES_PRICE_TEMPLATE)은
15044	 *      `FN_NEXT_DAY(T_VER.YYYYMMDD,'MON')` 로 **VARCHAR2 를 DATE 파라미터에 암묵 전달**한다.
15045	 *      FN_NEXT_DAY 는 (P_DATEVAL DATE, P_WEEK VARCHAR2) 이므로 세션 NLS_DATE_FORMAT 에 의존하며,
15046	 *      기본값 'DD-MON-RR' 세션(=BE 기본)에서 **ORA-01861** 로 실패한다(2026-09-21 실측 — 원본도 동일 재현).
15047	 *      이식본은 `TO_DATE(T_VER.YYYYMMDD,'YYYYMMDD')` 로 명시 변환한다 (hook #109 NLS 암묵 형변환).
15048	 *      ⇒ 원본 PKG_DMF0020 쪽은 본 회차 범위 밖이라 미수정 — 별건으로 보고.
15049	 *   시그니처 : 17 IN + 2 OUT **무변경** — SCM_ADM_PROC_M(DMF0082/FcstNPrice/FORM_DATA) 재배선 불필요.
15050	 *   AS-IS MNU 파라미터 대응 : P_USER_ID <- p_ses_user_id / P_MNU_ID <- 'DMF0082' (EDIT_YN 권한 뷰 조회용).
15051	 *   division 가드 : AS-IS 도 81 사업부 전용(DMF0020_SalesFuturePrice.cs L203/L2579
15052	 *                   `clbDivisions.ControlValues.Substring(0,2)=="81"` 일 때만 버튼 노출) — 기존 가드 유지.
15053	 *   결과커서 MONTH_LIST : FE 계약 보존용으로 유지(양식 라벨은 FE 가 M{n} -> YYYYMM 로 환산).
15054	 * ============================================================================================ */
15055	    V_MONTH_LIST                VARCHAR2(400 CHAR);
15056	    n_key_cnt                   PLS_INTEGER := 0;
15057	
15058	    V_PROC_INPUT                VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR);
15059	    V_BACKTRACE                 VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR);
15060	    V_SQL_CODE                  VARCHAR2(100  CHAR);
15061	    V_SQL_ERRMSG                VARCHAR2(1000 CHAR);
15062	    n_log_count                 PLS_INTEGER := 0;
15063	    V_PROGRAM_NAME              VARCHAR2(150 CHAR) := $$PLSQL_UNIT;
15064	BEGIN
15065	    --[E20260806-DS2] GT/GTT 사용 SP 표준 — 클라우드 optimizer_dynamic_sampling=0 방어
15066	    EXECUTE IMMEDIATE 'ALTER SESSION SET optimizer_dynamic_sampling = 2';
15067	
15068	    /* [E20260819-XLS81LOC] Excel Form = Division 81 계열 전용 (AS-IS 동일 — .cs L203 실측).
15069	       비대상 division 은 처리 없이 결과커서 CODE '1' + MSG01014. FE 는 81 외 버튼 비노출(서버 권위 방어). */
15070	    IF p_division_id IS NULL OR p_division_id NOT LIKE '81%' THEN
15071	        OPEN o_rc1 FOR SELECT CAST(NULL AS VARCHAR2(10)) AS DIVISION_ID FROM DUAL WHERE 1 = 0;
15072	        OPEN o_result_cursor FOR
15073	            SELECT '1' AS RESULT_CODE, 'MSG01014' AS RESULT_MSG, '0' AS EVENT
15074	                 , CAST(NULL AS VARCHAR2(400)) AS MONTH_LIST FROM DUAL;
15075	        RETURN;
15076	    END IF;
15077	
15078	    /* [E20260921-XLSFORMASIS] 월 목록 CSV(YYYYMM 13개) — 값이 아니라 **라벨 축** 전달용.
15079	       M0=당월 … M12=당월+12 (업로드 저장이 M{n} -> ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE,n) 로 해석하는 축과 동일). */
15080	    SELECT LISTAGG(TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE, LV - 1), 'YYYYMM'), ',') WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY LV)
15081	      INTO V_MONTH_LIST
15082	      FROM (SELECT LEVEL AS LV FROM DUAL CONNECT BY LEVEL <= 13);
15083	
15084	    -- ================================================================
15085	    -- 메인 조회 — AS-IS DMF0020_GET_SALES_PRICE_TEMPLATE 원문 1:1
15086	    -- ================================================================
15087	    OPEN o_rc1 FOR
15088	    WITH TEMP_VERSION_NO AS (
15089	        --DIV별 최신 버전(2024.10.04)---------------------
15090	        SELECT /*+ MATERIALIZE */
15091	               A.DIVISION_ID, A.VERSION_NO, A.YYYYMMDD, B.BASIS_YYYYMM AS YYYYMM
15092	        FROM
15093	        (
15094	            SELECT DIVISION_ID, MAX(VERSION_NO) AS VERSION_NO, MAX(YYYYMMDD) AS YYYYMMDD
15095	            FROM DM_MST_VERSION
15096	            WHERE DIVISION_ID = p_division_id
15097	            AND VERSION_TYPE = 'SP_W'
15098	            GROUP BY DIVISION_ID
15099	        ) A
15100	        INNER JOIN SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M B
15101	                ON B.BASIS_YYYYMMDD = A.YYYYMMDD
15102	    )
15103	    SELECT DISTINCT
15104	        A.DIVISION_ID
15105	        , A.DEPT_NAME_KO AS DEPT_NAME
15106	        , A.END_CUSTOMER
15107	        , A.PROJECT_CODE
15108	        , MAX(A.CURRENCY) AS CURRENCY -- Model별로 하나의 CURRENCY만 있어야 하므로 MAX값 취함
15109	        , A.ITEM_ID
15110	        /* [E20260921-XLSFORMASIS] 양식 = 사용자 입력칸. AS-IS 원문과 동일하게 전부 빈칸. */
15111	        , '' AS M0
15112	        , '' AS M1
15113	        , '' AS M2
15114	        , '' AS M3
15115	        , '' AS M4
15116	        , '' AS M5
15117	        , '' AS M6
15118	        , '' AS M7
15119	        , '' AS M8
15120	        , '' AS M9
15121	        , '' AS M10
15122	        , '' AS M11
15123	        , '' AS M12
15124	        , EDIT_YN
15125	    FROM
15126	    (
15127	        SELECT
15128	        SP.DIVISION_ID
15129	        , TEAM.DEPT_ID
15130	        , TEAM.DEPT_NAME_KO
15131	        , MS.END_CUSTOMER
15132	        , SP.PROJECT_CODE
15133	        , MP.PROJECT_NAME
15134	        , SP.ITEM_ID
15135	        , SP.SCM_SITE_ID
15136	        /* [E20260923-XLSFORMCURCHAIN] CURRENCY 폴백 체인 (사용자 지시 2026-09-23 — "CURRENCY 가 존재하지 않는 ROW 가 없도록").
15137	           AS-IS(DMF0020 L74/L119)는 L1→L2 2단뿐이고 MSSQL 에서는 그것으로 빈칸이 안 난다.
15138	           TO-BE 는 [E20260923-XLSFORMCURRENCY] 로 2단을 복원한 위에, 데이터 변동에도 빈칸 0 을 보장하도록 L3~L5 를 덧댄다.
15139	           COALESCE 는 좌→우 단락평가라 앞 단계가 채우면 뒤 서브쿼리는 실행되지 않는다(현 데이터는 L2 에서 100% 종료). */
15140	        , COALESCE( NULLIF(TRIM(SP.CURRENCY), '')                                    /* L1 판매계획 실적 통화 */
15141	                  , NULLIF(TRIM(SI.CURRENCY), '')                                    /* L2 SSPM SITE_ITEM 4키 — AS-IS 동일 단계 */
15142	                  , (SELECT MAX(FP.CURRENCY) FROM SCM_MST_FUTURE_PRICE FP            /* L3 같은 키의 기존 단가 통화(저장 원장) */
15143	                      WHERE FP.DIVISION_ID  = SP.DIVISION_ID
15144	                        AND FP.PROJECT_CODE = SP.PROJECT_CODE
15145	                        AND FP.SCM_SITE_ID  = SP.SCM_SITE_ID
15146	                        AND FP.ITEM_ID      = SP.ITEM_ID)
15147	                  , (SELECT MAX(S2.CURRENCY) FROM SCM_MST_SITE_ITEM S2                /* L4 SITE_ITEM 3키(프로젝트 무시) */
15148	                      WHERE S2.DIVISION_ID = SP.DIVISION_ID
15149	                        AND S2.SCM_SITE_ID = SP.SCM_SITE_ID
15150	                        AND S2.ITEM_ID     = SP.ITEM_ID
15151	                        AND S2.VALID_YN    = 'Y')
15152	                  , NULLIF(TRIM(MP.CURRENCY), '')                                     /* L5 프로젝트 기본통화 — 키 194/194 전수 커버 */
15153	                  ) AS CURRENCY
15154	        , NVL(T2.EDIT_YN,'N') AS EDIT_YN
15155	
15156	        FROM DM_PLN_SALES_PLAN_C SP
15157	
15158	            INNER JOIN (SELECT * -- EDIT_YN
15159	                FROM SCM_ADM_USER_MNU_EDIT_AUTH_V
15160	                WHERE USER_ID = p_ses_user_id
15161	                AND MNU_ID = 'DMF0082'
15162	                AND UNIT_TP_CD = 'D') T2
15163	            ON SP.DIVISION_ID = T2.UNIT_ID
15164	
15165	        INNER JOIN SCM_MST_SITE MS
15166	            ON MS.DIVISION_ID = SP.DIVISION_ID
15167	            AND MS.SCM_SITE_ID = SP.SCM_SITE_ID
15168	            AND MS.VALID_YN = 'Y'
15169	        -- 2026-09-07 hook#150: site 대표부서 = (DIVISION_ID, SCM_SITE_ID) 기준 MAX(DEPT_ID) 1건.
15170	        --   AS-IS 는 IM 을 DIV+SITE 단독 INNER JOIN 하여 SITE_ITEM 행수만큼 증식한다(실측: 키당 최대 3,506행).
15171	        INNER JOIN (SELECT SA.DIVISION_ID, SA.SCM_SITE_ID, MAX(SA.DEPT_ID) AS DEPT_ID
15172	                      FROM SCM_MST_SITE_ITEM SA
15173	                     INNER JOIN SCM_MST_SSO_DEPT_INFO SD
15174	                        ON SD.DEPT_ID  = SA.DEPT_ID
15175	                       AND SD.VALID_YN = 'Y'
15176	                     WHERE SA.DEPT_ID IS NOT NULL
15177	                     GROUP BY SA.DIVISION_ID, SA.SCM_SITE_ID) IM
15178	            ON MS.DIVISION_ID = IM.DIVISION_ID
15179	            AND MS.SCM_SITE_ID = IM.SCM_SITE_ID
15180	
15181	        INNER JOIN SCM_MST_SSO_DEPT_INFO DI
15182	            ON DI.DEPT_ID = IM.DEPT_ID
15183	            AND DI.VALID_YN = 'Y'
15184	
15185	        INNER JOIN SCM_MST_PROJECT MP
15186	            ON MP.DIVISION_ID = SP.DIVISION_ID
15187	            AND MP.PROJECT_CODE = SP.PROJECT_CODE --2026-07-03 perf: AS-IS 원문 활성 조인 복원
15188	
15189	        INNER JOIN SCM_MST_SITE_ITEM SI
15190	            ON SI.DIVISION_ID = SP.DIVISION_ID
15191	            AND SI.PROJECT_CODE = SP.PROJECT_CODE
15192	            AND SI.SCM_SITE_ID = SP.SCM_SITE_ID
15193	            AND SI.ITEM_ID = SP.ITEM_ID
15194	            AND SI.VALID_YN = 'Y'
15195	        INNER JOIN TEMP_VERSION_NO T_VER--DIV별 최신버전(2024.10.04)
15196	            ON SP.DIVISION_ID = T_VER.DIVISION_ID
15197	            AND SP.VERSION_NO = T_VER.VERSION_NO
15198	        -- 2026-09-06 hook#150 AJS: DEPT_ID 원천 = SCM_MST_SITE_ITEM.DEPT_ID (OWNER 경유 폐기)
15199	        LEFT JOIN SCM_MST_SSO_DEPT_INFO TEAM
15200	            ON TEAM.DEPT_ID = SI.DEPT_ID
15201	        WHERE SP.DIVISION_ID = p_division_id
15202	        AND SP.QTY <> 0
15203	        AND SP.MEASURE = 'RTF_SYS_RP'   /*[E20260923-XLSFORMMEASUREOFF] 임시 합집합(MKT_FCST 추가) 해제 — AS-IS 파리티 복귀 (사용자 지시 2026-09-23).
15204	           AS-IS DMF0020_GET_SALES_PRICE_TEMPLATE L105/L150 과 동일한 단일 measure.
15205	           건수 부족의 원인은 measure 가 아니라 컷오프 스냅샷 결함이었다 — 원장 RTF_SYS_RP 6,152행 정상인데
15206	           컷오프 SP_W_20260923_V001(13:12)은 RTF_PR 0 / SalesAmt_* 0 / RTF_SYS_RP 8 로 RTF·금액 계열 5종이 통째 누락
15207	           (원장 6,152행 전부 13:12 갱신 = 컷오프가 금액배치 전/중에 읽음). 근본 해소 = 컷오프 재수행. */
15208	        --버전의 날짜로 처리(2024.10.04)
15209	        AND SP.YYYYMMDD >= TO_CHAR(FN_NEXT_DAY(TO_DATE(T_VER.YYYYMMDD, 'YYYYMMDD'), 'MON') - 7, 'YYYYMMDD')
15210	        AND SP.YYYYMMDD <= TO_CHAR(TO_DATE(TO_CHAR(FN_NEXT_DAY(TO_DATE(T_VER.YYYYMMDD, 'YYYYMMDD'), 'MON') - 7, 'YYYYMMDD'), 'YYYYMMDD') + (52 * 7) - 1, 'YYYYMMDD')
15211	        UNION ALL
15212	
15213	        SELECT
15214	        SP.DIVISION_ID
15215	        , TEAM.DEPT_ID
15216	        , TEAM.DEPT_NAME_KO
15217	        , MS.END_CUSTOMER
15218	        , SP.PROJECT_CODE
15219	        , MP.PROJECT_NAME
15220	        , SP.ITEM_ID
15221	        , SP.SCM_SITE_ID
15222	        /* [E20260923-XLSFORMCURCHAIN] CURRENCY 폴백 체인 (사용자 지시 2026-09-23 — "CURRENCY 가 존재하지 않는 ROW 가 없도록").
15223	           AS-IS(DMF0020 L74/L119)는 L1→L2 2단뿐이고 MSSQL 에서는 그것으로 빈칸이 안 난다.
15224	           TO-BE 는 [E20260923-XLSFORMCURRENCY] 로 2단을 복원한 위에, 데이터 변동에도 빈칸 0 을 보장하도록 L3~L5 를 덧댄다.
15225	           COALESCE 는 좌→우 단락평가라 앞 단계가 채우면 뒤 서브쿼리는 실행되지 않는다(현 데이터는 L2 에서 100% 종료). */
15226	        , COALESCE( NULLIF(TRIM(SP.CURRENCY), '')                                    /* L1 판매계획 실적 통화 */
15227	                  , NULLIF(TRIM(SI.CURRENCY), '')                                    /* L2 SSPM SITE_ITEM 4키 — AS-IS 동일 단계 */
15228	                  , (SELECT MAX(FP.CURRENCY) FROM SCM_MST_FUTURE_PRICE FP            /* L3 같은 키의 기존 단가 통화(저장 원장) */
15229	                      WHERE FP.DIVISION_ID  = SP.DIVISION_ID
15230	                        AND FP.PROJECT_CODE = SP.PROJECT_CODE
15231	                        AND FP.SCM_SITE_ID  = SP.SCM_SITE_ID
15232	                        AND FP.ITEM_ID      = SP.ITEM_ID)
15233	                  , (SELECT MAX(S2.CURRENCY) FROM SCM_MST_SITE_ITEM S2                /* L4 SITE_ITEM 3키(프로젝트 무시) */
15234	                      WHERE S2.DIVISION_ID = SP.DIVISION_ID
15235	                        AND S2.SCM_SITE_ID = SP.SCM_SITE_ID
15236	                        AND S2.ITEM_ID     = SP.ITEM_ID
15237	                        AND S2.VALID_YN    = 'Y')
15238	                  , NULLIF(TRIM(MP.CURRENCY), '')                                     /* L5 프로젝트 기본통화 — 키 194/194 전수 커버 */
15239	                  ) AS CURRENCY
15240	        , NVL(T2.EDIT_YN,'N') AS EDIT_YN
15241	        FROM DM_PLN_SALES_PLAN_RESULT_C SP
15242	
15243	        INNER JOIN (SELECT * -- EDIT_YN
15244	                FROM SCM_ADM_USER_MNU_EDIT_AUTH_V
15245	                WHERE USER_ID = p_ses_user_id
15246	                AND MNU_ID = 'DMF0082'
15247	                AND UNIT_TP_CD = 'D') T2
15248	            ON SP.DIVISION_ID = T2.UNIT_ID
15249	
15250	        INNER JOIN SCM_MST_SITE MS
15251	            ON MS.DIVISION_ID = SP.DIVISION_ID
15252	            AND MS.SCM_SITE_ID = SP.SCM_SITE_ID
15253	            AND MS.VALID_YN = 'Y'
15254	
15255	        INNER JOIN (SELECT SA.DIVISION_ID, SA.SCM_SITE_ID, MAX(SA.DEPT_ID) AS DEPT_ID
15256	                      FROM SCM_MST_SITE_ITEM SA
15257	                     INNER JOIN SCM_MST_SSO_DEPT_INFO SD
15258	                        ON SD.DEPT_ID  = SA.DEPT_ID
15259	                       AND SD.VALID_YN = 'Y'
15260	                     WHERE SA.DEPT_ID IS NOT NULL
15261	                     GROUP BY SA.DIVISION_ID, SA.SCM_SITE_ID) IM
15262	            ON MS.DIVISION_ID = IM.DIVISION_ID
15263	            AND MS.SCM_SITE_ID = IM.SCM_SITE_ID
15264	
15265	        INNER JOIN SCM_MST_SSO_DEPT_INFO DI
15266	            ON DI.DEPT_ID = IM.DEPT_ID
15267	            AND DI.VALID_YN = 'Y'
15268	
15269	        INNER JOIN SCM_MST_PROJECT MP
15270	            ON MP.DIVISION_ID = SP.DIVISION_ID
15271	            AND MP.PROJECT_CODE = SP.PROJECT_CODE
15272	        INNER JOIN SCM_MST_SITE_ITEM SI
15273	            ON SI.DIVISION_ID = SP.DIVISION_ID
15274	            AND SI.PROJECT_CODE = SP.PROJECT_CODE
15275	            AND SI.SCM_SITE_ID = SP.SCM_SITE_ID
15276	            AND SI.ITEM_ID = SP.ITEM_ID
15277	            AND SI.VALID_YN = 'Y'
15278	        INNER JOIN TEMP_VERSION_NO T_VER--DIV별 최신버전(2024.10.04)
15279	            ON SP.DIVISION_ID = T_VER.DIVISION_ID
15280	            AND SP.VERSION_NO = T_VER.VERSION_NO
15281	        LEFT JOIN SCM_MST_SSO_DEPT_INFO TEAM
15282	            ON TEAM.DEPT_ID = SI.DEPT_ID
15283	        WHERE SP.DIVISION_ID = p_division_id
15284	        AND SP.QTY <> 0
15285	        AND SP.MEASURE = 'RTF_SYS_RP'   /*[E20260923-XLSFORMMEASUREOFF] 임시 합집합(MKT_FCST 추가) 해제 — AS-IS 파리티 복귀 (사용자 지시 2026-09-23).
15286	           AS-IS DMF0020_GET_SALES_PRICE_TEMPLATE L105/L150 과 동일한 단일 measure.
15287	           건수 부족의 원인은 measure 가 아니라 컷오프 스냅샷 결함이었다 — 원장 RTF_SYS_RP 6,152행 정상인데
15288	           컷오프 SP_W_20260923_V001(13:12)은 RTF_PR 0 / SalesAmt_* 0 / RTF_SYS_RP 8 로 RTF·금액 계열 5종이 통째 누락
15289	           (원장 6,152행 전부 13:12 갱신 = 컷오프가 금액배치 전/중에 읽음). 근본 해소 = 컷오프 재수행. */
15290	        AND SP.YYYYMMDD >= T_VER.YYYYMM || '01'
15291	        AND SP.YYYYMMDD < TO_CHAR(FN_NEXT_DAY(TO_DATE(T_VER.YYYYMMDD, 'YYYYMMDD'), 'MON') - 7, 'YYYYMMDD')
15292	    ) A
15293	    GROUP BY
15294	    A.DIVISION_ID
15295	    , A.DEPT_ID
15296	    , A.DEPT_NAME_KO
15297	    , A.END_CUSTOMER
15298	    , A.PROJECT_CODE
15299	    , A.PROJECT_NAME
15300	    , A.ITEM_ID
15301	    , A.SCM_SITE_ID
15302	    , A.EDIT_YN
15303	    /* [E20260923-XLSFORMSORT] 정렬 신설 (사용자 지시 2026-09-23) — 양식 표시 컬럼 순서와 동일.
15304	       AS-IS 원문(DMF0020_GET_SALES_PRICE_TEMPLATE)에는 ORDER BY 가 없어 순서가 비결정적이었다.
15305	       Currency 는 MAX() 집계값이라 정렬 키 제외. Team 미지정(NULL) 행은 뒤로 보낸다. */
15306	    ORDER BY DIVISION_ID, DEPT_NAME NULLS LAST, END_CUSTOMER, PROJECT_CODE, ITEM_ID
15307	    ;
15308	
15309	    OPEN o_result_cursor FOR
15310	        SELECT '0' AS RESULT_CODE, 'SUCCESS' AS RESULT_MSG, TO_CHAR(n_key_cnt) AS EVENT
15311	             , V_MONTH_LIST AS MONTH_LIST
15312	        FROM DUAL;
15313	
15314	EXCEPTION
15315	    WHEN OTHERS THEN
15316	        V_SQL_CODE   := TO_CHAR(SQLCODE);
15317	        V_SQL_ERRMSG := SQLERRM;
15318	        V_PROC_INPUT := JSON_OBJECT(
15319	            'p_division_id'    VALUE SUBSTR(p_division_id, 1, 50)
15320	          , 'p_ses_user_id'    VALUE SUBSTR(p_ses_user_id, 1, 50)
15321	          , 'p_from_yyyymmdd'  VALUE SUBSTR(p_from_yyyymmdd, 1, 50)
15322	          , 'p_to_yyyymmdd'    VALUE SUBSTR(p_to_yyyymmdd, 1, 50));
15323	        V_BACKTRACE  := DBMS_UTILITY.FORMAT_ERROR_BACKTRACE;
15324	
15325	        BEGIN
15326	            SCM_MGR.PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH(
15327	                V_PROGRAM_NAME, 'ERROR', n_log_count, 'EXCEPTION',
15328	                '', SYSDATE, V_PROC_INPUT, V_SQL_CODE, V_SQL_ERRMSG, V_BACKTRACE
15329	            );
15330	        END;
15331	
15332	        RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(-20001,
15333	          '[' || V_PROGRAM_NAME || '] SQLCODE=' || V_SQL_CODE ||
15334	          ' / SQLERRM=' || V_SQL_ERRMSG ||
15335	          ' / BACKTRACE=' || DBMS_UTILITY.FORMAT_ERROR_BACKTRACE ||
15336	          ' / PARAMS=' || V_PROC_INPUT
15337	        );
15338	        RAISE;
15339	
15340	END P_DMF0082_GET_EXCEL_FORM_DATA;

15342	
15343	-- ====================================================================================================
15344	-- [2026.08.15 AJS 신뢰도검사 발견1] MSSQL DMF0082_POPUP_GET_SPREAD_TITLE_DATE (원본 34~50행) 이관.
15345	--   기존에는 SCM_MGR 에 대응 객체가 없고 SCMC(전환 테스트용 스키마)에만 남아 있었다.
15346	--   본문은 MSSQL 원본과 동일하며, 표준 예외 처리와 결과 커서만 패키지 규약에 맞춘다.
15347	-- ====================================================================================================
15348	PROCEDURE P_DMF0082_POPUP_GET_SPREAD_TITLE_DATE
15349	(
15350	    p_start_yyyymmdd  IN  VARCHAR2
15351	  , p_end_yyyymmdd    IN  VARCHAR2
15352	  , p_created_by      IN  VARCHAR2
15353	  , O_RC1             OUT SYS_REFCURSOR
15354	  , O_RESULT_CURSOR   OUT SYS_REFCURSOR
15355	)
15356	AS
15357	/*
15358	 * Program ID   : DMF0082_POPUP_GET_SPREAD_TITLE_DATE
15359	 * Description  :
15360	 * Program History
15361	 *===================================================================================================
15362	 *   Date       In Charge      Description
15363	 *===================================================================================================
15364	 * 2025-02-25   dsiol     Initial Release [PJ2025-50017]LGES SCM DM 소형사업부 매출계획 동기화 개선
15365	 * 2026.08.15   AJS       신뢰도 검사 — MSSQL 원본 미이관분 이관
15366	 */
15367	    V_PROGRAM_NAME  VARCHAR2(200 CHAR);
15368	    V_PROC_INPUT    VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR);
15369	    V_BACKTRACE     VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR);
15370	    V_SQL_CODE      VARCHAR2(100 CHAR);
15371	    V_SQL_ERRMSG    VARCHAR2(1000 CHAR);
15372	    V_LOG_TYPE      VARCHAR2(100 CHAR);
15373	    V_LOG_MESSAGE   VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR);
15374	    V_DESCRIPTION   VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR);
15375	    N_LOG_COUNT     PLS_INTEGER := 0;
15376	    D_EVENT_DATE    DATE := SYSDATE;
15377	BEGIN
15378	    IF UTL_CALL_STACK.SUBPROGRAM(1).COUNT > 1 THEN
15379	        V_PROGRAM_NAME := $$PLSQL_UNIT || '.' || UTL_CALL_STACK.SUBPROGRAM(1)(2);
15380	    ELSE
15381	        V_PROGRAM_NAME := $$PLSQL_UNIT;
15382	    END IF;
15383	
15384	    OPEN O_RC1 FOR
15385	        SELECT MIN(BASIS_START_DATE)         AS BASIS_START_DATE
15386	             , BASIS_YYYYMM
15387	             , WEEK_NAME
15388	             , SUBSTR(BASIS_START_DATE, 5, 4) AS DATE_SHORT1_NAME
15389	          FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M
15390	         WHERE BASIS_YYYYMMDD >= p_start_yyyymmdd
15391	           AND BASIS_YYYYMMDD <= p_end_yyyymmdd
15392	         GROUP BY BASIS_YYYYMM, WEEK_NAME, SUBSTR(BASIS_START_DATE, 5, 4)
15393	         ORDER BY BASIS_START_DATE;
15394	
15395	    OPEN O_RESULT_CURSOR FOR
15396	        SELECT '0' AS CODE,
15397	               'SUCCESS' AS MSG,
15398	               CASE WHEN 1 = 2 THEN '-' ELSE NULL END AS EVENT
15399	          FROM DUAL;
15400	
15401	EXCEPTION
15402	    WHEN OTHERS THEN
15403	        ROLLBACK;
15404	
15405	        V_LOG_TYPE    := 'ERROR';
15406	        V_LOG_MESSAGE := 'EXCEPTION';
15407	        V_DESCRIPTION := '';
15408	        D_EVENT_DATE  := SYSDATE;
15409	        V_SQL_CODE    := TO_CHAR(SQLCODE);
15410	        V_SQL_ERRMSG  := SQLERRM;
15411	        V_PROC_INPUT  := JSON_OBJECT(
15412	                             'p_start_yyyymmdd' VALUE p_start_yyyymmdd,
15413	                             'p_end_yyyymmdd'   VALUE p_end_yyyymmdd,
15414	                             'p_created_by'     VALUE p_created_by
15415	                         );
15416	        V_BACKTRACE   := DBMS_UTILITY.FORMAT_ERROR_BACKTRACE;
15417	
15418	        BEGIN SCM_MGR.PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH(V_PROGRAM_NAME, V_LOG_TYPE, N_LOG_COUNT, V_LOG_MESSAGE, V_DESCRIPTION, D_EVENT_DATE, V_PROC_INPUT, V_SQL_CODE, V_SQL_ERRMSG, V_BACKTRACE); EXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN NULL; END;
15419	
15420	        RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(
15421	            -20001,
15422	            '[' || V_PROGRAM_NAME || '] SQLCODE=' || V_SQL_CODE ||
15423	            ' / SQLERRM=' || V_SQL_ERRMSG ||
15424	            ' / BACKTRACE=' || V_BACKTRACE ||
15425	            ' / PARAMS=' || V_PROC_INPUT
15426	        );
15427	END P_DMF0082_POPUP_GET_SPREAD_TITLE_DATE;

15429	    -- [2026-09-15 UIPKG] standalone PR_DM_PLN_CFM_TO_MKT_FCST 취합

15527	    -- [E20251223-000672]_굳이 TEMP로 계속 담아 작업을 하지 않아도됨. 간략하게 진행하기 위해 원소스 주석 후 생성한다 (END)

15531	    -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
15532	    -- 1). #T_MAIN 테이블 데이터를 DM_PLN_MKT_FCST 적재기준으로 -> TEMP TABLE[#TEMP_DM_PLN_CFM_MKT_DEMAND_PLAN]에 INSERT (START)
15533	
15534	    INSERT INTO SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM_TEMP_MKT_DEMAND_PLAN
15535	    ( DIVISION_ID, PROJECT_CODE, SCM_SITE_ID, ITEM_ID, DOM_EXP, FCST_TYPE, INCOTERMS, MEASURE, YYYYMMDD, PLANT_SITE_ID
15536	    , QTY )
15537	    SELECT DIVISION_ID, PROJECT_CODE, SCM_SITE_ID, ITEM_ID, DOM_EXP, FCST_TYPE, INCOTERMS, MEASURE, YYYYMMDD, PLANT_SITE_ID
15538	           --[E20260309-000271]_ISNULL처리 추가 (START)
15539	           --, SUM(QTY_PRICE) AS QTY --[E20260309-000271]_원소스 주석처리
15540	           , SUM(NVL(QTY_PRICE, 0)) AS QTY --[E20260309-000271]_ISNULL처리 추가
15541	           --[E20260309-000271]_ISNULL처리 추가 (END)
15542	    FROM SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_T_MAIN  --[E20260922-TMAINFIX] GT_PRDM_T_MAIN->GT_PRDM1_T_MAIN (SAVE 적재 GTT 일치)
15543	    WHERE DIVISION_ID   = v_division_id
15544	    AND   MEASURE       IN ('MKT_FCST', 'SCM_FCST', 'VMI_In_Req')          -- [PJ2025-50188] 기준수요 포함  --[E20260922-TMAINFIX] 'VMI_IN_REQ'->'VMI_In_Req' (Oracle 대소문자 구분, 저장값 실측)
15545	    AND   YYYYMMDD      >= v_plan_start_yyyymmdd
15546	    GROUP BY  DIVISION_ID, PROJECT_CODE, SCM_SITE_ID, ITEM_ID, DOM_EXP, FCST_TYPE, INCOTERMS, MEASURE, YYYYMMDD, PLANT_SITE_ID;
15547	
15548	    DBMS_STATS.GATHER_TABLE_STATS(OWNNAME => 'SCM_MGR', TABNAME => 'GT_PRDM_TEMP_MKT_DEMAND_PLAN', NO_INVALIDATE => FALSE);
15549	
15550	    -- 1). #T_MAIN 테이블 데이터를 DM_PLN_MKT_FCST 적재기준으로 -> TEMP TABLE[#TEMP_DM_PLN_CFM_MKT_DEMAND_PLAN]에 INSERT(END)
15551	    -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
15552	    -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
15553	    -- 2). 당주 월요일 이후 TEMP 테이블에 적재된 데이터와 동일한 DM_PLN_MKT_FCST 데이터를 삭제한다 (START)
15554	
15555	    DELETE SCM_MGR.DM_PLN_MKT_FCST A
15556	    WHERE EXISTS (
15557	        SELECT 1
15558	        FROM SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM_TEMP_MKT_DEMAND_PLAN B
15559	        WHERE A.DIVISION_ID   = B.DIVISION_ID
15560	          AND A.PROJECT_CODE  = B.PROJECT_CODE
15561	          AND A.SCM_SITE_ID   = B.SCM_SITE_ID
15562	          AND A.ITEM_ID       = B.ITEM_ID
15563	          AND A.DOM_EXP       = B.DOM_EXP
15564	          AND A.FCST_TYPE     = B.FCST_TYPE
15565	          AND A.INCOTERMS     = B.INCOTERMS
15566	          AND A.MEASURE       = B.MEASURE
15567	          AND A.YYYYMMDD      = B.YYYYMMDD
15568	          AND A.PLANT_SITE_ID = B.PLANT_SITE_ID
15569	    )
15570	    AND A.DIVISION_ID = v_division_id
15571	    AND A.YYYYMMDD   >= v_plan_start_yyyymmdd
15572	    AND A.MEASURE IN ('MKT_FCST', 'SCM_FCST', 'VMI_In_Req');  --[E20260922-TMAINFIX] 'VMI_IN_REQ'->'VMI_In_Req' (Oracle 대소문자 구분, 저장값 실측)
15573	
15574	    -- 2). 당주 월요일 이후 TEMP 테이블에 적재된 데이터와 동일한 DM_PLN_MKT_FCST 데이터를 삭제한다 (END)
15575	    -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
15576	    -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
15577	    -- 3). TEMP TABLE[#TEMP_DM_PLN_CFM_MKT_DEMAND_PLAN]데이터로 -> MKT FCST 데이터를 적재한다 (START)
15578	
15579	    INSERT INTO SCM_MGR.DM_PLN_MKT_FCST
15580	    ( DIVISION_ID, PROJECT_CODE, SCM_SITE_ID, ITEM_ID, DOM_EXP, FCST_TYPE, INCOTERMS, MEASURE, PLANT_SITE_ID, YYYYMMDD
15581	    , QTY, CREATED_BY, CREATION_DATE, LAST_UPDATED_BY, LAST_UPDATE_DATE )
15582	    SELECT A.DIVISION_ID, A.PROJECT_CODE, A.SCM_SITE_ID, A.ITEM_ID, A.DOM_EXP, A.FCST_TYPE, A.INCOTERMS, A.MEASURE, A.PLANT_SITE_ID, A.YYYYMMDD
15583	         , A.QTY, p_created_by, SYSDATE, p_created_by, SYSDATE
15584	    FROM SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM_TEMP_MKT_DEMAND_PLAN A;
15585	
15586	    -- 3). TEMP TABLE[#TEMP_DM_PLN_CFM_MKT_DEMAND_PLAN]데이터로 -> MKT FCST 데이터를 적재한다 (END)
15587	    -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
15588	    -- 1. 받아온 데이터 기준으로 DM_PLN_MKT_FCST 데이터 동기화 (END)
15589	    --=============================================================================================================================================
15590	    --=============================================================================================================================================
15591	    -- 2. 받아온 데이터 기준으로 SCM_MST_FUTURE_PRICE 데이터 동기화 (START)
15592	    -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
15593	    -- 1). #T_MAIN 테이블 데이터를 DM_PLN_CFM_SELLING_PRICE 적재기준으로 -> TEMP TABLE[#TEMP_DM_PLN_CFM_SELLING_PRICE]에 INSERT (START)
15594	    -- 데이터가 하나 이상인 경우가 존재할 수 있으므로, 평균값으로 저장한다.
15595	
15596	    INSERT INTO SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM_TEMP_SELLING_PRICE
15597	    ( DIVISION_ID, PROJECT_CODE, SCM_SITE_ID, PLANT_SITE_ID, ITEM_ID, YYYYMMDD, CURRENCY, MEASURE, DOM_EXP, FCST_TYPE
15598	    , INCOTERMS, PRICE )
15599	    SELECT A.DIVISION_ID, A.PROJECT_CODE, A.SCM_SITE_ID, A.PLANT_SITE_ID, A.ITEM_ID, A.YYYYMMDD, A.CURRENCY, A.MEASURE, A.DOM_EXP, A.FCST_TYPE
15600	         , A.INCOTERMS
15601	         --[E20260309-000271]_ISNULL처리 추가 (START)
15602	         --, AVG(A.QTY_PRICE) AS PRICE--[E20260309-000271]_원소스 주석처리
15603	         , AVG(NVL(A.QTY_PRICE, 0)) AS PRICE
15604	         --[E20260309-000271]_ISNULL처리 추가 (END)
15605	    FROM SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM1_T_MAIN A  --[E20260922-TMAINFIX] GT_PRDM_T_MAIN->GT_PRDM1_T_MAIN (SAVE 적재 GTT 일치)
15606	    WHERE A.DIVISION_ID = v_division_id
15607	      AND A.MEASURE IN ('LOC', 'KRW', 'USD')
15608	      AND A.YYYYMMDD >= v_plan_start_yyyymmdd
15609	    GROUP BY A.DIVISION_ID, A.PROJECT_CODE, A.SCM_SITE_ID, A.PLANT_SITE_ID, A.ITEM_ID, A.YYYYMMDD, A.CURRENCY, A.MEASURE, A.DOM_EXP, A.FCST_TYPE
15610	           , A.INCOTERMS;
15611	
15612	    DBMS_STATS.GATHER_TABLE_STATS(OWNNAME => 'SCM_MGR', TABNAME => 'GT_PRDM_TEMP_SELLING_PRICE', NO_INVALIDATE => FALSE);
15613	
15614	    -- 1). DM_PLN_CFM_SELLING_PRICE테이블 데이터를 SCM_MST_FUTURE_PRICE 적재기준으로 -> TEMP TABLE[#TEMP_DM_PLN_CFM_SELLING_PRICE]에 INSERT (END)
15615	    -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
15616	    -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
15617	    -- 2). 당주 월요일 이후 TEMP 테이블에 적재된 데이터와 동일한 SCM_MST_FUTURE_PRICE 데이터를 삭제한다 (START)
15618	
15619	    DELETE SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_FUTURE_PRICE A
15620	    WHERE EXISTS (
15621	        SELECT 1
15622	        FROM SCM_MGR.GT_PRDM_TEMP_SELLING_PRICE B
15623	        WHERE A.DIVISION_ID   = B.DIVISION_ID
15624	          AND A.PROJECT_CODE  = B.PROJECT_CODE
15625	          AND A.SCM_SITE_ID   = B.SCM_SITE_ID
15626	          AND A.PLANT_SITE_ID = B.PLANT_SITE_ID
15627	          AND A.ITEM_ID       = B.ITEM_ID
15628	          AND A.YYYYMMDD      = B.YYYYMMDD
15629	          AND A.MEASURE       = B.MEASURE
15630	          AND A.DOM_EXP       = B.DOM_EXP
15631	          AND A.FCST_TYPE     = B.FCST_TYPE
15632	          AND A.INCOTERMS     = B.INCOTERMS
15633	    )
15634	    AND A.DIVISION_ID = v_division_id
15635	    AND A.YYYYMMDD   >= v_plan_start_yyyymmdd
15636	    AND A.MEASURE IN ('LOC', 'KRW', 'USD');
15637	
15638	    -- 2). 당주 월요일 이후 TEMP 테이블에 적재된 데이터와 동일한 SCM_MST_FUTURE_PRICE 데이터를 삭제한다 (END)
15639	    -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
15640	    -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
15641	    -- 3). TEMP TABLE[#TEMP_DM_PLN_CFM_SELLING_PRICE]데이터로 -> SCM_MST_FUTURE_PRICE 데이터를 적재한다 (START)
15642	
15643	    INSERT INTO SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_FUTURE_PRICE
15644	    ( DIVISION_ID, PROJECT_CODE, SCM_SITE_ID, PLANT_SITE_ID, ITEM_ID, YYYYMMDD, CURRENCY, MEASURE, DOM_EXP, FCST_TYPE

15827	    -- [2026-09-15 UIPKG] standalone PR_SCM_MST_EXCHANGE_RATE 취합
15828	PROCEDURE P_PR_SCM_MST_EXCHANGE_RATE

15830	      P_BATCH_YN  VARCHAR2 DEFAULT 'Y'  /* [PJ2025-50188]소형 매출계획 동기화 개선 고도화 : P_BATCH_YN 파라미터 추가하여 배치수행/화면수행 구분 */

15833	    * Program ID   : PR_SCM_MST_EXCHANGE_RATE
15834	    * Description  : Chempion 환율 I/F Procedure
15835	    * PARAMETER    : N/A

15841	    *===================================================================================================
15842	    * 2021.05.13   P.C.I          PJ20209642R Chempion 환율 I/F
15843	    * 2022.03.25   sclee          PJ20216891 LGES Digital SCM 3 Level 가속화를 위한 개선 - 새사업부 추가
15844	    * 2022.05.11   HWI1993        GM JV 수정
15845	    * 2022.11.17   kdw2819        [C20221116-000471]환율정보 수신 배치 오류
15846	    * 2022.11.28   chano0612      [C20221124-000290]환율 IF관련 수정 요청 건
15847	    * 2025.06.24   kjiyoon        [PJ2025-50188]소형 매출계획 동기화 개선 고도화 : 사용자 환율팝업 적용 - 화면 호출용 프로시저 수행 성공/실패 output 추가

15849	    * 2025.09.15   김준규           IT20250117A NERP ROLLIN 3차 현재 CNY를 RMB로 변경 저장하는 것을 CNY 그대로 저장변경
15850	    * 2026.03.24   김준규           [PJ20264226] NextSCM Pilot 설계 및 개발
15851	 * 2026.07.06	ZIONEX		[PJ202650304] NextSCM Migration
15852	    */
15853	 AS
15854	    /* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
15855	    C_DIVISION_ID       VARCHAR2(30 CHAR);
15856	    C_SCENARIO_TYPE_CD  VARCHAR2(06 CHAR);
15857	    C_PLN_PERIOD_YYYYMM VARCHAR2(06 CHAR);
15858	    C_CURRENCY_CD       VARCHAR2(15 CHAR);
15859	    C_IF_SEQ            NUMBER(16, 0);
15860	    V_RECEIVE_CNT       NUMBER(16, 0);
15861	    V_BATCH_USER        VARCHAR2(150 CHAR) := 'scm_batch';
15862	    V_PROGRAM_NAME      VARCHAR2(150 CHAR) := $$PLSQL_UNIT;
15863	    V_LOG_TYPE          VARCHAR2(30 CHAR);
15864	    N_LOG_COUNT         INTEGER := 0;
15865	    V_LOG_MESSAGE       VARCHAR2(1000 CHAR);
15866	    V_DESCRIPTION       VARCHAR2(500 CHAR);
15867	    D_EVENT_DATE        DATE;
15868	    N_HISTORY_SEQ       NUMBER;
15869	    V_IFSEQNO           VARCHAR2(30 CHAR);
15870	    V_IN_PARA_DATA      VARCHAR2(4000 CHAR);
15871	    V_RESULT_CURSOR     SYS_REFCURSOR;
15872	    CUR_E               SYS_REFCURSOR;
15873	
15874	    CURSOR CUR_D IS
15875	        SELECT DIVISION_ID
15876	          FROM GT_SMER_I_DIVISION;
15877	BEGIN
15878	
15879	    /* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
15880	    /* LOG 용 변수 설정 */
15881	    /* 시작 LOG */
15882	    V_LOG_TYPE    := 'START';
15883	    V_LOG_MESSAGE := '########## Start of procedure ##########';
15884	    V_DESCRIPTION := ('P_BATCH_YN = ' || NVL(P_BATCH_YN, 'X')); /* [PJ2025-50188]소형 매출계획 동기화 개선 고도화 : P_BATCH_YN 파라미터 추가 */
15885	    D_EVENT_DATE  := SYSTIMESTAMP;
15886	    PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH(V_PROGRAM_NAME, V_LOG_TYPE, N_LOG_COUNT, V_LOG_MESSAGE, V_DESCRIPTION, D_EVENT_DATE);
15887	
15888	    /* GTT 초기화 : TRUNCATE 는 DDL 이라 암시 COMMIT 이 발생해 상위 트랜잭션의 ROLLBACK 을 무력화한다 -> DELETE 사용 */
15889	    DELETE FROM GT_SMER_I_DIVISION;
15890	
15891	    /* 자동차, ESS, 소형 작업 */
15892	    /* [PJ2025-50188]소형 매출계획 동기화 개선 고도화 : P_BATCH_YN 파라미터 추가 START */
15893	    /* 기존로직 주석처리 */
15894	    /* INSERT +NO_PARALLEL INTO GT_SMER_I_DIVISION (division_id) */
15895	    /* SELECT DIVISION_ID */
15896	    /* FROM SCM_MST_DIVISION */
15897	    /* WHERE ATTRIBUTE3 IN ('40','81', '82' ) */
15898	    IF P_BATCH_YN = 'Y' THEN
15899	        /* 대상 사업부 */
15900	        INSERT
15901	          INTO GT_SMER_I_DIVISION
15902	             ( DIVISION_ID )
15903	        SELECT DIVISION_ID
15904	          FROM SCM_MST_DIVISION
15905	         WHERE ATTRIBUTE3 IN ('81', '82');
15906	
15907	        /* 대상 IF 환율 */
15908	
15909	        /* CUR_E CURSOR LOCAL FOR */
15910	        OPEN CUR_E FOR
15911	        SELECT SCENARIO_TYPE_CD
15912	             , PLN_PERIOD_YYYYMM
15913	             , CURRENCY_CD
15914	             , IF_SEQ
15915	          FROM (SELECT E.SCENARIO_TYPE_CD
15916	                     , E.PLN_PERIOD_YYYYMM
15917	                     , E.CURRENCY_CD
15918	                     , E.IF_SEQ
15919	                  FROM XIF_GSC_MOVE_EXCHANGE_RATE_SN E
15920	                 WHERE NVL(E.TRANSF_FLAG, 'N') = 'N'
15921	                   AND SCENARIO_TYPE_CD IN ('ML', 'RF') /* [C20221124-000290] SCENARIO_TYPE_CD를 ML, RF만 등록한다.. */
15922	                 GROUP BY E.SCENARIO_TYPE_CD, E.PLN_PERIOD_YYYYMM, E.CURRENCY_CD, E.IF_SEQ) X
15923	         ORDER BY PLN_PERIOD_YYYYMM, CURRENCY_CD, IF_SEQ, SCENARIO_TYPE_CD;
15924	    ELSE
15925	        /* 대상 사업부 */
15926	        INSERT
15927	          INTO GT_SMER_I_DIVISION
15928	             ( DIVISION_ID )
15929	        SELECT DIVISION_ID
15930	          FROM SCM_MST_DIVISION
15931	         WHERE ATTRIBUTE3 IN ('40');
15932	
15933	        /* 대상 IF 환율 */
15934	
15935	        /* CUR_E CURSOR LOCAL FOR */
15936	        OPEN CUR_E FOR
15937	        SELECT SCENARIO_TYPE_CD
15938	             , PLN_PERIOD_YYYYMM
15939	             , CURRENCY_CD
15940	             , IF_SEQ
15941	          FROM (SELECT E.SCENARIO_TYPE_CD
15942	                     , E.PLN_PERIOD_YYYYMM
15943	                     , E.CURRENCY_CD
15944	                     , E.IF_SEQ
15945	                  FROM XIF_GSC_MOVE_EXCHANGE_RATE_SN E
15946	                 WHERE E.TRANSF_DATE = (SELECT MAX(A.TRANSF_DATE)
15947	                                          FROM XIF_GSC_MOVE_EXCHANGE_RATE_SN A
15948	                                         WHERE 1 = 1
15949	                                           AND A.SCENARIO_TYPE_CD IN ('ML', 'RF'))
15950	                   AND SCENARIO_TYPE_CD IN ('ML', 'RF') /* [C20221124-000290] SCENARIO_TYPE_CD를 ML, RF만 등록한다.. */
15951	                 GROUP BY E.SCENARIO_TYPE_CD, E.PLN_PERIOD_YYYYMM, E.CURRENCY_CD, E.IF_SEQ) X
15952	         ORDER BY PLN_PERIOD_YYYYMM, CURRENCY_CD, IF_SEQ, SCENARIO_TYPE_CD;
15953	    END IF;
15954	
15955	    /* [PJ2025-50188]소형 매출계획 동기화 개선 고도화 : P_BATCH_YN 파라미터 추가 END */
15956	    /* DELETE FROM DM_MST_EXCHANGE_RATE WHERE YYYYMMDD BETWEEN TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE, 0), 'YYYYMMDD') || '01' AND TO_CHAR(ADD_MONTHS(SYSDATE, 12), 'YYYYMMDD') || '01' */
15957	    /* [C20221124-000290] DM_MST_EXCHANGE_RATE 에 존재하는 기존 데이터를 삭제한다. */
15958	    /* 위로 로직 이동해서 아래 CURSOR 주석처리 - [PJ2025-50188]소형 매출계획 동기화 개선 고도화 : P_BATCH_YN 파라미터 추가 */
15959	    /* CURSOR 환율 정보 수신 (KEY : SCENARIO_TYPE_CD, PLN_PERIOD_YYYYMM, CURRENCY_CD, IF_SEQ) */
15960	
15961	    /* CUR_E CURSOR LOCAL FOR */
15962	    /* select SCENARIO_TYPE_CD, PLN_PERIOD_YYYYMM, CURRENCY_CD, IF_SEQ */
15963	    /* from ( select E.SCENARIO_TYPE_CD */
15964	    /*           , E.PLN_PERIOD_YYYYMM */
15965	    /*           , E.CURRENCY_CD */
15966	    /*           , E.IF_SEQ */
15967	    /*       from XIF_GSC_MOVE_EXCHANGE_RATE_SN E */
15968	
15969	    /*       AND SCENARIO_TYPE_CD IN ('ML', 'RF') [C20221124-000290] SCENARIO_TYPE_CD를 ML, RF만 등록한다.. */
15970	    /*       group by E.SCENARIO_TYPE_CD */
15971	    /*           , E.PLN_PERIOD_YYYYMM */
15972	    /*           , E.CURRENCY_CD */
15973	    /*           , E.IF_SEQ */
15974	    /*           ) x */
15975	    /* ORDER BY PLN_PERIOD_YYYYMM, CURRENCY_CD, IF_SEQ, SCENARIO_TYPE_CD */
15976	    /* CURSOR 환율 정보 수신 (KEY : SCENARIO_TYPE_CD, PLN_PERIOD_YYYYMM, CURRENCY_CD, IF_SEQ) */
15977	
15978	    /* CUR_D CURSOR FOR */
15979	
15980	    NULL;
15981	
15982	    /* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
15983	
15984	    /* Start 환율 Cursor */
15985	    V_RECEIVE_CNT := 0;
15986	
15987	    FETCH CUR_E
15988	        INTO C_SCENARIO_TYPE_CD, C_PLN_PERIOD_YYYYMM, C_CURRENCY_CD, C_IF_SEQ;
15989	    WHILE (CUR_E%FOUND) LOOP
15990	        /* ----------------------------------------------------------------- */
15991	        /* * IT20250117A NERP ROLLIN 3차 현재 CNY를 RMB로 변경 저장하는 것을 CNY 그대로 저장변경 시작 */
15992	        /* 변경전 상태 USING ( SELECT B.DIVISION_ID, A.YYYYMMDD, A.CURRENCY, REPLACE(A.UNIT_CURRENCY, 'CNY', 'RMB') UNIT_CURRENCY, A.EXCHANGE_PRICE */
15993	        MERGE INTO DM_MST_EXCHANGE_RATE DME
15994	        USING (SELECT B.DIVISION_ID
15995	                    , A.YYYYMMDD
15996	                    , A.CURRENCY
15997	                    , /*REPLACE(A.UNIT_CURRENCY, 'CNY', 'RMB')*/ A.UNIT_CURRENCY AS UNIT_CURRENCY
15998	                    , A.EXCHANGE_PRICE
15999	                 FROM (SELECT PLN_YYYYMM || '01' AS YYYYMMDD
16000	                            , 'KRW' AS CURRENCY
16001	                            , CURRENCY_CD UNIT_CURRENCY
16002	                            , KRW_CNVT_RATE EXCHANGE_PRICE
16003	                         FROM XIF_GSC_MOVE_EXCHANGE_RATE_SN
16004	                        WHERE SCENARIO_TYPE_CD = C_SCENARIO_TYPE_CD
16005	                          AND PLN_PERIOD_YYYYMM = C_PLN_PERIOD_YYYYMM
16006	                          AND CURRENCY_CD = C_CURRENCY_CD
16007	                          AND IF_SEQ = C_IF_SEQ
16008	                        UNION ALL
16009	                       SELECT PLN_YYYYMM || '01' AS YYYYMMDD
16010	                            , 'USD' AS CURRENCY
16011	                            , CURRENCY_CD UNIT_CURRENCY
16012	                            , USD_CNVT_RATE EXCHANGE_PRICE
16013	                         FROM XIF_GSC_MOVE_EXCHANGE_RATE_SN
16014	                        WHERE SCENARIO_TYPE_CD = C_SCENARIO_TYPE_CD
16015	                          AND PLN_PERIOD_YYYYMM = C_PLN_PERIOD_YYYYMM
16016	                          AND CURRENCY_CD = C_CURRENCY_CD
16017	                          AND IF_SEQ = C_IF_SEQ
16018	                        UNION ALL
16019	                       SELECT PLN_YYYYMM || '01' AS YYYYMMDD
16020	                            , 'EUR' AS CURRENCY
16021	                            , CURRENCY_CD UNIT_CURRENCY
16022	                            , EUR_CNVT_RATE EXCHANGE_PRICE
16023	                         FROM XIF_GSC_MOVE_EXCHANGE_RATE_SN
16024	                        WHERE SCENARIO_TYPE_CD = C_SCENARIO_TYPE_CD
16025	                          AND PLN_PERIOD_YYYYMM = C_PLN_PERIOD_YYYYMM
16026	                          AND CURRENCY_CD = C_CURRENCY_CD
16027	                          AND IF_SEQ = C_IF_SEQ) A
16028	                    , GT_SMER_I_DIVISION B) XXX
16029	        /* * IT20250117A NERP ROLLIN 3차 현재 CNY를 RMB로 변경 저장하는 것을 CNY 그대로 저장변경 종료 */
16030	        ON (DME.DIVISION_ID = XXX.DIVISION_ID AND DME.YYYYMMDD = XXX.YYYYMMDD AND DME.CURRENCY = XXX.CURRENCY AND DME.UNIT_CURRENCY = XXX.UNIT_CURRENCY)
16031	        WHEN MATCHED THEN
16032	            UPDATE SET DME.EXCHANGE_PRICE = XXX.EXCHANGE_PRICE
16033	                     , DME.LAST_UPDATED_BY = 'SYSTEM'
16034	                     , DME.LAST_UPDATE_DATE = SYSDATE
16035	        WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN
16036	            INSERT
16037	                 ( DIVISION_ID
16038	                 , YYYYMMDD
16039	                 , CURRENCY
16040	                 , UNIT_CURRENCY
16041	                 , EXCHANGE_PRICE
16042	                 , CREATED_BY
16043	                 , CREATION_DATE
16044	                 , LAST_UPDATED_BY
16045	                 , LAST_UPDATE_DATE )
16046	            VALUES ( XXX.DIVISION_ID
16047	                   , XXX.YYYYMMDD
16048	                   , XXX.CURRENCY
16049	                   , XXX.UNIT_CURRENCY
16050	                   , XXX.EXCHANGE_PRICE
16051	                   , 'SYSTEM'
16052	                   , SYSDATE
16053	                   , 'SYSTEM'
16054	                   , SYSDATE );
16055	        /* ----------------------------------------------------------------- */
16056	        /* 배치일때만 업데이트하도록 조건추가 - [PJ2025-50188]소형 매출계획 동기화 개선 고도화 : P_BATCH_YN 파라미터 추가 */
16057	        IF P_BATCH_YN = 'Y' THEN
16058	            /* I/F Table TRANSF_FLAG 변경 */
16059	            UPDATE XIF_GSC_MOVE_EXCHANGE_RATE_SN
16060	               SET TRANSF_FLAG = 'Y'
16061	             WHERE SCENARIO_TYPE_CD = C_SCENARIO_TYPE_CD
16062	               AND PLN_PERIOD_YYYYMM = C_PLN_PERIOD_YYYYMM
16063	               AND CURRENCY_CD = C_CURRENCY_CD
16064	               AND IF_SEQ = C_IF_SEQ;
16065	        END IF;
16066	
16067	        V_RECEIVE_CNT := V_RECEIVE_CNT + 1;
16068	        /* ----------------------------------------------------------------- */
16069	        FETCH CUR_E
16070	            INTO C_SCENARIO_TYPE_CD, C_PLN_PERIOD_YYYYMM, C_CURRENCY_CD, C_IF_SEQ;
16071	    END LOOP;
16072	    CLOSE CUR_E;
16073	
16074	    /* End  환율 Cursor */
16075	    /* Start  Sales Price, Sales Plan Cursor */
16076	    /* select 'V_RECEIVE_CNT', V_RECEIVE_CNT */
16077	    IF (V_RECEIVE_CNT > 0) THEN
16078	
16079	        OPEN CUR_D;
16080	        FETCH CUR_D
16081	            INTO C_DIVISION_ID;
16082	        WHILE (CUR_D%FOUND) LOOP
16083	
16084	            /* select 'C_DIVISION_ID', C_DIVISION_ID */
16085	            /* Sales Price Update */
16086	            PKG_DMF0020.P_DMF0020_SAVE_SCM_MST_FUTURE_PRICE_CONVERSION(C_DIVISION_ID, V_RESULT_CURSOR);   /* [E20260824-PKGCONSOL] 단독 SP -> PKG 내부 프로시저. 시그니처 동일(2-arg) 실측 확인 */
16087	
16088	            /* Sales Plan Update */
16089	            PR_DM_ANL_SALES_PLAN_AMT_CREATE_LOCAL_KRW(C_DIVISION_ID, 'EXCHANGE_RATE');
16090	
16091	            FETCH CUR_D
16092	                INTO C_DIVISION_ID;
16093	        END LOOP;
16094	        CLOSE CUR_D;
16095	
16096	    END IF;
16097	    /* End  Sales Price, Sales Plan Cursor */
16098	    /* 트랜잭션 종료 */
16099	    /* 종료 LOG */
16100	    V_LOG_TYPE    := 'END';
16101	    V_LOG_MESSAGE := '########## END of procedure ##########';
16102	    V_DESCRIPTION := NULL;
16103	    D_EVENT_DATE  := SYSTIMESTAMP;
16104	
16105	    PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH(V_PROGRAM_NAME, V_LOG_TYPE, N_LOG_COUNT, V_LOG_MESSAGE, V_DESCRIPTION, D_EVENT_DATE);
16106	
16107	    COMMIT;
16108	    NULL; /* [PJ2025-50188]소형 매출계획 동기화 개선 고도화 : 사용자 환율팝업 적용 */
16109	
16110	
16111	EXCEPTION
16112	    WHEN OTHERS THEN
16113	
16114	        /* ERROR LOG */
16115	        V_LOG_TYPE    := 'ERROR';
16116	        V_LOG_MESSAGE := SUBSTR(SQLERRM, 1, 1000);
16117	        V_DESCRIPTION := 'ERROR_LINE : ' || TO_CHAR(NULL);
16118	        D_EVENT_DATE  := SYSTIMESTAMP;
16119	
16120	        PR_SCM_SYS_LOG_BATCH(V_PROGRAM_NAME, V_LOG_TYPE, N_LOG_COUNT, V_LOG_MESSAGE, V_DESCRIPTION, D_EVENT_DATE);
16121	
16122	        NULL;
16123	        /* [PJ2025-50188]소형 매출계획 동기화 개선 고도화 : 사용자 환율팝업 적용 */
16124	
16125	
16126	        RAISE_APPLICATION_ERROR(-20001
16127	                               ,'[' || $$PLSQL_UNIT || '] SQLCODE=' || SQLCODE || ' / SQLERRM=' || SQLERRM || ' / BACKTRACE=' ||
16128	                                DBMS_UTILITY.FORMAT_ERROR_BACKTRACE || ' / PARAMS=' || V_IN_PARA_DATA);
16129	END P_PR_SCM_MST_EXCHANGE_RATE;
16130	
16131	    -- [2026-09-15 UIPKG] standalone PR_SCM_MST_EXCHANGE_RATE_UI 취합
16132	PROCEDURE P_PR_SCM_MST_EXCHANGE_RATE_UI (
16133	    P_BATCH_YN      IN  VARCHAR2 DEFAULT 'N',
16134	    O_RESULT_CURSOR OUT SYS_REFCURSOR
16135	) AS
16136	    V_ERR        VARCHAR2(200 CHAR);
16137	    V_TARGET_CNT NUMBER;
16138	BEGIN
16139	    --[E20260915-BPSIFNODATA] 반영 대상 0건 안내 (사용자 지시 2026-09-15).
16140	    --  공유 배치 PR_SCM_MST_EXCHANGE_RATE 는 대상이 없어도 오류 없이 끝나 이 래퍼가 항상 success 를 돌려줬고,
16141	    --  화면은 "정상 반영" 토스트 후 값이 그대로여서 반영된 것으로 오인됐다(사내 XIF 0행 실측).
16142	    --  → 배치 CUR_E 와 **동일 조건**으로 대상 건수를 먼저 세고, 0건이면 배치를 호출하지 않고 안내를 돌려준다.
16143	    --  CODE <> '0' 이면 FE(ExchangeRateModal handleBpsIf)가 RESULT_MSG 를 Alert 로 표시한다 — FE 무변경.
16144	    --  공유 배치 본문은 무수정(야간 배치 P_BATCH_YN='Y' 경로 영향 없음).
16145	    IF P_BATCH_YN = 'Y' THEN
16146	        SELECT COUNT(*)
16147	          INTO V_TARGET_CNT
16148	          FROM SCM_MGR.XIF_GSC_MOVE_EXCHANGE_RATE_SN E
16149	         WHERE NVL(E.TRANSF_FLAG, 'N') = 'N'
16150	           AND E.SCENARIO_TYPE_CD IN ('ML', 'RF');
16151	    ELSE
16152	        SELECT COUNT(*)
16153	          INTO V_TARGET_CNT
16154	          FROM SCM_MGR.XIF_GSC_MOVE_EXCHANGE_RATE_SN E
16155	         WHERE E.TRANSF_DATE = (SELECT MAX(A.TRANSF_DATE)
16156	                                  FROM SCM_MGR.XIF_GSC_MOVE_EXCHANGE_RATE_SN A
16157	                                 WHERE A.SCENARIO_TYPE_CD IN ('ML', 'RF'))
16158	           AND E.SCENARIO_TYPE_CD IN ('ML', 'RF');
16159	    END IF;
16160	
16161	    IF V_TARGET_CNT = 0 THEN
16162	        OPEN O_RESULT_CURSOR FOR
16163	            SELECT '1' AS CODE, '반영할 환율 정보가 없습니다' AS RESULT_MSG, CAST(NULL AS VARCHAR2(200 CHAR)) AS EVENT FROM DUAL;
16164	        RETURN;
16165	    END IF;
16166	
16167	    P_PR_SCM_MST_EXCHANGE_RATE(P_BATCH_YN);
16168	    OPEN O_RESULT_CURSOR FOR
16169	        SELECT '0' AS CODE, 'success' AS RESULT_MSG, CAST(NULL AS VARCHAR2(200 CHAR)) AS EVENT FROM DUAL;
16170	EXCEPTION
16171	    WHEN OTHERS THEN
16172	        V_ERR := SUBSTR(SQLERRM, 1, 200);
16173	        ROLLBACK;
16174	        OPEN O_RESULT_CURSOR FOR
16175	            SELECT '9' AS CODE, 'fail' AS RESULT_MSG, V_ERR AS EVENT FROM DUAL;
16176	END P_PR_SCM_MST_EXCHANGE_RATE_UI;
16177	
16178	END PKG_DMF0082;
